### [1] [Novel Dynamics in Models of Angiogenesis with p-Laplacian diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07675)
*Wenbo Zhang,Hossein Asgaribakhtiari,Aishwarya Pawar,Rana D. Parshad*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个经典的血管生成（angiogenesis）两物种数学模型，包含细胞和VEGF种群。与传统模型使用正常扩散（p=2）不同，该研究假设细胞遵循p-Laplacian扩散（包括快扩散1<p<2和慢扩散p>2），并发现这一改进可产生多种新颖的动力学行为，如双峰/多峰解、规律性增益、防止有限时间爆破、有限时间灭绝以及图灵斑图，并将其应用于心脏健康的数字孪生框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺血性心脏病是全球最主要的死亡原因，血运重建（重新恢复堵塞血管的血流）显示出治疗前景。血管生成的数学模型已被广泛研究，但经典模型通常假设正常扩散。作者希望通过引入p-Laplacian扩散来更好地刻画细胞运动的非线性特征，探索其能否带来更丰富的、此前未被发现的动力学行为，从而为心脏健康的数字孪生模拟提供更精确的模型基础。

Method: 采用两物种（细胞与VEGF）的血管生成经典模型，将细胞扩散项替换为p-Laplacian扩散算子，涵盖三种情况：快扩散（1<p<2）、正常扩散（p=2）和慢扩散（p>2）。首先在弱意义下证明系统在p>3/2且初始数据足够小时的良好适定性；随后通过理论分析揭示p-Laplacian引发的多种新颖动力学现象，包括双峰和多峰稳态解、正则性增强、有限时间爆破的抑制、有限时间灭绝及图灵斑图形成。

Result: 证明了当p>3/2时，系统在足够小的初始数据下具有弱解的适定性。更重要的是，p-Laplacian扩散带来了多种经典模型（p=2）未报道的新动力学行为：1) 通过双峰和多峰解增强细胞增殖；2) 获得正则性增益；3) 防止有限时间爆破；4) 通过有限时间灭绝实现细胞耗竭；5) 产生图灵斑图。这些结果在心脏健康的数字孪生框架中具有潜在应用价值。

Conclusion: p-Laplacian扩散为血管生成模型引入了比经典正常扩散更丰富的动力学行为，提供了更灵活和精确的建模框架。这些发现不仅扩展了对血管生成过程的理论理解，还通过数字孪生框架为缺血性心脏病的血运重建策略提供了新的数值模拟和预测工具。

Abstract: Ischemic heart diseases represent the leading cause of mortality worldwide. Revascularization, the process to restore blood flow in blockages, shows promise. To this end, mathematical models for angiogenesis, the process by which new blood vessels form from existing ones, have been extremely well investigated. In the current work, we consider a classical two species model for angiogenesis, consisting of cell and VEGF populations. However, we assume the cells move according to p-Laplacian diffusion, which could be both ``fast" ($1<p<2$) and ``slow" ($p>2$), in addition to normal diffusion ($p=2$). We first show that the system is well posed in a weak sense when $p>\frac{3}{2}$, for sufficiently small initial data. Next, we show that the p-Laplacian can lead to several novel dynamics not reported earlier; these include increased cellular proliferation via bi-modal and multi spike solutions, gain of regularity, prevention of finite time blow-up, cell depletion via finite time extinction, and Turing patterns. We discuss applications of these results for cardiac health via a digital twins framework.

</details>


### [2] [Large time behavior of the solution to the Cauchy problem for viscous Hamilton-Jacobi equation on infinite graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07682)
*Alan A. Tedeev*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限加权图上带有梯度吸收的非线性扩散方程解的长时行为，在多项式体积增长条件下，建立了解的总质量随时间趋于零的锐利衰减估计，并确定了与Fujita型临界指数相一致的阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续欧几里得空间中，带梯度吸收的非线性扩散方程的质量衰减行为已有充分研究，但在离散的加权图结构上这一问题尚缺乏系统的理论分析。理解图上的非线性扩散与吸收耦合过程的长时间演化规律，对于研究离散结构上的物理、生物及网络系统中的动力学过程具有重要意义。

Method: 将离散p-Laplacian扩散项与Hamilton-Jacobi型非线性吸收项相结合，在图的体积满足多项式增长条件及参数满足特定序关系的假设下，利用Hardy不等式与Hölder不等式，结合一个依赖时间的、适配图几何的缩放参数，对解的总质量进行定量估计。

Result: 证明了当吸收指数低于临界阈值时，解的总质量随时间趋于零；该临界指数显式依赖于图的体积增长率与扩散指数，并与连续欧几里得背景下已知的Fujita型阈值一致。该结果在线性扩散情形下也是新的，且适用于整数格和有限生成幂零群的Cayley图等广泛图类。

Conclusion: 本文在离散图结构上建立了质量衰减的锐利估计，确定了临界吸收指数，展示了离散情形与连续情形在Fujita型现象上的统一性，并为更广泛图类上的退化扩散方程研究提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We study the long-time behavior of nonnegative solutions to a nonlinear diffusion equation with gradient absorption on infinite weighted graphs. The equation combines a discrete p-Laplacian diffusion term with a nonlinear absorption term of Hamilton-Jacobi type, modeling processes where both diffusion and nonlinear damping occur on discrete structures. Under the assumptions that the graph satisfies a polynomial volume growth condition and that the parameters satisfy certain ordering relations, we establish sharp decay estimates for the total mass of the solution. We prove that the total mass decays to zero as time tends to infinity whenever the absorption exponent lies below a critical threshold. This critical exponent depends explicitly on the volume growth rate of the graph and the diffusion exponent, and coincides with the Fujita-type threshold known for analogous equations in the continuous Euclidean setting. Our proof combines Hardy and Hölder inequalities with a carefully chosen time-dependent scaling parameter adapted to the graph geometry. The results are new even in the linear diffusion case and extend naturally to a broad class of graphs, including integer lattices and Cayley graphs of finitely generated nilpotent groups.

</details>


### [3] [Relative Faber--Krahn inequalities and Trudinger's equation on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07691)
*Philipp Sürig*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了黎曼流形上的Trudinger方程，证明了相对p-Faber-Krahn不等式等价于体积倍增条件与非负有界弱下解的次高斯上界估计的合取，并在一致p-Faber-Krahn不等式下推导出改进的长时间上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黎曼流形上研究非线性退化抛物型方程（如Trudinger方程）的热核估计，建立几何分析条件（如Faber-Krahn不等式、体积倍增）与解析性质（次高斯热核估计）之间的等价关系，以深入理解流形上非线性扩散过程的行为。

Method: 通过建立p-Faber-Krahn不等式与体积倍增条件之间的联系，并利用这些几何解析工具来估计Trudinger方程非负有界弱下解的衰减行为，从而证明等价性与改进的长时间估计。

Result: 证明了相对p-Faber-Krahn不等式与体积倍增+次高斯上界估计的等价性；在一致p-Faber-Krahn不等式假设下，获得了改进的长时间上界估计。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了非线性p-Laplacian型退化抛物方程（Trudinger方程）的热核估计与流形几何条件之间的等价性刻画，为理解流形上非线性扩散理论提供了新的分析工具和结果。

Abstract: We consider on Riemannian manifolds the Trudinger equation \begin{equation*}\partial _{t}u=Δ_{p}u^{\frac{1}{p-1}},\end{equation*} where $p>1$. We prove that a relative $p$--Faber--Krahn inequality is equivalent to the conjunction of volume doubling and a sub-Gaussian upper estimate for non-negative bounded weak subsolutions of Trudinger's equation. We also derive an improved long-time upper estimate under a uniform $p$--Faber--Krahn inequality.

</details>


### [4] [Transient instability and recurrent aggregation in a two-species chemotaxis-competition system under environmental periodicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07701)
*Federico Herrero-Hervás,Pedro Hernández-Alonso,Mihaela Negreanu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了两物种竞争型趋化系统在承载能力随时间周期变化情况下的重复性斑图形成。通过将非自治ODE系统全局吸引的周期解作为空间均匀基态，推导出第一物种临界趋化敏感性的充分条件；当超过该阈值时，周期态在每个环境周期内的非空时间集合上线性失稳，产生不稳定与稳定区间的循环交替。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统趋化系统的斑图形成研究多假设参数不随时间变化，但真实生态系统中环境周期性波动（如季节变化导致的承载能力波动）普遍存在。理解时间周期性承载能力如何影响趋化驱动的斑图形成，有助于揭示周期性环境中物种空间分布模式的演化机制。

Method: 采用线性稳定性分析方法：以非自治ODE系统的全局吸引周期解为空间均匀基态，推导第一物种临界趋化敏感性χ_crit的充分条件；通过理论分析与数值模拟相结合，在周期共存和竞争排斥两种生态学制度下进行验证。

Result: 证明了当趋化敏感性超过临界阈值时，周期基态在实际时间集合（每个环境周期内的非空时间段）上变得线性不稳定，导致空间扰动在部分时间被放大、其他时间被抑制，形成重复性（周期性）不稳定-稳定区间交替的斑图生成机制。

Conclusion: 研究发现时间周期性承载能力可导致系统的重复性失稳-稳定循环，并讨论了不稳定集合的时间定位以及负临界阈值（即无需正趋化强度即可失稳）的可能性，拓展了对动态环境中趋化斑图形成的理解。

Abstract: This work investigates recurrent pattern formation in a two--species chemotaxis system with competitive dynamics under the assumption that the carrying capacities of both species are periodic in time. By considering a globally attracting periodic solution to the associated nonautonomous ODE system as a spatially homogeneous base state, we derive sufficient conditions for the existence of a critical chemotactic sensitivity of the first species, denoted by $χ_{\text{crit}}$. Whenever this threshold is exceeded, the periodic state becomes linearly unstable over a nonempty set of times within each environmental cycle. This gives rise to a recurrent alternation of unstable and stable intervals, during which spatial perturbations are respectively amplified and damped. The analytical derivation is complemented with numerical simulations, in both periodic coexistence and competitive exclusion regimes. Finally, we discuss the temporal location of the instability sets and the possibility of negative instability thresholds.

</details>


### [5] [From a Scalar Parabolic Oscillator to Topological Thermostats: Selective Feeback Control of Harmonic Flow Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07768)
*Sandro Merino*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Hodge理论的反馈框架，用于处理图上的流值状态。该框架将边空间分解为割空间与调和环空间，理想拓扑温控器调节割分量并以指数衰减抑制调和分量。在有限物理通道实现下，第一Betti数给出最小传感/驱动秩条件，使调和扇区可被精确压缩。作者将该框架应用于传输网络的非线性摆幅闭合系统，在角度同相同步平衡点线性化，分析了被动切向运动对调和线流分量的保持性质、理想闭合的指数衰减特性，以及有限通道实现的压缩与解耦条件。通过theta网络、微电网、环形网络和IEEE 14节点等实例验证了传递调节、环流阻尼与局部化瞬态行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量抛物型恒温控制问题中存在局部传感器与双源结构，作者希望将这类热力学反馈思想推广到图上的流值状态（如传输网络中的线流），建立系统的Hodge理论框架，解决割空间与调和环空间的耦合与控制问题，并回答有限物理通道下能否精确实现理想拓扑控制这一核心问题。

Method: 采用Hodge分解将图的边空间分解为割空间与调和环空间；理想拓扑温控器对割分量进行目标跟踪调节并对调和分量施加阻尼；通过第一Betti数刻画最小传感/驱动秩条件；对传输网络建立理想与有限通道的非线性线驱动摆幅闭合模型，在角度协同同步平衡点线性化完整闭环系统；利用Bregman平衡构造目标中心化的非线性耗散，实现理想度量兼容控制器与协作式有限通道反馈。

Result: 证明被动切向摆幅运动保持任何已有的调和线流分量（在驱动器固定时）；理想闭合将守恒律替换为指数衰减并附加稳定调和块，而不改变约化的被动节点割谱；在通道秩条件下，有限实现可复现调和压缩，但自主调和衰减与完整约化状态恢复需要额外的解耦和Hurwitz条件。theta网络、微电网、环形网络和IEEE 14节点算例验证了传递调节、环流阻尼和局部化瞬态。

Conclusion: Hodge理论框架为流值状态的反馈控制提供了统一的数学结构；最小秩条件仅保证调和扇区的可压缩性，并不保证非局部Hodge投影可本地实现，剩余的割-调交叉块反映溢出与强迫效应。实际设备动力学、约束条件和大规模验证仍是开放问题。

Abstract: This paper develops a Hodge-theoretic feedback framework for flow-valued states, motivated by scalar parabolic thermostat problems with localized sensors and dual sources. On a connected graph, the edge space decomposes into a cut space and a harmonic cycle space. The ideal topological thermostat regulates the cut component toward a prescribed cycle-free transfer target and damps the harmonic component. For realizations through selected physical edge channels, the first Betti number gives the minimal sensing and actuation ranks required for exact compression of the full harmonic sector. Minimal rank does not imply local realization of the nonlocal Hodge projector; the remaining cut-harmonic blocks quantify spillover and forcing.
  For transmission networks, we formulate ideal and finite-channel nonlinear line-actuated swing closures and linearize the complete closed systems at an angle-cohesive synchronous equilibrium. With the actuator fixed, passive tangent swing motion preserves any pre-existing harmonic line-flow component. The ideal closure replaces this conservation law by exponential decay and appends a stable harmonic block without changing the reduced passive nodal-cut spectrum. Under the channel-rank conditions, finite realizations reproduce the harmonic compression, whereas autonomous harmonic decay and full reduced-state recovery require additional decoupling and Hurwitz conditions. A target-centred Bregman balance yields nonlinear dissipation for the ideal metric-compatible controller and for canonical colocated finite-channel feedback. Explicit theta-network, microgrid, ring, and synthetic IEEE 14-bus examples illustrate transfer regulation, cycle-flow damping, and localization-induced transients. Realistic device dynamics, constraints, and large-scale validation remain open.

</details>


### [6] [Sharp non-uniqueness of singular solutions to the one-dimensional periodic cubic nonlinear Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07866)
*Qirui Peng*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过凸积分方法构造了周期三次非线性薛定谔方程（立方NLS）的一个非零奇异弱解，该解在时间上具有紧支撑、初始值为零，且属于空间$C_t^0 H_x^\alpha$（$\alpha<1/6$）和$C_t^0 L_x^p$（$p<3$）。构造中克服了空间常数分量导致的零输出障碍，采用双载子扰动策略，并通过符号绝对Fourier可和性条件定义非线性项。由于$H^{1/6}\hookrightarrow L^3$且无条件唯一性在$C_t^0 H_x^{1/6}$中成立，因此$1/6$这一光滑度阈值在该奇异解类中是尖锐的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NLS方程的研究中，构造奇异弱解并确定其最优正则性阈值是一个核心问题。作者旨在通过凸积分方法构造立方NLS的奇异弱解，并证明$1/6$是正则性的尖锐阈值。与以往工作中可忽略的非线性结构不同，全立方NLS的非线性项会产生空间常数分量，这一"零输出障碍"必须被克服，从而需要新的构造策略。

Method: 采用凸积分方法，改编了Gismondi-Ma-Pathak-Radu的间歇性板块（intermittent slab）基本模块。具体核心策略是双载子扰动（two-carrier perturbation）：利用唯一的零载子-零板块输出相互作用来精确复现全部旧误差（含其零Fourier模式），而其余相互作用在负Wiener范数下是可扰动的。非线性项通过符号绝对Fourier可和性条件定义，并证明它是所有容许Fourier截断的公共极限。

Result: 成功构造了满足$u\in\bigcap_{\alpha<1/6}C_t^0H_x^\alpha\cap\bigcap_{1\leq p<3}C_t^0L_x^p$的非零紧支撑奇异弱解。证明了当奇异解属于$C_t^0L_x^3$时，所定义的非线性项与普通乘积$|u|^2u$一致。利用Guo-Kwon-Oh的无条件唯一性结果及Sobolev嵌入$H^{1/6}\hookrightarrow L^3$，证明了$1/6$是该奇异解类中的尖锐阈值。

Conclusion: 通过双载子扰动策略成功克服了立方NLS的空间常数分量障碍，构造了指定正则性类中的非平凡奇异弱解，并确立了$1/6$为尖锐的正则性阈值。该结果丰富了NLS方程弱解理论，说明了在$H^{1/6}$以下存在奇异弱解，而在该阈值以上由于无条件唯一性而无法构造此类解。

Abstract: We construct, via convex integration, a nonzero singular weak solution of the one-dimensional periodic cubic nonlinear Schrödinger equation that is compactly supported in time, has zero initial datum, and satisfies $u\in\bigcap_{α<1/6} C_t^0 H_x^α\cap \bigcap_{1\leq p<3} C_t^0 L_x^p$. The construction adapts the intermittent slab building block of Gismondi, Ma, Pathak, and Radu. For the full cubic NLS, the spatially constant component generated by the nonlinearity cannot be discarded. We overcome this zero-output obstruction with a two-carrier perturbation: its unique zero-carrier, zero-slab-output interaction reproduces the full old error, including its zero Fourier mode, while the remaining interactions are perturbative in a negative Wiener norm. The nonlinear term is defined through a signed absolute-Fourier summability condition and is the common limit generated by every admissible Fourier cutoff. Whenever a singular solution belongs to $C_t^0 L_x^3$, this nonlinear term agrees with the ordinary product $|u|^2u$. Since $H^{1/6}(\mathbb T)\hookrightarrow L^3(\mathbb T)$ and unconditional uniqueness holds in $C_t^0 H_x^{1/6}$ by a result of Guo, Kwon, and Oh, the threshold $1/6$ is sharp within the singular solution class considered here.

</details>


### [7] [A Vibrating Clamped Plate Resembles a Vibrating Membrane at High Frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07872)
*Or Kuperman,Zhengjiang Lin,Dan Mangoubi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了夹具平板特征值问题，证明了夹具平板特征函数可以在L^2范数下由其振荡分量（满足Helmholtz方程）近似，且在任意紧子域上误差随频率指数衰减。同时建立了夹具平板特征函数边界局域化的上界，与膜特征函数的已知结论相类似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夹具平板特征值问题是一个经典的数学物理问题，理解其特征函数的性质对谱理论、振动分析和相关应用领域具有重要意义。该工作旨在深化对夹具平板特征函数结构的行为理解，特别是其在频率趋向无穷时的渐近行为和边界局域化规律。

Abstract: We study radial nonlinear instability of compactly supported nonrotating equilibria of the three-dimensional Euler--Poisson system with a physical-vacuum boundary. Under general structural assumptions on the pressure law, suppose that $n^u(μ)>0$ and that the equilibrium is not a mass extremum, $M'(μ)\neq0$. Conditional on the existence of a sufficiently regular radial solution on the relevant time interval, we establish two nonlinear escape criteria. First, every perturbation with Hamiltonian strictly below that of the equilibrium exits a fixed neighborhood on a logarithmic time scale controlled by the least unstable linear growth rate. For the subclass of data for which the associated Lyapunov functional is initially nonnegative, we also obtain an explicit exponential lower bound in the weighted displacement norm. Second, sufficiently small perturbations whose Riesz projection onto the finite-dimensional unstable subspace is not too small escape on a logarithmic time scale. The second argument uses the exponential trichotomy of the linearized Hamiltonian flow and an invariant-cone estimate. In the polytropic class, the conditional estimates combine with the radial physical-vacuum local theory to yield unconditional nonlinear instability in the corresponding classical-solution topology. The results complement Jang's nonlinear instability theorem for Lane--Emden stars by treating mechanisms that do not require initial alignment with a leading growing eigenmode and by applying, conditionally, to unstable branches for general equations of state.

Abstract: We study the spatial regularity of regular Lagrangian flows associated with vector fields in the DiPerna--Lions class \(\boldsymbol b\in L^1((0,T);W^{1,1}_{\loc}(\mathbb R^d)), \) under the standard growth and compressibility assumptions. For vector fields in \(L^1_tW^{1,p}_{\loc,x}\), with \(p>1\), the flow \(\boldsymbol X(t,\cdot)\) is known to satisfy a quantitative local Lipschitz estimate, which implies that it is Lipschitz continuous in the Lusin sense. We prove that, at the endpoint \(p=1\), this estimate admits an Osgood-type counterpart. More precisely, we construct an increasing function \(G\), with \(G(0+)=-\infty\), determined by the integrability of \(D\boldsymbol b\), such that \[ G\bigl(|\boldsymbol X(t,x)-\boldsymbol X(t,y)|\bigr) \leq G\bigl(|\boldsymbol X(s,x)-\boldsymbol X(s,y)|\bigr) + \int_s^t \bigl(k(τ,x)+k(τ,y)\bigr)\,\ddτ, \] where \(k\) is locally integrable. As a consequence, the flow \(\boldsymbol X(t,\cdot)\) is uniformly continuous outside a set of arbitrarily small measure, with an explicit modulus of continuity determined by the integrability properties of \(D\boldsymbol b\). The resulting moduli include Hölder and log-Lipschitz regimes, as well as substantially weaker Osgood moduli. Our approach is based on a new family of weighted maximal operators associated with slowly varying functions in the sense of Karamata. We also provide examples showing that the resulting estimates are sharp in several respects and that the classical Lipschitz-type estimate may fail at the endpoint \(p=1\). Finally, we apply the flow estimates to transport equations, obtaining weighted logarithmic Sobolev regularity for transported scalars and corresponding lower bounds on functional and geometric mixing scales in the \(W^{1,1}\) setting.

Abstract: We present an asymptotic analysis of two diffusive capture problems in general smooth closed three-dimensional geometries with multiple small reactive boundary patches of arbitrary shapes. Our explicit expansions quantify local effects on diffusive capture through the sizes, shapes, and reactivities of the patches together with the principal curvatures of the manifold at each patch. In turn, we examine global effects on diffusive capture such as the spatial configuration of patches on the manifold, as encloded by the associated surface Neumann Green's function and its regular part. The accuracy of our asymptotic formulas is validated against a full numerical solution for an ellipsoidal domain. Overall, our results yield new insights on how geometry and stochasticity combine to shape the dynamics of various biological processes.

  -Δ_p u = u^{p^*-1} h(u), \qquad 1 < p < n, $$ where $h$ is a positive, bounded, continuous, and nonincreasing function. Abstract: The cosine measure of a set of vectors in $\mathbb{R}^n$ measures how well the set covers all directions in $\mathbb{R}^n$. It identifies the direction furthest, in angle, from the set. It is used in the convergence theory of various optimization algorithms, but also highlights interesting geometric properties of sets. For example, the cosine measure of a set $S$ is greater than zero if and only if given any $\mathcal{C}^1$ function $f$ at a point $\mathbf{x}$ where the gradient is nonzero, $S$ must contain a descent direction of $f$ at $\mathbf{x}$. In this paper, we examine the question of what can be said when the function $f$ is non-differentiable or if it has a gradient equal to the zero vector. To examine these cases, we introduce the novel concept of the {\em cosine measure of a function} at a point. This value provides an infimum on the value of the cosine measure that a set of vectors requires to guarantee it contains a descent direction of the function at the point of interest. We present mathematical theory around this concept, including examples showing that the cosine measure of a smooth function can have any value in $[-1,1]$. We further present algorithms to compute the cosine measure of a function, and examples demonstrating the algorithm on smooth and nonsmooth functions. These results also shed light on the the cosine measure of infinite sets and nonconvex cones.

</details>

Abstract: The computation of finite-thrust extremal arcs in the elliptic restricted three-body trajectory optimization problem is considered. Libration-point orbits are approximated to near-machine precision using a fast Fourier transform of the sampled values of the state vector at Chebyshev-Gauss-Lobatto points. Checkable optimality conditions are derived by applying Pontryagin's principle to minimum-time and time-constrained minimum-propellant problems. These necessary conditions include criteria for optimal departure and arrival points. For propellant consumption, a recently developed computational model is employed. This model is agnostic to the specific impulse of the propellant and varies as the inverse quadratic of a cosine term. Candidate optimal solutions are generated by combining the universal Birkhoff theory for trajectory optimization with the fast, guess-free spectral algorithm. The extremality of the Birkhoff-computed solution is validated against the Hamiltonian minimization condition and the transversality conditions. It is shown that the Birkhoff-theoretic spectral algorithm can generate verifiable extremals without any assistance or initialization from dynamical systems theory.

Abstract: Quadratic optimization becomes hard as soon as either the matrix in the quadratic form has an unfavorable curvature or the feasible set is discrete, combinatorial, or otherwise nonconvex. A complementary phenomenon is also well known in the signal-processing and optimization communities: when the matrix in the quadratic form has small rank, some hard-looking quadratic programs admit exact polynomial-time algorithms for fixed rank. We study the common positive-semidefinite geometry behind this phenomenon. If $Q=BB^\top$ is positive semidefinite, the objective depends on $x$ only through the rank-space shadow $y=B^\top x$. Every optimal shadow $y^*$ uniquely maximizes the linear functional defined by its own direction and satisfies a quantitative quadratic margin. Thus nonlinear optimality collapses to a low-dimensional, self-generated linear exposure direction. We call this the rank-collapse principle. The principle alone does not imply a finite candidate set: efficient exact optimization additionally depends on the projected or active geometry of the feasible family. We organize this distinction through projected-shadow scattering and active-structure collapse, relate it explicitly to established zonotope, convex-combinatorial, edge-skeleton, projected-normal-fan, and fixed-rank sparse-PCA methods, and derive tie-safe consequences for binary and finite-phase vectors, cardinality constraints, matroid bases, and sparse PCA. We also give directional-stability and approximately low-rank certificates, together with reproducible experiments. The paper's contribution is a unified, careful framework and a set of quantitative consequences, rather than a claim to originate the known fixed-rank tractability results that motivate it.

</details>

Motivation: Langevin Monte Carlo (LMC)等基于梯度的采样方法比非梯度方法混合更快，但在实际应用中目标对数密度的梯度往往不可用（如随机或黑盒模拟器），且函数评估带有噪声。现有零阶LMC方法(ZO-LMC)的维度依赖性和噪声相关的精度缩放不够理想，需要一种仅依赖带噪函数评估即可高效采样的方法，并改善其收敛保证。

Method: 提出LMC-SPSA（结合Langevin Monte Carlo与Simultaneous Perturbation Stochastic Approximation），每次迭代仅用两次带噪函数评估来近似目标对数密度的梯度。在理论上：（1）在噪声梯度估计下证明Wasserstein距离上的分布收敛；（2）构造减步长调度使Wasserstein误差界趋于零，推广到非恒定步长场景；（3）将主导维度依赖从O(p^4)改进到O(p^2)；（4）分析平衡调度下带噪零阶反馈的显式收敛率。实验上，与ZO-LMC在相同oracle预算下比较经验采样误差。

Result: LMC-SPSA达到W_2精度ε所需的总噪声oracle复杂度为O(p/ε^2+δ^2p^3/ε^3)，其中δ为成对噪声水平。相比ZO-LMC，噪声依存的精度缩放得到改善。减步长和扰动序列下Wasserstein误差随迭代次数趋于零。数值实验验证了在相同函数评估预算下，LMC-SPSA的经验采样误差小于ZO-LMC。

Conclusion: LMC-SPSA是一种实用的无梯度LMC采样方法，通过SPSA梯度逼近在带噪函数评估的环境中实现高效的分布收敛，其在Wasserstein距离下的收敛保证覆盖恒定步长和递减步长两种设置，且维度依赖的改进使得该方法在高维问题中更具竞争力。

Abstract: In sampling problems, gradient-based schemes such as Langevin Monte Carlo (LMC) mix faster than non-gradient-based methods, but their applicability is limited by access to the gradient of the target log-density. Furthermore, we construct a diminishing step-size schedule that still drives the Wasserstein error bound to convergence, extending convergence guarantees beyond the constant-step setting. Further, we sharpen the dominant dimension dependence of the Wasserstein error from $O(p^4)$ to $O(p^2)$ (with $p$ denoting the dimension), and support this analysis with numerical results. We show that LMC-SPSA achieves $W_2$-accuracy $\varepsilon$ with total noisy-oracle complexity of $O(p/\varepsilon^2+δ^2p^3/\varepsilon^3)$, where $δ$ is the paired-noise level. This improves the noise-dependent accuracy scaling relative to the ZO-LMC method of Roy et al. We further establish asymptotically vanishing Wasserstein error as the number of iterations $\to\infty$ under diminishing step-size and perturbation sequences and derive an explicit convergence rate for a balanced schedule under noisy zeroth-order feedback. Empirical experiments are conducted to verify the performance of LMC-SPSA with noise. We provide an oracle-budget-matched comparison with the ZO-LMC method, showing smaller empirical sampling errors under the same function-evaluation budget.

Abstract: This article studies the computation of the maximal controlled invariant set (MCIS) for neural network control systems (NNCSs) with a nominal plant model and a neural network residual model. Based on this finite characterization, verification-guided algorithms iteratively construct inner and outer approximations of the the MCIS, which are naturally parallelizable. Numerical examples demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

Abstract: We investigate a computable and empirically implementable framework for continuous-time mean--variance optimal portfolio selection with random market coefficients. These results demonstrate the practical potential of combining stochastic Riccati equations, neural BSDE solvers, and multifactor market modeling for dynamic asset allocation.

Abstract: We study feedback stabilization for the discrete-time system $x_{t+1}=f(x_t)+u_t+w_{t+1}$ in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with unknown $f$ and arbitrary bounded disturbances. If $L<Γ_d$, one causal feedback law stabilizes every plant in the uncertainty class against every bounded disturbance sequence. Abstract: Backward differences are a standard digital realization of derivative action in proportional-integral-derivative control. This note proves that, for multivariable state-space plants, this implementation can be unstable no matter how small the sampling period is. The exact lifted model reveals fast eigenvalues created by the stored previous output sample. Away from boundary spectra, stability is equivalent to ideal loop stability plus Schur stability of the product of the output, input, and derivative gain matrices.

</details>

Result: Halpern-NPE方法在MVI上达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-2})$的收敛率，显著优于原有$\mathcal{O}(T^{-1.5})$。p阶推广的Halpern-ATM方法达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-p})$的收敛率，对$p\ge 2$均优于此前所有结果，且在$p=1$时退化为经典外梯度法的$\mathcal{O}(T^{-1})$速率，体现了方法的统一性。

Conclusion: 本文通过Halpern迭代与高阶锚定方法的巧妙结合，在理论上首次将MVI问题的收敛率提升至$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-2})$，并建立了统一的p阶加速框架。该结果不仅解决了此前关于MVI最优复杂度的开放问题，也展示了Halpern类加速机制在高阶优化算法中的普适性。

Abstract: We study second- and higher-order methods for solving smooth monotone variational inequalities (MVI). Monteiro and Svaiter (SIAM J. Optim., 2012) showed that a second-order method, NPE, converges at the rate of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1.5})$. For convex-concave minimax optimization, a subset of MVI problems, Chen, Liu, Luo, and Zhang (COLT 2025) recently improved the complexity to $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}( T^{-1.75})$ . However, it is open whether the conjectured complexity for MVI can be improved. In this paper, by using a large-step inexact Halpern iteration, we propose a novel Halpern-NPE method that achieves an even faster rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-2})$ for solving MVIs. We also provide the $p$th-order generalization of our method. We first introduce an Anchored Tensor Method (ATM) that achieves the rate of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-(p-1)})$, and then combine it with the Halpern iteration to achieve a faster convergence rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-p})$. This improves all prior results for $p \ge 2$ and matches the classical extragradient method for $p=1$.

</details>

*Avinash Bhardwaj,Hritiz Gogoi,Vishnu Narayanan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从节点特征角度研究Max-k-Cut问题，提出特征平衡和特征主导两种结构条件下的SDP松弛精确性充分条件，并证明贪心特征平衡算法保留1-1/k的近似保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Max-k-Cut问题研究多从图结构出发，忽视了节点特征信息。在许多实际应用中，节点天然带有特征向量，边权由特征内积决定。作者希望从这一特征视角出发，建立SDP松弛精确性与问题结构之间的联系，并探索新的算法途径。

Method: 本文方法包括：(1) 利用正规锥（normal-cone）论证，给出Frieze-Jerrum SDP松弛精确性的一般充分条件；(2) 证明Max-k-Cut目标等价于最小化k个部分聚合特征向量范数平方之和，将问题与向量平衡（vector balancing）联系起来；(3) 证明贪心特征平衡算法保留1-1/k的最坏情形近似比，并在特征主导条件下恢复最优划分；(4) 对秩1非负特征图，利用Chandra和Wong的贪心负载平衡经典界，给出仅依赖所返回划分的可计算后验最优性差距证书。

Result: 作者识别出两种使得SDP松弛精确成立的特征结构体制：完美特征平衡（各部分聚合特征向量相等）和特征主导（少量大非负特征向量决定最优划分结构）。贪心特征平衡算法在一般情形下保持经典近似比，在特征主导情形下可恢复最优解。对于秩1非负特征图，可获得无需知道最优值的后验最优性差距证书。

Conclusion: 本文将Max-k-Cut问题与特征几何和向量平衡理论建立联系，提供了SDP精确性的新刻画条件，并为特定特征结构下提供有效算法和可计算的证明证书，拓展了该问题在手性特征图上的理论理解。

Abstract: We study the Max-$k$-Cut problem from a node-feature perspective, where each vertex is associated with a feature vector and edge weights are given by pairwise inner products. We first examine the semidefinite relaxation of Max-$k$-Cut from this perspective. Using a normal-cone argument, we derive a general sufficient condition for exactness of the Frieze--Jerrum relaxation and show that it is satisfied in two feature-structural regimes: perfect feature balance, where the aggregate feature vectors of the parts are equal, and feature dominance, where a small set of large nonnegative feature vectors determines the structure of an optimal partition. We then show that the Max-$k$-Cut objective is equivalent to minimizing the sum of squared norms of the aggregate feature vectors assigned to the $k$ parts, thereby connecting the problem to vector balancing. Motivated by this observation, we show that a greedy feature-balancing algorithm retains the classical $1-1/k$ worst-case approximation guarantee and recovers an optimal partition under feature dominance. For rank-$1$ feature graphs with nonnegative features, classical bounds of Chandra and Wong for greedy load balancing yield a computable \emph{a posteriori} optimality-gap certificate that depends only on the returned partition and requires no knowledge of the optimum.

</details>

*Teruki Akahoshi,Hiroki Ueda,Tomonari Kitahara*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种创新的最大边际数据包络分析（MM-DEA）模型，将机器学习中的边际最大化思想引入效率评估框架。与传统CCR模型不同，MM-DEA排除目标DMU并为其分数设置显式上界，确保所有效率得分保持在[0,1]区间内。该模型是CCR模型的广义扩展（当权衡参数α=0时退化为CCR），且其分数规划可转化为线性规划以保证计算效率。实验证明MM-DEA在高维合成数据集上能够产生唯一排名，而CCR模型无法区分任何DMU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典CCR-DEA模型在输入输出变量数相对DMU数量较多时存在判别力不足的问题，导致大量DMU被评估为效率为1，无法有效排序或区分顶尖绩效单位。这一缺陷严重限制了DEA在高维场景下的实用性。

Method: 提出MM-DEA模型，核心机制包括：(1) 借鉴机器学习中结构风险最小化的边际最大化概念，将其融入效率度量框架；(2) 排除目标DMU的参考集（类似超效率模型），但区别于超效率模型的是对目标DMU自身分数施加显式上界，确保分数不超出[0,1]范围；(3) 通过权衡参数α实现从CCR模型的推广，当α=0时退化为CCR模型；理论证明分数规划可严格变换为线性规划问题。

Result: 理论分析表明MM-DEA是CCR模型的广义扩展，且分数规划可严格转化为LP问题确保计算效率。在高维合成数据集上，MM-DEA成功产生了唯一排名，而CCR模型未能区分任何DMU，验证了其在判别力方面的显著优势。

Conclusion: MM-DEA有效地解决了CCR模型在高维场景下的判别力不足问题；"边际"这一指标可作为管理韧性和竞争优势的关键度量，为DEA在高维管理场景下的实际应用提供了新工具。

Abstract: Data Envelopment Analysis (DEA) is a widely used non-parametric method for evaluating the relative efficiency of decision-making units (DMUs). However, the classic Charnes-Cooper-Rhodes (CCR) model often suffers from a lack of discriminatory power, particularly when the number of input and output variables is large relative to the number of DMUs. Under such conditions, many DMUs are evaluated as "CCR-efficient" with a score of unity, making it difficult to rank or distinguish between top performers. To address this limitation, we propose a novel Maximum-Margin DEA (MM-DEA) model that integrates the concept of "margin maximization" -- inspired by structural risk minimization in machine learning -- directly into the efficiency measurement framework. Like the traditional super-efficiency model, the proposed MM-DEA model excludes the target DMU from the reference set used to evaluate it; unlike the super-efficiency model, however, MM-DEA imposes an explicit upper bound on the target DMU's own score, ensuring that every score remains within the standard $[0,1]$ range rather than potentially exceeding one. We theoretically demonstrate that the MM-DEA model is a generalized extension of the CCR model, reducing to the latter when the trade-off parameter $α$ is zero. Furthermore, we show that the fractional programming formulation of MM-DEA can be rigorously transformed into a linear programming (LP) problem, ensuring computational efficiency. In our numerical experiments, MM-DEA produced a unique ranking for a high-dimensional synthetic dataset on which the CCR model failed to discriminate among any of the DMUs. Our results suggest that the "margin" serves as a critical metric for managerial resilience and competitive advantage.

</details>


### [69] [Integrated Learning and Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08718)
*Cheng Tan,Yuchen Mao,Shuming Wang,Huan Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成学习与鲁棒优化（ILRO）框架，在训练阶段和部署阶段均使用鲁棒决策问题，同时实现鲁棒性和学习-决策对齐，并开发凸代理RSPO+、一阶计算方法及有限样本超额风险界，实验表明在决策质量上优于多个基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多运筹决策需要在线性规划的代价向量未知时根据上下文信息进行预测，而预测与决策仅弱对齐。已有的集成学习与优化（ILO）范式通过下游问题训练预测器，但预测不可避免存在不精确性，需要在决策阶段引入鲁棒性。因此需要一种既能实现鲁棒性又能保持学习-决策对齐的框架。

Method: 提出ILRO框架，用鲁棒决策问题同时定义训练问题（RSPO损失）和部署决策。开发凸代理RSPO+并刻画其Fisher一致性条件；利用RSPO损失的梯度信息设计一阶计算方法；推导RSPO和RSPO+预测器的有限样本超额风险界。

Result: 在交通和投资组合问题上的数值实验表明，所提框架在决策质量上优于多个基准，尤其在样本量有限、决策维度高和模型设定错误的情况下优势更为显著。

Conclusion: ILRO框架同时实现了鲁棒性和学习-决策对齐，能够有效应对预测不精确性带来的挑战，在有限样本、高维决策和模型误设等困难场景下表现尤为突出。

Abstract: Many operational decisions require solving a linear program whose cost vector is unknown at decision time and must be predicted from contextual information. Because prediction and decision are only weakly aligned, the emerging integrated learning and optimization (ILO) paradigm trains the predictor through the downstream problem, judging a prediction by the decision it induces. However, predictions are inevitably imprecise, so robustness often enters the decision stage. To address this issue, we propose an integrated learning and robust optimization (ILRO) framework, where a robust decision problem is used both to define the training problem (termed the RSPO loss problem), and to produce the deployed decision. Thus, this framework simultaneously achieves both robustness and learning-decision alignment. To tackle its computational challenges, we develop a convex surrogate, RSPO+, and characterize when it is Fisher consistent. Moreover, the RSPO loss possesses informative gradients, allowing us to develop first-order computational methods. We also derive finite-sample excess risk bounds for both RSPO and RSPO+ predictors. Numerical experiments on transportation and portfolio problems, in comparison with multiple benchmarks, show the advantage in decision quality of the proposed framework. The gain is more pronounced for scenarios with limited samples, high-dimensional decisions, and model misspecification.

</details>


### [70] [Simultaneous Group-Envelope Bounds for $Γ$-Robust Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08861)
*Zi Yuan Eric Shao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对多选择背包松弛问题族（每个不确定性阈值对应一个）的联合高效求解方法：通过消去阈值相关基线，将每组贡献简化为两个包络，并在所有阈值上统一求值，实现线性或近线性时间完成整个阈值族的界定，同时保证区间界有效、与单阈值普通松弛匹配且不弱于分组团比较器。在自适应搜索中实现2.37倍几何平均加速，并在全部60个实例级中位数比较中获胜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多鲁棒规划问题需要针对每个不确定性阈值分别构建和求解一系列普通的优化松弛（如多选择背包问题）。重复构建与求解这些松弛会主导整体运行时间，成为计算瓶颈。作者希望避免逐阈值重复求解，而是将整个阈值族作为一个整体同时界定。

Method: 当每个决策恰好从每组中选择一个选项时，利用抵消操作消除阈值特定基线，将每组贡献降维为两个简单包络（envelopes）。经过排序后，用一个乘子即可在全部阈值上以线性时间（选项数量上近线性）统一求值。该方法提供有效区间界，在单一可行阈值上与普通松弛一致，且不弱于匹配的分组团比较器。采用带认证的一维搜索控制乘子误差，对数值模糊的可行性与比较情形用精确检查解决。

Result: 在与普通方法逐项一致的实验设置下，该方法在全部60个实例级中位数比较中胜出，实现2.37倍几何平均加速。通过额外消融实验、独立线性规划校验以及来自已发表数据集的九个转换实例验证了机制有效性。一个整数审计实验揭示了方法边界：当整数搜索占主导时，更快的松弛界并无帮助。

Conclusion: 将阈值松弛族联合界定的策略在松弛主导计算成本的场景中是高效且有效的，能带来显著加速；但该收益具有边界性——当问题瓶颈转移到整数搜索时，该方法的加速效果不再体现。

Abstract: Many robust planning problems are solved by checking a family of ordinary optimization problems, one for each uncertainty threshold. Repeatedly building and solving those relaxations can dominate runtime. We show that, when a decision chooses exactly one option from each group, the entire threshold family of multiple-choice knapsack relaxations can instead be bounded together. A cancellation removes threshold-specific baselines and reduces each group's contribution to two simple envelopes. After sorting, one multiplier is evaluated across all thresholds in time linear in their number and nearly linear in the number of options. The resulting interval bound is valid, matches the ordinary relaxation on a single feasible threshold, and is at least as strong as the matched group-clique comparator. A certified one-dimensional search controls multiplier error, while exact checks resolve numerically ambiguous feasibility and comparison cases. In an otherwise identical adaptive search, the method wins all 60 instance-level median comparisons and achieves a 2.37-fold geometric-mean speedup. Additional ablations, independent linear-program checks, and nine instances transformed from a published archive test the mechanism beyond the internal generator. A separate integer audit shows the boundary: faster relaxation bounds need not help when integer search dominates.

</details>


### [71] [Regional factors determine the feasibility of an elimination strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08956)

Abstract: We provide an explicit mathematical characterization of regional characteristics and the resulting epidemiology to evaluate whether elimination or suppression is feasible using targeted measures, such as case isolation and contact tracing. We derive an epidemic model where community members can be infected by either travellers or other community members, and with constraints on the public health resources available to support isolation of arriving travellers and infected community members. We prove that the optimal controls are to immediately implement public health measures at their maximum levels. We find that elimination or suppression strategies are feasible with targeted measures if public health capacity is high, the transmission rate is low, or if the arrival rate of infected travellers is low. Our results illustrate that a particular country that implemented a mitigation strategy during the pandemic may not necessarily have achieved better outcomes if it had instead implemented elimination because elimination may not have been feasible in countries with low public health capacity to support control measures, high transmission rates, or high traveller arrival rates. Combinations of regional characteristics determine whether an elimination, suppression or mitigation strategy without resurgence is feasible. We find that regions with the low traveller arrival rates and the capacity to implement and enforce isolation or quarantine of arriving travellers are less likely to exceed their capacity to contact trace and isolate community members. Travel measures prevent only a small number of community infections via their direct effect, but indirectly, by protecting contact tracing capacity, travel measures can prevent epidemic resurgence and substantially reduce the number of people infected during a pandemic before a vaccine or therapy is developed.

Abstract: Optimization under zeroth-order (i.e., bandit) feedback is central to many engineering problems where the analytic forms of objectives and/or constraints are unavailable. In modern applications, such as online control and online learning, optimization problems often evolve with time, requiring adaptive optimization methodologies. Yet, existing methods in this seting are largely confined to adaptations of methodologies developed for time-invariant or first-order optimization, and thus often rely on gradient surrogates that fail to fully exploit the zeroth-order structure of the available information. In this paper, we propose a randomized two-point direct-search algorithm for nonconvex time-varying optimization and derive iteration-complexity bounds under both constant and diminishing probing ratios. The resulting analysis yields explicit stationarity bounds in terms of the temporal variability of the problem and possible oracle errors. Our complexity bounds recover the complexity of existing zeroth-order methods in the time-invariant setting, while extending direct- search methods beyond static settings. As an illustrative application, we show that the methodology is naturally suited to solve optimal (equilibrium-selection) control problems for dynamical systems. In this setting, the analysis yields explicit stationarity bounds in terms of the temporal variability of the problem, measured through the effects of plant dynamics and exogenous disturbance variations.

Abstract: We prove a sharp lower bound for smooth nonconvex stochastic optimization with uniformly bounded gradient noise. Abstract: In this paper, we develop a systematic framework for deriving explicit error bounds for semidefinite feasibility problems without assuming strict feasibility (Slater's condition), a setting in which existing results are limited. Our main technical contribution is the introduction of radial-type Hölder error bounds, where the error bound constant depends explicitly on the norm of the reference matrix through radial modulus functions. By combining facial reduction with recently developed facial residual functions, we obtain explicit descriptions of these modulus functions, yielding qualitative radial-type Hölder error bounds without imposing any constraint qualifications. Our results complement the classical work of Sturm by providing explicit estimates for the constants involved in Sturm's local Hölder error bounds over bounded sets with a given size. We further analyze the asymptotic behavior of these bounds as the dimension of the underlying matrix space grows, identifying regimes in which they can be asymptotically tight up to a dimensional-free constant. As an application, we establish explicit error bounds for the optimality system of semidefinite programs by reformulating them as feasibility problems, a setting where Slater's condition typically fails. Under the generically satisfied strict complementarity condition, we derive radial-type error bounds without assuming the usual solution uniqueness requirement, and demonstrate their asymptotic tightness through an explicit example.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we develop provable parameter-free and adaptive fixed-point algorithms for contractive mappings, with an emphasis on automatically exploiting hidden contractivity without requiring prior knowledge of the contraction factor. Our first method is a completely parameter-free variant of the Halpern fixed-point iteration. It requires no line search, bisection, or prior estimate of the contraction factor, while retaining essentially the same per-iteration computational cost as classical fixed-point schemes. We establish explicit linear convergence rates for both the fixed-point residual and the distance to the unique fixed point. The second algorithm is an adaptive Halpern method that requires only an upper bound on the contraction factor and reduces to an existing adaptive Halpern scheme in the nonexpansive case. This method also enjoys explicit linear convergence guarantees. We further extend these ideas in two directions. First, by combining the proposed fixed-point schemes with Tikhonov regularization, we obtain a parameter-free method for solving co-coercive equations and establish an iteration complexity of $\mathcal{O}({ε^{-1}\ln(ε^{-1})})$ for computing an $ε$-solution. Second, using the relation between Halpern iterations and Nesterov's accelerated fixed-point schemes, we derive parameter-free Nesterov's accelerated variants that inherit linear convergence in the contractive setting. Numerical experiments on several examples demonstrate that the proposed algorithms are competitive with, and often outperform, existing adaptive fixed-point methods. In particular, the methods successfully exploit contractive behavior when it is present while remaining effective on nonexpansive problems.

Abstract: We present PDHCG-CQP, a GPU-accelerated first-order solver for large-scale conic convex quadratic programming. PDHCG-CQP supports affine constraints and Cartesian products of nonnegative, second-order, rotated second-order, exponential, and three-dimensional power cones. At its core is a restarted averaged primal-dual hybrid gradient (PDHG) method, whose primal update is computed inexactly by solving a conic quadratic proximal subproblem with projected gradient iterations. We establish local linear convergence of the restarted averaged scheme with both exact and inexact primal proximal evaluations under a uniform local quadratic-growth condition on the smoothed primal-dual gap. We further show that this condition holds under strict complementarity by exploiting a rotated second-order-cone lifting together with local primal and dual regularity conditions. Our C/CUDA implementation combines matrix-free linear algebra, batched cone projections, adaptive inner solves, reflected-Halpern acceleration, and fully device-resident KKT residual computations. It also supports multi-GPU execution through a two-dimensional partitioning of the problem data. Extensive experiments on standard and large-scale quadratic programming (QP), convex quadratically constrained quadratic programming (QCQP), second-order cone programming (SOCP), and quasilinear Fisher equilibrium benchmarks demonstrate that PDHCG-CQP achieves state-of-the-art robustness among first-order solvers while scaling efficiently to 8 GPUs and instances with up to $4.4\times10^8$ stored primal coordinates. PDHCG-CQP is open source and available at https://github.com/Lhongpei/PDHCG.

Abstract: Large-scale renewable power-to-hydrogen (ReP2H) plants in cold regions suffer from prolonged startup and repeated thermal stress during frequent startup-shutdown operation. In addition, by identifying each Gaussian mixture with a finite metric measure space of Gaussian components, we establish the metric property and large-penalty limit of the partial mixture Gromov--Wasserstein distance. Finally, numerical experiments on synthetic Gaussian mixtures and point clouds illustrate the effects of the penalty and entropic regularization and the robustness of partial matching to outliers.

Abstract: We develop a primal--dual interior-point method for nonsymmetric conic optimization based on a conjugate-free scaling matrix. Methods Softw., 37 (2022), pp. 1027--1064] and matching the best-known complexity order for interior-point methods. Abstract: Let $T$ be an exponentially stable strongly continuous semigroup on a Hilbert space $X$, $A$ its generator, $X_1$ the domain of $A$ with the norm $\|v\|_1 := \|Av\|$, and $C$ an admissible observation operator taking values in a Hilbert space $Y$. We disprove this modified conjecture by constructing a counterexample in which $A$ is normal, $X$ has an orthonormal basis of eigenvectors of $A$ and $C: X_1 \to Y$ is Hilbert-Schmidt. Since $A$ is normal and $T$ is exponentially stable, it follows that $T$ is a contraction semigroup. In contrast, we prove that the Hautus test implies exact observability for self-adjoint $A$. Finally, we show that if $A$ is normal and $C:X_1 \to Y$ is compact, then the Hautus test implies that $A$ has compact resolvent. Consequently, for normal $A$ and finite-dimensional $Y$ the Hautus test implies exact observability.

</details>

Result: 在仅假设二次增长和所有最优解同秩的条件下，新方法实现了全局的、与环境维度无关的线性收敛率，且该方法是确定性的、无参数的，不需要预先知道目标函数的光滑常数，也不需要严格互补性假设。

Conclusion: 本文成功移除了先前Frank-Wolfe型方法在谱面最小化问题上的所有主要限制（随机性、预热阶段、参数依赖、严格互补性假设），在更宽松的条件下获得了与环境维度无关的确定性全局线性收敛率，是该领域的重要理论进展。

Abstract: We consider smooth convex minimization over the spectrahedron using Frank-Wolfe-type methods based only on extreme-eigenvector computations. In our recent work \cite{garber2026randomized} we presented the first ambient-dimension-independent linear convergence rate under quadratic growth. However, the method makes an additional strong strict complementarity assumption, it is randomized, its linear rate holds only after a burn-in phase and in expectation, and it requires the objective smoothness constant. We show that these limitations can be removed. ### [82] [Input convex neural networks as surrogates in mathematical optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09707)

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文主张在优化问题中使用输入凸神经网络（ICNN）替代传统的前馈ReLU神经网络作为代理模型。ICNN在结构上具有优势：其MIP公式具有更紧的LP松弛，且在特定条件下可基于epigraph表示构建LP重述；当epigraph嵌入不完全精确时，利用ICNN的凸性质可在盒域上构造凸包，即最强连续松弛。基于此开发了分支定界算法，直接对输入变量分支，案例研究（人道主义粮食援助、油井路径规划、葡萄酒调配）表明ICNN在精度匹配FNN的同时，在求解时间和可扩展性上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于ReLU激活的前馈神经网络（FNN）作为优化问题中的代理模型时，其精确的MIP重述随着网络规模增大计算量大、LP松弛松驰。当底层响应函数近似凸或凹时，需要结构上更优的代理模型来提升求解效率。

Method: 1) 提出ICNN-MIP公式，相比FNN-MIP具有更紧的LP松弛；2) 利用ICNN的凸架构，通过ReLU激活的epigraph表示构建LP重述（并非总是精确）；3) 当epigraph不精确时，在盒域上构造ICNN图的凸包作为最强连续松弛（下界为epigraph，上界为凹包络），该构造在输入凸性下可计算，但对一般ReLU网络是困难的；4) 基于此开发分支定界算法，在每节点构建该松弛，直接对输入变量而非MIP重述中的中间变量分支，当epigraph嵌入有效时在根节点终止。

Result: 在三个人道主义粮食援助、油井路径规划和葡萄酒调配案例研究中，ICNN代理模型在精度上匹配FNN，同时在求解时间和可扩展性上表现更优，支持ICNN作为底层函数为凸、凹或可近似为凸凹时的默认代理选择。

Conclusion: ICNN在结构上优于FNN作为优化问题中的代理模型，不仅提供了更紧的松弛，还支持高效的LP重述和可计算的凸包构造，为凸/凹响应函数的代理建模提供了更高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Embedding trained neural networks as surrogates within optimisation problems is an established practice in operations research. The prevailing approach uses feedforward neural networks (FNNs) with ReLU activations, whose piecewise-linear structure admits an exact but computationally intensive mixed-integer programming (MIP) reformulation as the networks grow. We advocate input convex neural networks (ICNNs) as structurally superior surrogates when the underlying response is approximately convex or concave. The convex architecture offers two computational advantages. First, the ICNN-MIP formulation tends to yield a tighter linear programming (LP) relaxation than its FNN-MIP counterpart, with no integrality gap in favourable instances. Second, ICNNs uniquely admit an LP-based reformulation via epigraph representations of ReLU activations, though this embedding is not always exact. When it is not, we exploit the properties of ICNNs to construct the strongest continuous relaxation over box domains, namely, the convex hull of the ICNN's graph, bounded below by the epigraph and above by the concave envelope; this construction is tractable under input convexity but hard for general ReLU networks. On this basis, we develop a branch-and-bound algorithm that builds this relaxation at each node, branches directly on input variables rather than intermediate variables as in MIP reformulations, and terminates at the root node whenever the epigraph embedding is valid. Case studies on humanitarian food aid, oil well routing, and wine blending show that ICNN surrogates match FNN accuracy and deliver gains in solve time and scalability, supporting ICNN as the default surrogate when the underlying function is convex, concave, or well-approximated as such.

</details>

Result: 通过两个计算实验——随机轨道目标和模拟飓风场景——验证了SDDiP方法在求解随机MCRP时优于其他随机问题求解方法，能够有效处理目标随机性并满足可见时间窗口与机动可行性约束。

Conclusion: 随机MCRP有效解决了星座重构在观测任务中的目标不确定性调度问题，SDDiP被证明是最有效的求解方法，为卫星运营中的动态观测任务调度提供了可行的理论框架。

Abstract: Observation tasking is critical to satellite operations, enabling observation of planetary phenomena, orbital debris monitoring, and space domain awareness. To improve the effectiveness of satellite operations, orbital maneuverability is introduced as a leading-edge concept of operations within constellation reconfigurability for response to dynamic events. Previous investigations regarding constellation reconfigurability consist of deterministic mission environments, in which the formulations consider a priori knowledge of the environment; however, observation objectives inherently involve uncertainty at any given time. As such, scheduling satellite operations must account for uncertainties to ensure adequate observation tasking. In response, we present a stochastic variant of the Multistage Constellation Reconfiguration Problem (MCRP) which is solved using Stochastic Dual Dynamic Integer Programming (SDDiP). We additionally explore other stochastic problem-solving techniques for solution quality comparison. To demonstrate each solution method, two computational experiments with stochastic target properties are conducted. The first concerns random orbital targets, and the second concerns simulated hurricanes. The results of the experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the SDDiP solution approach over other stochastic problem-solving methods. Overall, the stochastic MCRP accounts for target stochasticity while obeying visible time windows and maneuver feasibility.

</details>

Method: 在流形框架下提出QO-free算法：每步求解三个共享同一线性算子的线性系统确定主搜索方向；通过求解降维线性最小二乘子问题获得高阶校正方向以规避Maratos效应；在当前迭代点的切空间内执行黎曼弧搜索生成新迭代点。

Abstract: The quadratic optimization-free (QO-free) method is a class of powerful and effective algorithms for solving nonlinearly constrained optimization problems in Euclidean spaces. The aim of the present work is to extend this method to solve optimization problems on manifolds with additional equality and inequality constraints. We first present a specific algorithm in the manifold setting. At each iteration, three linear systems sharing a common linear operator are solved to determine the master search direction. In addition, a higher-order correction direction is obtained by solving a reduced linear least squares subproblem to circumvent the Maratos effect which is assumed not to arise in existing related literature. A Riemannian arc search is then performed within the tangent space of the current iterate to generate the new iterate. Under appropriate assumptions, we establish the global and strong convergence of the proposed method. Moreover, we prove that the unit step size will eventually be accepted by the arc search, upon which the superlinear convergence of the algorithm is established. Finally, numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method is very competitive compared with other existing approaches.

</details>

*Anier Soria-Lorente,Jean-Marie Vilaire,Junior Michel,Lázaro Alvarado-Barrios*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出ADPSO-ERLS，一种离散群智能算法用于求解旅行商问题（TSP），将探索与局部强化之间的搜索预算分配作为显式可调设计变量。该方法融合记忆引导的交换变异、异构初始化、进化过程中的候选受限2-opt以及仅针对精英解的最终精炼（含候选受限/完整2-opt与double-bridge扰动）。在统一评估预算（100,000次候选解评估）下，ADPSO-ERLS在五个对称TSPLIB实例上均取得最低最优成本与平均成本，最佳Gap为1.93%-4.17%。Friedman检验排名第一，25个Wilcoxon比较全部显著优于基线。消融实验表明各组件贡献质量提升，候选限制可将运行时间缩短高达38倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSP是研究群体方法如何在探索与渐强局部强化之间分配固定搜索预算的经典场景。已有方法缺乏对该分配过程的显式建模与可控性。

Method: 提出了ADPSO-ERLS离散群算法：将探索-强化预算分配作为显式可调设计变量；采用记忆引导的交换变异（不含速度、惯性与加速度系数）；异构初始化；进化中候选受限2-opt局部搜索；精英解的最终精炼阶段（候选受限与完整2-opt、double-bridge扰动）。所有方法用Rust实现，统一硬件与评估预算。

Result: 在五个TSPLIB实例上均取得最低最优/平均成本，最佳Gap为1.93%-4.17%，相对误差3.04%-5.50%；Friedman检验排名第一，全部25个Wilcoxon检验显著占优；消融证实各组件有效，候选限制可加速约38倍；扩展到16,862个城市时Gap低于6.7%，最大规模实例在11分钟内求解。

Conclusion: ADPSO-ERLS通过将探索-强化分配显式化并在统一评估预算下公平比较，证明其在不同规模TSP实例上均具有优异且稳定的求解质量，同时运行时间可控，验证了该设计思路的有效性。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem is a canonical setting for studying how a
  population-based method should allocate a fixed search budget between
  exploration and progressively stronger local intensification. We propose
  ADPSO-ERLS, a discrete swarm algorithm that treats this allocation as an
  explicit, tunable design variable. It couples memory-guided swap mutation,
  heterogeneous initialization, selective candidate-restricted 2-opt during
  evolution, and an incumbent-only final refinement combining candidate-restricted
  and optional full 2-opt with double-bridge perturbations. The method is
  PSO-inspired, using personal and global memories yet dispensing with velocity,
  inertia, and acceleration coefficients. All six algorithms are implemented in
  Rust, run on identical hardware, and stopped at a strict, recorded limit of
  100,000 candidate-solution assessments, so that programming language, hardware,
  and evaluation budget are held common across methods; wall-clock time is
  reported separately because equal assessment counts need not correspond to
  equal arithmetic work. Over 50 runs on five symmetric TSPLIB instances under the integer
  \texttt{EUC\_2D} convention, ADPSO-ERLS attains the lowest best and mean cost on
  every instance, with best-tour Gap of $1.93$--$4.17\%$ and relative error of
  $3.04$--$5.50\%$. It ranks first under the Friedman test, and all twenty-five
  multiplicity-controlled Wilcoxon comparisons favor it with large, near-complete
  distributional separation. A paired ablation with common seeds links
  initialization, in-run local search, and final refinement to quality gains,
  while candidate restriction chiefly cuts runtime, by up to a factor of roughly
  $38$. Further experiments up to $16{,}862$ cities keep best-tour Gaps below
  $6.7\%$, solving the largest case in under eleven minutes.

</details>

*Rene Carmona,Xiaoyu Cui*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对含运行最大值（running maximum）的随机控制问题进行了完整分析，该问题源于数据中心面临重合峰值电费与需求响应计划参与激励的电力成本最小化。作者证明了值函数的局部Lipschitz连续性，推导了动态规划原理与粘性解框架，建立了含反射Neumann边界条件与斜边界条件的HJB方程，并证明了粘性解存在性与比较定理（条件唯一性），最后给出数值结果说明数据中心两种相互冲突激励间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心在面临重合峰值电费（coincident peak charges）且可能参与需求响应计划时，其电力成本最小化问题需要引入受控状态过程的运行最大值作为成本项，这在数学上构成了一类新的奇异随机控制问题，此前缺乏完整的理论分析。

Method: 将原问题转化为平面上闭合楔形区域内的奇异随机控制问题，边界部分带反射。主要技术工具包括：耦合反射负载的gap-monotonicity估计与两个运行最大值差值的确定性全变差界（证明值函数局部Lipschitz连续性）；对普通渐进可测控制推导动态规划原理与粘性公式；建立含下边界反射Neumann条件和对角边界斜边界条件的HJB方程，在粘性意义下证明解的存在性与比较定理。

Result: 证明了值函数在空间上的局部Lipschitz连续性；推导了动态规划原理和粘性解表述；证明了HJB方程粘性解的存在性以及比较定理（在相应粘性解类中给出条件唯一性）；提供了数值结果展示重合峰值电费与需求响应激励之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为含运行最大值的奇异随机控制问题提供了完整的理论框架，包括正则性、动态规划、粘性解存在唯一性等关键结果，并通过数值实验验证了模型对数据中心成本优化问题的适用性与经济意义。

Abstract: The goal of the paper is to provide a complete analysis of a stochastic control problem when the cost to minimize involves the running maximum of the underlying controlled state process. The model is motivated by the electricity cost minimization of a data center facing coincident peak charges and possibly benefiting from participation in a demand-response program. From a mathematical standpoint, the main challenge comes from the inclusion of the running maximum in the state of the system forcing the original problem into a singular stochastic control problem on a closed wedge in the plane with reflection on parts of the boundary. We prove that the value function is locally Lipschitz continuous in space using a gap-monotonicity estimate for coupled reflected loads and a deterministic total-variation bound for the difference of two running maxima. The dynamic programming principle and viscosity formulation are then derived for ordinary progressively measurable controls. The corresponding HJB equation includes a reflecting Neumann condition on the lower boundary for the load direction as well as an oblique boundary condition on the diagonal part of the boundary of the domain due to the effect of the running-maximum process. We prove existence of a solution in the viscosity sense as well as a comparison theorem providing conditional uniqueness in the corresponding viscosity class. Finally we provide numerical results illustrating the trade-off between the two conflicting incentives comprising the expected cost of the data center.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆编队行驶能显著降低能耗、减少排放、提高道路利用率、增强安全性和减轻驾驶员疲劳。随着智能驾驶技术发展，编队规模将大幅增大，车辆的高效排序与重排序变得日益重要，但在变长路段路网上对该问题缺乏系统的计算复杂度分析和高效算法。

Method: (1) 对车辆编队排序与重排序问题在变长路段路网上按两个目标（最小化总能耗、最小化最大能耗）进行建模； (2) 针对典型车辆与道路特征组合，给出完整的计算复杂度分类——要么设计多项式时间算法，要么证明NP难等计算难解性； (3) 对若干难解情形设计全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS）和带可证明性能保证的多项式时间启发式算法； (4) 针对仅掌握位置相关能耗节省的有限信息场景，开发具有有界最坏情况性能的启发式； (5) 针对仅允许有限位置变化的道路实时重排序场景，提出高效算法。

Result: 计算实验表明所提启发式算法的平均解在最优解的1%以内；针对有限信息的场景提供有界最坏情况性能的启发式算法；对有限位置变化的道路重排序提出了高效算法。研究成果形成了一套完整的能源高效车辆编队排序与重排序算法框架。

Conclusion: 通过完整的复杂度分类、多项式时间算法、近似方案和启发式算法的组合，本文为不同场景下的车辆编队排序与重排序提供了综合性的算法框架，覆盖了从精确求解到近似求解的多种需求，在计算复杂性与实际可用性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Vehicle platooning offers significant benefits, including reduced energy consumption, lower emissions, improved road utilization, enhanced safety, and reduced driver fatigue. As intelligent driving technologies continue to advance, platoon sizes are expected to increase substantially, making the efficient sequencing and resequencing of vehicles increasingly important. We study the vehicle platoon sequencing and resequencing problem on road networks with varying segment lengths under two fundamental objectives: minimizing total energy consumption and minimizing the maximum energy consumption of any vehicle. For the typically encountered combinations of vehicle and road characteristics, we provide a complete computational complexity classification, either developing polynomial-time algorithms or proving computational intractability. For several intractable cases, we design fully polynomial-time approximation schemes and polynomial-time heuristics with provable performance guarantees. A computational study demonstrates that the proposed heuristics achieve average solutions within 1\% of optimal. We also consider settings in which only limited information about position-dependent energy savings is available and develop a heuristic with bounded worst-case performance. In addition, we present an efficient algorithm for on-road vehicle resequencing when only limited position changes are permitted. Together, these results provide a comprehensive algorithmic framework for energy-efficient vehicle platoon sequencing and resequencing.

</details>


### [88] [Safe Start: Configuring Optimization Algorithms for Decision-Making under Extreme Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09872)
*Henry Lam,Wasin Meesena*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究随机优化中不仅优化平均目标，还需缓解罕见灾难性事件发生的问题，提出"安全起点"（safe start）这一新概念，结合方差缩减技术实现尾部风险目标的有限时间误差控制，并在投资组合优化和神经网络鲁棒分类中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全感知决策和AI训练等场景中，单纯优化平均目标不足以应对罕见灾难性事件。现有方法试图将方差缩减集成到随机梯度下降优化中，但即使以合理的自适应方式执行，由于尾部目标对决策变量极度敏感，导致无论选择何种步长都会出现收敛的二分化失败，难以保证实际运行时间。这促使作者寻找新的解决方案。

Method: 本文提出"安全起点"（safe start）这一新概念，允许高效的有限时间误差控制；在此基础上将安全起点与方差缩减技术相结合，推导出采样复杂度的有利标度。通过理论分析论证了传统方法在尾部风险目标下的失败机制，并提供新的方法论框架来克服这一困境。

Result: 在投资组合优化和基于神经网络鲁棒分类的实验中，验证了所提出方法在缓解罕见灾难性事件方面的有效性和采样效率优势。

Conclusion: 安全起点与方差缩减的结合为尾部风险感知的随机优化提供了可行的理论框架和实践工具，能够克服传统SGD算法在此类问题上的收敛困境，为安全关键场景下的决策优化开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We consider stochastic optimization where the goal is not only to optimize an average-case objective, but also to mitigate the occurrence of rare catastrophic events. This problem is motivated by safety-aware decision-making and AI training. We first argue that, in the presence of a simulation model, natural attempts to integrate variance reduction into optimization, even executed in a reasonable adaptive fashion, encounter fundamental challenges in guaranteeing realistic runtime when using common stochastic gradient descent algorithms. This challenge arises from the extreme sensitivity of tail-based objectives with respect to the decision variables, which renders a dichotomic failure of convergence regardless of what step size we select. We offer remedies based on a new notion of safe start that allows for efficient finite-time error control, and show how the sampling complexity scales favorably under the combination of safe start and variance reduction. We illustrate our methodologies on examples in portfolio optimization and robust classification with neural networks.

</details>

### [89] [Nonadiabatic Molecular Dynamics on Real-time Excited-State Surfaces via Machine Learning Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08095)
*Changwei Zhang,Yang Zhong,Zhi-Guo Tao,Yingzhou Li,Zhenggang Lan,Oleg V. Prezhdo,Xin-Gao Gong,Weibin Chu,Hongjun Xiang*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了N²AMD（Neural Network NAMD）机器学习框架，通过等变神经网络预测系统哈密顿量，实现了固体中实时非绝热分子动力学（NAMD）模拟，能以极低的成本提供激发态能量、力和非绝热耦合矢量，并首次支持杂化泛函精度的NAMD模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟电子和原子核的耦合非平衡动力学是化学、物理和材料科学中的核心挑战，但缺乏一种通用、准确且高效的方法来建模完整的激发态景观（包括势能面、力和多个电子态的非绝热耦合）。机器学习虽已彻底改变了基态模拟，但对于凝聚态体系的多态问题，仍缺乏统一框架。

Method: 采用等变神经网络（equivariant neural network）预测系统哈密顿量，从而获得激发态能量、力和非绝热耦合矢量。该框架支持实时NAMD模拟，并可使用杂化泛函精度进行计算，突破了以往NAMD无法达到的精度水平。

Result: 通过三个典型案例展示能力：①修正MoS₂/WS₂异质结中传统方法预测的载流子动力学数量级误差；②模拟此前无法实现的激光诱导铁电极化开关；③在杂化泛函精度下捕捉TiO₂中实时极化子形成过程。

Conclusion: N²AMD突破了平衡态理论的局限，为远非平衡条件下材料的预测性第一性原理设计建立了新范式。

Abstract: Simulating the coupled, nonequilibrium dynamics of electrons and nuclei is a central challenge in chemistry, physics, and materials science, governing phenomena from photocatalysis to quantum information. The primary bottleneck has been the lack of a general, accurate, and efficient method for modeling the complete excited-state landscape: the potential energy surfaces, forces, and non-adiabatic couplings for multiple electronic states. While machine learning has revolutionized ground-state simulations and shown promise for excited states in molecules, a unified framework that solves the complete multi-state problem for general condensed matter systems has remained elusive. Here we introduce on-the-fly N${^2}$AMD (Neural network NAMD), a machine learning framework that makes on-the-fly NAMD in solids a reality. By employing an equivariant neural network to predict the system Hamiltonian, the framework delivers excited-state energies, forces, and non-adiabatic coupling vectors at a fraction of the cost of ab initio calculations. Crucially, it allows simulations with hybrid functional accuracy, a level of approach previously inaccessible for NAMD. We showcase its capabilities with three topical examples: correcting order-of-magnitude errors in carrier dynamics predicted by conventional procedure in a MoS$_2$/WS$_2$ heterostructure, simulating previously inaccessible photoinduced ferroelectric switching, and capturing real-time polaron formation in TiO$_2$ at the hybrid-functional level. On-the-fly N${^2}$AMD moves beyond the limitations of equilibrium theory, establishing a new paradigm for the predictive, first-principles design of materials operating far from equilibrium.

</details>


### [90] [Coordinate-Residual Physics-Driven Neural Network for Electromagnetic Inverse Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09382)
*Yutong Du,Zicheng Liu,Bo Qi,Yali Zong,Peixian Han*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种坐标残差物理驱动神经网络（CRPDNN）用于三维电磁逆散射成像，无需初步重建的区域选择，直接利用归一化空间坐标和残差卷积网络重建未知对比度分布。在无噪声三维合成案例中，CRPDNN平均相对误差为2.10%，优于CSI（7.97%）和L2/3-FBE-WCIE（3.99%），速度分别提升约5.5倍和12.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁逆散射是非线性且不适定问题，三维成像中测量限制、噪声和高计算成本导致精确重建困难。尽管物理驱动神经网络可减少对标注数据的依赖，但现有加速PDNN框架常依赖基于初步重建的区域选择，区域不准确时会引入不稳定性。因此需要一种不依赖初步重建、直接进行三维重建的稳定高效方法。

Method: 提出坐标残差物理驱动神经网络（CRPDNN），核心设计包括：使用归一化空间坐标作为输入，通过残差卷积网络直接重建未知对比度分布，完全避免初步重建和区域选择步骤。该网络以物理驱动方式训练，减少对标签数据依赖，同时利用残差网络结构提升重建精度和稳定性。

Result: 在无噪声三维合成案例中，CRPDNN平均相对误差为2.10%，显著优于CSI（7.97%）和L2/3-FBE-WCIE（3.99%），计算速度分别提升约5.5倍和12.1倍。二维补充对比进一步验证了其相对现有PDNN框架的稳定性和计算效率。在有噪声测量下仍保持可靠重建性能，三维Fresnel实验表明其在实际成像应用中的潜力。

Conclusion: CRPDNN通过消除对初步重建区域选择的依赖，有效解决了现有加速PDNN框架的不稳定性问题，在三维电磁逆散射中实现了更优的精度和显著的计算效率提升。该方法在噪声鲁棒性和实际成像应用方面均展现出良好前景，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Electromagnetic inverse scattering is a nonlinear and ill-posed problem, where accurate reconstruction is challenging due to measurement limitations, noise, and high computational costs, especially for 3-D imaging. Although physics-driven neural networks (PDNNs) reduce the dependence on labeled training data, existing accelerated PDNN frameworks often rely on preliminary reconstruction-based region selection, which may introduce instability when the selected region is inaccurate. In this paper, a coordinate-residual physics-driven neural network (CRPDNN) is proposed for 3-D electromagnetic inverse scattering. The proposed solver directly reconstructs the unknown contrast distribution using normalized spatial coordinates and a residual convolutional network, without requiring a preliminary reconstruction. For the reported noise-free 3-D synthetic cases, CRPDNN achieves an average relative error of 2.10\%, compared with 7.97\% for CSI and 3.99\% for $L_{2/3}$-FBE-WCIE, while providing approximately 5.5- and 12.1-fold speedups over the two baselines, respectively. Supplementary 2-D comparisons further confirm its stability and computational efficiency relative to existing PDNN frameworks. CRPDNN also maintains reliable reconstruction performance under noisy measurements, and the 3-D Fresnel experiments further indicate its potential for practical imaging applications. The related code is available at https://github.com/Physics-driven-methods.

</details>


### [91] [Autonomous Lindblad Realizability of Nonunitary Linear Dynamics with a Carleman Lattice Boltzmann Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09808)
*Muhammad Idrees Khan*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明有限线性端点可被自主GKSL（开放量子）动力学实现，当且仅当其可逆且有界幂。通过显式构造，将非幺正映射直接实现为开放系统演化（无需端点后选择，仅需一次编码和一次解码），并应用于D2Q9 MRT-LB时间步的Carleman提升，验证了经典Carleman轨迹的再现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carleman提升将非线性多项式动力学转化为有限线性系统，但截断后的线性系统通常非幺正，无法直接对应物理量子演化。因此需要建立一般性判据，判断何种有限非幺正动力学可被自主开放量子（GKSL）动力学实现。

Method: （1）证明可逆且有界幂是有限线性端点存在自主GKSL实现的充要条件；（2）给出显式构造方案，将非幺正映射直接实现为开放系统动力学，无需端点后选择；（3）将完整的D2Q9多弛豫时间（MRT）格子玻尔兹曼时间步（碰撞+周期流）编译为单一Carleman端点，并验证其GKSL演化复现经典Carleman轨迹。

Result: 得到的GKSL演化在多个时间步上复现经典Carleman轨迹，与非线性LB动力学的偏差仅为预期的Carleman截断误差。该结果建立了有限非幺正动力学自主开放量子实现的一般判据，并以Carleman-LB动力学作为具体实例。

Conclusion: 这项工作为有限非幺正动力学的开放量子系统实现提供了严格判据和显式构造方法，为Carleman提升在量子模拟框架下逼近格子玻尔兹曼等非线性动力学提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Carleman lifting converts nonlinear polynomial dynamics into finite linear systems, but the resulting truncations are generally nonunitary and need not correspond to physical quantum evolution. We prove that a finite linear endpoint admits an autonomous Gorini--Kossakowski--Sudarshan--Lindblad (GKSL) realization on vacuum coherences if and only if it is invertible and power bounded. The construction is explicit and realizes the nonunitary map directly as open-system dynamics, with no endpoint postselection and with one encoding and one decoding over repeated timesteps. We apply the result to the complete D2Q9 multiple-relaxation-time lattice Boltzmann (LB) timestep by compiling collision and periodic streaming into a single Carleman endpoint. The resulting GKSL evolution reproduces the classical Carleman trajectory over multiple timesteps, while the remaining discrepancy from nonlinear LB dynamics is the expected Carleman truncation error. The result establishes a general criterion for autonomous open-quantum realization of finite nonunitary dynamics, with Carleman--LB dynamics as a concrete example.

</details>

### [92] [Leveraging generative models to assist Monte Carlo sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07648)
*Marylou Gabrié*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该教程综述探讨了生成模型（如归一化流和扩散模型）作为采样工具的新范式，用于高效采样仅已知到归一化常数的高维概率分布，解决了传统蒙特卡洛方法在高维尺度和多模态分布采样中的固有局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维概率分布采样是科学计算的核心任务，但传统方法（如马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛、退火方法、集体变量增强采样）面临两大挑战：高维扩展性差以及对具有亚稳态特征的多模态分布采样效率低下。文章旨在探索机器学习与计算统计物理交叉领域中出现的新范式——将生成模型作为采样工具，以克服这些局限。

Method: 本文是教程综述，系统回顾了生成模型（归一化流、扩散模型）在采样场景下的应用。这些模型不用于传统的数据驱动场景，而是作为灵活的生成式概率模型，协助对仅已知归一化常数的分布进行采样。文章涵盖两类关键方法论方向：基于生成模型的精确采样器，以及在缺乏数据条件下训练此类模型的策略，并讨论各方法的优缺点。

Result: 文章提供了该快速发展领域的早期发展综述，选取了关键思想和方法进行阐述，包括精确采样器的构建和无数据训练策略，并对这些方法的优势与局限性进行了系统讨论。

Conclusion: 该综述面向物理和机器学习两个领域的读者，作为入门教程提供了该研究方向的起点，旨在为有兴趣探索这一新兴领域的研究人员提供可及的参考框架和方法概览。

Abstract: Sampling high-dimensional probability distributions is a central task in scientific computing, with applications ranging from Bayesian inference to statistical physics and molecular simulation. Despite decades of methodological developments, two major challenges remain: scaling to high dimensions and efficiently exploring multimodal distributions characterized by metastable states. Classical approaches such as Markov chain Monte Carlo, tempering methods, or enhanced sampling based on collective variables have achieved major successes, but they also face intrinsic limitations. This tutorial review explores a new paradigm that has recently emerged at the interface of machine learning and computational statistical physics: the use of generative models as tools for sampling. In this context, models such as normalizing flows and diffusion models are not used in their traditional data-driven setting, but rather as flexible probabilistic models that can assist the sampling of distributions known only up to a normalization constant. This manuscript reviews the early development of this rapidly evolving field and discusses several methodological directions, including exact samplers based on generative models and strategies to train such models in the absence of data. While an exhaustive survey of the literature is not attempted, we present a selection of key ideas and methods, along with a discussion of their strengths and limitations. The review is intended to be an accessible tutorial for both physics and machine learning audiences, and it aims to provide a starting point for researchers interested in exploring this exciting area of research.

</details>


### [93] [RouteGuard: Certifying Routing Gain in LLM Multi-Agent Systems When Complementarity Is Not Enough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07583)
*Anchen Sun,Kaiqi Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出RouteGuard部署认证框架，证明多智能体LLM路由系统中路由增益G=πΔ_E由条件遗憾泛函Φ决定而非AUC，提供有限样本认证区间、Le Cam下界与鲁棒性相变，并在RouterBench和OpenRCA两个基准上验证其作为护栏的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过路由在模型间调度，但部署者在发布前几乎无法知晓路由是否真的能带来增益。现有路由器以优化门控AUC为目标并假设顾问互补性足以保证性能，然而本文证明AUC和顾问互补性都无法决定可部署增益，因此亟需一种部署认证框架。

Method: 将路由增益分解为G=πΔ_E，证明可达增益由条件遗憾泛函Φ决定而非AUC。提出RouteGuard认证框架，构建有限样本认证括区间并配合匹配的Le Cam下界（在固定活动类别上常数锐利），同时给出鲁棒性相变分析。通过RouterBench（11个跨家族模型）和OpenRCA（三个Gemini顾问）两个基准验证，并采用预注册的半合成对照验证校准性。

Result: 在RouterBench上结论取决于采样单元：提示级采样协议认证了对GPT-4的增益，而工作负载聚类重采样下不予认证，原因是增益仅依赖于86个工作负载单元中的3个。在OpenRCA上三个Gemini顾问统计上冗余，所有221个RouterBench池和三个OpenRCA分布下实现的oracle均处于或低于独立性基线，协议正确拒绝认证。半合成对照确认协议在m≥m*时认证真实增益，且不认证真实零假设。

Conclusion: 路由增益不应由AUC或表面互补性来衡量，而需通过条件遗憾泛函Φ进行部署级认证。RouteGuard提供了一套常数锐利、具备匹配下界与相变分析的认证框架，可有效识别真实增益与虚假增益，作为多智能体LLM系统部署前的重要护栏。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems route among model-backed advisors, yet a deployer rarely knows before shipping whether routing will help at all. Prevailing routers optimize a gate's AUC and presume that advisor complementarity suffices. We show that neither determines the deployable gain. We introduce RouteGuard, a deployment-certification framework. Routing gain decomposes as $G = πΔ_E$, and the achievable gain is governed by a conditional-regret functional $Φ$, not by AUC. A finite-sample certification bracket comes with a matching Le Cam lower bound, constant-sharp over the fixed-activity class, and a robustness phase transition. On two benchmarks the framework acts as a guardrail. On RouterBench (11 cross-family models) the verdict depends on the sampling unit: the protocol certifies a gain over GPT-4 under prompt-level sampling and withholds it under workload-cluster resampling, because the gain rests on 3 of 86 workload cells. On OpenRCA (three Gemini advisors) the advisors are statistically redundant: the realized oracle sits at or below the independence baseline in all pools we tested (221 RouterBench pools and three OpenRCA distributions), so the protocol correctly refuses to certify. A pre-registered semi-synthetic control confirms calibration: the protocol certifies a genuine gain once $m \ge m^\star$ and does not certify a true null. Code and frozen artifacts will be released with the published version.

</details>


### [94] [Stochastic gradient descent with discontinuity across a manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07618)
*Vivek S. Borkar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文分析损失函数在低维流形上不连续时随机梯度下降（SGD）的微分方程极限行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际机器学习问题中，损失函数可能在低维流形处不连续，传统SGD分析通常假设损失函数光滑或至少连续，因此需要理论框架来理解此类情形下SGD的收敛行为。

Method: 通过研究SGD的微分方程极限（即随机近似/ODE方法）来分析不连续损失函数的梯度下降动态，将离散的SGD迭代转化为连续时间的微分方程进行理论分析。

Result: 给出了跨低维流形不连续损失函数下SGD微分方程极限的分析结果，揭示了该极限行为及其理论性质。

Conclusion: 该研究为不连续损失函数下的SGD提供了严格的微分方程极限分析框架，扩展了SGD理论分析的适用边界。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent for a loss function discontinuous across lower dimensional manifolds is analyzed by studying its differential equation limit.

</details>


### [95] [Conformal Calibration for Multi-Modal Regression with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07795)
*Ilia Azizi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模态感知的共形校准层，用于多模态回归中的预测区间校准。该方法为每个模态训练或复用预测器，计算模态间的不一致分数，并结合两种互补策略（连续不一致缩放方法和Mondrian分层方法）在严格的拆分协议下进行共形校准。在四个多模态数据集上，该方法在60次配对运行中分别在CRPS和区间宽度上超越或持平基线59/60次和52/60次，并在缺失模态压力测试中最多恢复19.5个百分点的覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态回归（包含表格变量、文本、图像等多种输入源）中，当不同模态信息相互矛盾或部分模态缺失时，预测区间的校准变得困难。现有单一全局分位数方法将所有模态模式混在一起取平均，无法针对测试时观测到的具体模态模式进行校准，导致区间预测不准确。

Method: 提出一个模态感知的共形校准层，核心思路包括：(1) 为每个模态独立训练或复用已有预测器；(2) 根据各模态预测结果计算不一致分数（disagreement score）；(3) 在严格拆分协议下使用该分数进行拆分共形校准（split conformal calibration）。该分数被用于两种互补方式：一是连续的不一致缩放方法，跨样本重新分配区间宽度同时保持边缘拆分共形保证；二是Mondrian（分层）方法，在由不一致性或模态可用性预定义的组内进行校准，在联合可交换性假设下提供组级保证。

Result: 在四个多模态数据集上，不一致缩放层在区间连续排序概率分数（CRPS）上60次配对运行中有59次匹配或优于共形基线，在区间宽度上有52/60次，同时保持经验覆盖率接近95%目标。在缺失模态压力测试中，掩码匹配的重校准在最难的固定掩码场景下可恢复最多19.5个百分点的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该方法是一个简单、模型无关的多模态回归可靠性层，能够在模态不一致或缺失的情况下提供更精确且保持覆盖保证的预测区间，作为现有多模态回归系统的即插即用校准模块。

Abstract: Prediction intervals for multi-modal regression with tabular variables, text, images, or other input sources are difficult to calibrate when those sources disagree or one is missing. A single global quantile averages these regimes together instead of calibrating to the modality pattern observed at test time. We address this through a modality-aware conformal calibration layer. The layer trains or reuses one predictor per modality, computes a disagreement score from their predictions, and uses that score in split conformal calibration under a strict split protocol. We use the score in two complementary ways. First, a continuous disagreement-scaled method reallocates interval width across examples while preserving the usual marginal split-conformal guarantee. Second, a Mondrian (stratified) method calibrates within groups defined by disagreement or modality availability fixed before calibration, giving group guarantees under joint exchangeability of the calibration and test examples. Across four multi-modal datasets, the disagreement-scaled layer matches or improves the marginal conformal baseline in 59 of 60 paired runs for interval continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) and in 52 of 60 for interval width, while keeping empirical coverage near the 95% target. In stress tests with missing modalities, mask-matched recalibration recovers up to 19.5 percentage points of coverage in the hardest fixed-mask regime. The result is a simple, model-agnostic reliability layer for multi-modal regression systems. A project page is available at https://unco3892.github.io/modality-aware-conformal.

</details>


### [96] [The Spectral Neuron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08003)
*Alex Shtoff*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了"频谱神经元"概念：一种标量化模型 f(x)=λ_k(A_0+∑x_i A_i)，通过学习实对称矩阵，从仿射矩阵函数的特征值中读取预测结果。该模型在极值特征值下可产生凸/凹函数，通过半定约束可实现单调性，在保持可解释性的同时随矩阵维度增长获得更强表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简单线性模型透明可解释但表达能力有限，而神经网络表达能力强但不可解释。作者希望寻找一个中间地带：既能随规模扩展表达能力，又保持结构上的可解释性。频谱神经元通过在矩阵特征值层面引入非线性，使得非线性的来源在数学上仍然显式、可解析，从而弥合这二者之间的鸿沟。

Method: 作者提出了频谱神经元模型：f(x)=λ_k(A_0+∑_{i=1}^n x_i A_i)，其中A_0,...,A_n为可学习的实对称矩阵。输入通过仿射矩阵函数进入模型，预测则通过读取该矩阵的一个特征值获得。模型利用极值特征值产生凸/凹函数，通过系数矩阵的半定约束施加单调性，关联特征空间可刻画局部特征敏感性。作者系统结合多个数学领域的谱理论结果来刻画该模型的表达性、鲁棒性、可解释性和形状控制属性。

Result: 作者系统研究了该模型族的鲁棒性、结构可解释性和形状控制属性，并验证了其在实践中可以被学习和扩展（随矩阵维度增长）。模型表现出介于线性模型与神经网络之间的性能特性：随维度扩展表达能力提升，同时保留通过矩阵结构实现的可解释性。

Conclusion: 频谱神经元提供了一种有用的中间方案：模型随矩阵维度增长而更具表达力，同时通过可学习的矩阵保留一定程度的可解释性。该工作为构造兼具可解释性与表达能力的模型家族提供了理论基础和系统性分析方法。

Abstract: As machine learned models increase in complexity and expressive power, features of simpler models, such as interpretability and control over the shape of the modeled function are lost. On the one edge of the spectrum we have simple linear models are transparent and possess good interpretability and explainability properties, but have a limited expressive power.
