On the other edge we have neural networks, that have expressive power that improves with scaling, but are mostly opaque. In this work we develop the \emph{spectral neuron} concept: a scalar model given by $f(\vx)=λ_k \left(A_0+\sum_{i=1}^n x_i A_i\right)$, with learned real symmetric matrices \(A_0,\ldots,A_n\). The input enters the model through an affine matrix function, but the prediction is obtained by reading one of its eigenvalues. Thus, the model is nonlinear, but the source of nonlinearity is still mathematically explicit. This gives us a useful middle ground: the model can become more expressive as the matrix dimension grows, while retaining a degree of structural interpretability through the learned matrices. For example, extremal eigenvalues yield convex or concave functions, semidefinite constraints on the coefficient matrices impose monotonicity, and the associated eigenspaces characterize local feature sensitivity. We study the robustness, structural interpretability, and shape-control properties of this model family, and then test whether it can be learned and scaled in practice. We develop a systematic study of this model family, bringing together spectral results from several mathematical literatures to characterize its expressivity, robustness, interpretability, and shape-control properties.

</details>


### [97] [Conditional Diffusion for Nonparametric Instrumental Variable Quantile Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08204)
*Xingdong Feng,Xinhong Jiang,Yuling Jiao,Lican Kang,Junwei Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种深度非参数工具变量分位数回归（IVQR）的两阶段估计器，结合条件扩散模型与核平滑条件矩公式，用于因果推断中的结构分位数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非参数IVQR方法在高维协变量和工具变量场景下表现不佳，且现有理论保证通常局限于轻尾分布假设，缺乏对重尾分布下估计器误差的统一刻画。本文旨在通过深度学习与扩散模型的结合，突破这些局限。

Method: 两阶段估计器：第一阶段使用方差保持的条件扩散模型估计结果变量与内生协变量在给定工具变量下的联合条件分布；第二阶段通过蒙特卡洛采样和核平滑代理指标函数近似条件矩算子，并在深度神经网络上通过经验风险最小化估计结构分位数函数。

Result: 建立了估计器的超额风险界，并在无界支撑下给出了条件扩散模型的端到端全变差保证（涵盖分数估计、早停和离散化误差）。理论在多项式尾包络假设下成立，并当尾指数增大时连续退化到指数分布情形，获得趋于非参数回归极小极大最优速率的结果。模拟和真实数据显示所提方法优于现有非参数IVQR方法，且在维度升高时优势更显著。

Conclusion: 该方法通过扩散模型与核平滑矩条件的结合，在高维设置下显著优于现有IVQR方法，且理论上统一了重尾与轻尾分布下的保证，实现了更广泛的适用性和更优的经验性能。

Abstract: This work proposes deep nonparametric Instrumental variable quantile regression (IVQR), a two-stage estimator that combines conditional diffusion modeling with a kernel-smoothed conditional moment formulation. In the first stage, we estimate the joint conditional distribution of the outcome and endogenous covariates given the instrument using a variance-preserving conditional diffusion model. In the second stage, we approximate the conditional moment operator through Monte Carlo sampling and a kernel-smoothed surrogate for the indicator function, and then estimate the structural quantile function by empirical risk minimization over deep neural networks. We establish an excess-risk bound for the proposed estimator and derive end-to-end total variation guarantees for the conditional diffusion model under unbounded support, explicitly accounting for score estimation, early stopping, and discretization errors. Our theory is developed under a polynomial-tail envelope on the data distribution and degenerates continuously to the exponential setting: as the tail index grows, the obtained excess-risk rate converges to the minimax-optimal rate of nonparametric regression, thus our heavy-tailed theory covers the classical light-tailed nonparametric guarantees as a limiting case. Simulation studies and a real-data application demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing nonparametric IVQR approaches, with gains that become increasingly pronounced as the dimensionality of the covariates and instruments increases.

</details>


### [98] [ARC: Augmented-Rank Conformalization for Changepoint Localization --- Finite-Sample Validity and Distribution-Robust Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08424)
*Chenchen Peng,Mixia Wu,Qijing Yan,Zhiqi Shen,Jie Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出ARC（增强秩保形化）一族仅依赖于段内秩的分数，用于变点定位，在保持有限样本覆盖的同时实现效率转移，使置信集长度在单调变换下不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的共形变点定位方法虽能提供有限样本覆盖保证，但效率缺乏保障。实际分数常估计密度比，在重尾、偏斜和分布漂移下集合长度会膨胀，且无任何长度保证。这促使研究者探索不依赖具体数据分布的分数设计。

Method: ARC分数族仅通过段内秩依赖数据，包括rank-CUSUM位置与尺度通道、它们的固定组合，以及在合成训练后冻结的轻量级神经分数。每个ARC分数对任意冻结的权重配置（含随机初始化和错误训练）都继承有限样本覆盖。核心结果是效率转移定理：整个ARC置信集在严格递增边际变换下几乎必然不变，集合长度分布仅依赖于数据对的秩结构。

Result: 模拟证实所有分数（包括被破坏的网络）达到名义覆盖；在单调变换下ARC集合保持相同而插件分数膨胀；在well-log基准上ARC将标注偏移定位到3-5个候选并可通过空集标志失配。经典秩检验理论将ARC定位为以有限代价逼近最优不变分数。

Conclusion: ARC通过秩化策略实现了变点定位的覆盖保证与效率保证的统一，同时在串行依赖和趋势型备择这两种边界条件下明确失效而非隐藏。该方法为分布自由的高效共形推断提供了新范式。

Abstract: Conformal changepoint localization turns any score into a confidence set for the changepoint with finite-sample coverage. Coverage is universal; efficiency is not. The oracle score is a likelihood ratio, so practical scores estimate density ratios, and set length deteriorates under heavy tails, skewness, and distribution shift, where no length guarantee applies. We propose ARC (Augmented-Rank Conformalization), a family of scores depending on the data only through within-segment ranks: rank-CUSUM location and scale channels, their fixed combinations, and a lightweight neural score frozen after synthetic training. Every ARC score inherits finite-sample coverage for every frozen weight configuration, including random initialization and mistraining. The main result is an efficiency transfer theorem: the entire ARC confidence set is almost surely invariant under strictly increasing marginal transforms, so the set length distribution depends on the data pair only through its rank structure, and lengths certified once hold verbatim across its monotone orbit, whereas a plug-in score's length changes with every re-expression. Across different rank structures lengths do change, and are reported as such. Classical rank-test theory positions ARC as targeting the optimal invariant score at bounded cost. Simulations confirm nominal coverage for all scores, including sabotaged networks, identical sets under monotone transforms where plug-in scores inflate, and smooth degradation where plug-in sets become vacuous; on the well-log benchmark ARC localizes annotated shifts to three to five candidates and flags misfit by an empty set. Two boundaries are stated rather than hidden: serial dependence destroys exactness, and trend-type alternatives lie outside the piecewise-exchangeable model.

</details>


### [99] [LazyHMC: Hamiltonian Monte Carlo Simulation for Lazy, Infinite Dimensional Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08588)
*Maria-Nicoleta Crăciun,C. -H. Luke Ong,Tom Schrijvers,Sam Staton*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了在无限维参数空间（通过Haskell惰性求值实现）中进行基于梯度的Hamiltonian Monte Carlo（HMC）推理的新方法，并引入了"cylindrical analytic partition下的分段解析"（PACAP）新概念来证明惰性无限维程序中似然函数的梯度是有限支持的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准HMC需要梯度和有限维参数空间，而Haskell的惰性求值允许概率编程表达随机过程等隐式无限维非参数贝叶斯模型，但现有HMC方法无法直接应用于这种无限维设置。

Method: （1）针对自动微分，提出PACAP（piecewise analytic under cylindrical analytic partition）分析框架，证明即使程序是无限维且惰性定义的，似然函数梯度仍是有限支持的；（2）针对蒙特卡洛方法，开发了多种HMC变体以及No-U-Turn Sampler（NUTS），这些方法在无限维参数空间上运作，但由于惰性求值仍然保持产出性。

Result: 在多个实验上验证了方法的有效性，包括高斯混合聚类、随机游走以及带有Poisson过程变点检测的分段常数回归。

Conclusion: 通过惰性求值和PACAP分析框架，可以成功地将基于梯度的HMC推广到无限维参数空间，使得概率编程能够处理更广泛的非参数贝叶斯模型。

Abstract: Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) is a successful generic inference method in probabilistic programming, but in its ordinary formulation it needs gradients and finite-dimensional parameter spaces. In Haskell, lazy evaluation lets probabilistic programs express stochastic processes and other non-parametric Bayesian models over implicit infinite-dimensional spaces. This paper develops new formulations of gradient-based HMC for this infinite-dimensional setting, via lazy evaluation. For automatic differentiation, we provide an analysis based on a new notion of "piecewise analytic under cylindrical analytic partition" (PACAP), to show that even if a program is infinite-dimensional and defined lazily, the gradient of the likelihood function is finitely supported. For the Monte Carlo method itself, we develop several HMC variants and a No-U-Turn Sampler that operate over the infinite-dimensional parameter space but are still productive because of lazy evaluation. Experiments cover Gaussian mixture clustering, random walks, and piecewise-constant regression with Poisson-process changepoints.

</details>


### [100] [Multi-kernel spectral clustering: Entrywise eigenvector perturbation bounds and exact recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08704)
*Zeqin Lin,Guangming Pan,Zhixiang Zhang,Yinbing Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多核谱聚类方法，通过聚合多个不同带宽的核来应对高维数据中多尺度成对距离的问题，并建立了严格的数学理论，证明在多尺度混合模型下可实现精确聚类恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单带宽核谱聚类在处理具有多个特征成对距离尺度的数据时表现不足，这一问题在高维场景中尤为突出。现有方法缺乏对多尺度数据的自适应处理能力，需要一种更灵活的核选择策略来捕捉数据中不同尺度的结构。

Method: 提出多核聚合框架，带宽选自成对平方距离的经验分位数，无需先验总体尺度信息。在异质簇中心和协方差几何的多尺度混合模型下，构建经验多核矩阵的低秩分块常数近似，推导其主导谱分量的行向ℓ2,∞扰动界，以及归一化拉普拉斯矩阵的相应界。

Result: 得到了观察级别的谱嵌入控制（比传统全局特征空间扰动估计更精细）；在合适的特征间隙和簇分离条件下，证明对多核谱嵌入应用近似K-means可实现高概率的精确恢复。

Conclusion: 多核谱聚类优于单带宽方法，在理论层面建立了多尺度高维数据下的精确恢复保证，为核带宽自适应的谱聚类提供了可靠的数学基础。

Abstract: Kernel spectral clustering with a single bandwidth can be inadequate for data exhibiting multiple characteristic pairwise-distance scales, a problem particularly prevalent in the high-dimensional regime. We address this issue through a multi-kernel formulation that aggregates kernels with different bandwidths. The bandwidths are selected as prescribed empirical quantiles of the pairwise squared distances, thereby capturing the relevant distance scales without requiring prior population-scale information.
  We develop a rigorous theoretical analysis of the resulting method under a general high-dimensional, multi-scale mixture model with heterogeneous cluster centers and covariance geometries. We construct a blockwise constant, low-rank informative approximation to the empirical multi-kernel matrix and establish row-wise $\ell_{2,\infty}$ perturbation bounds for its leading spectral components, as well as for the associated normalized Laplacian matrix. These bounds yield observation-level control of the spectral embedding, which is more informative than conventional global eigenspace perturbation estimates. Under suitable eigen-gap and cluster-separation conditions, we show that approximate $K$-means applied to the multi-kernel spectral embedding achieves exact recovery with high probability.

</details>


### [101] [A Distribution Mapping Approach to Counterfactually Fair Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08743)
*Jianhan Zhang,Jitao Wang,John D. Piette,Donglin Zeng,Chengchun Shi,Zhenke Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据预处理算法，通过与策略学习结合，在强化学习（RL）中实现反事实公平（CF），通过分位数分布映射方法顺序估计反事实状态和奖励，并理论证明每步反事实不公平性和无限时域次优性差距有界，且在数值实验和真实数字健康数据集中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗保健等高风险场景中部署强化学习时，策略可能系统性地限制某些子群体获取有价值服务，违背利益相关者的价值观和目标。反事实公平（CF）框架基于因果推理可解决此问题，但缺乏有效的实现方法。

Method: 提出一种数据预处理算法，依赖新颖的分位数分布映射方法，在预处理步骤中顺序估计反事实状态和奖励。该方法将常见的可加性假设作为特例纳入，可与策略学习联合使用以实现RL中的反事实公平。理论上在温和正则条件下证明每步反事实不公平性和无限时域次优性差距的可有界性。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提算法在温和正则条件下，每步反事实不公平性和无限时域次优性差距均能得到有界保证。数值实验以及在真实世界数字健康干预数据集上的应用均验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该数据预处理算法为在高风险RL场景中实现反事实公平提供了一条可行的路径，通过分位数分布映射实现反事实状态和奖励的估计，并具备理论保障和实证支撑。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) seeks to optimize sequential decisions to maximize population-level benefits over time. However, when deployed in high-stakes settings such as healthcare, RL decisions might systematically restrict some subpopulation's access to valuable services in a manner contrary to the values and goals of stakeholders. Counterfactual fairness (CF) offers a promising framework to address this problem based on causal reasoning. This paper develops a data preprocessing algorithm that, when used in tandem with policy learning, enables CF in RL. Our algorithm relies on a novel quantile distribution mapping method for sequentially estimating the counterfactual states and rewards in the data preprocessing step, subsuming common additivity assumptions used for counterfactual prediction as a special case. We theoretically prove that the per-step level of counterfactual unfairness and infinite-horizon suboptimality gap can be bounded under mild regularity conditions. We also empirically test our algorithm in numerical experiments as well as in application to a real-world interventional digital health dataset.

</details>


### [102] [A Mean-Field Framework for Inference-Time Distributional Control of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08770)
*Samuel Howard,Nikolas Nüsken*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于均值场框架的加权交互粒子方案，用于在扩散模型推理时实现对分布级奖励的可控采样。该框架将分布级控制形式化为 targeting 一个倾斜测度，推广了点级奖励控制作为特例，并为现有的批量级控制方法提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型常被用作可控采样器，可通过奖励函数在推理时引导生成。现有奖励大多定义在单个样本上，但许多应用需要分布级奖励（如校准群体信息或鼓励多样性）。虽然直接将奖励梯度引入动力学在实践中有效，但缺乏对采样分布的理论保证。点级奖励已有基于粒子重加权的倾斜分布框架，但分布级奖励缺乏理论依据的方法。

Method: 本文将推理时的分布级控制形式化为在均值场框架下targeting一个倾斜测度，并推导出一个加权交互粒子方案来有原则地逼近该目标。该框架将点级奖励引导作为特例恢复，同时为现有的批量级引导方法提供理论支撑。

Result: 在可处理的低维场景中验证了所提过程能正确targeting规定的分布；并在高维蛋白质构象任务中研究了该方法的行为表现。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了分布级奖励控制缺乏理论基础的方法的空白，提供了一个统一的加权交互粒子框架，统一了点级和分布级奖励的推理时控制，并在低维验证与高维应用中表现出有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly used as controllable samplers, whose generations can be steered at inference time according to a chosen reward function. While such rewards are typically defined on individual samples, for many applications it is desirable to steer according to distribution-level rewards, for example to calibrate with population-level information or to encourage diversity. In both cases, simply incorporating the reward gradient into the dynamics, while often effective, comes with few theoretical guarantees on the sampled distribution. For pointwise rewards, recent work has therefore sought to develop a principled framework for targeting a prescribed tilted distribution using particle reweighting. However, an analogous theoretically-grounded approach for distributional rewards is currently lacking. In this work, we formulate inference-time distributional control as targeting a tilted measure under a mean-field framework, and derive a weighted interacting particle scheme to target it in a principled manner. Our framework recovers pointwise-reward steering as a special case, while providing a theoretical foundation for existing batch-level steering methods. Empirically, we verify that the procedure correctly targets the prescribed distribution in tractable low-dimensional settings, and investigate its behaviour in higher-dimensional protein conformation tasks.

</details>


### [103] [Physics-Informed Learning for Robust Acoustic Localization with Calibrated Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08911)
*Jennifer N. Kampe,Changwoo J. Lee,Xin Shen,Ari Lehtiö,Sandro von Brandenburg,Ossi Nokelainen,David B. Dunson,Otso Ovaskainen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合物理信息声学特征的学习模型，用于修正快速双曲线定位求解器在复杂户外声景中的不理想解，从而显著减少灾难性最坏情况误差，同时保持中位精度，并提供几何感知的不确定性估计，适用于大规模被动声学监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动声学监测（PAM）可大规模获取生态空间点过程数据，但真实户外声景中多径效应、近场效应和复杂传播违背了经典定位方法（如双曲线定位和基于评分的方法）的假设，导致即使在小型探测阵列中也可能出现极端误差。

Method: 提出一种学习方法：通过物理信息声学特征进行操作，修正快速双曲线求解器在产生不切实际解的场景下的输出，替代统计性替换底层物理模型的做法，从物理层面精炼模型以提高非理想条件下的鲁棒性，并提供校准的、几何感知的不确定性估计。

Result: 在真实和模拟户外环境的分布式麦克风阵列上评估，该方法显著减少了灾难性最坏情况误差，同时匹配了快速双曲线求解器的中位精度，并提供了适用于下游空间模型的校准不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了鲁棒的、不确定性感知的声源定位方法，为在复杂声学环境中实现可扩展的自动化野生动物监测迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Recent advances in Passive Acoustic Monitoring (PAM) offer an opportunity to obtain ecological spatial point-process data at unprecedented scale. However, realizing this opportunity necessitates the development of accurate and scalable localization methods. In real-world outdoor soundscapes, however, the assumptions underlying classical localization methods such as hyperbolic and score-based localization are routinely violated by multipath dominance, near-field effects, and complex propagation. Under these conditions, classical localization methods become brittle, with extreme errors possible even in small detection arrays. Rather than statistically replacing the underlying physics, we propose a method to refine it and increase robustness outside of ideal operating conditions: a learned model operating on physics-informed acoustic features corrects a fast hyperbolic solver where it produces implausible solutions, substantially reducing catastrophic worst-case errors while matching its median accuracy on field data. We further provide calibrated, geometry-aware uncertainty estimates suitable for propagation into downstream spatial models. Evaluating on distributed microphone arrays in real and simulated outdoor environments, we demonstrate that the proposed method yields robust, uncertainty-aware localization, providing a step toward scalable automated wildlife monitoring in complex acoustic environments.

</details>


### [104] [Personalized Federated Learning via Variance-Aware Nonparametric Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09074)
*Jae Ho Chang,Arnab Auddy,Subhadeep Paul*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非参数经验贝叶斯（NPEB）的个性化联邦学习方法，通过将局部参数估计视为带噪观测来估计未知的共享先验分布。关键创新在于提出了方差感知的非参数经验贝叶斯（VANEB）框架，引入了广义Tweedie公式来处理异方差情形，并给出了密度估计的非渐近误差界和oracle去噪不等式。该方法在理论和实验上均展现了良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化联邦学习在异构客户端场景下具有重要应用价值，但现有的NPEB方法假设方差已知且固定，这与实际中局部M估计器的方差依赖于参数且未知的情况不符，限制了NPEB在联邦学习中的应用。

Method: 1）利用局部参数估计的渐近正态性，将其建模为带噪观测来估计未知共享先验；2）提出VANEB框架，利用局部M估计器参数相关的渐近方差；3）推导异方差设置下的广义Tweedie公式；4）建立密度估计在平均平方Hellinger距离下的非渐近误差率，并推导oracle去噪不等式；5）针对DNN场景提出VANEB-head和VANEB-FT两种启发式扩展，通过近似对角方差估计器个性化最后一层全连接层。

Result: 在理论上，建立了密度估计的非渐近误差率和oracle去噪不等式；在实验上，VANEB方法在MNIST和CIFAR-10视觉数据集上使用卷积神经网络架构表现出强性能。

Conclusion: VANEB框架通过方差感知的非参数经验贝叶斯方法有效解决了异方差个性化联邦学习问题，理论保证和实证结果均验证了方法的有效性，并展示了向现代深度学习联邦学习场景扩展的潜力。

Abstract: We develop a new approach to Personalized Federated Learning across heterogeneous clients using Nonparametric Empirical Bayes (NPEB). Leveraging the asymptotic normality of local parameter estimates obtained from Empirical Risk Minimization or M-estimation, our method formulates these estimates as noisy observations to estimate an unknown shared prior via Nonparametric Maximum Likelihood. A key challenge in applying NPEB in this setting is that existing approaches assume known fixed variances, which is not true in practice. To address this, we introduce a Variance-Aware Nonparametric Empirical Bayes (VANEB) framework that leverages the parameter-dependent asymptotic variance of local M-estimators. A key technical contribution is a generalized Tweedie's formula for this heteroskedastic setting. We then establish non-asymptotic error rates for density estimation in the average squared Hellinger distance and derive an oracle denoising inequality that provides error bounds for our estimator. While our theoretical guarantees are rooted in the asymptotic regime of M-estimators, we empirically explore heuristic extensions of VANEB to modern federated learning settings involving Deep Neural Networks (DNNs). For DNNs, we propose VANEB-head and VANEB-FT, which personalize the last fully connected layer via an NPEB step using an approximate diagonal variance estimator. We show that our method has strong performance on popular vision datasets MNIST and CIFAR-10, using a convolutional neural network architecture.

</details>


### [105] [CPDA: Class-Conditional Path Distribution Alignment for Unsupervised Time-Series Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09193)
*Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了CPDA（类条件路径分布对齐）框架，一种非对抗性的基于差异的时间序列无监督域自适应方法，通过复合签名-谱核对齐源域与目标域的类条件潜在路径分布，并在13个基准数据集上超越了30个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列域自适应方法通常仅通过对齐边缘特征分布（通过对抗训练、最优传输或矩差异）来缓解分布偏移，但忽略了类条件分布的结构信息，导致跨域迁移时类信息可能丢失或混淆。

Method: CPDA引入复合签名-谱核，联合捕获池化语义特征、时间路径结构、频域信息和低秩路径签名动力学；利用源域标签和目标域软伪标签执行类保持对齐；并提供理论分析证明CPDA定义有效的核差异、将现有矩匹配方法作为特例包含，并获得类条件目标风险边界。

Result: 在CNN、ResNet18和TCN三种骨干网络上，对13个不同的时间序列域自适应基准数据集进行广泛实验，CPDA在30个差异、对抗和伪标签基线中表现出有效性。

Conclusion: CPDA通过对齐类条件潜在路径分布而非仅全局特征边缘分布，实现了更精细的跨域分布对齐，为时间序列无监督域自适应提供了兼顾理论保证和实际性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised time-series domain adaptation (DA) addresses the challenge of transferring a classifier from a labeled source domain to an unlabeled target domain under distribution shifts induced by different users, sensors, devices, acquisition conditions, or temporal dynamics. Existing methods typically mitigate this shift by aligning marginal feature distributions through adversarial training, optimal transport, or moment-based discrepancies. In this paper, we propose Class-Conditional Path Distribution Alignment (CPDA), a non-adversarial discrepancy-based framework that aligns source and target class-conditional latent path distributions rather than only global feature marginals. CPDA introduces a composite signature-spectral kernel that jointly captures pooled semantic features, temporal path structure, frequency-domain information, and low-rank path-signature dynamics, while using source labels and target soft pseudo-labels to perform class-preserving alignment. We further provide a theoretical analysis showing that CPDA defines a valid kernel discrepancy, admits existing moment-matching methods as restricted cases, and yields a class-conditional target-risk bound. Extensive experiments with CNN, ResNet18, and TCN backbones on 13 different time-series DA benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of CPDA against 30 discrepancy, adversarial, and pseudo-labeling baselines.

</details>


### [106] [Logarithmic-Free Moment and Generalization Bounds for Uniformly Stable Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09870)
*Thanh Nguyen-Cung,Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文回答了Bousquet等人(2020)提出的开放问题，证明了一个矩不等式中的log n因子可以被移除，将上界从O(nβ log n + M√(pn))改进为O(pnβ + M√(pn))，并匹配了已知的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀稳定性是控制学习算法泛化误差的经典工具。Bousquet、Klochkov和Zhivotovskiy(2020)将均匀稳定性问题归结为弱相互作用函数和的矩不等式，但其上界中包含一个额外的log n因子。作者希望回答该论文中提出的问题：这个log n因子是否可以被移除以得到更紧的界。

Method: 作者首先在Rademacher立方体上建立所需的估计，然后通过一个"双副本随机化"（two-copy randomization）论证将该估计转移到任意乘积分布上。利用E[g_i(Z)|Z_{-i}]=0和|E[g_i(Z)|Z_i]|≤M的条件，对任意p≥2证明了||Σg_i(Z)||_p ≤ 16pnβ + M√(2pn)。

Result: 对于任意p≥2，证明了矩不等式||Σg_i(Z)||_p ≤ 16pnβ + M√(2pn)，移除了之前界中的log n因子，并在Bousquet等人构造所覆盖的范围内与下界匹配到通用常数。

Conclusion: 该矩不等式是最优的（在通用常数意义下），回答了Bousquet等人提出的开放问题，从而为均匀稳定性框架下的泛化界提供了严格更紧的分析。

Abstract: Uniform stability is a classical tool for controlling the generalization error of a learning algorithm. Bousquet, Klochkov, and Zhivotovskiy (2020) showed that the problem can be reduced to a moment inequality for a sum of weakly interacting functions of independent random variables. Their bound contains an additional factor $\log n$, and they asked whether this factor can be removed. We answer this upper-bound question affirmatively. More specifically, let $Z=(Z_1,\ldots,Z_n)$ have independent coordinates and let $g_i(Z)$ satisfy $$ \mathbb E[g_i(Z)\mid Z_{-i}]=0, \qquad \left| \mathbb E[g_i(Z)\mid Z_i]\right|\le M, \qquad \forall i = \overline{1, n} $$ while changing any coordinate $Z_j$, $j\neq i$, changes $g_i$ by at most $β$ and $Z_{-i}$ denotes all coordinates except $Z_i$. We prove that, for every $p\ge2$, $$ \left\| \sum_{i=1}^n g_i(Z)\right\|_p \le 16pnβ+M\sqrt{2pn}. $$ This removes the $\log n$ factor from the previous bound and matches the lower bound of Bousquet, Klochkov, and Zhivotovskiy up to universal constants in the range covered by their construction. Our proof first establishes the required estimate on the Rademacher cube, then transfers it to arbitrary product distributions by a two-copy randomization argument.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Disconnected multigraded Hilbert schemes on $\mathbb{P}^2\times\mathbb{P}^1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07704)
*Yairon Cid-Ruiz*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造了双射影空间上无穷多个不连通的多分次Hilbert概形，对任意整数a≥2及特定多项式p_a，Hilb_{p_a}(P²×P¹)都是不连通的，从而证明即使是在仅含五个变量的标准双分次多项式环上，也存在无穷多个不连通的Haiman-Sturmfels多分次Hilbert概形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多分次Hilbert概形（Haiman-Sturmfels多分次Hilbert概形）的连通性是一个基本且重要的问题。此前关于分次Hilbert概形不连通性的结果较为有限，尤其是在低维、少变量情形下。作者希望找到尽可能简单背景下（如五变量标准双分次）的无穷族不连通例子。

Method: 作者在双射影空间P²_k ×_k P¹_k上，针对多项式p_a(z1,z2)=2az1+az2+3a-2a²构造多分次Hilbert概形，通过构造特定的闭点子方案，证明Hilb_{p_a}可分解为至少两个不同的不可约分支或连通分支，从而验证其不连通性。

Result: 对任意整数a≥2，多分次Hilbert概形Hilb_{p_a}(P²×P¹)均为不连通的。因此，存在无穷多个不连通的Haiman-Sturmfels多分次Hilbert概形，且这些例子出现在仅含五个变量的多项式环的标准双分次设置下。

Conclusion: 该结果将不连通多分次Hilbert概形的存在性推广至非常低维且低变量数的情形，表明即使在大分次结构最简单的背景下，Hilbert概形的连通性也难以保证，丰富了人们对多分次Hilbert概形拓扑结构的认识。

Abstract: We exhibit an infinite family of disconnected multigraded Hilbert schemes on the biprojective space $\mathbb{P}^2_{\mathbb{k}} \times_\mathbb{k} \mathbb{P}^1_{\mathbb{k}}$. More precisely, for any integer $a \ge 2$ and $p_a(z_1,z_2) = 2az_1+az_2+3a-2a^2 \in \mathbb{Q}[z_1,z_2]$, the multigraded Hilbert scheme ${\rm Hilb}_{p_a}(\mathbb{P}^2_{\mathbb{k}}\times_\mathbb{k} \mathbb{P}^1_{\mathbb{k}})$ is disconnected. As a consequence, there exist infinitely many disconnected Haiman-Sturmfels multigraded Hilbert schemes even for a standard bigrading on a polynomial ring in only five variables.

</details>


### [108] [The dimension threshold for Batyrev's non-negativity conjecture on stringy Hodge numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07836)
*Jiahui Huang,Matthew Satriano*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Batyrev非负性猜想在维数不超过4时成立，并在维数至少为5时给出了反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Batyrev非负性猜想是镜像对称、动机积分和McKay对应领域中的一个重要指导性猜想，验证或推翻该猜想对理解这些数学分支的深层结构具有重大意义。

Method: 作者在不同维数下对Batyrev非负性猜想进行分析验证：在维数≤4时证明猜想成立，在维数≥5时构造反例来否定该猜想。

Result: Batyrev非负性猜想在维数不超过4时成立，但在维数至少5时存在反例，即猜想在较高维数下不成立。

Conclusion: Batyrev非负性猜想的成立范围有一个精确的维数界限（≤4成立，≥5不成立），这为相关领域的后续研究提供了明确的边界条件和修正方向。

Abstract: Batyrev's non-negativity conjecture has been a major guiding conjecture in mirror symmetry, motivic integration, and the McKay correspondence. We show the conjecture is true in dimension at most 4 and give counter-examples in dimension at least 5.

</details>


### [109] [A remark on Banecki's theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07843)
*Shulim Kaliman*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了所有光滑完全有理簇在Gromov意义下都是代数椭圆的，将Banecki关于光滑射影有理簇的结果推广到了更一般的完全簇情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Banecki证明了每个光滑射影有理簇都是代数椭圆的（Gromov意义下）。本文的目标是将其结果从射影簇推广到更一般的完全簇，因为完全簇是比射影簇更宽泛的代数簇类别。

Method: 通过修改Banecki的证明方法，使其适用于光滑完全有理簇。具体来说，调整证明中的技术细节以处理完全簇与射影簇之间的差异，从而将结论推广到更一般的设定。

Result: 成功证明了每个光滑完全有理簇都是代数椭圆的，将Banecki的结果从射影情形推广到了完全情形。

Conclusion: 所有光滑完全有理簇都满足代数椭圆性，这一结果完善了Gromov椭圆性理论中对有理簇的刻画，将其覆盖范围从射影情形扩展到完全情形。

Abstract: J. Banecki proved that every smooth projective rational variety is algebraically elliptic in the sense of Gromov. Modifying his proof we show that the same is true for every smooth complete rational variety.

</details>


### [110] [Deformations, local freeness, and base change for higher Du Bois singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07849)
*Haoming Ning*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了严格的更高Du Bois奇点在小形变下的不变性，并基于此证明了相对Du Bois复形的基变换定理，回答了Kovács–Taji的一个问题。同时展示了1-Du Bois纤维在形变不变性和基变换方面的失效反例，说明严格性条件本质上是尖锐的。作为基变换定理的应用，将Friedman–Laza的局部自由性定理推广到非局部完全交情形，并证明了一般基上家族的Hodge数不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kovács–Taji提出了关于相对Du Bois复形基变换的问题，这一问题关系到代数簇族中奇点性质的形变行为。此前对1-Du Bois纤维的研究表明基变换不一定成立，需要引入严格的更高Du Bois条件来获得理想结果。同时，Friedman–Laza的局部自由性定理仅限于局部完全交（lci）情形，作者希望突破这一限制。

Method: 作者首先证明严格更高Du Bois奇点（strict higher Du Bois singularities）在小形变下保持稳定，这是证明基变换定理的关键技术基础。基变换定理处理了相对Du Bois复形在具有严格更高Du Bois纤维的族中的行为。通过构造反例展示1-Du Bois条件不足以保证形变不变性和基变换，从而论证严格条件的必要性。在应用方面，将Friedman–Laza的定理从lci设置推广到光滑曲线上的任意族，并推导出任意基上家族的Hodge数恒定性与基变换定理相结合。

Result: 成功证明了严格更高Du Bois奇点的小形变不变性；建立了满足严格更高Du Bois纤维条件的相对Du Bois复形基变换定理，解决了Kovács–Taji的问题；构造了1-Du Bois情形的反例，表明放宽严格性条件会导致失败；将Friedman–Laza局部自由性定理推广到非lci情形；证明了任意基上的家族Hodge数不变性。

Conclusion: 严格更高Du Bois条件是基变换定理的充分且本质性必要条件（在1-Du Bois层面），该工作不仅回答了Kovács–Taji的开放问题，还开辟了新的应用方向，包括Hodge理论在更一般代数簇族中的应用，并为Friedman–Laza定理的推广提供了新的技术框架。

Abstract: We prove that strict higher Du Bois singularities are invariant under small deformations. Using this, we prove a base change theorem for the relative Du Bois complex with strict higher Du Bois fibers, answering a question of Kovács--Taji. We exhibit failures of deformation invariance and base change for $1$-Du Bois fibers, showing that the strictness condition is essentially sharp. As applications of base change, we generalize the local-freeness theorem of Friedman--Laza beyond the local complete intersection setting for families over a smooth curve, and prove constancy of Hodge numbers for families over an arbitrary base.

</details>


### [111] [A note on geometry of Bridgeland Moduli spaces on Gushel-Mukai threefolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07853)
*Songtao Kenneth Ma*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明Gushel-Mukai三维簇的Kuznetsov分量中的Bridgeland模空间是正规的，并在特定数值条件下（数值类本原且X为一般时）证明该模空间是整的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Gushel-Mukai三维簇上的Kuznetsov分量中半稳定对象的Bridgeland模空间的几何性质，特别是其正规性和整性，这是模空间理论中的重要问题。

Method: 通过分析Kuznetsov分量中的Bridgeland半稳定对象的模空间，利用数值条件和一般性假设，建立模空间的正规性和整性证明。

Result: 证明了所有Bridgeland模空间的正规性；在数值类本原且X为一般的条件下，证明了相应模空间的整性。

Conclusion: 给出了Gushel-Mukai三维簇Kuznetsov分量中Bridgeland模空间的正规性和整性结果，深化了对该类模空间几何结构的理解。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a Gushel--Mukai threefold and let $\operatorname{Ku}(X)$ be its Kuznetsov component. We show that the Bridgeland moduli spaces of semistable objects in $\operatorname{Ku}(X)$ are normal. Moreover, under certain numerical conditions, if the numerical class is primitive and $X$ is general, we prove that the corresponding Bridgeland moduli space is integral.

</details>


### [112] [Automorphisms of very general blow up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07983)
*Supravat Sarkar*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于维数≥2且非有理曲面的射影簇X，在足够多的一般点上做爆破后，所得簇没有非平凡自同构。此前类似结果仅对\mathbb{P}^2和\mathbb{P}^3已知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究爆破后的代数簇的自同构群何时变为平凡群，是代数几何中一个基本且重要的问题。此前结果局限于射影平面和射影三维空间，本文将其推广到更广泛的射影簇类。

Method: 通过选取足够多的一般点（一般为非常一般点）进行爆破，利用自同构必须保持爆破例外除子结构这一约束，结合射影簇的维数和几何性质（排除有理曲面的情况）来证明自同构只能为恒等映射。

Result: 对任意维数≥2且非有理曲面的射影簇X，在足够多的一般点处爆破后得到的簇具有平凡的自同构群。

Conclusion: 该结果将关于\mathbb{P}^2和\mathbb{P}^3的已知定理推广到所有非有理曲面的高维射影簇，填补了代数簇自同构平凡性研究的重要空白。

Abstract: We show that if $X$ is a projective variety of dimension $\geq 2$ that is not a rational surface, then the blow up of $X$ at sufficiently many very general points has no nontrivial automorphism. Similar results were known before for $\mathbb{P}^2$ and $\mathbb{P}^3.$

</details>


### [113] [Pseudo-hyperbolicity of Horikawa surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08276)
*Anibal Aravena,Jaime Negrete,Wern Yeong*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了非常一般的Horikawa曲面（最小时丛斜率的一般型极小复代数曲面）在特定条件下仅包含有限条有理曲线或椭圆曲线，并给出了这些曲线的显式刻画与计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Horikawa曲面是一类具有极小Chern斜率的重要一般型曲面，理解其上低亏格（有理或椭圆）曲线的分布对于研究曲面双有理几何结构和有理点问题具有重要意义。

Method: 通过对Horikawa曲面的Chern数条件（c2=5c1²+36或c2=5c1²+30）进行分类分析，利用代数几何中的标准技巧（如曲线的亏格公式、交数计算等）来刻画并计数有理曲线和椭圆曲线。

Result: 证明了当p_g≥5时，非常一般的Horikawa曲面仅含有有限条有理曲线或椭圆曲线，并对这些曲线给出了显式刻画与计数；该结果同样适用于第一类且p_g∈{3,4}的非常一般Horikawa曲面。

Conclusion: 非常一般的Horikawa曲面上的有理曲线和椭圆曲线是有限的且可显式分类的，这一结论在足够大的几何亏格条件下成立，并部分延伸到较小的亏格情形。

Abstract: Horikawa surfaces are minimal complex algebraic surfaces of general type with minimal Chern slope, satisfying either $c_2=5c^2_1+36$ if $c_1^2$ is even, or $c_2=5c^2_1+30$ if $c_1^2$ is odd. We prove that very general Horikawa surfaces with $p_g\ge 5$ contain only finitely many rational or elliptic curves. Moreover, we provide an explicit characterization and count of these curves. Our results also apply to very general Horikawa surfaces of the first kind with $p_g\in \{3,4\}.$

</details>


### [114] [Fundamental forms and infinitesimal symmetries of projective varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08442)
*Jun-Muk Hwang,Qifeng Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文给出了射影簇Z⊂PV的线性自同构群维数的一个上界，该上界由其在一般点处的基本形式决定，并证明该上界恰在Z射影等价于Euler对称簇时达到。作为副产品，论文确定了Euler对称簇的无穷小自同构李代数，并获得了关于保持基本形式同构型的Euler对称簇特化的刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究射影簇的线性自同构群维数与几何不变量（基本形式）之间的关系，旨在建立自同构群大小的几何刻画，并揭示达到最大维数的簇的结构特征。

Method: 利用射影簇在一般点处的基本形式（fundamental forms）作为工具，推导线性自同构群维数的上界；通过分析该上界达到的等号条件，刻画Euler对称簇；进一步计算Euler对称簇的无穷小自同构李代数，并研究保持基本形式同构型的特化刚性。

Result: 建立了线性自同构群维数关于基本形式的上界，精确刻画了等号成立的情形（射影等价于Euler对称簇），确定了Euler对称簇的无穷小自同构李代数，并证明了关于基本形式同构型保持的特化刚性结果。

Conclusion: 线性自同构群维数达到最大值当且仅当该簇射影等价于Euler对称簇，且这一结论同时给出了无穷小自同构李代数的完整描述及特化刚性结果。

Abstract: We give a bound on the dimension of the linear automorphism group of a projective variety $Z \subset \mathbb{P} V$ in terms of its fundamental forms at a general point. Moreover, we show that the bound is achieved precisely when $Z \subset \mathbb{P} V$ is projectively equivalent to an Euler-symmetric variety. As a by-product, we determine the Lie algebra of infinitesimal automorphisms of an Euler-symmetric variety and also obtain a rigidity result on the specialization of an Euler-symmetric variety preserving the isomorphism type of the fundamental forms.

</details>


### [115] [Tangent discontinuity in the oper stratification of de Rham moduli spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08450)
*Pengfei Huang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在亏格$g\geq4$的每条光滑复射影曲线上，给出了秩2情况下Simpson关于flat bundle模空间$\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{dR}}(X,r)$上oper分层纤维构成光滑叶状结构猜想的一个反例。核心论证是证明切平面沿一条穿过两个相邻分层的全纯曲线不连续，从而叶状结构断言失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Simpson在稳定轨迹上构造了oper分层，其纤维为Lagrangian子簇，并提出了一个著名猜想（即叶状结构猜想）：这些纤维是否闭合且能拼成一个光滑叶状结构。验证或反驳该猜想对理解de Rham模空间与oper层的几何结构具有重要意义。

Method: 作者在秩2、亏格$g\geq4$的情况下，构造了一条穿过两个相邻oper分层的全纯曲线，并证明沿这条曲线切平面是不连续的。通过展示切平面场的间断性，直接排除了这些Lagrangian纤维构成光滑叶状结构的可能性，从而构造出该猜想的反例。

Result: 在秩2且亏格$g\geq4$的任意光滑复射影曲线上，成功构造了Simpson叶状结构猜想的反例，证明了该猜想在此情形下不成立。

Conclusion: Simpson的叶状结构猜想在一般情形下是错误的；至少在秩2、亏格$\geq4$的曲线上，oper分层纤维无法构成光滑叶状结构，这为理解模空间的分层几何提供了新的反面例证。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth complex projective curve of genus $g$, and let $\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{dR}}(X,r)$ be the moduli space of flat bundles of rank $r$. Over the stable locus, Simpson showed the oper stratification with Lagrangian fibers and asked whether these fibers are closed and fit together into a smooth foliation. This question is often referred to as the foliation conjecture. In this paper, we give a counterexample to this conjecture in rank two on every curve of genus $g\geq4$. The main idea is to show that the tangent planes are discontinuous along a holomorphic curve crossing two adjacent strata, hence the foliation assertion fails.

</details>


### [116] [$E_1$-Degeneration for Irregular Hodge Filtrations on Deligne--Mumford Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08539)
*Haoxu Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了光滑分离的Deligne–Mumford叠（粗空间为准射影）在任意有理指数处不规则Hodge滤过的E1-退化性，该结论在一切好的叠紧化和NC有理叠紧化上均成立，从而可在任意这类紧化上计算滤过及其分次维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Esnault–Sabbah–Yu关于光滑射影簇上不规则Hodge滤过E1-退化的定理推广到光滑分离的Deligne–Mumford叠的情形，因为叠的紧化不唯一且需要证明退化性不依赖于紧化的选取。

Method: 利用Kontsevich复形的有限平坦下降技术，将射影叠的情形归约到Esnault–Sabbah–Yu关于光滑射影簇的已知定理。

Result: 在粗空间为准射影的光滑分离DM叠上，不规则Hodge滤过在一切有理指数处E1-退化，且该结果在任意好的叠紧化和任意NC有理叠紧化上都成立；滤过及其分次维数可在任意这类紧化上计算。

Conclusion: 不规则Hodge滤过的E1-退化性对光滑分离DM叠是紧化无关的，从而为叠上退化性的计算提供了统一且稳定的框架。

Abstract: Let $(\mathscr U,w)$ be a smooth separated Deligne--Mumford stack over $\mathbb{C}$. Assume that its coarse space is quasi-projective. We prove $E_1$-degeneration at every rational index for its irregular Hodge filtration. The result holds on every good stack compactification and every NC rational stack compactification. In particular, the filtration and its graded dimensions can be computed on any such compactification. The proof uses finite flat descent for Kontsevich complexes. It reduces the projective stack case to the theorem of Esnault--Sabbah--Yu for smooth projective varieties.

</details>


### [117] [A Torelli-type theorem for hyperkähler quotients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08680)
*Ryota Kotani*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了由四元数向量空间H^n的hyperkähler商导出的代数渐近hyperkähler结构的模空间，在其上考虑周期映射，并在假设Kirwan映射满射的条件下证明了Torelli型定理（周期映射的双射性），推广了Kronheimer关于ALE引力瞬时子的Torelli定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Kronheimer（1989）针对ALE引力瞬时子证明的Torelli型定理推广到更一般的代数几何框架中。ALE引力瞬时子是四维的asymptotically locally Euclidean hyperkähler流形，而本文考虑更高维的、由四元数向量空间的hyperkähler商得到的代数渐近hyperkähler结构，旨在建立这类结构上周期映射的双射性。

Method: 研究由四元数向量空间H^n经hyperkähler商得到的代数渐近hyperkähler结构的模空间，在该模空间上定义并考察周期映射，借助Kirwan映射满射的假设来证明周期映射的双射性（Torelli型定理）。证明框架通过超Kähler约化的代数几何化技术实现。

Result: 在Kirwan映射满射的假设下，证明了几何模空间上周期映射是双射的（即Torelli型定理成立）。该定理适用于环面超Kähler簇和Nakajima箭图簇这两个重要特例，给出了Kronheimer ALE引力瞬子Torelli定理的代数几何推广。

Conclusion: 本文建立了代数渐近hyperkähler商结构的一类Torelli型定理，将Kronheimer的四维经典结果推广到更广的代数几何范畴，并为环面超Kähler簇和Nakajima箭图簇提供了具体的适用场景，这为后续研究这类模空间的几何性质奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the moduli space of algebraic asymptotic hyperkähler structures on hyperkähler quotients arising from a quaternionic vector space $\mathbb{H}^n$. We consider the period map on this moduli space, and prove a Torelli-type theorem (i.e., the bijectivity of the period map) assuming the surjectivity of the Kirwan map. This work provides an algebro-geometric generalization of the Torelli-type theorem for ALE gravitational instantons by Kronheimer (1989). In particular, this theorem applies to toric hyperkähler varieties and Nakajima quiver varieties.

</details>


### [118] [Essential p-dimension and Chern numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08877)
*Olivier Haution*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对光滑射影几何连通簇X（定义在含p阶单位根的域k上），若X具有一个素数p的Chern数，则任何有限p群在X上的作用都通过GL_n(k)的某个子群分解（n=dim X）。这得出一个对基域算术敏感的定点定理，以及Cremona群中循环p子群阶数的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有限p群的表示论性质迁移到它们在代数簇上的作用，并推导出比已知结果更精细、对基域算术敏感的定点定理以及Cremona群中p元素的阶数界。

Method: 方法基于一个独立有趣的观察：对特征零域上的仿射代数群G，ed_p(G)+dim G等于具有泛自由G作用的光滑射影簇Y的最小维数，使得度映射CH_G(Y)→F_p非零。核心输入是Karpenko和Merkurjev关于p群本质p维数的计算结果。

Result: 证明了有限p群在X上的作用可通过GL_n(k)的子群分解；推得对基域算术敏感的定点定理；得到Cremona群中循环p子群阶数的界，例如当p≥n+2时Cr_n(Q)不含p^2阶元素。

Conclusion: 通过本质p维数与簇的Chern数条件建立了有限p群作用的结构定理，统一并推广了已知的定点定理和Cremona群相关的阶数界结果。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth, projective, geometrically connected variety over a field $k$ containing a root of unity of order $p$. If $X$ has a Chern number prime to $p$, we show that every action of a finite $p$-group on $X$ factors through a subgroup of $\operatorname{GL}_n(k)$, where $n=\dim X$. This allows one to transfer properties of representations of finite $p$-groups to their actions on $X$. We deduce a fixed-point theorem which, unlike previously known results of this kind, is sensitive to the arithmetic of the base field. We also obtain a bound on the orders of cyclic $p$-subgroups of the Cremona groups: for instance $\operatorname{Cr}_n(\mathbb{Q})$ contains no element of order $p^2$ when $p \ge n+2$.
  The method is based on the following observation, of independent interest. For an affine algebraic group $G$ over a field of characteristic zero, $\operatorname{ed}_p(G) + \dim G$ is the least dimension of a smooth projective variety $Y$ with a generically free $G$-action such that the degree map $\operatorname{CH}_G(Y) \to \mathbb{F}_p$ is nonzero.
  A key input for our result is Karpenko and Merkurjev's computation of the essential $p$-dimension of $p$-groups.

</details>


### [119] [The universal cover of the second-type locus of a cubic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08909)
*Frank Gounelas*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一般四次三维簇（cubic fourfold）上第二类直线构成的曲面，其基本群阶为2。其万有覆盖（由Huybrechts构造）通过考虑每条第二类直线上Gauss映射的两个分支点得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一般四次三维簇上第二类直线的几何性质，特别是这些直线所构成曲面的拓扑结构（基本群），以深入理解四次三维簇的代数几何结构。

Method: 利用Huybrechts构造的万有覆盖，该覆盖通过分析每条第二类直线上Gauss映射的两个分支点来实现，从而研究该曲面的基本群。

Result: 证明了一般四次三维簇上第二类直线构成的曲面的基本群阶为2。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了四次三维簇上第二类直线曲面的拓扑性质，表明其万有覆盖是二重覆盖，且与Gauss映射的分支点结构密切相关。

Abstract: We prove that the surface of second-type lines on a general cubic fourfold has fundamental group of order two. Its universal cover, constructed by Huybrechts, is obtained by considering the two ramification points of the Gauss map along each second-type line.

</details>


### [120] [Geometric characterization of $p$-exceptional monomial GAPN functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08969)
*Masamichi Kuroda,Kentaro Mitsui*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出了p-例外单项式GAPN函数的几何刻画，通过建立有限域F_q[x,y]中无绝对不可约因子多项式的几何特征，将GAPN函数分类问题转化为代数几何问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PN和APN函数作为高度非线性函数类已被广泛研究，其p-例外单项式情形已完成分类，但GAPN函数对应的p-例外问题至今未解决，需要一种新的方法来解决这一开放问题。

Method: 本文采用代数几何方法，首先对素数幂q建立F_q[x,y]中无绝对不可约因子（定义于F_q）的非零多项式的几何刻画，然后利用这一刻画来给出p-例外单项式GAPN函数的几何特征。

Result: 成功给出了p-例外单项式GAPN函数的几何刻画，为后续完全分类GAPN函数奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 通过几何方法，本文将近有限域上高度非线性函数（GAPN）的分类问题转化为代数几何中关于绝对不可约因子的判定问题，为最终解决GAPN函数的p-例外分类问题提供了关键工具。

Abstract: On finite fields of characteristic $p$, PN (perfect nonlinear) functions for odd $p$ and APN (almost perfect nonlinear) functions for even $p$ are well-known classes of highly nonlinear functions. GAPN (generalized almost perfect nonlinear) functions were introduced as a generalization of APN functions for even $p$ to all $p$. One of the main targets of studies on such highly nonlinear functions is their classification. While $p$-exceptional monomial PN and $2$-exceptional monomial APN functions have been classified, the corresponding problem for GAPN functions remains open. Here, a polynomial over $\mathbb{F}_p$ is called a $p$-exceptional PN (resp. APN, resp. GAPN) function if it is a PN (resp. APN, resp. GAPN) function on $\mathbb{F}_{p^n}$ for infinitely many positive integers $n$.
  In this paper, we give a geometric characterization of $p$-exceptional monomial GAPN functions. To this end, for a prime power $q$, we establish a geometric characterization of non-zero polynomials in $\mathbb{F}_{q}[x,y]$ having no absolutely irreducible factor defined over $\mathbb{F}_{q}$.

</details>


### [121] [Mori dream Jacobian elliptic surfaces of Kodaira dimension one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09002)
*Antonio Laface,Sichen Li,Jihao Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究复射影线上Jacobian椭圆曲面X的Mori锥与Mori dream性质。当Mordell-Weil群有限且仅有单个I_n型可约纤维时，证明了n≤4χ时闭Mori锥为有理多面体锥，n≤2χ+3时X为Mori dream曲面。通过Mori锥的显式描述与Artin准则结合证明。同时构造反例说明Kodaira维数1时有限的MW群和自同构群不蕴含Mori锥多面体性；在(χ,n)=(3,11)构造Picard数12的非Mori dream曲面；并对每个ρ≥2构造了Kodaira维数1、Picard数ρ的Mori dream曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代数曲面研究中，Mori dream曲面与Mori锥的有理多面体性是最核心的问题之一。对于Kodaira维数为1的曲面（椭圆曲面），人们希望从数值不变量（如欧拉示性数χ、可约纤维类型）出发判定Mori锥的几何形状，并理解Mordell-Weil群和自同构群的有限性与Mori锥多面体性之间的关系。

Method: 1) 对X给出Mori锥的显式刻画：利用椭圆纤维化的结构描述极值射线，借助可约纤维I_n的零点/原点的自相交信息。2) 将Mori锥对偶的nef锥中极值射线对应的null locus，用Artin准则判定其在纤维上的算术性质，从而证明多面体性。3) 利用构造性例证（含具体数值与Picard数）剖析境界情形，说明有限性条件与多面体性的不蕴含关系，并对一般ρ构造Mori dream曲面。

Result: 证明了：当n≤4χ时Mori锥为有理多面体锥；当n≤2χ+3时X为Mori dream曲面。构造了Kodaira维数1、MW群与自同构群均有限但Mori锥非多面体性的反例；在(χ,n)=(3,11)时构造了Picard数12且存在big and nef非semiample除子的非Mori dream曲面；对每个ρ≥2构造了Kodaira维数1、Picard数ρ的Mori dream曲面。

Conclusion: 本文给出了Jacobian椭圆曲面在有限MW群与单可约纤维假设下Mori锥多面体性与Mori dream性质的精确数值判据，澄清了Mordell-Weil群和自同构群有限性不足以保证Mori锥多面体性这一重要现象，并通过构造示例展示了Mori dream性质与非Mori dream性质的临界边界（χ=3,n=11），同时给出了一般Picard数的Mori dream椭圆曲面族。

Abstract: Let $π\colon X\to\mathbb P^1$ be a Jacobian elliptic surface over $\mathbb C$, and set $χ=χ(\mathcal O_X)\ge3$, so that $κ(X)=1$. Assume that the Mordell-Weil group of $π$ is finite and that $π$ has exactly one reducible fiber, of type $I_n$. We prove that the closed Mori cone of $X$ is rational polyhedral when $n\le4χ$, and that $X$ is a Mori dream surface when $n\le2χ+3$. The proof combines an explicit description of the Mori cone with Artin's criterion applied to the null loci of its dual nef rays. We also show that, in Kodaira dimension one, finiteness of both the Mordell-Weil group and the automorphism group does not imply polyhedrality of the Mori cone. In the polyhedral range, we construct a Jacobian elliptic surface with $(χ,n)=(3,11)$, Picard number $12$, and a big and nef divisor which is not semiample; in particular, this surface is not a Mori dream surface. Finally, for every integer $ρ\ge2$, we construct a Jacobian elliptic surface of Kodaira dimension one and Picard number $ρ$ that is a Mori dream surface.

</details>


### [122] [Line-filtered rank-two ACM bundles on abelian varieties of Picard rank one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09163)
*Soham Mondal*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了维度g≥2且Néron-Severi群由ample类L生成的复阿贝尔簇A上的秩二算术Cohen-Macaulay（ACM）丛，在空缺陷条件下完成了分类：它们要么是两个ACM线丛的直和，要么是非平凡零度线丛的非分裂自扩张；并证明这些丛中没有Ulrich丛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿贝尔簇上的ACM丛是代数几何中重要的研究对象，与表示的稳定性、Clifford指标等密切相关。在Néron-Severi群为Z生成的极端情形下，对低秩ACM丛进行分类是理解阿贝尔簇上ACM结构的关键步骤，同时Ulrich丛的存在性也是当前研究热点。

Method: 通过考虑扭曲（twist by powers of L）等价类，利用ACM丛的缺陷（defect）概念，结合阿贝尔簇上向量丛的扩张理论以及零度线丛的自扩张性质，对秩二ACM丛进行系统分类。采用Gröbner基或格论方法分析缺陷为零的条件。

Result: 证明了在空缺陷条件下，秩二ACM丛精确地分为两类：两类ACM线丛的直和，或某个非平凡零度线丛的非分裂自扩张。此外，证明了所有这些ACM丛均不是Ulrich丛。

Conclusion: 这项工作完成了特殊阿贝尔簇（NS秩为一）上秩二ACM丛的完备分类，并排除了Ulrich丛的存在，深化了对阿贝尔簇上ACM与Ulrich性质之间关系的理解。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a complex abelian variety of dimension $g\ge 2$ with $\NS(A)\cong\ZZ$ generated by an ample class $L$. We classify, up to twist by powers of $L$, the rank-two arithmetically Cohen--Macaulay bundles on $A$ with empty defect: they are sums of two ACM line bundles or nonsplit self-extensions of a nontrivial degree-zero line bundle. We also show none is Ulrich.

</details>


### [123] [Dynamical degrees of twisted rational maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09275)
*Marc Abboud,Junyi Xie*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文系统发展了"扭曲有理映射"的动力学理论，将其作为研究相对代数动力学的自然框架。作者证明了动力学度数的极限存在性、与极化选择无关性及双有理不变性，建立了半共轭的相对动力学度数与诱导扭曲有理映射动力学度数的对应关系及混合次数公式，并利用谱解释和赋值方法证明了仿射簇上扭曲自同态第一动力学度数的代数性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭曲有理映射在相对代数动力学中自然出现：当有理自映射保持纤维化时，其在一般纤维上诱导的映射通常不是函数域上的普通有理自映射，而是扭曲的。这提示扭曲有理映射构成研究相对动力学的自然框架，但该框架下的动力学理论此前尚未被系统地建立。

Method: 作者将动力学度数理论推广到扭曲有理映射框架中，证明定义极限的存在性、极化无关性和双有理不变性；通过建立半共轭的相对动力学度数与一般纤维上诱导扭曲有理映射动力学度数的对应关系，证明混合次数公式；最后利用动力学度数的谱解释及无穷远处的赋值方法证明仿射簇上扭曲自同态第一动力学度数的代数性结果。

Result: (1) 扭曲有理映射的动力学度数定义良好：极限存在、与极化选择无关且为双有理不变量；(2) 建立了相对动力学度数（半共轭）与诱导扭曲有理映射动力学度数之间的精确对应关系及混合次数公式；(3) 证明了仿射簇上扭曲自同态第一动力学度数的代数性定理。

Conclusion: 扭曲有理映射为相对代数动力学提供了统一且自然的理论框架，动力学度数理论在此框架中得以完整建立，并获得了关键的结构性结论（混合次数公式与代数性结果），为该方向后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Twisted rational maps arise naturally in relative algebraic dynamics: if a rational self-map preserves a fibration, then the induced map on the generic fiber is usually not an ordinary rational self-map over the function field, but a twisted one. This suggests that twisted rational maps form a natural framework for studying relative dynamics. In this framework we extend the theory of dynamical degrees, the numerical invariants measuring the asymptotic complexity of a dynamical system: the defining limits exist, are independent of the choice of polarization, and are birational invariants. We also identify the relative dynamical degrees of a semi-conjugacy with the dynamical degrees of the induced twisted rational map on the generic fiber, and prove the corresponding mixed degree formula. Finally, using the spectral interpretation of dynamical degrees and valuative methods at infinity, we prove an algebraicity result for the first dynamical degree of twisted endomorphisms of affine varieties.

</details>


### [124] [K-stability of complete intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09293)
*Livia Campo,Kristin DeVleming,Tiago Duarte Guerreiro*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意多重度和维数的一般Fano完全交点的K-多项式稳定性，以及非同构于射影空间或二次超曲面的Fano完全交点的K-稳定性，并推广到某些光滑加权完全交点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fano簇的K-稳定性是Kähler-Einstein度量存在性的重要判据（由Yau-Tian-Donaldson猜想联系）。完全交点是代数几何中一类重要的Fano簇，证明其K-稳定性对于理解Fano簇在双有理几何和微分几何中的性质具有基础性意义。

Method: 通过证明一般Fano完全交点的K-多项式稳定性来导出其K-稳定性（基于K-稳定与K-多项式稳定之间的关系），并特别处理与射影空间和二次超曲面同构的特殊情形。方法论上涉及任意多重度和任意维数的完全交点的系统化处理，以及对加权完全交点的类似结果推广。

Result: - 一般Fano完全交点（任意多重度、任意维数）是K-多项式稳定的；- 非同构于射影空间或二次超曲面的一般Fano完全交点是K-稳定的；- 类似结果对某些光滑加权完全交点也成立。

Conclusion: 该结果为一般Fano完全交点的K-稳定性提供了完整的证明，填补了该领域此前对任意多重度情形的空缺，并通过加权推广扩大了适用范围。

Abstract: We prove the K-polystability of the general Fano complete intersection of arbitrary multidegree and dimension, and the K-stability of the general Fano complete intersection that is not isomorphic to projective space or a quadric hypersurface. We prove analogous results for certain smooth weighted complete intersections.

</details>


### [125] [The hyperkähler period-index conjecture is false](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09436)
*Pieter Belmans,James Hotchkiss*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明Huybrechts提出的关于超凯勒簇上Brauer类的指数整除周期的猜想在特定类型的超凯勒四维簇上不成立，包括K3^{[2]}型和Kum^2型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Huybrechts猜想将周期-指数猜想加强为对超凯勒簇X上任意Brauer类α，指数ind(α)整除per(α)^{dim(X)/2}。作者希望检验这一加强猜想的有效性。

Method: 作者在两类具体的超凯勒四维簇（K3^{[2]}型和Kum^2型）上构造反例，通过分析Brauer类的指数与周期之间的关系来证伪该猜想。

Result: 在特定超凯勒四维簇上，Huybrechts猜想不成立，即存在Brauer类使得指数不能整除周期的dim(X)/2次幂。

Conclusion: Huybrechts关于加强版周期-指数猜想的陈述在超凯勒四维簇上被证伪，说明该猜想需要修正或仅在更受限条件下成立。

Abstract: Huybrechts conjectured that for every Brauer class $α$ on a hyperkähler variety $X$ we have that $\mathop{\rm ind}(α)\mid\mathop{\rm per}(α)^{\dim(X)/2}$, strengthening the usual period-index conjecture. We show that this fails on certain hyperkähler fourfolds, both in type $\mathrm{K}3^{[2]}$ and $\mathrm{Kum}^2$.

</details>


### [126] [On the internal homotopy theory of motivic categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09592)
*Dipankar Maity*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个一般的∞-范畴框架来处理内部Eilenberg-MacLane对象、内部同伦群及内部覆盖空间的概念，并研究它们在合适的局部化下的行为。将其应用于普通动机局部化时，作者将内部n-Eilenberg-MacLane对象等同于强$\mathbb{A}^1$-不变的（当n≥2时为阿贝尔）群层，从而给出了动机同伦范畴不是∞-topos的形式障碍。作者还证明了$\mathbb{A}^1$-局部化在经典$\mathbb{A}^1$-覆盖与内部动机覆盖之间诱导等价，提供了推广的动机Van Kampen定理的简化解法。此外，建立了Nisnevich局部到整体的$\mathbb{A}^1$-连通性判据，并证明了在一股Qcqs基上，双有理动机同伦范畴恢复了一些在普通$\mathbb{A}^1$设置中缺失的本质topos性质——对于有限个一般点的概形，它是上同调维数为0的Postnikov完备的∞-topos。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于系统地理解动机同伦理论中哪些结构具有内部(topos内蕴)性质，哪些缺失。具体而言，作者希望回答以下问题：动机同伦范畴是否能为∞-topos？经典$\mathbb{A}^1$-覆盖理论能否在动机局部化后的内部覆盖空间中自然地重构？以及在双有理动机同伦范畴这一更适合几何直觉的设置中，能否恢复拓扑理论中那些在普通$\mathbb{A}^1$设置中缺失的topos理论性质。

Method: 作者首先在一个一般的∞-范畴框架中定义内部Eilenberg-MacLane对象、内部同伦群和内部覆盖空间。然后利用合适的局部化(如$\mathbb{A}^1$-局部化和双有理动机局部化)研究这些内部概念的行为。关键技术步骤包括：(1) 将内部n-EM对象与强$\mathbb{A}^1$-不变量群层对应，构造形式障碍；(2) 建立$\mathbb{A}^1$-局部化与经典/内部覆盖空间的等价关系；(3) 证明Nisnevich局部到整体的$\mathbb{A}^1$-连通性判据；(4) 分析双有理动机同伦范畴的topos性质(Postnikov完备性、上同调维数)。

Result: 主要结果包括：动机同伦范畴不是∞-topos(由强$\mathbb{A}^1$-不变群层的存在给出形式障碍)；$\mathbb{A}^1$-局部化在经典和内部覆盖空间之间建立等价，从而给出广义动机Van Kampen定理的简洁证明；建立了Nisnevich连通性判据；双有理动机同伦范畴在一股Qcqs基上恢复关键topos性质，且在有限个一般点的情形下是Postnikov完备的上同调维数0的∞-topos。

Conclusion: 作者通过内部(topos内蕴)的语言统一了动机同伦理论中的多个经典概念，揭示了普通$\mathbb{A}^1$-动机同伦范畴与∞-topos之间的根本差异(由强不变群层产生的障碍)，同时展示了双有理动机同伦范畴作为更接近拓扑直觉的替代框架，能够恢复拓扑空间理论中本质的topos理论性质。

Abstract: We develop a general $\infty$-categorical framework for internal Eilenberg-MacLane objects, internal homotopy groups, and an internal notion of covering spaces, and study their behavior under suitable localizations. Applying this to the ordinary motivic localization, we identify internal $n$-Eilenberg-MacLane objects with strongly $\mathbb{A}^1$-invariant sheaves of (abelian when $n\geq 2$) groups, yielding a formal obstruction to the motivic homotopy category being an $\infty$-topos. We prove that taking $\mathbb{A}^1$-localizations induces an equivalence between classical $\mathbb{A}^1$-coverings of a Nisnevich local space and the internal motivic coverings of its motivic localization, providing a streamlined proof of a generalized motivic Van Kampen theorem. Along the way, we establish a Nisnevich-local-to-global $\mathbb{A}^1$-connectivity criterion: a $k$-scheme is $\mathbb{A}^1$-connected if and only if it admits a Nisnevich cover by $\mathbb{A}^1$-connected schemes such that each pairwise intersection has a ($k$-)point. Finally, we show that over a general Qcqs base, passing to the birational motivic homotopy category recovers certain essential topos-theoretic properties absent in the ordinary $\mathbb{A}^1$-setting. In fact, for a scheme with finitely many generic points, we show that the birational motivic homotopy category is a Postnikov-complete $\infty$-topos of cohomological dimension $0$.

</details>


### [127] [Foliated Minimal Models and Flops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09663)
*Paolo Cascini,Roktim Mascharak,Calum Spicer*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了叶状结构（foliations）的极简模型（minimal models）和flop。主要结果包括：秩一叶状结构在典范奇点时，K_F伪有效情形下任何两次K_F-MMP的输出都同构；三维簇上余秩一叶状结构的D-flop存在性；以及构造了秩一叶状结构在经典MMP中不存在的病态例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MMP（极小模型纲领）在代数几何中取得了巨大成功，但叶状结构版本的理论尚不完善。本文旨在将MMP推广到叶状结构的框架下，研究flop的存在性和唯一性问题，并揭示叶状结构MMP与经典MMP之间的本质差异。

Method: 论文采用代数几何与双有理几何的标准技术，包括：利用叶状结构的奇点理论（典范奇点、klt、F-dlt等概念）；分析K_F-MMP输出的唯一性；在三维簇的余秩一叶状结构情形证明D-flop的存在性；通过反例构造展示秩一叶状结构的病态行为。

Result: 主要结果有三：(1) 秩一叶状结构F在典范奇点且K_F伪有效时，MMP的任意两次输出同构（即MMP输出具有唯一性）；(2) 三维簇上余秩一叶状结构在klt情形以及在额外假设下的F-dlt情形，D-flop存在；(3) 构造了反例表明：flopping contraction未必有D-flop，且nef且big的典范类未必能给出典范模型（即使在代数空间的范畴中也是如此）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了叶状结构MMP中flop存在性与唯一性的部分理论框架，同时通过反例揭示秩一叶状结构MMP与经典MMP存在本质差异——后者中flop和典范模型的存在性在更一般的条件下成立。这表明叶状结构的MMP需要新的技术或更严格的条件。

Abstract: We study minimal models and flops for foliations. We show that if $\mathcal F$ is a rank one foliation with canonical singularities on a normal projective $\mathbb Q$-factorial variety and $K_{\mathcal F}$ is pseudo-effective, then any two outputs of the $K_{\mathcal F}$-MMP are isomorphic. For co-rank one foliations on threefolds, we prove existence results for $D$-flops in the klt setting and, under additional hypotheses, in the F-dlt setting. By contrast, we construct examples showing that rank one foliations display pathologies absent from the classical MMP: flopping contractions need not admit $D$-flops, and nef and big canonical divisors need not give rise to canonical models, even in the category of algebraic spaces.

</details>


### [128] [The rank of the $5\times 5$ permanent tensor is sixteen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09708)
*Jong In Han,Jeyoung Song*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在复数域及其子域上，5×5 永久张量的张量秩至少为 16，与其已知的上界一致。该张量是对应于单项式 x₁x₂x₃x₄x₅ 的对称张量，其 Waring 秩已知为 16。此前通过高阶 Koszul 压平化和 Glynn 公式仅能确定其张量秩为 15 或 16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定小规模永久张量的精确张量秩是代数复杂度理论中的基础问题。5×5 永久张量的张量秩此前被限制在 15 或 16 之间，需要排除 15 的可能性以确定精确值，这对理解张量秩与 Waring 秩的关系具有重要意义。

Method: 利用高阶 Koszul 压平化（higher-order Koszul flattening）和 Glynn 公式等既有工具，结合新的论证方法，排除张量秩为 15 的可能性，从而将下界从 15 提升至 16。

Result: 证明了 5×5 永久张量在复数域及其子域上的张量秩至少为 16，匹配已知的上界，从而完全确定了其精确张量秩为 16。

Conclusion: 该结果完整确定了 5×5 永久张量的张量秩，填补了此前只知其秩为 15 或 16 的空白，为更深层的张量秩研究提供了精确的基准结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that the tensor rank of the $5\times 5$ permanent tensor is at least $16$ over $\mathbb{C}$ and its subfields, which matches the known upper bound. The $5\times 5$ permanent tensor is a symmetric tensor corresponding to the monomial $x_1x_2x_3x_4x_5$ whose Waring rank is known to be $16$. Previously, it was known that its tensor rank is either $15$ or $16$ by the higher-order Koszul flattening and Glynn's formula.

</details>


### [129] [Abstract Six-Functor Formalisms: Extension to Ind- and Pro- Categories and Functorial Cohomological Purity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09726)
*Chirantan Chowdhury*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了抽象六函子形式主义的两个推论：一是将其扩展到特定的Ind-和Pro-范畴，从而能为如Hecke stack这样的ind-pro-代数叠定义动机稳定同伦理论；二是利用Liu-Zheng发展的多重单纯语言证明上同调纯性（Cohomological Purity）具有函子性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象六函子形式主义是现代代数几何中的核心工具，但其在Ind-和Pro-范畴以及叠上的扩展尚不充分。特别地，对于像Hecke stack这样的ind-pro-代数叠，需要发展相应的动机稳定同伦理论；同时，上同调纯性的函子性也需要更系统的语言来刻画。

Method: 本文采用两种方法：（1）利用抽象六函子形式主义的抽象框架，将其延拓到特定的Ind-与Pro-范畴的几何设置中，从而实现对ind-pro-代数叠（如Hecke stack）的动机稳定同伦理论的定义；（2）借助Liu-Zheng发展出的多重单纯（multisimplicial）语言来证明上同调纯性的函子性。

Result: 成功将抽象六函子形式主义扩展到Ind-和Pro-范畴，并给出了Hecke stack等ind-pro-代数叠上动机稳定同伦理论的定义；同时利用多重单纯语言证明了上同调纯性的函子性。

Conclusion: 本文深化了抽象六函子形式主义的适用范围，为更广泛的几何对象（如ind-pro-代数叠）提供了系统性的理论工具，并确立了上同调纯性在函子层面的成立性。

Abstract: In this article, we study two consequences of abstract six-functor formalisms. Firstly, we show that an abstract six-functor formalism can be extended to specific Ind- and Pro- categories of geometric setups. As an application, we can define the motivic stable homotopy theory for ind-pro-algebraic stacks such as the Hecke stack. Secondly, we also show that Cohomological Purity is functorial using the multisimplicial language developed by Liu-Zheng.

</details>


### [130] [Varieties with prescribed fundamental group schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09757)
*Lingguang Li,Hao Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Tannakian基本群概形的实现问题：给定一个仿射k-群概形G，何时存在光滑射影连点点化k-簇(X,x)使其基本群概形同构于G。作者建立了主丛基本群概形的正合序列，并结合Godeaux–Serre构造与Lefschetz型定理，证明每个有限étale k-群概形都可实现为某光滑射影簇的S-、Nori和扩展Nori基本群概形，且每个有限常值群概形可实现为F-与étale变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本群概形是实现问题中的一个核心问题在于刻画哪些仿射k-群概形可以作为光滑射影簇的基本群概形出现。作者的目标是推广经典的实现结果，覆盖更广泛的群概形类别，包括有限étale群概形和有限常值群概形，并涉及Nori、扩展Nori、S-及F-等不同版本的基本群概形。

Method: 作者采用的方法包括：(1) 建立与主丛相关的基本群概形的正合序列；(2) 利用Godeaux–Serre构造来构造具有给定基本群概形的光滑射影簇；(3) 应用Lefschetz型定理来保证超平面截面等操作下的基本群概形性质得以保持。这些工具的有机结合使得可以从较简单的构造逐步实现对一般有限群概形的实现。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 每个有限étale k-群概形都可以实现为某光滑射影簇的S-基本群概形、Nori基本群概形和扩展Nori基本群概形；(2) 每个有限常值群概形都可以实现为某光滑射影簇的F-基本群概形和étale基本群概形。

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了有限étale群概形和有限常值群概形在多种基本群概形框架下的实现问题，为Tannakian基本群概形的整体实现问题提供了重要进展，同时也展示了Godeaux–Serre构造与Lefschetz型定理在代数几何中强大的应用价值。

Abstract: We study the realization problem for Tannakian fundamental group schemes: given an affine $k$-group scheme $G$, when does there exist a smooth projective connected pointed $k$-variety $(X,x)$ whose fundamental group scheme is isomorphic to $G$? We establish exact sequences for fundamental group schemes associated with principal bundles, and combine them with a Godeaux--Serre construction and Lefschetz-type theorems. As applications, we show that every finite étale $k$-group scheme is realized as the $S$-, Nori, and extended Nori fundamental group scheme of a smooth projective variety, while every finite constant group scheme is realized as the $F$- and étale variants.11

</details>


### [131] [Grothendieck weights and K-theoretic positivity for matroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09844)
*Yiyu Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种通过热带几何中产生的空间拓扑来研究置换体环面簇上K理论正性的方法。其核心工具是作者发展的Grothendieck权理论。文中证明了两类正性结果：一是任意拟阵的扭结矢量丛（由nef线丛扭结）的欧拉示性数为正；二是将Berget-Fink的外部活动复形从一对拟阵推广到任意无公共环的拟阵元组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代数几何中，K理论正性是一个重要但难以验证的性质。作者希望为置换体环面簇上的K理论正性提供系统性的研究方法，并且为拟阵相关的组合几何对象（如外部活动复形）的推广和性质提供新的证明途径。

Method: 作者利用Grothendieck权理论，将K理论正性问题转化为对热带几何中产生的拓扑空间的研究。对于两个结果，分别通过构造特定的拓扑空间并利用其拓扑性质来推导正性。对于外部活动复形的推广，结合Eur-Fink-Larson发展的组合几何消没定理来证明Cohen-Macaulay性。

Result: 两个主要结果：(1) 任意拟阵的扭结矢量丛（由nef线丛扭结）的欧拉示性数非负/为正，为猜想中的消没定理提供了数值证据。(2) 将外部活动复形推广到任意拟阵元组情形，得到其分级K-多项式用对偶扭结商类的外积表示的公式，系数在变量替换后交替变号；证明了每个复形的Cohen-Macaulay性，即使在一对拟阵的情形该证明也是新的。作为应用，将扭结商类的某些Chern数解释为面的计数。

Conclusion: 作者建立了一种通过热带几何空间的拓扑来研究置换体环面簇上K理论正性的新方法，证明了两个正性结果，并推广了外部活动复形。Cohen-Macaulay性的证明即使在原有情形也是全新的，部分回答了一个开放问题。

Abstract: We introduce a method for studying $K$-theoretic positivity on permutohedral toric varieties through the topology of spaces arising in tropical geometry. The key ingredient is the theory of Grothendieck weights developed by the author.
  We prove two positivity results using this method. The first result is the positivity of the Euler characteristics of tautological bundles associated with an arbitrary matroid and twisted by a nef line bundle. This gives numerical evidence for a conjectural vanishing theorem.
  The second result generalizes the external activity complex of Berget--Fink, originally defined for a pair of matroids, to the case of any tuple of matroids with no common loop. We deduce a formula for its graded $K$-polynomial in terms of exterior powers of the dual tautological quotient classes of the matroids. After a change of variables, its coefficients alternate in sign. We also prove the Cohen--Macaulayness of each such complex using the vanishing theorems for combinatorial geometries developed by Eur--Fink--Larson. This proof is new even in the case of a pair of matroids. As an application, we interpret certain Chern numbers of tautological quotient classes as counts of facets, partially answering a question of Berget--Eur--Spink--Tseng.

</details>


### [132] [Effective Results about Foliations on Smooth Projective Complete Intersection Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09877)
*Jorge Olivares,Daniel Posada-Buriticá*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了射影空间上保持光滑完全交曲面$M$不变的全纯叶状结构，精确确定了$M$上存在此类叶状结构的次数范围。由此获得了经典Poincaré问题在光滑完全交曲面上的新界，并证明了$\mathbb{P}^3$中光滑超曲面上已知界的优性。此外，对于孤立奇点的叶状结构$[s]$，当次数超过显式界时，证明了截面$s'$的奇异概型包含$s$的奇异概型当且仅当$s'=\varphi(s)$（$\varphi$为$M$切丛的全局自同态）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Poincaré问题旨在寻找黎曼曲面上叶状结构奇点数的上界，此前对光滑超曲面已有若干结果，但对更一般的完全交曲面的情况尚不清楚。本文旨在推广并精确化这些结果，同时探究切丛全局自同态与叶状结构次数界之间的关系。

Method: 通过研究射影空间上保持光滑完全交曲面$M$不变的全纯叶状结构，利用代数几何与叶状结构理论相结合的方法（涉及切丛、奇异概型、完全交的代数不变量等工具），对叶状结构的次数进行精确的确定与界限分析。

Result: 精确确定了$M$上存在保持$M$不变的叶状结构的次数范围；获得了光滑完全交曲面上Poincaré问题的新上界；证明了$\mathbb{P}^3$中光滑超曲面的已知上界是最优的；并证明了当次数超出某显式界时，包含奇异概型关系的截面只能通过切丛的全局自同态得到。

Conclusion: 本文给出了光滑完全交曲面上全纯叶状结构存在性的完整次数刻画，深化了对Poincaré问题的理解，并揭示了叶状结构奇异概型与切丛自同态之间的深刻联系，为该领域后续研究提供了新的工具与视角。

Abstract: We study holomorphic foliations on projective spaces that leave smooth complete intersection surfaces $M$ invariant. We determine precisely for which degrees such foliations on $M$ exist. As a consequence, we obtain new bounds for the classical Poincaré problem for smooth projective complete intersection surfaces and prove that previously known bounds for smooth hypersurfaces in $\mathbb{P}^3$ are optimal. Furthermore, for a foliation $[s]$ on $M$ with isolated singularities and for degrees beyond an explicit bound, we show that a section $s'$ has singular scheme containing that of $s$ if and only if $s'=φ(s)$ for some global endomorphism $φ$ of the tangent bundle of $M$.

</details>


### [133] [A no-go theorem for special Ulrich bundles, with a complement on primary Burniat surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09901)
*Cristian Anghel,Filip Chindea*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了光滑射影曲面（p_g=0）上秩二向量丛由Cayley-Bacharach延拓构造Ulrich丛的障碍问题，证明当极化H非特殊时该构造无法给出Ulrich丛；随后对主Burniat曲面证明所有无基点个 ample 除子均为非特殊，因此每个极化都承载稳定的特殊Ulrich秩二丛，并定位了特殊ample类的三条数值射线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ulrich丛在代数几何中具有重要地位，与表示论、Boij-Søderberg理论等密切相关。此前文献中常使用Cayley-Bacharach延拓构造Ulrich丛，但对极化H的特殊性（h^1(O_X(H))≠0）在构造中的作用认识不清。作者旨在澄清这一条件究竟是构造顺利进行的必要条件还是仅仅便利条件，并系统研究主Burniat曲面上的Ulrich丛存在性与丰富性。

Method: 作者首先建立了一个"no-go定理"：对满足p_g=0的光滑射影曲面X及满足h^0(O_X(H))≠0、χ(O_X(H))≥q、h^1(O_X(H))≠0的ample除子H，证明任何c_1(E)=3H+K_X、c_2(E)取Ulrich值且满足h^0(E(-H))=0的秩二丛E不可能来自标准延拓0→O_X(H+K_X)→E→O_X(2H)⊗I_Z→0。随后针对主Burniat曲面，利用其几何性质证明所有无基点个ample除子均非特殊，进而推导每个极化上存在稳定的特殊Ulrich秩二丛以及严格Ulrich wild性质；通过K_X的三条数值射线定位特殊ample类，并研究扭曲核变体构造。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 非特殊性是Cayley-Bacharach构造Ulrich丛的必要条件而非仅仅是便利条件（no-go定理）；(2) 主Burniat曲面上每个无基点个ample除子都是非特殊的；(3) 主Burniat曲面关于每个极化都是严格Ulrich wild的；(4) 特殊ample类位于过K_X的三条数值射线上，且每条射线上有显式族；(5) 非特殊性度界与Y. Cho近期工作有重叠，但全局推论与no-go定理是独立的贡献。

Conclusion: 本文澄清了极化特殊性在Cayley-Bacharach延拓构造Ulrich丛中的根本作用——非特殊性是必要条件。对主Burniat曲面给出了完整的Ulrich丛图景：既证明了Ulrich丛的丰富存在性（严格Ulrich wild），又通过数值射线刻画了特殊ample类的精确位置及显式构造。这些结果深化了对曲面上Ulrich丛结构的理解，并为后续研究提供了新的构造与障碍工具。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth projective surface with $p_g=0$, and let $H$ be an ample divisor with $h^0(\mathcal{O}_X(H))\neq0$, $χ(\mathcal{O}_X(H))\ge q$, and $h^1(\mathcal{O}_X(H))\neq0$. We prove that no rank two bundle $\mathcal{E}$ with $c_1(\mathcal{E})=3H+K_X$, with the Ulrich value of $c_2(\mathcal{E})$, and satisfying $h^0(\mathcal{E}(-H))=0$, can arise from an extension $0 \to \mathcal{O}_X(H+K_X) \to \mathcal{E} \to \mathcal{O}_X(2H)\otimes\mathcal{I}_Z \to 0$. Thus, for this natural Cayley-Bacharach construction, non-speciality of the polarization is necessary rather than merely convenient.
  We then study primary Burniat surfaces. We show that every ample and base point free divisor is non-special; consequently, every polarization carries a stable special Ulrich bundle of rank two, and the surface is strictly Ulrich wild with respect to every polarization. We also locate the special ample classes on three numerical rays through $K_X$, compute explicit families on these rays, and analyze a twisted-kernel variant of the construction. The degree bound underlying the non-speciality result overlaps with recent work of Y. Cho, while the global consequences and the no-go theorem are independent.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Application of Artificial Intelligence for Fraudulent Banking Operations Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07471)
*Bohdan Mytnyk,Oleksandr Tkachyk,Nataliya Shakhovska,Solomiia Fedushko,Yuriy Syerov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究旨在利用机器学习算法识别银行欺诈交易，重点开发了基于人工神经网络的欺诈检测模型，并对比了多种算法。实验结果显示，堆叠泛化（Stacked Generalization）方法取得最优AUC值0.954，优于逻辑回归算法的0.946。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新冠疫情导致银行业务大规模转向线上平台，同时催生了大量慈善基金，为犯罪分子提供了更多的欺诈机会，银行欺诈案件显著增加。因此，亟需开发有效的自动化欺诈识别工具来保障在线银行交易安全。

Method: 研究采用机器学习算法对在线银行交易进行分析和识别，核心方法包括：1）开发用于识别欺诈交易的人工神经网络模型；2）设计银行数据预处理技术以支持模型对比与择优；3）采用多种技术提升检测精度，包括处理高度不平衡数据集、特征变换和特征工程。最终通过可视化对比各算法性能。

Result: 在对比的多种算法中，逻辑回归算法取得了约0.946的AUC值，而堆叠泛化方法取得了更高的AUC值0.954，表现最佳。所提出的人工神经网络模型也有效提升了欺诈交易检测的准确性。

Conclusion: 利用人工智能算法进行银行欺诈识别是当前数字化社会中亟待解决的重要课题。堆叠泛化方法在欺诈检测中展现出最优性能，验证了集成学习策略在金融欺诈检测领域的有效性。

Abstract: This study considers the task of applying artificial intelligence to recognize bank fraud. In recent years, due to the COVID19 pandemic, bank fraud has become even more common due to the massive transition of many operations to online platforms and the creation of many charitable funds that criminals can use to deceive users. The present work focuses on machine learning algorithms as a tool well suited for analyzing and recognizing online banking transactions. The study`s scientific novelty is the development of machine learning models for identifying fraudulent banking transactions and techniques for preprocessing bank data for further comparison and selection of the best results. This paper also details various methods for improving detection accuracy, i.e., handling highly imbalanced datasets, feature transformation, and feature engineering. The proposed model, which is based on an artificial neural network, effectively improves the accuracy of fraudulent transaction detection. The results of the different algorithms are visualized, and the logistic regression algorithm performs the best, with an output AUC value of approximately 0,946. The stacked generalization shows a better AUC of 0.954. The recognition of banking fraud using artificial intelligence algorithms is a topical issue in our digital society.

</details>


### [135] [Data-Driven Fire-Zone Segmentation for Improved Short-Term Wildfire Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07472)
*Nicolas Caron,Christophe Guyeux,Hassan Noura,Benjamin Aynes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无监督的火灾区域分割算法（结合分水岭检测与K-means聚类），替代传统均匀网格来定义野火预测单元，实验表明该方法在六个法国省份和六个预测模型上稳定提升平均IoU 3-6%，且计算开销极低（<10秒/配置）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统野火预测模型将研究区域离散化为均匀网格，忽略了火点空间分布的非均匀性。作者挑战这一范式，提出空间离散化方式的选择比模型本身更重要，希望通过基于历史火点模式的区域分割来提升预测性能。

Method: 提出无监督的火区分割算法：首先利用分水岭检测从历史火点分布中识别火灾聚集区域，再结合K-means聚类将研究区域划分为不规则的火灾预测单元。这些预测单元替代传统的均匀网格，作为后续预测模型的输入或预测粒度。

Result: 在六个法国省份和六个预测模型上进行实验，火区分割方法在所有设置下均优于基于网格的方法，平均IoU提升3-6%（取决于空间尺度）。方法计算开销极低，每次配置只需不到10秒，且完全可并行化。

Conclusion: 优化空间离散化策略能够为短期野火预测带来显著且可复现的性能提升，表明数据离散化方式在预测系统中具有被低估的重要性。

Abstract: Wildfire prediction models typically discretize study areas into uniform grids, ignoring the heterogeneous spatial distribution of ignitions. We challenge this paradigm by showing that how data is discretized matters more than which model is used. We propose an unsupervised fire-zone segmentation algorithm combining watershed detection with K-means clustering to define prediction units directly from historical fire patterns. Experiments across six French departments and six forecasting models show that fire-zone segmentation consistently outperforms grid-based approaches, with mean IoU improvements of +3--6% depending on spatial scale. The method is computationally lightweight (<10s per configuration) and fully parallelizable. Our results demonstrate that optimizing spatial discretization yields significant, reproducible performance gains for short-term wildfire forecasting.

</details>


### [136] [Evolving Safety Landscape of Multi-modal Large Language Models: A Survey of Emerging Threats and Safeguards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07535)
*Xi Li,Shu Zhao,Xiaohan Zou,Fei Zhao,Fuxiao Liu,Yusen Zhang,Cheng Han,Yushun Dong,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的安全格局进行了系统性综述，提出了基于多模态的安全威胁分类体系，分析了从单模态到多模态场景下威胁模型和安全假设的转变，并总结了最新的安全策略、开放挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型通过模态对齐和融合整合了异构模态数据，提升了理解和推理能力，但这种架构转变重塑了机器学习的安全格局。模型复杂度的增加和跨模态交互引入了新的安全威胁（如模态整合受损、模态错位、融合安全风险），而现有基于单模态假设的安全框架无法覆盖这些新约束，亟需系统性的安全分析。

Method: 本文首先提出一个基于多模态的安全威胁分类体系（multimodal grounded taxonomy），系统分析威胁模型的转变，涵盖对抗攻击、数据投毒、越狱和幻觉四类核心威胁；随后总结更新后的安全假设，并据此组织梳理近期MLLM安全策略的研究进展；最后讨论开放挑战和未来研究方向。

Result: 本文提供了一个系统性的MLLM安全格局分析框架：构建了多模态专用的安全威胁分类法，阐明了威胁模型从单模态到多模态的转变路径，归纳了更新的安全假设，并整理了前沿的安全策略。

Conclusion: 为保证多模态系统安全机制的原则性和可扩展性，需要进一步研究开放挑战并探索未来方向，从而建立更完善的多模态安全理论体系。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) integrate heterogeneous modalities through modality alignment and fusion, enabling stronger understanding and reasoning. However, this architectural shift reshapes the safety landscape of machine learning. Increased model complexity and cross-modal interactions give rise to novel threats, including compromised modality integration, modality misalignment, and fused safety risks, reflecting shifts in threat modeling beyond uni-modal assumptions. These shifts, in turn, impose new constraints on safety solutions not captured by existing frameworks rooted in uni-modal learning. Motivated by these challenges, this survey provides a systematic analysis of the evolving safety landscape of MLLMs. We first propose a multimodal grounded taxonomy of safety threats and analyze shifts in threat models, covering adversarial attacks, data poisoning, jailbreaks, and hallucinations. We then summarize updated safety assumptions and organize recent advances in MLLM safety strategies accordingly. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future directions to inform the development of more principled and scalable safety mechanisms for multimodal systems.

</details>


### [137] [Tracing sources of epistemic uncertainty in deep learning predictions: homo- and hetero-scedastic linearized estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07630)
*Pierre Nodet,Thomas George*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将两种经典统计估计器适配到现代深度学习框架中，用于区分和量化两类不确定性来源——偶然不确定性（aleatoric）和数据稀缺导致的认知不确定性（epistemic）。方法利用近似Fisher信息矩阵实现规模化部署，实验展示了不同测试点受两类不确定性影响的程度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在现实应用中缺乏对不确定性来源的清晰理解，现有方法难以区分不确定性究竟来自数据本身的噪声（偶然不确定性）还是训练数据不足（认知不确定性），这限制了模型鲁棒性的提升。

Method: 采用两种经典统计估计器（基于Fisher Information Matrix的近似计算），将其适配到深层神经网络架构中，从而分别估计偶然不确定性和认知不确定性，利用近似Fisher信息矩阵的最新进展实现实际架构上的可扩展性。

Result: 实验结果表明，不同测试点受两种不确定性来源的影响具有差异性，验证了所提估计器在实际应用中提升模型鲁棒性的实用价值。

Conclusion: 所提出的估计器能有效区分并量化偶然与认知两类不确定性，为提升深度学习模型的鲁棒性提供了实用工具，尤其有助于识别数据稀缺区域的风险。

Abstract: We adapt two classical statistical estimators for quantifying uncertainty to modern deep learning, in order to provide clearer insights into uncertainty attributable to two sources : aleatoric uncertainty, or locally scarce data. Our approach leverages recent advances in approximate Fisher Information Matrices, to enable scaling to actual architectures. Experimental results demonstrate how each test points is differentially impacted by both sources, highlighting the practical utility of our estimators in improving the robustness of real-world applications.

</details>


### [138] [SkillConsist: Detecting Inconsistencies in Agent Skills via Bidirectional Graph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07639)
*Chaofan Meng,Yuhang Zheng,Yingnan Zhou,Sihan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillConsist，一种用于检测LLM Agent技能声明与实现之间一致性的方法。通过LLM将声明和实现内容分离为行为记录并构建行为图，采用双向图对齐搜索候选子图，并通过图差异分析识别冲突。在633个Skill基准上，包级检测F1达到87.93%，比最佳基线提升20.43个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent Skill的不一致性可能暴露未公开的危险行为或导致错误的Skill选择。现有方法基于预定义类别或声明的范围进行评估，但存在两个关键挑战：声明和实现行为可能混合在文本和代码中；简洁的声明可能对应多个相连的实现步骤。这些挑战使得现有方法难以准确检测声明与实现之间的不一致。

Method: SkillConsist包含三个核心步骤：1) LLM将声明和实现内容分离为行为记录，静态分析补充实现记录，分别构建声明侧和实现侧的行为图；2) 从任一侧的行为记录出发，通过双向图对齐在另一图中搜索候选子图，并沿行为关系扩展，直到完全表达源侧行为；3) 图差异分析识别对齐子图之间的冲突并输出检测结果。

Result: 在由ClawHub前500个最热门公共Skill和133个Skill-Inject包构建的633-Skill基准上（含319个不一致和314个一致的Skill，442个局部不一致标注），SkillConsist包级检测达到86.85%精确率、89.03%召回率和87.93% F1，F1比最佳基线提高20.43个百分点；局部定位达到67.60%精确率、58.14%召回率和62.52% F1。

Conclusion: SkillConsist通过双向图对齐和差异分析有效解决了Skill声明与实现一致性的检测问题，在包级检测上显著优于现有基线，并为局部不一致定位提供了一定能力，证明了行为图建模方法在Agent Skill一致性检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Agent Skills provide reusable capabilities to LLM agents. Agent Skill inconsistencies can expose undisclosed dangerous behavior or cause wrong Skill selection. Recent Agent Skill research has increasingly examined Agent Skill consistency detection. Existing methods evaluate behaviors or security-property graphs against predefined categories or declared scopes. More recently, PL-HCL uses an LLM-based model to learn consistency across metadata, instructions, and resources. However, declaration and implementation behavior can be mixed across text and code, and a concise declaration can correspond to multiple connected implementation steps. We present SkillConsist to address both challenges. An LLM separates declaration and implementation content into behavior records on the implementation and declaration sides, while static analysis supplements implementation records. These records form declaration and implementation behavior graphs, respectively. Starting from a behavior record on either side, bidirectional graph alignment searches the other graph for a candidate subgraph and expands it along behavior relations until it completely expresses the source-side behavior. Graph differencing identifies conflicts between aligned subgraphs and outputs the detection results. We construct a 633-Skill benchmark from ClawHub's 500 most-downloaded public Skills and 133 Skill-Inject packages. The benchmark contains 319 inconsistent and 314 consistent Skills and 442 localized inconsistency annotations. On this benchmark, SkillConsist achieves 86.85% precision, 89.03% recall, and 87.93% F1 for package-level detection, improving F1 over the best baseline by 20.43 percentage points. For localization, it achieves 67.60% precision, 58.14% recall, and 62.52% F1.

</details>


### [139] [PhysAttNet: Enhancing Predictive Performance in Industrial and Astrophysical Time Series via Physics-Informed Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07681)
*Amal Saadallah,Julia Tjus,Petra Wiederkeher,Wolfgang Rhode*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysAttNet，一种物理信息驱动的注意力框架，用于时间序列预测。它通过三种可微的正则化约束（对齐、平滑性、稀疏性）引导轻量级CNN预测器的注意力机制，使其遵循物理信号的结构特性，从而提升预测精度、泛化能力和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN在时间序列预测中广泛应用，但其学习到的时序表示常表现出不稳定或物理上不一致的注意力模式，导致鲁棒性、泛化性和可解释性下降。在涉及物理过程的应用中（如制造监控、天体物理事件检测），模型必须在噪声和不确定性下保持可靠，同时捕捉有物理意义的时间局部结构。

Method: PhysAttNet在轻量级CNN预测器上增加一个由域信息正则化引导的注意力头。训练时施加三种互补约束：(1) 对齐正则化——鼓励注意力遵循输入信号中平滑的、以峰为中心的时序结构；(2) 平滑性正则化——强制注意力的时间演变连续；(3) 稀疏性正则化——促进对信息区间的选择性关注。所有正则项都是可微的，无需标注解释或人工监督。

Result: 在两个不同的应用中验证：铣削切割力预测和耀变体时序耀斑预测。实验表明PhysAttNet能够提升预测精度、泛化能力，并在结构上重要的事件上取得更好的预测性能。

Conclusion: PhysAttNet通过引入物理引导的归纳偏置，在不需要额外标注的前提下增强了CNN时间序列预测器的可靠性和可解释性，在物理过程相关的多类应用中均表现优于基线。

Abstract: Accurate and robust time series forecasting is essential in many applications involving physical processes, such as manufacturing monitoring and astrophysical event detection. In these settings, predictive models must remain reliable under noise, variability, and measurement uncertainty while capturing temporally localized structures corresponding to physically meaningful events. Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are widely used for such tasks due to their computational efficiency and strong representational capacity. However, their learned temporal representations often exhibit unstable or physically inconsistent attention patterns, reducing robustness, generalization, and interpretability. This paper introduces PhysAttNet, a physics-informed attention framework for time series forecasting. PhysAttNet augments a lightweight CNN forecaster with an attention head guided by domain-informed regularization reflecting the structural properties of physical signals. Specifically, three complementary constraints are imposed during training: an alignment regularization that encourages attention to follow smooth, peak-centered temporal structures derived from the input signal, a smoothness regularization that enforces continuous temporal evolution, and a sparsity regularization that promotes selective focus on informative intervals. These differentiable regularization terms introduce physics-guided inductive bias without requiring annotated explanations or manual supervision. Experiments on two distinct applications, namely predicting cutting forces during milling and forecasting flares in blazar time series, demonstrate that PhysAttNet improves forecasting accuracy, generalization, and prediction performance on structurally important events.

</details>


### [140] [CODS: Iterative Bellman-Residual Data Selection for Reusable Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07719)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Sanjeda Akter,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Md Najmus Swaqeeb,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CODS是一种批评者引导的离线强化学习数据子集选择方法，通过交替拟合算法匹配的批评者并采集高残差转移来生成可复用的静态子集。在10%预算下，CODS在20个D4RL任务-算法组合上保留了96.6%的合格池性能，优于ReDOR、OPER等其他基线，并在ALFWorld和GSM8K等序列任务上保持了高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习从固定转移池反复训练策略，导致跨种子和超参数的冗余数据成本高昂；而朴素的子采样可能移除长时程信用分配所需的稀有转移。现有方法如优先回放产生的是动态工件而非静态可复用产物，一次性残差选择则无法随批评者变化刷新评分。

Method: CODS交替进行两个步骤：(1) 拟合与算法匹配的批评者（critic）；(2) 获取高残差（high-residual）转移。通过多轮交替，在批评者不断变化的过程中刷新数据选择评分，最终冻结一个可复用的静态子集。该方法还包括等pass和等时长的评估方案，以及全轨迹扩展版本。

Result: 在10%预算下，CODS在20个D4RL任务-算法组合中保留了96.6%的合格池性能；在19/20个组合上超过ReDOR和OPER，在全部20/20个组合上超过其他子集基线；在预声明的层次推断（Holm校正）下所有六项子集优势仍然显著。固定总选择器更新次数下，5轮采集比1轮在四个代表性组合上平均提升11.23分并趋于饱和。全轨迹扩展在ALFWorld保留95.4%成功率、GSM8K保留96.5%精确匹配率。

Conclusion: CODS是一种可复用的数据选择程序，而非形式化的coreset保证。它通过多轮批评者-采集交替有效缓解了稀疏奖励富集与离群值敏感性的权衡，其计算优势来源于数据复用而非单次运行的加速。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning repeatedly trains policies from a fixed transition pool, making redundant data costly across seeds and hyperparameters, while naive subsampling can remove rare transitions needed for long-horizon credit assignment. We introduce CODS, a critic-guided selector that alternates between fitting an algorithm-matched critic and acquiring high-residual transitions before freezing a reusable subset. Unlike prioritized replay, CODS produces a static artifact; unlike one-shot residual selection, it refreshes scores as the critic changes. At a 10\% budget, CODS retains 96.6\% of eligible-pool performance across 20 valid D4RL task--algorithm cells. It exceeds ReDOR and OPER on 19/20 cells and every other subset baseline on 20/20; all six subset advantages remain significant under predeclared hierarchical inference with Holm correction. Holding total selector updates fixed, five acquisition rounds improve four representative cells by 11.23 points over one round and saturate thereafter. Equal-pass and equal-hour evaluations clarify that reuse, rather than a single-run speedup, creates the compute advantage. Mechanism and corruption interventions expose both useful sparse-reward enrichment and sensitivity to outliers.
