Finally, a whole-trace extension retains 95.4\% of pooled ALFWorld success and 96.5\% of pooled GSM8K exact match. CODS is therefore a reusable selection procedure, not a formal coreset guarantee.

</details>


### [141] [Neural Operators for Immersed-Boundary Soft Swimmers Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07722)
*Mohammad Sadegh Eshaghi,Yizheng Wang,Navid Valizadeh,Xiaoying Zhuang,Timon Rabczuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于神经算子代理模型的方法，用于预测平面和三维鳗鱼游泳者周围的流体动力学场，替代高保真浸没边界仿真，在保持全场精度（平面3.51%，三维速度3.44%/涡量5.58%/压力19.2%）的同时大幅降低计算成本，实现随时间演化的水动力场快速预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真浸没边界仿真能够精确解析变形游泳者与其周围流场的耦合运动，但计算成本过高，难以支撑工程设计中反复评估、参数研究和控制等需大量迭代的场景。因此需要开发高效的代理模型来替代昂贵的仿真。

Method: 设计神经算子代理模型，在自适应流固耦合仿真输出的规则网格场上训练，以游泳者几何和雷诺数为条件。平面模型联合预测两个速度分量、标量涡量和压力；体积公式采用三个目标特定模型（速度、涡量、压力）共享多通道输入，实现三维水动力场的时空预测。

Result: 在五个保留的高雷诺数轨迹上，平面模型全场全局相对L2误差为3.51%；体积模型在五个保留范围内轨迹上，速度、涡量和压力的全场全局相对L2误差分别为3.44%、5.58%和19.2%。

Conclusion: 结果表明场解析神经代理模型用于移动边界游泳者流场预测是可行的，但压力预测精度较低（19.2%），物理一致性也需改进，这两方面是未来发展的优先方向。

Abstract: High-fidelity immersed-boundary simulation resolves the coupled motion of a deforming swimmer and its surrounding flow, but the resulting cost limits repeated evaluations for engineering design, parameter studies, and control. We develop neural-operator surrogates for temporal prediction of the hydrodynamic fields generated by planar and volumetric eel swimmers. The surrogates are trained on regular-grid fields exported from adaptive fluid--structure simulations and are conditioned on swimmer geometry and Reynolds number. The planar model jointly predicts two velocity components, scalar vorticity, and pressure. On five held-out high-Reynolds-number trajectories, its full-domain global relative L^2 error is 3.51 %. The volumetric formulation uses three target-specific models with a common multichannel input: one model predicts three-dimensional velocity, one predicts vorticity, and one predicts pressure. Their full-domain global relative L^2 errors on five held-out within-range trajectories are 3.44 %, 5.58 %, and 19.2 %. Together, the results demonstrate the feasibility of field-resolved neural surrogates for moving-boundary swimmer flows while identifying pressure accuracy and physical consistency as priorities for further development.

</details>


### [142] [Finite Constant Frontiers and Auditable Regret Certificates for Average-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07725)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Md Najmus Swaqeeb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对平均奖励强化学习遗憾的数值常数问题，引入常数感知比较协议，构造了通信MDP的显式有限下界证书（基于二元树状两状态块），并提出通用封闭包络将下界系数从0.015提升至0.0200~0.0291（提升94%），极限系数为(1/32)√((A-3)/A)。对上界给出可审计的合成规则但未声明具体系数。同时形式化了期望转换与常数可比性，并通过受控诊断验证了直径依赖、奖赏-宽度交互、跨度误设等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均奖励强化学习遗憾的渐进上界已在对数因子意义上已知，但已发表保证的数值常数因概率模式、结构参数、对数归一化、先验信息和规划假设不同而难以直接比较。需要一个统一的常数感知比较协议以及显式的有限时间下界证书来客观评估算法优劣。

Method: 构建一个由二元树状两状态块组成的有限MDP下界构造，利用精确轨迹级Bernoulli KL散度证明，显式追踪行动预算、直径、占用率、导航成本和终端偏差。推导通用封闭形式包络以改进现有下界常数，并提出跨度约束乐观学习器的可审计合成规则用于上界。形式化有效期望转换和常数可比性条件。

Result: 下界常数从已发表的0.015提升至中等条件下0.0200，在更强的行动数、直径和视界条件下可达0.0291，提升94%。极限系数为(1/32)√((A-3)/A)。上界部分未声明具体系数，但给出了可审计的合成框架。受控诊断验证了直径依赖、奖赏-宽度交互、跨度误设等效应。

Conclusion: 本文建立了平均奖励RL遗憾常数比较的统一框架，显著改进了现有下界常数，并为未来上界常数的确定提供了可审计的指导方针。该工作使得不同算法的遗憾保证之间可以进行公平、定量的比较。

Abstract: Average-reward reinforcement-learning regret is known up to logarithmic factors, but the numerical content of published guarantees is difficult to compare because probability mode, structural parameter, logarithmic normalization, prior information, and planning assumptions differ. We introduce a constant-aware comparison protocol and derive an explicit finite lower certificate for communicating MDPs. The construction is a binary tree of two-state blocks; its proof uses exact trajectory-level Bernoulli KL divergence and keeps action budget, diameter, occupancy, navigation cost, and terminal bias explicit. A common closed-form envelope improves the published coefficient $0.015$ across a finite frontier: $0.0200$ in a moderate regime and up to $0.0291$ under stronger action, diameter, and horizon conditions, a $94\%$ increase. The limiting coefficient is $\frac1{32}\sqrt{(A-3)/A}$. For upper bounds, we give an auditable composition rule for a span-constrained optimistic learner, but do not claim a coefficient while adaptive directional-variance and planning certificates remain open. We also formalize valid expectation conversion and constant comparability. Controlled diagnostics test diameter dependence, bonus-by-width interactions, span misspecification, and the finite lower certificate on its exact family.

</details>


### [143] [LUCID: Latent-Skill Unified Control via Imagined Dynamics for Long-Horizon Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07746)
*Cheng Guo,Mingzhe Ni,Angelo Cangelosi,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LUCID是一个分层模型强化学习框架，通过预训练可复用的低级技能策略和冻结的宏动态世界模型，在想象回滚中优化高级策略，从而解决长时程人形机器人移动操作任务中的组合性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人长时程移动操作方法依赖脚本化规划器、有限状态机或任务特定的无模型策略来协调预训练技能，难以处理复杂任务序列的组合。需要一个能够自主规划并重用技能的通用框架。

Method: LUCID采用分层模型增强学习：首先通过对抗模仿学习训练结构化潜变量条件的低级策略；然后冻结低级策略，联合学习高级策略和宏动态世界模型；世界模型预测潜决策引起的时域扩展状态转移，使高级策略能通过想象回滚进行优化。

Result: 在多个模拟多目标重排场景中，LUCID相比先前基线方法提升了完整任务成功率和部分完成率，验证了其在复杂序列移动操作任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: LUCID通过分层模型强化学习有效结合了可重用的低级技能与基于世界模型的高层规划，为人形机器人长时程移动操作任务提供了更灵活、更可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation requires composing versatile whole-body skills and reliable high-level decision making. Existing methods often coordinate pretrained skills with scripted planners, finite-state machines or task-specific model-free policies, restricting their ability to handle complex task sequences. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{LUCID}, a hierarchical model-based reinforcement learning framework that plans over reusable skills through imagined rollouts of a learned dynamics model. LUCID first trains a structured latent-conditioned low-level policy via adversarial imitation and then freezes it while jointly learning a high-level policy and macro-dynamics world model. The world model predicts the temporally extended state transitions induced by latent decisions, enabling high-level policy optimization through imagined rollouts. We evaluate our framework across various simulated multi-object rearrangement scenarios. Experimental results show that LUCID improves the full-task success and partial-completion rates compared to prior baseline methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in complex sequential loco-manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [144] [From Benchmark Performance to Tool Deployment: Human-in-the-Loop Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07770)
*Mike Szklarzewski,CJ George,Gavin Smithson,Christopher Stokes,Dakota Fulp,William M. Jones,Benjamin Wynn,Alexander Ur,Agit Yesiloz,Clint Kallenbach,Mark Swartz,Nathan DeBardeleben,Sharmistha Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了19种无监督异常检测模型在BowTie制造数据集上的表现，发现与MVTec AD等基准不同，模型性能不稳定、对预处理敏感且跨条件不一致。基于此发现，作者开发并部署了一个集成人工参与（human-in-the-loop）的制造零件检测框架，包含图像标注、AI辅助缺陷检测和验证引擎，以替代传统人工目检流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化异常检测方法在学术基准上表现优异，但实际工业场景下的行为尚不明确。作者希望通过在具有挑战性的制造数据集上进行系统性评估，揭示基准性能与部署现实之间的差距，并提供一个实用的解决方案来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 首先在BowTie数据集（含反射表面、细微缺陷和轮廓变化）上评估19种无监督异常检测模型，并与MVTec AD基准结果对比。然后设计并部署一个统一的人工参与框架，结合图像标注、AI辅助缺陷检测和集成验证引擎，支持热力图引导缺陷审查、SAM优化候选区域供检查员接受/拒绝/调整边界、存在标注时的掩码评估，以及用于检查员一致性和入职的审查历史记录。

Result: 在BowTie上模型性能不稳定，高度依赖预处理，跨条件表现不一致，没有单一方法普遍稳健；共识审计表明名义数据质量影响部署。所提出的框架已成功初步部署，替代了原有手工目检与文档流程。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了基准性能与实际部署之间的显著差距，并提供了一套实用的、以人工参与为核心的制造零件检测框架，兼顾AI辅助效率与人工验证可靠性，为该领域从学术研究向工业落地提供了参考路径。

Abstract: Automated anomaly detection methods often report strong performance on curated academic benchmarks, but their behavior under real-world industrial conditions is less clear. In this work, we evaluate 19 unsupervised anomaly detection models on the BowTie dataset, a challenging manufacturing dataset with reflective surfaces, subtle defects, and profile-specific variation. In contrast to benchmark results, we observe that model performance is less stable than typically reported on standard benchmarks such as MVTec AD, highly sensitive to preprocessing, and inconsistent across conditions, with no single approach emerging as uniformly robust; a consensus audit further indicates that nominal-data quality affects deployment.
  Motivated by these findings, we developed and initially deployed a unified human-in-the-loop framework for manufactured-part inspection that combines image annotation, AI-assisted defect detection, and an integrated validation engine, replacing a prior manual visual inspection and documentation workflow. The system supports heatmap-guided defect review, SAM-refined candidate regions for inspector acceptance, rejection, or boundary adjustment, mask evaluation where annotations exist, and review history for inspector consistency and onboarding. Together, the results highlight the gap between benchmark performance and deployment reality, and provide a practical framework for addressing it.

</details>


### [145] [The Sample Complexity of Policy Learning with Mu-Resets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07772)
*Gene Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在μ-resets交互协议下基于策略的强化学习，解决了策略可实现性在该问题样本复杂度中的作用这一开放问题，并揭示了重置分布覆盖率概念对地平线H依赖性的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kakade和Langford提出的μ-resets交互协议允许学习者在探索性重置分布μ之外采样轨迹。作者希望解决KLS25提出的关于策略可实现性在样本复杂度中作用的问题，并理解地平线H的依赖关系如何受重置分布覆盖率概念的影响。

Method: 该工作通过理论分析对不同覆盖率假设下的样本复杂度进行了刻画：在有界全策略集中率（all-policy concentrability）下证明了exp(Ω(H))的样本复杂度下界；在有界前推集中率（pushforward concentrability）下，证明地平线依赖关系紧致地表征为exp(Θ(√H))。

Result: 在all-policy concentrability假设下，样本复杂度下界为exp(Ω(H))；在pushforward concentrability假设下，地平线依赖被精确刻画为exp(Θ(√H))，两者之间存在根本性的差异。

Conclusion: 重置分布的覆盖率概念是决定问题地平线依赖的关键因素，不同的覆盖率假设会导致指数级不同的样本复杂度下界。

Abstract: We study policy-based reinforcement learning under the $μ$-resets interaction protocol of Kakade and Langford [KL02]. This interaction protocol enables the learner to sample trajectories from a given exploratory reset distribution $μ$, in addition to the starting distribution. We resolve the question raised by [KLS25] on the role of policy realizability for the sample complexity of this problem. Critically, the dependence on horizon $H$ is governed by the notion of coverage assumed of the reset distribution. Under bounded all-policy concentrability, we show a $\exp(Ω(H))$ sample complexity lower bound; with bounded pushforward concentrability, we show the dependence on horizon is tightly characterized as $\exp(Θ(\sqrt H))$.

</details>


### [146] [Hierarchical rank-evolving representation for physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09483)
*Ruoyang Su,Xi-Le Zhao,Kun Li,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层级秩演化（HRE）表示方法用于多元函数，通过自动秩确定机制替代传统T-PINNs中手动调优的低秩张量分解，并构建了相应的HRE-PINNs，在多个高维静态问题和非线性时变问题中取得了优于现有方法的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有张量物理信息神经网络（T-PINNs）依赖预先指定的低秩张量分解且需要手动调节秩，这限制了其捕获多元解函数潜在结构的能力，也阻碍了实际部署应用。

Method: 提出层级秩演化（HRE）表示：将目标多元函数分解为一个小规模内层张量与沿各模式的一组单变量函数的组合，并对小规模内层张量使用定制的张量网络分解来捕获其结构。关键超参数（秩）在分解过程中自适应地揭示，无需人工调优。在此基础上构建HRE-PINNs。

Result: 在包括高维静态问题（Helmholtz方程、Poisson方程）、非线性时变问题（Klein-Gordon方程）以及复杂流体动力学问题（流动混合方程、Navier-Stokes方程）的广泛数值实验中，HRE-PINNs在精度上持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: HRE表示通过自动秩确定机制解决了T-PINNs中手动秩调优的根本性挑战，能够忠实捕获多元函数的底层结构，使方法更具实际部署价值，并在多个基准问题上展现了显著的精度优势。

Abstract: Recently, tensor-based physics-informed neural networks (T-PINNs) have received increasing attention. However, existing T-PINNs still face a fundamental challenge: they mainly rely on pre-specified low-rank tensor decompositions with manually tuned ranks, which limits their ability to capture the underlying structures of multivariate solution functions and hinders their practical deployment. To address this challenge, we propose a hierarchical rank-evolving (abbreviated as HRE) representation for multivariate functions, which endows us to faithfully capture the underlying structure of the targeted multivariate function accompanying with automatic rank determination. Concretely, in the hierarchical design of HRE representation, the target multivariate function is decomposed as a small-scale inner tensor with a set of univariate functions along each mode, where a customized tensor network decomposition can be readily deployed to capture the underlying structure of the small-scale inner tensor. In HRE representation, the crucial hyperparameters, ranks, can be adaptively revealed during the decomposition, freeing us from manual rank tuning and making HRE practically applicable to real-world problems. Besides, we build the HRE-PINNs correspondingly. Extensive numerical experiments, including high-dimensional static problems (Helmholtz equation and Poisson equation), nonlinear time-dependent problems (Klein-Gordon equation), and complex fluid-dynamics problems (flow mixing equation and Navier-Stokes equation), demonstrate that HRE-PINNs consistently outperform existing state-of-the-art approaches in terms of accuracy.

</details>


### [147] [Shape Mutating Expert Compression:LorExperts and BTExperts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07814)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,Sourjya Roy,Bowen Bao,Thiago Crepaldi,Spandan Tiwari,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LorExperts，一种保留路由器的MoE专家压缩方法。它基于"专家按功能性共激活形成社区（而非权重相似性）"的发现，将专家聚类，每个簇保留一个全精度主导专家，其余专家用低秩校正表示。在Qwen3-30B-A3B和Gemma-4-26B-A4B上，约50%压缩率下性能优于D²-MoE等基线，且优势随专家数量增长。另提出重建微调流程和BTExperts树状组织以实现推理时计算复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型部署成本高，现有压缩方法存在缺陷：专家剪枝（如REAP）和合并牺牲精度且需重训路由器；低秩Delta分解（如D²-MoE）虽保留所有专家和路由器，但单个共享分量无法逼近大量近正交的专家，精度随专家数增加而急剧下降。需要一种既保留所有专家又不重训路由器的高效压缩方法。

Method: 1) 发现MoE专家权重虽近正交，但按功能共激活形成与权重相似性解耦的社区；2) 基于此提出LorExperts：将专家聚类，每个簇保留一个全精度主导专家，其余成员表示为对局部主导专家的低秩校正；3) 保留所有专家和原始路由器（无需路由器重训）；4) 提出重建微调流程；5) 提出BTExperts，将主导专家和校正组织成树结构，实现推理时共享计算的分期摊销。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B和Gemma-4-26B-A4B上进行约50%专家压缩时，LorExperts在大多数任务上比基线（如D²-MoE、REAP等）更好地保持下游准确率和困惑度；与D²-MoE的优势随专家数量E增加而扩大。

Conclusion: LorExperts通过利用MoE专家间的功能共激活社区结构，在不重训路由器、保留所有专家的情况下实现了优于现有方法的压缩性能，且其优势随专家规模增长而扩大。BTExperts进一步为推理时计算复用提供了树状组织方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models deliver high capacity at low per-token compute, but deploying them cheaply requires compressing their many expert weight matrices. Expert pruning (e.g., REAP) and merging reduce cost but sacrifice accuracy and require retraining the router; low-rank delta decomposition of experts (e.g., D^2-MoE) preserves all experts and the router, but degrades sharply as the expert count grows because a single shared component cannot approximate many near-orthogonal experts.
  Because MoE expert weights are near-orthogonal, a single shared component (as in prior delta decomposition) scales poorly with the expert count; we show that experts nonetheless organize into functional co-activation communities that are decoupled from weight similarity. Building on this, we introduce LorExperts, a router-preserving compression method that clusters experts, keeps one full-precision dominant per cluster, and represents the remaining members as low-rank corrections to their local dominant. LorExperts retains all experts and the original router (no router retraining). At ~50% expert compression on Qwen3-30B-A3B and Gemma-4-26B-A4B, LorExperts preserves downstream accuracy and perplexity better than the baselines on most of the tasks; the margin over D^2-MoE grows with expert count E. We further give a reconstruction fine-tuning procedure for LorExperts, and BTExperts, a tree organization of dominants and corrections that enables inference-time amortization of shared computation.

</details>


### [148] [From token probabilities to calibrated confidence: An empirical study of mathematical question answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07827)
*Avery Ma,Lorne Schell,Vin Bhaskara,Leila Pishdad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在数学问答任务中的置信度估计与校准问题。研究发现，虽然单个token概率容易过度自信，但将整个序列的token概率聚合可以捕捉正确与错误生成之间小而一致的区别，从而提供更有信息量的置信度估计。多轮方法（如自我验证和Monte Carlo Dropout）能产生校准良好的置信度。Platt缩放和等渗回归两种后处理校准方法能大幅降低域内校准误差，但其数据效率随数据集难度变化，且校准映射在跨数据集和模型间的迁移具有不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明token概率往往过度自信，本文旨在探究这些现成信号是否仍能为数学问答提供良好校准的置信度估计，并系统比较单轮与多轮估计器的效果。

Method: 本文采用两类估计器：单轮估计器（复用原始生成的token概率）与多轮估计器（通过验证或随机前向传播获取额外置信度信号）。多轮方法包括两种：通过重新提示实现自我验证（含低成本的in-situ变体），以及Monte Carlo Dropout（利用随机前向传播的变异推导置信度）。此外评估了Platt缩放和等渗回归两种后处理校准方法。

Result: 单轮方法中，将token概率在整个序列上聚合能捕捉正确与错误生成间的细微差异，比单独token概率更具信息量。多轮方法能获得校准良好的置信度估计。两种后处理校准方法均大幅降低域内校准误差，但数据效率受数据集难度影响，且校准映射在跨数据集和模型间表现出不对称迁移。

Conclusion: 现成的token概率信号经过序列聚合后仍可提供有意义的置信度估计，多轮验证方法可达到良好校准。后处理校准方法虽然有效，但其跨域迁移能力有限，需要针对具体数据集和模型进行调整。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for large language models (LLMs) aims to estimate the probability that a generated answer is correct, while calibration aligns these estimates with empirical accuracy. Prior work has shown that token probabilities are often overconfident, we investigate whether these readily available signals can nevertheless provide well-calibrated confidence estimation for mathematical question answering. We compare single-pass estimators, which reuse token probabilities from the original generation, with multi-pass estimators, which obtain additional confidence signals through verification or stochastic forward passes. While individual token probabilities can be highly saturated, we find that aggregating token probabilities over the full sequence captures small but consistent differences between correct and incorrect generations, yielding more informative confidence estimates. Multi-pass methods can yield calibrated confidence estimates. We study two such approaches: self-verification through re-prompting, including a lower-cost in-situ variant, and Monte Carlo Dropout, which derives confidence from variation across stochastic forward passes. We further evaluate two post-hoc calibration methods, Platt scaling and isotonic regression, both of which substantially reduce in-domain calibration error. However, their data efficiency varies with dataset difficulty, and the calibration mappings often transfer asymmetrically across datasets and models.

</details>


### [149] [TEMPER: Tensorized Efficient Manifold-constrained Parameterization for Expressive Residual Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07851)
*Yuxuan Gu,Wuyang Zhou,Huijun Xing,Danilo Mandic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TEMPER提出了一种基于张量网络的参数化方法，用于高效表达残差路由（residual routing）。它将超连接（HC）中的稠密无结构生成器表示为多路张量并通过张量网络进行低秩参数化，在保持流形约束路由接口的同时大幅减少参数增长，并在语言建模和常识推理任务上达到或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有残差连接方法（如超连接HC及其流形约束变体mHC）虽然能提升深度网络的表达力，但其生成器（用于分支前聚合、残差混合和分支后再分配）是稠密且无结构的，导致参数量随残差流数量快速增长，成为生成器层面的瓶颈。

Method: TEMPER将HC中的三类生成器表示为跨输入流、特征和输出流三个模态的多路张量，并利用张量网络（tensor networks）对其进行结构化低秩参数化。该设计保留了token相关的流形约束路由接口，同时通过张量秩控制学习路由子空间的维度（满秩可恢复稠密路由），并通过生成器近似误差理论上界保证路由logits及路由块输出的差异有界。

Result: 在语言建模和常识推理任务上，TEMPER匹配或超越了现有方法，同时使用显著更少的额外参数。在8个残差流设置下，TEMPER取得了最佳CORE分数，比mHC减少了约84%的额外参数，展现出更强的性能-参数效率权衡。

Conclusion: TEMPER通过张量网络结构化解耦了残差路由的表达力与参数效率之间的矛盾，既保持了HC/mHC的动态信息流和训练稳定性优势，又解决了参数增长问题，并提供了路由子空间维度和近似误差的可解释性保证。

Abstract: Residual connections rely on a static residual pathway, and are essential for training deep neural networks. Hyper-connections (HC) increase the expressivity of residual routing by incorporating multiple residual streams and learning dynamic information flow, while manifold-constrained (mHC) variants stabilize training through doubly stochastic residual mixing. However, a generator-level bottleneck remains in existing methods: they use dense, unstructured generators for pre-branch aggregation, residual mixing, and post-branch redistribution, which results in parameter count growing rapidly with the number of streams. To address this issue, we propose \underline{\textbf{T}}ensorized \underline{\textbf{E}}fficient \underline{\textbf{M}}anifold-constrained \underline{\textbf{P}}arameterization for \underline{\textbf{E}}xpressive Residual \underline{\textbf{R}}outing (\textbf{TEMPER}), which represents these generators as multi-way tensors over the input-stream, feature, and output-stream modes, and parameterizes them using tensor networks. Such a structured low-rank formulation is shown to preserve token-dependent manifold-constrained routing interface while substantially reducing parameter growth. It also promotes interpretability and intuition, as: i) tensor ranks control the dimensionality of the learned routing subspace, with full ranks recovering dense routing; while ii) the generator approximation errors bound differences in routing logits and, consequently, in the routed-block outputs. Comprehensive experiments show that TEMPER matches or outperforms existing methods across language modeling and commonsense reasoning tasks, while requiring substantially fewer additional parameters. At eight residual streams, TEMPER achieves the best CORE score while using about $84\%$ fewer additional parameters than mHC, thus showing a stronger performance-parameter efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [150] [CommitKV: Lifecycle-Aware KV Cache Compression via Commit Transitions for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07855)
*Weizhong Huang,Jinchao Zhang,Xiawu Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CommitKV通过识别KV缓存的生命周期（commit transitions）来区分"暂时休眠"的信息与"已完成角色"的信息，从而在多轮ReAct代理中安全地压缩KV缓存，减少内存使用并加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮ReAct代理在推理过程中不断累积推理轨迹、工具调用和观察结果，导致KV缓存持续增长，增加内存占用和注意力计算成本。现有KV缓存压缩方法仅基于当前轮次的注意力分数进行逐出，无法区分"暂时休眠但未来可能重要"的信息与"已完成作用可安全删除"的信息，导致压缩准确性不足。

Method: CommitKV通过三个关键机制实现：1) 将代理事件划分为token页面，在工具调用提交前和提交后观察结果合并后分别测量每个候选页面的删除效果，通过成对测量区分休眠页面与高到低完成度候选；2) 应用贪心联合测试，仅在候选页面的联合提交后效果保持有界时接受其退役；3) 在后续压缩检查点，排除已接受的页面、保护等待提交后测量的有界页面集合，并在缓存预算内以相同的token索引保留键、值和绝对位置。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，CommitKV减少了代理内存使用，加速了端到端推理，并且在准确性上优于现有的KV缓存压缩方法。

Conclusion: CommitKV通过利用提交转换来识别KV生命周期，能够准确区分暂时休眠的信息与已完成角色的信息，实现了更安全有效的KV缓存压缩，在内存效率、推理速度和准确性方面均取得了改进。

Abstract: Multi-turn Reasoning-and-Acting (ReAct) agents accumulate growing trajectories of reasoning, tool calls, and observations. Their key-value (KV) caches grow accordingly, increasing memory use and attention cost during model inference. Existing KV cache compression methods reduce these costs by evicting states with low attention scores. However, low attention in the current turn does not imply future irrelevance, as temporarily inactive information may become important later. Snapshot-based eviction methods therefore do not explicitly distinguish temporarily dormant information from information that appears to have completed its role. In this paper, we present CommitKV, which identifies KV lifecycles through commit transitions. Specifically, CommitKV first divides completed agent events into token pages and compares each eligible page's deletion effect before a tool-call commit and after the commit's returned observation has been incorporated. Based on these paired measurements, CommitKV distinguishes dormant pages from high-to-low completion candidates. It then applies a greedy joint test, accepting candidates for retirement only when their combined post-commit effect remains bounded. Finally, at a later compression checkpoint, accepted pages are excluded, a bounded set of pages awaiting post-commit measurement is protected, and the remaining KV states are retained within the cache budget using the same token indices for keys, values, and absolute positions. These mechanisms ensure that CommitKV can distinguish dormant information from information that has completed its observed role and can be safely removed. Experiments on various benchmarks show that CommitKV reduces agent memory use, accelerates end-to-end inference, and achieves higher accuracy than existing KV cache compression methods.

</details>


### [151] [Adaptive KappaSharp: Condition-Number Shaping for Preferential Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07859)
*Ketong Shao,Jialu Wang,Xuekai Pei,Ali Mesbah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了KappaSharp方法，通过在原贝叶斯优化的似然Hessian矩阵上加对角校正来降低条件数，解决偏好贝叶斯优化中EUBO采集函数导致的秩亏Hessian问题。该方法仅在模型拟合阶段应用校正，不干扰查询选择，并提出了自适应变体。在11个基准（5-20维）上，包括16维等离子体医学控制器调参任务，自适应KappaSharp相比标准PBO基线最多提升+10.9%（p=0.003）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在偏好贝叶斯优化（PBO）中，标准的EUBO采集函数每次生成的查询对与前序查询不共享候选点，导致比较图形成孤立组件，使得似然Hessian矩阵秩亏（每个孤立组件移除一个自由度）。这一缺陷是结构性的，无法通过更换替代模型来解决。已有的修复方法要么浪费查询预算强制保持连通性，要么使用均匀正则化同时扰动已被观测数据良好约束的方向。

Method: KappaSharp通过对Hessian矩阵施加对角校正来降低其条件数，校正量在先验不确定性更高的方向上更大。校正仅在模型拟合阶段应用，不改变查询选择过程。此外提出自适应变体，仅当替代模型对最近的比较结果有信心时才激活校正，避免在问题条件良好时进行不必要的修正。

Result: 在11个基准测试（5-20维）上进行了评估，包括一个16维的等离子体医学控制器调参问题。自适应KappaSharp优于标准PBO基线，性能提升最高达+10.9%（p=0.003）。

Conclusion: KappaSharp通过针对性的对角Hessian校正有效解决了PBO中结构性秩亏问题，相比均匀正则化或强制连通性方法更为高效。自适应版本在保证条件良好时不做多余修正，在条件不良时激活校正，整体表现出优于标准基线的稳定性能。

Abstract: Preferential Bayesian optimization (PBO) optimizes objectives accessible only through pairwise user comparisons. The standard approach fits a Gaussian process surrogate for observed pairwise comparisons (PairwiseGP) using the Laplace approximation and selects queries with the Expected Utility of Best Option (EUBO) acquisition function. EUBO queries new candidates at each step, producing pairs that share no candidates with previous queries. Each such pair forms an isolated component in the comparison graph, removing one degree of freedom from the likelihood Hessian and making it rank-deficient. This deficiency is structural and cannot be resolved by changing the surrogate modeling approach. Existing approaches to remedy this issue either waste query budget by forcing comparisons to stay connected, or apply uniform regularization that also perturbs directions already well-constrained by the observed comparisons. We propose KappaSharp that enables a diagonal correction to the Hessian to reduce its condition number, with larger corrections where the prior uncertainty is higher. The correction is only applied in the model fitting step, not query selection. An adaptive variant of KappaSharp is also presented that activates the correction only when the surrogate is confident about recent comparisons, avoiding unnecessary corrections when the problem is well-conditioned. On 11 benchmarks (5--20 dimensions), including a 16-dimensional controller tuning problem in plasma medicine, Adaptive KappaSharp outperforms the standard PBO baseline, with up to +10.9% ($p{=}0.003$).

</details>


### [152] [CONFER: Conflict-Aware Evidence Negotiation for Regime-Calibrated Weak Supervision in Multimodal Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07867)
*Bojing Hou,Ruohao Li,Yitong Zhu,Luwen Yu,Yuyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CONFER框架，一种基于图的冲突感知证据协商方法，用于弱监督多模态情感识别。它将每个模态专家建模为节点，结合预测信念、边界不确定性和运行时可靠性，通过迭代消息传递实现模态间协商，并对样本进行弱标签校准。在AMIGOS、MAHNOB-HCI和DEAP上取得了有竞争力的结果（AMIGOS-V上0.873准确率，MAHNOB-V上0.854准确率，均为严格LOSO评估）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感识别方法通常将自报告标签视为可靠监督，忽视了自报告标签的不可靠性以及跨模态冲突的存在，这限制了模型在弱监督条件下的性能。

Method: CONFER将每个模态专家建模为图中的一个节点，每个节点带有预测信念、基于边界的确定性度量以及从历史折外性能和当前样本不确定性估计的运行时可靠性。通过不确定性感知的兼容性和可靠性导向的非对称边权重控制迭代消息传递协商，随后进行同伴支持的预测读出。根据冲突减少量、残差异议和平均模态不确定性进一步区分三种模式（共识、异议、模糊），用于样本特定的弱标签校准。

Result: 在AMIGOS、MAHNOB-HCI和DEAP三个数据集上，分别在主题相关10折和严格的留一主题（LOSO）协议下进行评估。CONFER在严格LOSO评估下于AMIGOS-V达到0.873准确率，在MAHNOB-V达到0.854准确率，取得有竞争力的性能。额外的分析显示，高分岐样本上的协商增益更大，且对弱标签损坏的鲁棒性有所改善。

Conclusion: 跨模态冲突不仅不是噪声，反而为模态方向性协调和监督可靠性估计提供了有用信息，能有效改善弱监督多模态情感识别的性能。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition often treats self-reported labels as reliable supervision while overlooking self-report unreliability and cross-modal conflict. We propose \textbf{CONFER}, a graph-based conflict-aware evidence negotiation framework for weakly supervised multimodal emotion recognition. CONFER represents each modality expert as a node with a predictive belief, boundary-based uncertainty, and runtime reliability estimated from historical out-of-fold performance and current-sample uncertainty. Uncertainty-aware compatibility and reliability-directed asymmetric edge weights govern iterative message-passing negotiation, followed by peer-supported prediction readout. Conflict reduction, residual disagreement, and mean modality uncertainty further characterize three regimes---Consensus, Dissent, and Ambiguity---for sample-specific weak-label calibration. We evaluate CONFER on AMIGOS, MAHNOB-HCI, and DEAP under subject-dependent 10-fold and strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocols. CONFER achieves competitive performance, reaching \textbf{0.873} accuracy on AMIGOS-V and \textbf{0.854} accuracy on MAHNOB-V under strict LOSO evaluation. Further analyses show larger negotiation gains on high-conflict samples and improved robustness to weak-label corruption, indicating that cross-modal conflict provides useful information for both directional modality coordination and supervision-reliability estimation.

</details>


### [153] [V-Simba: Unleashing the Architectural Potential of RL in Visual Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07870)
*Donghu Kim,Youngdo Lee,Hojoon Lee,Johan Obando-Ceron,Byungkun Lee,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro,Jaegul Choo,Clare Lyle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出V-Simba，一种受状态基RL中Simba架构启发的视觉RL架构。通过在SAC基础上添加归一化层和点卷积，V-Simba在DMC、Adroit和Meta-World基准上达到或超越SOTA方法，且比DrQ-v2更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉RL中样本效率是核心挑战，高维输入掩盖学习信号。已有工作多聚焦算法层面（如动力学模型、探索策略），但近期状态基RL研究表明仅架构设计即可显著提升样本效率。作者质疑这些架构原则能否迁移到视觉RL中。

Method: V-Simba基于SAC+数据增强构建，修改两点：(1) 添加归一化层以稳定训练；(2) 使用点卷积（pointwise convolutions）降低计算量。整体架构简单，受Simba架构启发。

Result: 在DMC、Adroit和Meta-World三个基准上匹配或超越SOTA方法，同时比DrQ-v2具有更高的计算效率。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 架构设计原则可以从状态基RL迁移到视觉RL，简单的架构修改（归一化+点卷积）就能带来显著的样本效率提升，表明架构选择在视觉RL中同样值得关注。

Abstract: Improving sample efficiency remains a core challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), especially in real-world settings like robotics, where data collection is costly. This challenge is pronounced in visual RL, where high-dimensional inputs often obscure learning signals. While prior work in visual RL has focused on algorithmic solutions, such as better dynamics models or exploration strategies, recent advances in state-based RL show that architectural design alone can lead to significant gains in sample efficiency. This raises an important question: Can these architectural principles transfer to visual RL? In response, we introduce V-Simba, a simple yet effective visual RL architecture inspired by the Simba architecture from state-based RL. Built on top of Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) with data augmentation, V-Simba modifies the architecture by adding normalization layers to stabilize training and using pointwise convolutions to reduce computation. Despite its simplicity, V-Simba matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art methods across the DMC, Adroit, and Meta-World benchmarks, while being more computationally efficient than DrQ-v2. We make our code publicly available at https://github.com/DAVIAN-Robotics/V-Simba.

</details>


### [154] [Router Sensitivity Under Lightweight Fine-Tuning Identifies Prunable Experts in Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07890)
*Ali Janati,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Xinyi Luo,Wenyuan Shen,Owen Zou,Yankai Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路由器敏感度的MoE专家剪枝方法：仅使用参数高效的LoRA适配器进行轻量级微调（仅训练0.002%的参数），通过路由器权重变化（ℓ2范数）对专家进行排序，一次性剪除变化最小的专家。在Mixtral-8×7B-Instruct上，剪掉一半专家后MMLU-Pro准确率达27.54%（优于全模块LoRA的24.42%），且信号质量随适配器范围扩大而下降；在最大压缩下准确率仍接近随机/基于幅度剪枝的1.8倍，内存减少49%、延迟降低37%。该方法可迁移至Qwen1.5-MoE数学任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽然解耦了总参数与单token计算量，但部署时仍需存储所有专家。已有理论证明在微调过程中剪除路由器范数变化最小的专家可以保持精度，但该理论假设全量微调。实际场景中全量微调成本高昂，本文旨在探索能否用轻量级适配方法恢复这一路由器敏感度信号，从而使专家剪枝变得实用。

Method: 1) 使用参数高效的LoRA适配器对模型进行简短微调；2) 根据微调前后路由器权重变化的ℓ2范数对专家进行排序；3) 一次性剪除变化最小的专家。实验对比了仅路由器LoRA、全模块LoRA、IA3、无约束加性适配器等不同适配方案，以确定哪种方案能最有效地传递路由器敏感度信号。

Result: 仅路由器LoRA（训练0.002%参数）剪除一半专家后MMLU-Pro达27.54%，优于同秩全模块LoRA的24.42%；准确率随LoRA rank单调提升至28.76%。IA3与直接路由器适配效果相当，而无约束适配器降低信号质量。路由器引导剪枝的准确率准线性衰减而非崩溃，最大压缩下约为幅度/随机剪枝的1.8倍，内存减少49%、延迟降低37%。Qwen1.5-MoE数学任务中剪除一半专家仍保留49.7%平均准确率，而随机剪枝跌至个位数。

Conclusion: 轻量级微调下的路由器敏感度信号可以替代全量微调来驱动MoE专家剪枝，使理论上被证明合理的专家剪枝方法在规模上变得切实可行。路由器权重重启信号质量的关键，且信号在随机/幅度基准面前显著更鲁棒。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models decouple total parameters from per-token compute, but deployment still requires storing every expert. Recent theory shows that pruning experts with the smallest router-norm changes during fine-tuning can preserve accuracy, but assumes full fine-tuning. We test whether lightweight adaptation can recover this signal. We briefly fine-tune with a parameter-efficient adapter, rank experts by the induced $\ell_2$ router change, and prune the least-changed experts in one shot. On Mixtral-8$\times$7B-Instruct (44.83% MMLU-Pro), router-only LoRA trains 0.002% of parameters and outperforms all-module LoRA at matched rank with half the experts removed (27.54% vs. 24.42%); signal quality declines as adaptation spreads to attention and expert weights. Accuracy improves monotonically with LoRA rank, reaching 28.76%. IA3, which leaves router weights frozen, matches direct router adaptation, whereas unconstrained additive adapters degrade the signal. Router-guided MMLU-Pro accuracy decays quasi-linearly rather than collapsing, remains nearly 1.8 times that of magnitude-based or random pruning at maximal compression, and reduces memory by 49% and per-token latency by 37%. At 25% compression, retention is competitive with methods using full activation statistics. The criterion also transfers to Qwen1.5-MoE fine-tuned for mathematics, retaining 49.7% mean accuracy over eleven benchmarks with half the experts removed while random pruning falls to single digits. Router sensitivity under lightweight fine-tuning therefore makes provably motivated expert pruning practical at scale.

</details>


### [155] [LLM-Based Embeddings for Program Analysis and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07894)
*Calvin Higgins,Marco Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将基于LLMCompiler（在大规模中间表示代码上预训练的LLM）生成程序嵌入，应用于程序分析和优化任务。通过将程序和IR代码分块嵌入并聚合，在算法分类任务上达到1.54%的错误率，相比当前最优方法提升12%，并在异构设备映射上取得有竞争力的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM（特别是LLMCompiler）大规模预训练于IR代码后生成的程序嵌入，在程序分析和优化任务中的潜力，为未来性能感知型LLM的代码优化应用奠定基础。

Method: 从源码和IR代码直接生成程序嵌入：将程序分割为块，用预训练LLM独立嵌入每个块，再聚合为单一的程序嵌入。实验对比了源码嵌入、IR嵌入及二者组合的效果。

Result: 结合源码和IR代码嵌入在算法分类上达到1.54%的错误率，较当前SOTA提升12%；异构设备映射任务上取得有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，使用IR代码嵌入进行程序分析具有显著优势，训练一个性能感知的LLM用于IR代码嵌入，有望在代码优化任务中取得SOTA结果。

Abstract: Recent advances have highlighted the potential of machine learning, particularly Large Language Models (LLMs), for analyzing and optimizing programs. We present the first application of program embeddings from LLMCompiler---an LLM massively pretrained on intermediate representation (IR) code---to representative program analysis and optimization tasks. We generate program embeddings directly from source and IR code using a simple approach: split programs into chunks, independently embed each chunk with pretrained LLMs, and then aggregate the chunk embeddings into a single program embedding. Our experiments show that combining source and IR code embeddings achieves an error rate of 1.54\% in algorithm classification, a 12\% improvement over the current state-of-the-art, and a competitive accuracy on heterogeneous device mapping. These findings suggest that training a performance-aware LLM for embedding IR code might yield state-of-the-art results in code optimization tasks.

</details>


### [156] [When Does Trace-Driven Evaluation Mislead MoE Expert Caching? Replay Semantics, Workload Contamination, and Operating Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07911)
*Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究MoE（混合专家）模型的专家缓存管理问题，发现评估方法高度脆弱。通过事件原子级的trace驱动模拟器（覆盖40/64/128专家模型），识别出三个会改变结论而非仅改变数值的评估维度：重放语义、工作负载污染和运行域。修正这些偏差后，与离线最优的稳定差距为44.2-45.9%，其中84.3-96.6%可归因于对未来使用最远专家先知的知识。因果型next-use预测器作为驱逐规则仅能恢复-11.4%的差距，表明离线最优的巨幅差距在代表性轻量因果机制下被大幅高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型已超出加速器内存，将专家权重卸载到主机内存成为标准做法，这使得专家缓存管理成为优化专家流量（每token的专家通信量）的关键杠杆。评估缓存策略的核心是命中率，但作者发现这一测量本身非常脆弱——不同的评估设置可能颠倒策略排名，因此需要系统性地识别并修正评估中的偏差。

Method: 作者构建了一个trace驱动的、事件原子级的模拟器，在三种MoE模型（40/64/128专家）上隔离三个评估轴进行分析：(1) 重放语义——对比融合事件流量契约下的不一致按访问重放与事件一致重放；(2) 工作负载污染——使用单指令模板的探针集与匹配对渲染干预的对比；(3) 运行域——跨模型归一化缺失率迁移、时间顺序置换、离线最优差距测量。最后使用forced-admission oracle分解差距来源，并评估因果next-use预测器等驱逐规则。

Result: 三个评估轴均会改变结论：不一致的按访问重放使基于recency的策略膨胀27-29%，使政策排名反转；匹配对渲染干预移动早期窗口效应19.4-31.9点并反转哪个工作负载最缓存友好；仅置换事件流的时间顺序将离线最优差距从44.9%变为30.8%。修正后稳定差距为44.2-45.9%。oracle分析显示84.3-96.6%的差距来自先知知识；因果next-use预测器恢复-11.4%的差距，最优受害者选择率仅3.4%（对比LRU/LFRU的20.6-22.1%）。

Conclusion: 作者的立场很明确：在评估的设置范围内，离线最优的巨大差距在代表性轻量因果机制下被大幅高估。评估方法论的脆弱性意味着缓存策略研究需要更严谨的实验设计——至少应报告每层容量对应的每步专家并集，同时注意重放语义和负载生成的潜在偏差。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have outgrown accelerator memory, and offloading expert weights to host memory is now standard. This makes expert cache management an attractive lever: a policy that raised the hit rate would cut expert traffic per token. Evaluating that is a measurement problem, and we find the measurement fragile.
  With a trace-driven, event-atomic simulator over three MoE models (40, 64, 128 experts), we isolate three evaluation axes that change conclusions, not just numbers. Replay semantics: under a fused-event traffic contract, an inconsistent per-access replay inflates recency-based policies by 27-29% while leaving frequency-based and static ones within 4%, inverting the policy ranking. Workload contamination: probe sets using one instruction template per category produce verbatim-identical generation prefixes; a matched-pair rendering intervention moves the measured early-window effect by 19.4-31.9 points and reverses which workloads look most cache-friendly. Operating regimes: normalized miss fractions do not transfer across models, so the per-step expert union relative to per-layer capacity must be reported -- yet permuting only the temporal order of an identical event stream moves the offline-optimal gap from 44.9% to 30.8%, so it is not sufficient.
  Corrected, a stable gap to the offline optimum remains (44.2-45.9% over 13 frozen workload compositions). A forced-admission oracle attributes 84.3-96.6% of it to knowing which resident expert is used furthest in the future. A causal next-use predictor, used as an eviction rule, recovers -11.4% of the gap; it picks an optimal victim 3.4% of the time, against 2.4% for a random resident block and 20.6-22.1% for LRU and LFRU. Our position is narrow: in our evaluated settings a large offline-optimal gap substantially overstates the gains recovered by representative lightweight causal mechanisms.

</details>


### [157] [SPECTRA: Pushing the KV Cache Beyond the 2-Bit Cliff via Spectral Transform Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07915)
*Jiamu Zhang,Liang Wu,Kelly Wan,Hanjie Chen,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPECTRA是一种免训练的KV缓存压缩编解码器，通过将缓存旋转到由其自身统计量计算出的坐标系中，去除通道间相关性，使少量通道承载大部分信息，从而将比特预算集中在重要通道上。在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B的长上下文基准上，SPECTRA在4倍压缩时接近无损，在8倍压缩时仍具竞争力，最高可达12倍压缩，突破了均匀量化在2比特处的质量悬崖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在代理时代越来越多地处理长输入，推理内存被KV缓存主导。现有量化方法将每个存储值压到相同低精度，但质量在接近2比特/值时急剧下降，因为离群值过多的通道导致量化级别被少数大条目占据，其余值塌缩为噪声。简单地按通道重要性分配更多比特行不通，因为原始缓存的通道高度相关，没有哪个通道显得更重要。

Method: 作者通过分析发现，当KV缓存旋转到由其自身统计量计算出的坐标系后，通道间的相关性被去除，此时少数通道承载了几乎所有信息。基于此分析，SPECTRA将缓存重新编码到该坐标系中，将比特预算集中在承载信号的通道上。该方法免训练、即插即用（drop-in），作为编解码器工作。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B的长上下文基准上，SPECTRA在4倍压缩时接近无损，在8倍压缩时（均匀量化已崩溃的场景）仍具竞争力，最高可达12倍压缩，将可用压缩推进到2比特悬崖之上。

Conclusion: SPECTRA通过旋转KV缓存去除通道相关性，将比特预算集中在极少数重要通道上，突破了传统均匀量化的2比特质量悬崖，使同一GPU能够承载更长的上下文和更大的批次。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly read long inputs in the agentic era, from whole documents and codebases to conversations across many turns. Their inference memory is then dominated by the key-value (KV) cache, the stored attention keys and values of every token the model has read and generated. Because the cache grows with context length and is re-read in full at every generated token, a longer context means more GPU memory.
  To reduce this cost, most existing methods compress the KV cache by lowering every stored value to the same low precision, a technique known as quantization. They can push this to nearly two bits per value, but rarely further, because quality drops sharply at this 2-bit cliff: four levels are too few for the cache's outlier-heavy values, where a few large entries consume the levels and collapse the rest into noise. A natural remedy is to spend more bits on the channels (feature dimensions) that matter and fewer on the rest, but the raw cache offers no handle: its channels are strongly correlated, so none stands out as more important.
  Our analysis shows that this handle appears once the cache is rotated into a coordinate system computed from its own statistics, removing these correlations. There, a small fraction of channels carries almost all the information, and spending the budget on those few is far more accurate than spreading it evenly.
  Guided by this analysis, we develop SPECTRA, a training-free, drop-in codec that re-encodes the cache into this coordinate system and concentrates the bit budget on the channels that carry the signal. On Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B over long-context benchmarks, SPECTRA is near-lossless at 4x compression, competitive at 8x where uniform quantization has collapsed, and reaches up to 12x, pushing usable compression past the 2-bit cliff so the same GPU holds longer contexts and larger batches.

</details>


### [158] [Spectral Outliers Reveal Dominant Learned Structure in Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07921)
*Kasun Dewage,Marianna Pensky,Suranadi De Silva,T. H. Bandara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文应用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵理论将预训练注意力权重分解为"随机类体"和"谱离群值"，并通过因果实验验证：在Mistral-7B中置零MP识别的离群值（信号）会使HellaSwag、MMLU和PIQA性能降至随机水平，而置零等量体奇异值仅造成较小但不可忽略的性能下降。研究在11个预训练Transformer中发现五个重复模式，并探讨了这些发现对参数高效微调和结构化剪枝的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解预训练Transformer中注意力权重矩阵的结构，将随机噪声与真正的学习信号分离，从而揭示模型内部的信息组织方式，为参数高效微调和结构化剪枝提供理论基础。

Method: 采用Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵理论对预训练注意力投影矩阵（Q、K、V、O）进行谱分解，将矩阵划分为随机类体（bulk）和谱离群值（outliers）；通过因果实验（置零离群值与置零等量体奇异值的对比）验证分解的有效性，并跨11个预训练Transformer进行模式归纳。

Result: 零化MP识别的离群值导致Mistral-7B在HellaSwag、MMLU和PIQA上性能骤降至随机水平，证明离群值是核心信号；零化等量体奇异值仅导致较小但仍非可忽略的退化。跨11个模型发现五个模式：离群值编码主导性的学习结构；Q投影含最多离群值；GQA下V投影缺乏清晰信号/噪声分离；条目级离群值在Q中形成行带、在O中形成列带；特定残差流维度在K和O中跨层持续作为带离群值。

Conclusion: MP谱分解能够有效区分预训练注意力权重中的信号与噪声，离群值承载大部分学习结构。这些结构模式（尤其是Q投影的离群值主导性、行带/列带结构、跨层持续维度）可为参数高效微调中参数的精准定位和结构化剪枝中冗余的识别提供指导。

Abstract: We apply Marchenko-Pastur (MP) random matrix theory to pre-trained attention weights in order to separate each projection matrix into a random-like bulk and a set of spectral outliers. We validate this decomposition causally: zeroing the MP-identified outliers (signal) in Mistral-7B drives HellaSwag, MMLU, and PIQA close to random-chance performance, whereas zeroing a count-matched subset of bulk singular values causes smaller but non-negligible degradation. Across 11 pre-trained transformers we identify five recurring patterns: spectral outliers encode a dominant component of the learned structure; Q projections carry the most outliers; V projections under grouped-query attention lack a clean signal/noise separation; entry-level outliers form structured row-bands in Q and column-bands in O; and specific residual-stream dimensions persist as band outliers across layers in K and O. We close by outlining how these observations could inform parameter-efficient fine-tuning and structured pruning.

</details>


### [159] [Information Routing across Batch Boundaries: Memory--Batch Tradeoffs in Lipschitz Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07922)
*Zicheng Lyu,Zengfeng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机Lipschitz bandit中"宽度-深度"权衡：学习者每轮最多保留W比特的奖励相关状态，并将拉动组织为最多B个批次。当W≳d log(eT)时，作者给出了最小最大期望伪遗憾的精确刻画（至对数因子），揭示了状态宽度与更新深度不可互换，其核心约束是信息路由：低遗憾迫使承诺动作转录编码Θ_d(s^{-d})个区域决策，而边界状态最多携带(B-1)W比特熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应学习同时需要保存观测含义的状态（宽度）和基于该状态行动的机会（深度/批次更新）。现有工作分别研究了顺序（深度充足）和无内存批处理（宽度受限）设置，但两者之间的权衡——即状态内存约束与批次更新约束如何交互——尚未被系统理解。本文旨在刻画这一交互如何影响随机Lipschitz bandit的极小极大遗憾。

Method: 理论上，作者建立了匹配的上界和下界。下界对所有W成立，揭示了信息路由约束：在区域尺度s上，低遗憾迫使承诺动作转录（transcript）编码Θ_d(s^{-d})个区域决策，而收集的边界状态最多携带(B-1)W比特熵，由此导出新的遗憾惩罚项T^{(d+2)/(d+3)}(1+(B-1)W)^{-1/[d(d+3)]}。上界通过匹配策略实现：流式地生成并擦除验证统计量，同时保留安全活动集的掩码（可在内存中或分片段存储）。

Result: 该定理恢复了全维度最坏情况下的批处理-only边界，并在完全顺序特化下恢复了对数内存的可实现性；静态批次边界与可预测的自适应批次边界相匹配。新惩罚项证明了状态宽度与更新深度不是可互换的资源。

Conclusion: 状态宽度与批次深度在随机Lipschitz bandit中产生非平凡的交互效应，二者不可互换；信息路由约束是理解这一权衡的核心。结果统一了顺序学习和批处理学习的已有边界，为约束学习系统（如内存受限的在线决策）提供了完整的遗憾刻画。

Abstract: Adaptive learning needs both a state that preserves what observations imply and opportunities to act on that state. We study this width--depth tradeoff in stochastic Lipschitz bandits. After each pull, the learner retains at most $W$ bits of live reward-dependent state and organizes its pulls into at most $B$ committed batches. For $W\gtrsim_d\log(eT)$, we characterize minimax expected pseudo-regret up to logarithmic factors; the lower bounds hold for every $W$. Besides the classical sequential and unrestricted-memory batch costs, the frontier contains the new penalty \[
  T^{\frac{d+2}{d+3}}
  \bigl(1+(B-1)W\bigr)^{-\frac1{d(d+3)}}, \] proving that state width and update depth are not interchangeable. The interaction is an information-routing constraint: at regional scale $s$, low regret forces the committed action transcript to encode $Θ_d(s^{-d})$ regional decisions, while the collected boundary states carry at most $(B-1)W$ bits of entropy. Matching policies stream and erase verification statistics while retaining a mask of a safe active set, either in memory or fragment by fragment. The theorem recovers the full-dimensional worst-case batch-only frontier and logarithmic-memory achievability in the fully sequential specialization; static batch boundaries match predictable adaptive ones.

</details>


### [160] [Second Order Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07924)
*Drake Brown,Yuhao Huang,Shih-Hsin Wang,Bao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出二阶漂移模型（Second-Order Drifting Models），通过将生成的样本提升到相空间并引入人工速度变量，使密度扰动在傅里叶空间中遵循加速的二阶动力学，将一阶漂移模型的谱刚度问题与优化理论中的Nesterov加速联系起来，在保持单步推理的同时缓解了细尺度结构的收敛缓慢问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶漂移模型虽然避免了迭代推理，但其基于核的漂移场在傅里叶空间中产生频率依赖的训练动力学：在线性化区域中，密度残差的每个傅里叶模式按核谱决定的速率衰减，导致精细结构恢复缓慢，即存在\"谱刚度\"问题。

Method: 将漂移动力学提升到相空间，为生成的样本引入人工速度变量；证明密度扰动在傅里叶空间中服从加速的二阶动力学，从而在理论上建立与Nesterov加速优化之间的联系；推导出实用的半隐式训练算法。

Result: 在合成分布匹配、序列数据生成和机器人控制三个任务上进行了评估，二阶漂移模型相比一阶漂移基线展现出更好的收敛行为，并实现了具有竞争力或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 二阶漂移模型为一阶漂移模型的谱刚度问题提供了有原理依据的缓解机制，同时保持了单步推理的优势，在多个任务上验证了其有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Drifting models are a recent class of one-step generative models that evolve the model distribution during training using a predefined sample-based drift field. Although they avoid iterative inference, their kernel-based drift fields induce frequency-dependent training dynamics: In the linearized regime, each Fourier mode of the density residual decays at a rate determined by the kernel spectrum, leading to slow recovery of fine-scale structure. We propose Second-Order Drifting Models, which lift drifting dynamics into phase space by augmenting generated samples with artificial velocity variables. We show that the resulting density perturbations obey accelerated second-order dynamics in Fourier space, connecting drifting models to the celebrated Nesterov acceleration from optimization theory. This provides a principled mechanism for mitigating the spectral stiffness of first-order drifting while preserving one-step inference. We derive a practical semi-implicit training algorithm and evaluate it on synthetic distribution matching, sequential data generation, and robotic control. Across these settings, the second-order drifting model improves convergence behavior and achieves competitive or superior performance over first-order drifting baselines.

</details>


### [161] [Causal State-Space Model for Causal Inference: Estimating Longitudinal Individual Treatment Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08288)
*Abisoye Abidakun,Mingjun Zhong,Georgios Leontidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CSSD和CSSPD两种因果状态空间模型，通过对比预测编码和局部信息最大化策略，解决纵向观测数据中反事实推断的"域混淆"（domain confusion）导致互信息冲突的问题，在MIMIC-III和癌症模拟数据集上优于Causal Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于对抗训练（域混淆）的反事实时序推断方法虽然能使表征对治疗分配不变，但这种不变性会抑制与治疗相关的协变量信号，而这些信号恰恰是准确结果预测所必需的。这种"平衡-预测"之间的互信息冲突此前未被形式化，导致时序反事实预测精度受限。

Method: 作者首先通过Jensen-Shannon散度界形式化了反事实预测误差与互信息冲突之间的理论关系。在此基础上提出两个互补模型：(1) CSSD采用选择性状态空间模型（SSM）搭配并行多步直接解码器，单次前向传播同时生成所有预测时域，消除累积滚动误差；(2) CSSPD在CSSD基础上引入对比预测编码（CPC）和局部信息最大化（LIM），强化平衡表征中的时序可预测性，并恢复域混淆破坏的局部协变量信息。

Result: 在MIMIC-III上，CSSPD在所有时域tau>=2上均取得低于Causal Transformer的反事实RMSE（增益范围0.02到0.07），编码器成本O(T)。在癌症模拟数据上，CSSPD在gamma<=3时优于CT（边际25.9%-37.0%），CSSD整体平均RMSE最低（较CT降低12.7%），验证了互信息冲突分析的准确性。

Conclusion: 本文首次形式化了平衡-预测互信息冲突，并通过互补的预测性和信息论训练目标提出了结构化的解决方案。实验结果表明该方法在多种混杂强度下均表现优越，为时序反事实推断提供了新的理论视角和实践框架。

Abstract: Estimating counterfactual outcomes over time from longitudinal observational data is central to clinical decision support. Existing methods rely on domain confusion -- adversarial training that renders representations invariant to treatment assignment -- yet this invariance creates a mutual information conflict: it suppresses treatment-correlated covariate signals necessary for accurate outcome prediction. We formalise this tension via a Jensen-Shannon divergence bound on counterfactual prediction error and develop two complementary models. CSSD (Causal State-Space model with Direct decoder) adapts selective State Space Models with a parallel multi-step decoder that eliminates accumulated rollout error by producing all prediction horizons simultaneously in a single forward pass. CSSPD (Causal State-Space model with Predictive regularisation and Direct decoder) augments CSSD with Contrastive Predictive Coding and Local Information Maximisation to reinforce temporal predictability in the balancing representation and recover local covariate information destroyed by domain confusion. On MIMIC-III, CSSPD achieves lower counterfactual RMSE than the Causal Transformer at every horizon tau >= 2 at O(T) encoder cost, with gains from 0.02 (2-step) to 0.07 (6-step). On Cancer Simulation across confounding strengths gamma in {0,1,2,3,4}, CSSPD outperforms CT at gamma <= 3 (margins 25.9%--37.0%), and CSSD achieves the lowest overall average RMSE (12.7% reduction over CT), confirming the MI conflict analysis. To our knowledge, this is the first work to formalise the balancing-prediction MI conflict and propose a structured resolution through complementary predictive and information-theoretic training objectives.

</details>


### [162] [Adaptive Supervised Anchoring for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07935)
*Meilin Yang,Zixuan Ding,Jianhao Nie,Weite Zhang,Yuxin Zhang,Zhiming Shao,Li Yu,Zhe Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的训练框架来缓解on-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）中因学生rollout偏离目标轨迹而导致的"rollout条件信号退化"问题，通过将rollout条件分布匹配与基于规范ground-truth上下文的监督交叉熵两条路径分离，提升任务获取能力同时保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD的有效性严重依赖于学生采样轨迹的质量，当学生rollout偏离目标轨迹时，教师在偏离前缀上施加的条件监督会显著弱化任务相关信号（称为rollout-conditioned signal degradation）。这成为OPSD训练的核心瓶颈，需要针对性解决。

Method: 提出统一训练框架，分离两条互补的监督路径：(1) 保留rollout条件分布匹配，为学生实际访问的状态提供指导；(2) 在规范ground-truth上下文上施加监督交叉熵，避免在错误的rollout前缀上强加目标token。通过token级rollout-目标对齐动态调整规范上下文锚点的强度，冷启动时加强、rollout质量改善后放松。

Result: 在多种模型规模、两个任务族和通用推理基准上的实验表明，该方法相比OPSD提升了任务获取能力，同时保持了通用能力，实现了更优的经验可塑性-稳定性权衡。

Conclusion: 研究发现上下文质量是on-policy自蒸馏的核心瓶颈，验证了将rollout条件指导与规范监督分离的价值，为自蒸馏方法的改进提供了新思路。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) adapts a language model by distilling guidance from a frozen teacher on trajectories sampled from the student. Its effectiveness, however, depends critically on the quality of those trajectories. We show that when student rollouts drift from target trajectories, conditioning the teacher on off-target prefixes substantially weakens its task-relevant supervision. Controlled prefix-corruption experiments expose this failure mode, which we term rollout-conditioned signal degradation. To address this problem, we propose a unified training framework that separates two complementary supervision pathways. The first retains rollout-conditioned distribution matching, providing guidance on states the student actually visits. The second applies supervised cross-entropy on canonical ground-truth contexts, avoiding the incompatibility of imposing target tokens on erroneous rollout prefixes. Token-level rollout-target alignment is used to adapt the strength of the canonical-context anchor, emphasizing it during cold start and relaxing it as rollout quality improves. Experiments across multiple model scales, two task families, and general-reasoning benchmarks show that the proposed approach improves task acquisition over OPSD while preserving general capabilities, resulting in a more favorable empirical plasticity-stability trade-off. These findings identify context quality as a central bottleneck in on-policy self-distillation and demonstrate the value of separating rollout-conditioned guidance from canonical supervision.

</details>


### [163] [Exact Rank and Convex Calibration Dimension Lower Bounds for the Multi-Label F1 Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08399)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多标签分类中实例级F1损失函数的数学结构，确定了其精确秩为s²−s+2，并通过对Bayes几何的直接分析证明了凸校准替代损失（convex calibrated surrogate）的最小维度下界为(2/(3√3)−o(1))s²，结合已有的二次上界，最终确立了CCdim(L^F1)=Θ(s²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已为F1分数矩阵构造了s²+1坐标的仿射和移位低秩表示，并据此构建了二次维度的凸校准替代损失。但精确秩本身不足以确定任意凸校准替代损失的维度下界，因此需要直接分析F1的Bayes几何，以建立校准维度的严格下界。

Method: 本文采用两条路径：(1) 通过将非空得分矩阵分解为子集关联矩阵与正定Cauchy矩阵的乘积来确定F1得分矩阵、移位损失矩阵和非移位损失矩阵的精确秩（均为s²−s+2），以及损失列仿射维数s²−s+1；(2) 构建一个特定分布使固定核心标签集的所有超集恰好为Bayes最优，证明相应的活动损失列在见证支撑上的仿射维度为hn（其中n=s−⌊s/3⌋，h=⌈(s⌊s/3⌋)^{1/2}⌉−1），再应用可行子空间下界得到CCdim的下界。

Result: 确定了F1相关矩阵的精确秩为s²−s+2，列仿射维数为s²−s+1；建立了凸校准维度的下界CCdim(L^F1)≥(2/(3√3)−o(1))s²；结合已知的二次上界，证明了CCdim(L^F1)=Θ(s²)。

Conclusion: F1损失函数的凸校准维度在s²阶上精确确定，即CCdim(L^F1)=Θ(s²)，且下界的常数因子为2/(3√3)≈0.3849。这为多标签分类中F1损失的结构分析提供了精确的秩理论和维度刻画。

Abstract: The instance-wise $F_1$ measure is a central performance measure for multi-label classification. For a problem with $s$ labels, it defines a $2^s\times 2^s$ loss matrix. Previous work exhibited $s^2+1$-coordinate affine and shifted low-rank representations and used them to construct quadratic-dimensional convex calibrated surrogates. We determine the exact rank. Under the convention $F_1(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, the $F_1$ score matrix, the shifted loss matrix, and the unshifted loss matrix all have rank $s^2-s+2$, while the column-affine dimension of the loss is $s^2-s+1$. The proof factors the nonempty score matrix through subset-incidence matrices and a positive-definite Cauchy matrix.
  Exact rank does not, by itself, lower-bound the dimension of an arbitrary convex calibrated surrogate. We therefore analyze the Bayes geometry of $F_1$ directly. We construct a distribution for which precisely all supersets of a fixed core label set are Bayes optimal, and show that the corresponding active loss columns, restricted to the witness support, have affine dimension $hn$, where $n=s-\lfloor s/3\rfloor$ and $h=\lceil(s\lfloor s/3\rfloor)^{1/2}\rceil-1$. Applying the feasible-subspace lower bound for convex calibration dimension gives \[
  \operatorname{CCdim}(L^{F_1})
  \ge \left(\frac{2}{3\sqrt{3}}-o(1)\right)s^2. \] Together with the quadratic upper bound, this establishes $\operatorname{CCdim}(L^{F_1})=Θ(s^2)$.

</details>


### [164] [Persistent Semantic Entities in Tool-Augmented LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07952)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文正式提出"持久语义实体"(PSEs)概念，描述工具增强型LLM智能体中隐藏的跨会话、跨边界状态污染，并在11个模型家族的24个模型上系统评估其传播机制、持久性和防御效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强型LLM智能体可携带隐式状态，这些状态跨会话持续存在、通过事件激活并在智能体边界间传播，而标准调试方法无法察觉，构成严重的安全和可靠性威胁。

Method: 定义PSEs的三个核心机制（名称绑定、事件触发、跨边界传播），在24个模型（1.5B-1T参数）上进行系统性评估，测试不同污染类型的持久性，并评估上下文隔离自验证和基于关键词的检测方法。

Result: 所有20个模型均可被攻击（20-100%）；偏好污染在所有模型上持久存在（t=10时100%），指令污染持久，人格注入部分衰减（90%→10%），事实注入因模型而异；上下文隔离自验证实现20-79%的降低（中位数36.5%）；污染沿四阶段pipeline累积1.9倍（40%→75%）。

Conclusion: 偏好和指令污染具有持久性、缺乏自我纠正且难以被标准监控捕获，对部署的智能体系统构成特别严重的攻击面；现有防御（关键词检测、自验证）效果有限。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents can harbor implicit state that persists across sessions, activates through events, and propagates across agent boundaries---largely invisible to standard debugging. We formalize this as Persistent Semantic Entities (PSEs): constructs defined by name binding, event triggering, and cross-boundary propagation, and evaluate them across 24 models from 11 families (1.5B--1T parameters). First, every tested model is susceptible (20--100% on the 20-model susceptibility panel), with name binding as the necessary and dominant mechanism: without it, contamination is 0%. Second, persistence depends on contamination type rather than scale or deployment: preference contamination persists undecayed on every model probed (100% at t=10) and instruction contamination persists wherever adopted, persona-style injection decays partially (90%$\to$10%), while factual injection is model-dependent---self-corrected on Llama-3.1-8B and GPT-4o-mini but held at ceiling on both Qwen2.5-coder variants, so we do not claim it self-corrects in general. The preference and instruction results hold across providers in our controlled setting. Third, context-isolated self-verification achieves 20--79% reduction (median 36.5%) without oracle references while keyword-based detection produces systematic false positives, and contamination compounds 1.9$\times$ along a four-stage agent pipeline (40%$\to$75%). Preference and instruction contamination---persistent, lacking self-correction, and poorly captured by standard monitoring---represent a particularly concerning attack surface for deployed agent systems.

</details>


### [165] [Constrained Learning with Universally Learnable Concept Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08414)
*Herlock SeyedAbolfazl Rahimi,Spyridon Pougkakiotis,Dionysis Kalogerias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在无限维假设类上的完全非凸设置中，研究约束统计学习问题，建立了对偶算法解的统一PACC可学习性（约束上概率近似正确），同时保证最优性和约束满足。通过引入闭包-实现间隙ε*∞和Tikhonov复杂度T^n_ε，作者证明当ε*∞=0时学习是精确的（近PACC则残差恰好为ε*∞），并证明无分布自由阈值不存在，因此普适性是大假设类上对偶算法的规范框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅建立了近PACC（近约束上概率近似正确）结果，其可行性残差无论多少数据都无法消除，缺乏对无限维假设类上非凸约束学习问题的精确可学习性刻画，需要在最优性与约束满足之间建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 将总体问题置于通用RKHS H_K上（在可分解包络中稠密），在增长半径的范数球上学习；引入Tikhonov复杂度T^n_ε（达到ε-最优拉格朗日水平集所需的最小RKHS范数）并证明其有限性；引入闭包-实现间隙ε*∞刻画H_K通过对偶化检索可行解的能力。最优性由Rademacher复杂度与强拉格朗日对偶性（依赖于Lyapunov凸性与可分解性）协调，样本阈值在源条件下关于1/ε是多项式的显式表达。

Result: 证明Tikhonov复杂度有限，获得最优值的精确可学习性，并在源条件下得到1/ε多项式显式样本阈值。可行性方面，当ε*∞=0（特别是对偶可微情形）学习是精确PACC；否则为残差恰好ε*∞的近PACC。此外证明了即使在无约束特化中也不存在无分布自由阈值，确立了普适性作为大假设类上对偶算法的规范框架。

Conclusion: 本文统一了无限维非凸约束学习中对偶算法的最优性与可行性保证，揭示了问题本身的闭包-实现间隙结构决定了学习的精确性边界；普适性（universality）而非分布自由保证，是无限维大假设类上对偶算法的正确理论框架。

Abstract: We study constrained statistical learning over infinite-dimensional hypothesis classes in the fully nonconvex setting, and establish universal PACC learnability of the solutions of dual algorithms: Probably Approximately Correct on Constraints, guaranteeing optimality and constraint satisfaction at once. This strengthens near-PACC results, whose feasibility residual no amount of data can remove. Optimality is caught between generalization, governed by Rademacher complexity and favoring small classes, and strong Lagrangian duality, which rests on Lyapunov convexity for vector measures and needs decomposability, a demand pulling the other way. We reconcile the two by posing the population problem over a universal RKHS $\mathcal{H}_K$, dense in a decomposable envelope, and learning over norm balls of growing radius. This yields the Tikhonov complexity $\mathfrak{T}^{\varepsilon}_{n}$, the least RKHS norm reaching an $\varepsilon$-optimal Lagrangian level set; we prove it finite, obtain exact learnability of the optimal value, and make the sample threshold explicit and polynomial in $1/\varepsilon$ under a source condition. Feasibility is harder: absent convexity the Lagrangian may not attain its infimum, and dual information pins down only an averaged constraint-risk vector, not the risks of any returned predictor. We introduce the closure-realization gap $\varepsilon^\star_\infty$, an index of how well $\mathcal{H}_K$ retrieves feasible solutions from dualization; it is a property of the problem, not of a modeling choice. Learnability is exact when $\varepsilon^\star_\infty=0$, in particular under dual differentiability, and near-PACC with residual exactly $\varepsilon^\star_\infty$ otherwise. Finally, no distribution-free threshold exists already in the unconstrained specialization, so universality is the canonical frame for dual algorithms over large hypothesis classes.

</details>


### [166] [From Uncertainty to Failure Attribution: Self-Diagnosing Models for Failure Attribution under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07953)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"自诊断模型"(self-diagnosing models)的概念，在分布偏移下不仅能检测OOD样本和预测不确定性，还能识别失败原因（协变量偏移、语义偏移、噪声损坏、对抗扰动四种类型），通过一致性正则化器训练，并在多个带预定义偏移机制的基准上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在分布偏移下仅限于检测OOD样本和预测不确定性标量，无法解释模型为何失效。作者希望从标量不确定性迈向量化失败归因，使模型不仅能检测异常，还能诊断出具体失败类型。

Method: 提出自诊断模型（self-diagnosing models），同时学习预测输出、预测不确定性和失败归因信号（failure attribution vector）。该向量通过神经网络生成，区分四种失败类型：协变量偏移、语义偏移、噪声损坏和对抗扰动。引入一致性正则化器（consistency regularizer），促进不确定性预测与失败归因预测之间的一致性。构建了多个具有预定义偏移生成机制的分布偏移基准用于评估。

Result: 论文提出了一种从标量不确定性向失败识别的范式转变，通过在多个构造的分布偏移基准上进行评估，验证了模型在检测OOD样本的同时归因失败原因的能力。

Conclusion: 通过将失败归因引入分布偏移框架，模型不仅能检测不可靠预测，还能提供结构化的失败原因表示，为模型的鲁棒性和可解释性提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Distribution shift poses a significant challenge to the robustness of machine learning models, but the current solutions only aim to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) samples and predict uncertainty levels. We introduce a problem setting for failure attribution under distribution shift, which enables the models not only to detect OOD samples, but also to find out the reason for their failure. The solution we propose is called self-diagnosing models, which are capable of jointly learning predictive output, predictive uncertainty, and a failure attribution signal. In particular, we use the failure attribution vector, produced by a neural network, which provides a structured representation of predictive unreliability by distinguishing four different types of failures: covariance shift, semantic shift, noise corruption, and adversarial perturbation. In other words, we move from scalar uncertainty towards failure identification. For training the model, we introduce a consistency regularizer that encourages consistency between uncertainty and failure attribution predictions. Moreover, to be able to evaluate the model on its ability to find the reasons for failure, we construct several distribution shift benchmarks with predefined mechanisms for generating distribution shifts.

</details>


### [167] [No Unique Minimizer, No Problem: On the Consistency of Robust Neural Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08489)
*Subhabrata Majumdar,Anand Deo,Partha Pratim Saha,Abhik Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对交叉熵训练的神经网络对标签噪声和对抗污染的敏感性问题，提出了基于S-divergence鲁棒分类器的统计一致性理论。在神经网络的非可辨识参数化假设下，作者证明经验S-divergence最小化器收敛到总体最优等价类，并验证了三种架构在该理论的适用条件，同时在视觉和语言基准上验证了方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉熵训练的神经网络对标签噪声与对抗污染高度敏感。尽管已有鲁棒替代方法提供有界影响和抗腐蚀性，但由于神经网络参数化不可辨识（population loss的最小化器是一个等价类而非唯一参数点），这些方法的统计基础在深度学习场景下不够坚实，缺乏严格的理论保障。

Method: 将训练建模为在不可辨识参数空间上的随机优化问题，基于S-divergence族开发鲁棒分类器的一致性理论。证明在温和的正则条件下经验S-divergence最小化器收敛到总体最优等价类，并验证了三种具体架构的适用条件。进一步证明鲁棒训练算法的极限点是经验目标的平稳点。

Result: 在视觉和语言基准数据集上的实验表明，S-divergence训练在保持干净数据精度的同时，其性能与现有鲁棒方法相当，具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作为鲁棒神经网络分类器提供了无需可辨识性假设的统计一致性理论基础，填补了深度学习鲁棒优化理论分析的空白，并通过实验验证了理论方法的实际有效性。

Abstract: Neural network classifiers trained by cross-entropy minimization are highly sensitive to label noise and adversarial contamination. While robust alternatives offer bounded influence and resistance to corruption, their statistical foundations in the deep learning setting are insufficient due to a fundamental difficulty: neural parameterizations are non-identifiable, so the population loss minimizer is an equivalence class of parameters, not a unique point. We develop a consistency theory for robust neural classifiers based on the S-divergence family that requires no identifiability assumption. Casting training as stochastic optimization over a non-identifiable parameter space, we prove that empirical S-divergence minimizers converge to the population-optimal equivalence class under mild regularity conditions, and verify these conditions for three architecture choices. We further establish that limit points of the robust training algorithm are stationary points of the empirical objective. Experiments on vision and language benchmark datasets confirm that S-divergence training maintains clean-data accuracy while exhibiting performance competitive with existing robust methods.

</details>


### [168] [EasyBalance: Cross-Layer Load Balancing in Distributed MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07964)
*Yize Wu,Ke Gao,Ling Li,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EasyBalance，一种跨层负载均衡策略，通过跨层联合调度MoE工作负载来缓解专家并行分布式推理中的负载不均衡问题，无需修改专家-设备映射，零额外开销，可将GPU空闲时间减少40%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家并行分布式推理中，MoE模型的路由分布通常偏斜，导致负载较轻的专家设备必须空闲等待最重的专家完成计算，造成效率低下。现有基于层内专家复制或迁移的负载均衡方法引入额外开销且灵活性和可扩展性受限。

Method: EasyBalance的核心思想是：(1) 将其他层的专家视为当前层的天然冗余资源；(2) 将跨层的MoE工作负载联合执行以缓解各自的负载不均衡。具体地，采用贪心策略在每个MoE步骤调度一部分跨层工作负载执行，并将剩余工作负载推迟到未来进行平衡，实现跨层不均衡缓解。

Result: 在多个模型、任务和配置上的广泛实验表明，EasyBalance持续加速分布式MoE推理，GPU空闲时间减少大多超过40%。

Conclusion: EasyBalance通过跨层负载均衡策略，在不修改专家-设备映射、无额外开销的情况下，有效解决了MoE分布式推理中的负载不均衡问题，为大规模MoE推理提供了更高效的负载均衡方案。

Abstract: Load Balancing has emerged as a critical problem in expert-parallel distributed inference of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. As routing distributions are typically skewed across experts, devices hosting lighter-loaded experts must idle to wait for the heaviest during expert computing, leading to inefficiency. Existing load-balancing approaches primarily rely on expert replication or migration within each layer, which introduce additional overhead and limit their flexibility and scalability. To address this problem, we propose EasyBalance, a cross-layer load balancing strategy that requires no modifications to the expert-device mapping, enabling instant adaptability and incurring essentially no additional overhead. Our key insights are that (1) experts of other layers can be viewed as naturally redundant for the current layer, and (2) cross-layer MoE workloads can be jointly executed to mitigate their individual imbalance. Based on these observations, EasyBalance greedily schedules a subset of cross-layer workloads to run at each MoE step and defers the remaining workloads for future balancing opportunities, effectively leveraging cross-layer imbalance mitigation. Extensive experiments across models, tasks, and configurations demonstrate that EasyBalance consistently accelerates distributed MoE inference, reducing GPU idling by mostly over 40%. Code is available at https://github.com/yize-wu/EasyInfra.

</details>


### [169] [When Can Fraud Operations Authorize Automation? A Decision-Support Framework for Fresh Audit Evidence and Review Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08577)
*Jie Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"新鲜度约束审计容量"(FCAC)框架，将欺诈操作中的自动化决策视为受行动风险、证据新鲜度和共享复核容量约束的授权问题，通过成熟随机审计和预设时间窗口评估候选动作区域，实现零漂移下的有限样本误授权控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈运营需要在自动批准、分析师复核和自动阻断之间分配事件，但评估这些动作所需的标签是选择性且有延迟的。预测分数可以对案件排序，但无法说明证据是否足够最新且具有代表性以将动作委托给模型，因此需要一个框架来管理这种授权决策。

Method: FCAC框架将自动化视为授权决策，受三类约束：行动风险、证据新鲜度（时效性）和共享复核容量。从成熟随机审计和预设时间允许窗口评估候选动作区域，支持的区域自动化执行，不支持的保留在复核中。在代表性随机审计、标签无关证据窗口以及连接历史与当前行动风险的预设条件下，推导出对不安全授权的同步有限样本控制。

Result: 在IEEE-CIS、ULB-Worldline和Elliptic++三个数据集上的时序模拟审计分别实现84.4%、67.4%和81.3%的零漂移自动化率，总复核工作量分别为24.1%、46.0%和43.1%。实验发现审计容量权衡：稀疏审计延迟授权，过度密集审计最终增加工作量。BAF压力测试表明回退阈值必须反映候选特定证据而非风险限额的固定比例。

Conclusion: 审计新鲜度和分析师容量是欺诈决策支持中的联合设计考量。在没有限制未观测标签演化的情况下，当前行动风险是不可识别的；该框架提供了在指定条件下对这种风险进行有限样本控制的方法。

Abstract: Fraud operations must allocate events among automatic approval, analyst review, and automatic blocking even though the labels needed to evaluate these actions are selective and delayed. Predictive scores order cases, but they do not show whether the evidence is current and representative enough to delegate an action to the model. We develop freshness-constrained audit capacity (FCAC), a decision-support framework that treats automation as an authorization decision constrained by action risk, evidence freshness, and shared review capacity. It evaluates candidate action regions from mature randomized audits and a prespecified temporal allowance. Supported regions are automated; unsupported regions remain in review. The resulting decision record reports evidence age, audit demand, total review workload, value exposure, and compatible temporal change. We show that current action risk is unidentified without restricting unobserved label evolution. Under representative randomized audits, label-independent evidence windows, and a prespecified condition linking historical and current action risk, we derive simultaneous finite-sample control of unsafe authorization. Chronological evaluations with simulated audits on IEEE-CIS, ULB-Worldline, and Elliptic++ yield zero-drift automation rates of 84.4%, 67.4%, and 81.3%, with total review workloads of 24.1%, 46.0%, and 43.1%. The experiments reveal an audit-capacity trade-off: sparse auditing delays authorization, whereas intensive auditing eventually increases workload. A separately specified BAF stress test further indicates that fallback thresholds must reflect candidate-specific evidence rather than a common fraction of the risk limit. These findings identify audit freshness and analyst capacity as joint design considerations for fraud decision support.

</details>


### [170] [ZeroLock: Concurrent Memory-Efficient LLM Training via Modular Update Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07974)
*Wentao Dai,Xuanran Li,Yuxiang Zhang,Ming Tang,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ZeroLock，一种在边缘设备上进行LLM微调的无反向传播（BP-free）算法，通过局部目标构造将模型更新解耦为独立的分块更新，打破BP的更新锁定机制，从而在算法层面提升吞吐量并减少激活存储带来的内存消耗，同时提供理论收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的LLM微调需要适配特定场景数据并保护隐私，但现有基于流水线并行的方法依赖反向传播（BP）训练。BP存在更新锁定的根本性局限，会导致严重的吞吐量瓶颈和内存压力，限制了边缘场景下的微调效率。

Method: ZeroLock通过局部目标构造将模型更新解耦为独立的分块更新，打破BP的更新锁定。论文提供了首个针对此类基于局部目标构造方法在通用模型分块划分下的理论框架，将局部目标映射到全局目标，并证明了ZeroLock的收敛率为O~(1/√T)，与BP仅相差多对数因子。系统实现上整合了早期转发和故障恢复等技术以提升效率和鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实原型系统上的实验表明，与基于BP的基线相比，ZeroLock减少了26.5%的内存占用，并提升了4.9%的吞吐量。

Conclusion: ZeroLock通过无BP的局部目标构造方法有效解决了边缘LLM微调中BP更新锁定带来的吞吐量和内存瓶颈问题，在保持与BP相近的收敛速度的同时显著降低了内存开销、提升了吞吐量，为边缘设备上的高效模型微调提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) fine-tuning at the edge adapts the model to scenario-specific data while preserving privacy. Although existing studies proposed pipeline parallelism to address the limited memory and computing resources of edge devices, they commonly rely on backpropagation (BP) training, which has a fundamental limitation of update locking and could experience severe throughput and memory bottlenecks. In this work, we propose a BP-free algorithm, called ZeroLock, that decouples the model updates into independent chunk updates by local objective construction. It breaks the update locking of BP and hence can improve throughput at the algorithm level and lower memory usage by reducing activation storage. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first theoretical framework for such local objective construction-based approaches under general model chunk division by mapping local objectives to the global objective. We prove that ZeroLock has a convergence rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{T})$, which differs from BP only by polylogarithmic factors. We design a system for ZeroLock and build real-world prototypes, incorporating techniques such as early forwarding and failure recovery for efficient and robust implementation. Experiments on the prototype show that compared to BP-based baselines, ZeroLock reduces the memory by 26.5% and improves throughput by 4.9%.

</details>


### [171] [Evaluator Ensembles Under Reward Hacking: Covariance Geometry and Finite-Search Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08002)
*Fariya Afrin,Ibne Farabi Shihab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过协方差几何刻画了语言模型裁判/奖励模型集成中"评估者误差利用"（evaluator exploitation）这一失败模式，证明共同模式误差（common-mode error）无法从内部裁判分数中识别，并给出了best-of-K搜索中的夸大效应与目标质量遗憾的紧致边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型裁判和奖励模型虽可实现可扩展监督，但有限优化过程会利用评估者的系统性误差而非真正提升响应质量。现有方法无法区分"稳健聚合"与"共享响应相关误差"的情况，需要从理论上刻画这一失败模式的本质。

Method: 利用评估者集成的协方差几何：对校准裁判，集成均值沿全一方向保留共同模式误差，而裁判间分歧仅捕获正交误差。证明共同模式误差不可从内部分数识别；在联合次高斯模型下，推导best-of-K选择夸大与目标质量遗憾的界，推广到条件校准下的可预测自适应搜索；提出有界双锚Bernstein证书用于有限搜索误差与遗憾；并通过120组固定种子高斯压力测试和真实模型审计验证理论。

Result: 搜索项以√log K尺度增长且对高斯投影误差渐近紧致；噪声质量代理会引入人工秩一协方差而不改变分歧；双锚Bernstein证书为有限搜索提供有界保证。实验在120组(J, ρ, K)配置及真实模型审计中验证了理论，同时揭示了在增大搜索压力下基于分歧的诊断方法的局限性。

Conclusion: 分歧（disagreement）不能作为搜索质量可靠性的充分诊断指标：高分歧可能来自稳健聚合，低分歧也可能伴随共享误差持续存在。需要基于协方差几何的证书化方法（如双锚Bernstein证书）来保证有限搜索中的误差与遗憾上界。

Abstract: Language-model judges and reward models enable scalable supervision, but finite optimization can exploit evaluator errors rather than improve response quality. We characterize this failure through the covariance geometry of evaluator ensembles. For calibrated judges, the ensemble mean retains common-mode error along the all-ones direction, whereas cross-judge disagreement captures only orthogonal error. Consequently, disagreement can be high despite robust aggregation, or low while shared response-dependent errors persist. We prove that common-mode error is not identifiable from internal judge scores alone. Under a joint sub-Gaussian model, we bound best-of-K selection overstatement and target-quality regret, extending the guarantees to predictably adaptive search under conditional calibration. The resulting search terms scale as the square root of log K and are asymptotically tight for Gaussian projected errors. We further show that noisy quality proxies introduce artificial rank-one covariance without changing disagreement, and propose a bounded two-anchor Bernstein certificate for finite-search error and regret. Fixed-seed Gaussian stress tests over 120 (J, rho, K) configurations and real-model audits validate the theory while revealing the limits of disagreement-based diagnostics under increasing search pressure.

</details>


### [172] [Online Learning of Scale Parameters in Score-Driven Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09218)
*Fabrizio Lillo,Giulia Livieri,Gianluca Palmari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究得分驱动滤波器中增益参数的在线学习问题。将增益作为决策变量，利用KL散度作为目标函数，通过镜像下降更新实现增益的在线自适应选择，并建立了动态遗憾界。模拟和实证表明，有界镜像增益在股票指数波动率预测中匹配或优于固定增益，避免了无界指数链接的极端波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 得分驱动滤波器（如GAS模型）中的增益参数控制更新幅度，但现有方法通常将其视为固定参数。作者希望将增益作为可学习的决策变量，研究其在线学习性质，从而提升滤波器的适应性和预测性能，特别是在多危机等结构变化的场景中。

Method: 将增益选择建模为条件预测决策问题，以KL散度为损失函数。推导出标量无缩放增益的随机梯度为连续得分的负乘积；aGAS缩放仅重新标定有效步长。单调可微的增益链接函数诱导有界增益域上的镜像下降几何，持久性通过Bregman项将增益拉向参考值。在凸性、紧致性和正则性条件下，为投影和折扣镜像更新建立针对时变、当前信息比较器的动态遗憾界。

Result: 模拟验证了缩放、链接几何、持久性和坐标维传输率的作用。基于股票指数波动率的面板外样本分析表明，有界镜像增益通常匹配或优于固定增益，同时避免了名义上无界指数链接导致的极端尖峰，在多危机市场中改进最为显著。

Conclusion: 增益的在线学习可以显著提升得分驱动滤波器的适应性。有界镜像更新提供了稳健的增益选择框架，在保持预测性能的同时避免极端更新，尤其在市场波动剧烈的环境下表现出优势。

Abstract: Score-driven filters multiply a scaled log-likelihood score by a gain that controls the update magnitude. We treat this gain as a decision variable and study its online learning. Conditional on the current state, observation, score, and scaling rule, each admissible gain induces a reachable next state and a one-step-ahead predictive density: scalar gains govern distance along a line, while diagonal gains govern coordinatewise transmission. Gain selection is therefore a conditional predictive decision problem with a Kullback-Leibler objective. For a scalar unscaled gain, the negative raw product of consecutive scores is the stochastic gradient of this loss; positive aGAS scaling only rescales the effective step. Monotone differentiable gain links induce mirror-descent geometries on bounded gain domains, while persistence yields a Bregman pull towards a reference gain. Under convexity, compactness, and regularity conditions, we establish dynamic-regret bounds for projected and discounted mirror updates relative to time-varying, current-information comparators. Simulations illustrate the roles of scaling, link geometry, persistence, and coordinatewise transmission rates. An out-of-sample panel of equity-index volatilities shows that the bounded mirror gain generally matches or outperforms a constant gain while avoiding the extreme spikes of a nominally unbounded exponential link, with the strongest improvements observed in multi-crisis markets.

</details>


### [173] [Quality-Diversity Stress Tests for Process Reward Models:What Archive Coverage Can and Cannot Certify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08008)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Fariya Afrin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将过程奖励模型（PRM）压力测试形式化为质量-多样性搜索问题（MAP-Elites），通过行为空间分区域记录最严重的正确性翻转编辑，给出可证明的修复认证边界，并在真实PRM上发现并修复了基于池化方式的对抗性漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PRM对中间推理步骤打分，广泛应用于搜索、排序和训练，但优化可能利用这些学习到的代理奖励，在增加奖励的同时将正确推理变成错误推理。当前缺乏系统性地发现和认证这些漏洞的方法。

Method: 将PRM压力测试形式化为质量-多样性搜索问题，使用MAP-Elites算法在每个行为空间区域内保留最严重的正确性翻转编辑，将搜索覆盖与利用覆盖分离。理论上在Lipschitz后修复损失和度量覆盖审计条件下，给出残差上界（档案拟合误差加Lipschitz常数乘以覆盖半径）的认证框架。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-PRM-7B上发现池化依赖的漏洞：均值池化下44个严格利用（最大增益0.294），最小读出下仅1个；RLHFlow值头模型有相同定性效果（最大增益0.005）。预声明的配对LoRA修复协议将利用率从0.148降至0.037再至0.074，最差攻击从0.333降至0.177再至0.212，提升排序AUROC且不损害best-of-4准确率。独立非配对复现确认了结果。

Conclusion: MAP-Elites压力测试框架既能系统性地暴露PRM的对抗性漏洞，又能提供可证明的认证边界；配对LoRA修复协议能有效降低利用率和攻击严重性，同时不损害模型性能，且增益来自对抗性微调而非档案多样性。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) score intermediate reasoning steps and are widely used for search, ranking, and training, but optimization can exploit these learned proxies by increasing reward while turning correct reasoning into incorrect reasoning. We formulate PRM stress testing as a quality-diversity search problem using MAP-Elites, retaining the most severe correctness-flipping edit in each behavior-space region while separating search coverage from exploit coverage. We characterize what such archives certify: finite-cell repair bounds covered-cell tail risk and average residual severity but cannot bound the worst remaining cell from covered fraction alone; under Lipschitz post-repair loss and metric-cover auditing, the residual is bounded by archive fitting error plus the Lipschitz constant times the covering radius. A controlled landscape validates this certificate and the impossibility of any fraction-only worst-case guarantee. On real PRMs, the search reveals an aggregation-dependent vulnerability in Qwen2.5-Math-PRM-7B: padding yields 44 strict exploits with maximum gain 0.294 under mean pooling versus one exploit under minimum readout; a matched syntactic control isolates the mechanism, and an RLHFlow value-head model shows the same qualitative effect with maximum gain 0.005. A predeclared paired LoRA repair protocol reduces exploit rates from 0.148 to 0.037 to 0.074, lowers the worst attack from 0.333 to 0.177 to 0.212, improves ranking AUROC without degrading best-of-4 accuracy, attributes gains to adversarial fine-tuning rather than archive diversity, and is confirmed by independent unpaired replications (44 to 1, clean-split worst gain 0.0092, MATH-500 41 to 0, clean ranking 40/40).

</details>


### [174] [Ground-Truth Neighborhood Regularization for Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08010)
*Jianqi Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Zeen Song,Changwen Zheng,Fanjiang Xu,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.LG
