We then characterize which design choices drive these gains: bank augmentation helps bandit sampling but not random sampling, "Exploratory" prompting that prioritizes new ideas surfaces the rare best-performing solutions, while increasing sampling-level exploration is counterproductive.

</details>


### [241] [Gradient Under Microscope: Benchmarking Resource Utilization of Memory-Efficient Gradient Computation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08961)
*Sarthak Mahapatra,Zihan Zhou,Khatoon Khedri,Mehdi Hosseinzadeh,Reza Rawassizadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在受限硬件条件下，系统比较了五种优化器（SGD、Adam、Adagrad、Adadelta、共轭梯度法）与三种内存策略（标准训练、梯度检查点、梯度累积）在四种Transformer架构（ViT、ModernBERT、Llama 3.1 1B、NanoVLM）上的表现。研究发现梯度累积是最可靠的内存策略，可显著降低训练损失且不增加GPU内存；Adam并非普遍最优，Adadelta和SGD在编码器与自回归架构上表现更佳；梯度检查点的效果高度依赖架构，且对内存受限模型最多可使训练时间增加60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI训练的算力和资源消耗日益加剧，对电力供应和碳排放造成压力，因此需要系统地研究在受限硬件条件下进行内存高效训练的可行方案，为资源受限场景下的模型训练提供实用指导。

Method: 在四种Transformer架构（ViT、ModernBERT、Llama 3.1 1B、NanoVLM）上，交叉组合五种梯度优化器（SGD、Adam、Adagrad、Adadelta、共轭梯度下降法）与三种内存策略（标准训练、梯度检查点、梯度累积），测量训练损失、GPU利用率、训练时间和内存使用量四个指标。

Result: 梯度累积是最可靠的策略，在视觉-语言模型上训练损失约降低一个数量级，在语言模型上约降低四倍，且不增加额外GPU内存；Adadelta和SGD在编码器和自回归架构上优于Adam；梯度检查点对ViT有改善但严重损害编码器模型，并使内存受限模型的训练时间增加高达60%；GPU利用率主要由架构决定，范围从内存受限语言模型的8-15%到计算受限视觉模型的96-99%。

Conclusion: 优化器和梯度策略的选择应因架构而异，Adadelta和SGD等传统优化器在特定架构下可替代普遍使用的Adam，梯度累积是在内存受限条件下最具性价比的训练策略，这些发现为资源高效模型训练和部署提供了实用选择指南。

Abstract: AI training's rising resource intensity is straining electricity supplies and carbon budgets, motivating systematic study of memory-efficient training on constrained hardware. We benchmark five gradient optimizers (SGD, Adam, Adagrad, Adadelta, and Conjugate Gradient Descent) under three memory strategies (standard training, gradient checkpointing, and gradient accumulation) across four transformer architectures (ViT, ModernBERT, Llama 3.1 1B, and NanoVLM), measuring training loss, GPU utilization, training time, and memory usage. Gradient accumulation emerges as the most reliable strategy, cutting training loss by roughly an order of magnitude on the vision-language model and about four-fold on the language model without additional GPU memory. Contrary to common practice, Adam is not universally superior: Adadelta and SGD outperform it on the encoder and autoregressive architectures. Gradient checkpointing's effect is strongly architecture-dependent, improving vision transformer loss while severely degrading the encoder model, and it increases training time by up to 60% on memory-bound models. GPU utilization is governed primarily by architecture, ranging from 8-15% for the memory-bound language model to 96-99% for compute-bound vision models. These findings provide practical guidelines for optimizer and gradient-strategy selection in resource-efficient model training and deployment.

</details>


### [242] [Math-Vision Diagrams: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Mathematical Diagram Generation Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08964)
*Harish Kashyap,Kiran Byadarhaly,Sriram Chakaravarthy,Sanyukta Tuti,Aryan Mistry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Math-Vision Diagrams，这是第一个专门用于评估大语言模型（LLMs）数学图表生成能力的基准测试，统一了文本到代码和文本到图像两种生成范式，涵盖2920个高质量竞赛问题图像，并通过LLM集成与领域专家人工审核相结合的方式构建数据管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学图表生成是LLM尚未充分探索的关键能力，对课程准备、题目自动排序和科学出版具有重要意义。然而，现有基准如MathVision、MathVista专注于数学推理，DiagramGenBenchmark和MermaidSeqBench则针对通用图表生成，缺乏专门标准化评估LLM数学图表生成能力的提示-图像对数据集。

Method: 基于Math-Vision基准，从3040张图像中筛选出2920张来自高质量竞赛题目且包含必要视觉上下文的图像子集。提出了一种结合LLM集成与领域专家（SME）人工审核的数据构建管线，并配套一套评估指标。

Result: 通过测试多个领先模型，实验表明LLMs在数学图表生成任务上表现困难，存在显著不足。

Conclusion: Math-Vision Diagrams是首个统一评估文本到代码与文本到图像两种范式的数学图表生成基准，全面开源代码、数据、构建管线与评测脚本，为后续研究提供了标准化的评估基础设施。

Abstract: The generation of mathematically precise diagrams from tex- tual prompts has emerged as a critical yet underexplored capability of Large Language Models (LLMs). This has been of interest to researchers in the areas of curriculum preparation, automated ranking of problem sets, and scientific publishing. For LLMs to achieve this, it requires per- fect coordination between Spatial Reasoning, Mathematical Reasoning, and Rendering systems. While existing benchmarks such as MathVision, MathVista are built for Math Reasoning or DiagramGenBenchmark, Mer- maidSeqBench on general purpose diagram generation, no prior work provides a standardized set of prompt, image pairs that can be used to evaluate the LLMs specifically on math diagram generation. This includes fields that span both both text-to-code and text-to-image paradigms. We introduce Math-Vision Diagrams, the first benchmark specifically designed to evaluate LLMs on mathematical diagram generation, and the first to assess text-to-code and text-to-image generation paradigms together in a single unified setting, agnostic of the underlying coding lan- guage or model type. Building on the Math-Vision benchmark, we select a subset of 2920 images out of 3040 from high-quality competition problems with essential visual context. A novel pipeline combining an ensemble of LLMs with Subject Matter Expert (SME) curation is presented, together with a suite of evaluation metrics. Testing several leading models against this benchmark, we demonstrate that LLMs struggle with math diagram generation. All code, data, curation pipeline, and evaluation scripts will be fully open-sourced.

</details>


### [243] [Label-Free Parkinson's Disease Screening from Face and Voice through Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08976)
*Jiaheng Su,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全无标签的帕金森病(PD)人脸+语音筛查方法，完全不使用任何PD标签进行训练，仅依赖冻结的预训练编码器（face-expression Vision Transformer和HuBERT）。语音模态通过合成构音障碍的对比激活添加(CAA)方向检测，人脸模态使用k近邻异常分数。在YouTubePD基准上，语音AUROC为0.765，人脸AUROC为0.751，等权晚期融合达到AUROC 0.802（NPV 0.95），支持排除性分诊的临床应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病是第二大常见的神经退行性疾病，但典型机器学习筛查方法需要PD标签训练，而可用数据受隐私问题和专家标注需求限制。作者旨在突破这一瓶颈，构建无需任何PD标签参与拟合的筛查系统。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 使用冻结的预训练编码器（face-expression Vision Transformer用于人脸，HuBERT用于语音）；(2) 语音模态通过时间拉伸和气息声退化合成健康语音，构建合成构音障碍的CAA方向；(3) 人脸模态使用k近邻异常分数评估与控制嵌入簇的距离；(4) 提出对齐原则(alignment principle)——当合成退化方向与真实疾病方向的余弦相似度>0时，CAA检测器才能有效工作；(5) 采用等权晚期融合策略。

Result: 在YouTubePD基准上：语音方向上合成与真实疾病方向余弦为+0.37（CAA有效，AUROC 0.765），人脸方向上余弦为-0.48（CAA失效，改用异常检测成功，AUROC 0.751）。等权晚期融合达到AUROC 0.802（95% CI [0.70,0.89]），NPV为0.95。过度拟合审计显示语音检测器干净迁移，但人脸侧及融合AUROC可能偏乐观，需外部验证。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了纯冻结预训练编码器+零PD标签条件下的多模态PD筛查可行性，提出了对齐原则作为合成退化方法的理论指导，并支持将系统用于规则排除性分诊(out-rule triage)场景。但人脸通道的结果需外部数据验证以确认泛化性。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) is the second most common neurodegenerative disorder. Typical machine learning screening methods require PD labels, but the available data is limited by privacy concerns and the need for expert annotation. We propose a label-free face-plus-voice PD screen built entirely on frozen pretrained encoders--a face-expression Vision Transformer and HuBERT--in which no PD label touches any fit; the reference is training controls only. The voice modality uses a synthetic-dysarthria contrastive activation addition (CAA) direction built from time-stretch and breathy degradation of healthy speech; the face modality uses a k-nearest-neighbor anomaly score to the control embedding cluster. We introduce the alignment principle, a post-hoc analysis showing that a synthetic-degradation CAA detector works when the cosine similarity between the synthetic and real disease directions exceeds zero. Measured on the YouTubePD benchmark, this cosine is +0.37 for voice (CAA works, AUROC 0.765) and -0.48 for face (CAA fails; anomaly succeeds, AUROC 0.751). Equal-weight late fusion reaches AUROC 0.802 (95% CI [0.70,0.89]) with NPV 0.95, supporting a rule-out triage interpretation. An overfitting audit shows the voice detector transfers cleanly, while the face-side--and thus fused--AUROC is potentially optimistic pending external validation.

</details>


### [244] [Twin Rollouts: Noise-Coupled Counterfactual Branching in Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08982)
*Yu Ma,Hongli Shi,Xinran Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在交互式视频世界模型的rollout中进行反事实生成的正式框架——噪声耦合孪生rollout，通过在动作干预点前共享前缀及外生噪声序列，使事实分支的自生成噪声精确已知，从而绕过了Pearl反事实程序中的近似反演问题，并定义了可验证的时空局部性度量和基于模拟器真值的奖励用于后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交互式视频世界模型虽然在动作流条件下自回归生成rollout，但训练和评估几乎完全基于事实预测，缺乏对反事实生成（即在同一轨迹前缀下，若动作不同会发生什么）的系统性研究。而传统的基于编辑的方法面临近似反演的难题，难以保证最小改动原则。

Method: 形式化定义噪声耦合孪生rollout：事实与反事实两个分支共享生成前缀及未来外生噪声序列，仅在干预点后动作流不同。由于事实分支是模型自生成的，其外生噪声可精确获得，使得Pearl反事实框架中的abduction步骤精确成立。同时，噪声耦合将最小改动原则转化为可逐样本验证的属性——定义时空局部性度量，只惩罚干预的因果后代之外的发散。通过从干预点t*分叉模拟器状态，获得真值反事实重渲染，作为可验证奖励进行后训练。

Result: 本文为理论/立场文章，建立了正式框架、度量定义和方法定位，实验为后续工作。

Conclusion: 该框架将反事实生成从需要学习评判器的模糊任务转化为具有可验证真值的严格形式化任务，噪声耦合设计使得精确abduction成为可能，并通过模拟器状态分叉提供可信的反事实奖励信号。实验验证留待后续工作。

Abstract: Interactive video world models generate rollouts autoregressively under an action stream, yet they are trained and evaluated almost exclusively on factual prediction. We study counterfactual generation inside the rollout: given a trajectory the model has itself generated, what would have happened had the actions differed from step t* onward? We formalize noise-coupled twin rollouts --- a factual and a counterfactual branch sharing the generated prefix and the future exogenous noise sequence, diverging only in the action stream at an intervention point. Because the factual branch is self-generated, its exogenous noise is known exactly: the abduction step of Pearl's counterfactual procedure is exact by construction, sidestepping the approximate-inversion problem faced by editing-based pipelines. Noise coupling further turns the minimal-change principle into a per-sample verifiable property: we define a spatiotemporal locality metric that penalizes divergence outside the causal descendants of the intervention, computable against simulator ground truth without a learned judge. Forking the simulator state at t* yields ground-truth counterfactual re-renders, which we use as verifiable rewards for post-training. This note establishes the formal framework, metric definitions, and positioning; experiments are forthcoming.

</details>


### [245] [SoftMCC: An MCC-Brier Calibration Bridge for Threshold-Free Model Selection under Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08984)
*Özkan Canay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftMCC是一个基于后训练概率混淆计数的MCC验证框架，通过MCC校准恒等式和共享池选择协议实现阈值无关的模型选择，在18个设置中取得最佳稳定性（平均秩2.31，Kendall's W=0.659），但对校准敏感且所选模型效用无优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MCC在类别不平衡二分类中依赖阈值化，导致验证排序与阈值选择密切相关。现有方法缺少一个既能保持MCC语义又不受阈值影响的验证指标，以提供稳定且可复现的模型选择。SoftMCC旨在通过概率化的混淆计数和后训练框架解决这一阈值依赖性问题。

Method: SoftMCC采用后训练验证框架，建立在概率值混淆计数上。核心方法包括：(1) 耦合MCC特定的校准恒等式；(2) 采用tie感知的共享池选择协议；(3) 核心分数为协方差归一化的概率-标签关联度量；(4) 对硬预测精确退化为MCC，且受Pearson界约束。通过18个设置、12次去重分组的重复实验进行系统评估，并与AUPRC、MCC@0.5、F1@best等基线比较，同时进行标签置换和温度缩放敏感性分析。

Result: SoftMCC在稳定性上表现最优：最佳稳定性平均秩2.31，最高的平均tie校正Kendall's W（0.659），Friedman检验显著（p=0.007）。Nemenyi事后分析将其与AUPRC和MCC@0.5区分开。但选择模型的效用无优势：6个预设比较中3个在测试MCC上为负差异，仅F1@best通过Holm校正（p=0.014），数据集级检验不显著（p=0.117）。标签置换将平均W降至0.092；温度缩放改变SoftMCC排序（Spearman=0.851），而基于秩和阈值优化的指标不变。

Conclusion: SoftMCC是一种校准敏感的MCC家族模型选择器，在稳定性方面优于现有基线，但在所选模型的实用效用上与现有方法无显著差异。其校准敏感性既是特征也是局限：在完美人群校准下等价于Brier技能分，但偏离该校准区域时无法识别校准误差。该工作为不平衡二分类中的阈值无关MCC验证提供了新的理论框架和经验基准。

Abstract: Model selection for imbalanced binary classification often uses the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC), but thresholding makes validation rankings threshold-dependent. SoftMCC is a post-training MCC validation framework on established probability-valued confusion counts, coupling an MCC-specific calibrated identity with a tie-aware, shared-pool selection protocol. Its core score is a covariance-normalized probability-label association, reduces exactly to MCC for hard predictions, and is Pearson-bounded. Under perfect population calibration it equals the Brier skill score with identical candidate ordering; outside that regime the gap does not identify calibration error. Across 18 settings with 12 duplicate-safe grouped repeats, SoftMCC attains the best stability mean rank (2.31) and highest mean tie-corrected Kendall's W (0.659), with a significant Friedman test (p=0.007); Nemenyi analysis separates it from AUPRC and MCC@0.5, while 14-source-family sensitivity retains only the latter. Selected-model utility shows no advantage. Three of six prespecified comparisons have negative mean test-MCC differences, only F1@best survives Holm correction (p=0.014), and the dataset-level test is not significant (p=0.117). Label permutation lowers mean W to 0.092; temperature scaling shifts SoftMCC rankings (mean Spearman 0.851) whereas rank-based and threshold-optimized metrics remain invariant. SoftMCC is a calibration-sensitive MCC-family selector with bounded stability and utility evidence.

</details>


### [246] [Dynamic Distribution-Aware Uncertainty Tracking in Vision-Language Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09011)
*Ao Zhou,Zhiwei Jiang,Zifeng Cheng,Cong Wang,Shufan Yang,Haoru Chen,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DDA-UQ框架，将后验不确定性量化从静态映射范式转变为动态分布感知过程，通过高斯混合模型建模VLM嵌入空间并在推理时动态响应数据分布变化，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后验不确定性量化方法仅拟合源域的失效模式，忽略了测试分布动态变化的特性，导致在分布漂移场景下可靠性不足。

Method: 提出DDA-UQ框架：训练阶段利用高斯混合模型（GMM）建模VLM嵌入空间并提取分布证据以动态推导不确定性估计；推理阶段设计动态响应机制以适应数据分布的变化。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在多项基准上显著优于现有最先进的不确定性量化方法。

Conclusion: 从静态映射转向动态分布感知是不确定性量化的重要方向，DDA-UQ为VLM在安全关键场景中的可靠性保障提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) aims to measure the reliability of model predictions, serving as a critical safeguard for deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in safety-critical scenarios. Post-hoc approaches are widely adopted due to their lightweight nature, mapping the outputs of VLMs to uncertainty measures through learnable modules or inductive summarization. However, Post-hoc approaches remain inherently confined to fitting the failure patterns of the source domain, ignoring the dynamic nature of test distributions. To address this challenge, we propose a Dynamic Distribution-Aware Uncertainty Quantification framework (DDA-UQ) that shifts the paradigm from static mapping to a dynamic distribution-aware process. During training, we leverage a Gaussian Mixture Model to model the VVLMs'embedding space and extract distributional evidence, thereby dynamically deriving uncertainty estimates. During inference, the design dynamically responds to changes in the data distribution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [247] [HOPPER: Learnable Hop Extraction for Linearized Graph Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09031)
*Isuru Herath,Arin Gopakumar,Sharan Sahu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HOPPER提出了一种可端到端学习的图序列提取器，扩展了线性化图序列模型（LGSM）的框架，通过学习如何提取跳（hop）序列（而非使用固定图算子），再交由现代状态空间模型处理，从而在不增加非线性变换层数的情况下实现长距离信息传播。该方法支持特征条件、结构感知、图和跳自适应等传播机制，同时保持排列等变性，并在ECHO-Synth和LRIM基准上取得SOTA或竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图神经网络通过重复的消息传递层传播信息，导致信息传播距离与非线性变换层数耦合，这会带来优化困难、过度平滑（over-smoothing）、过度压缩（over-squashing）以及长距离信息丢失等问题。已有LGSM通过分离信息深度与处理深度来缓解该问题，但使用固定图算子构建节点传播状态序列，无法根据输入图、节点特征和下游任务自适应地调整传播方式。

Method: HOPPER在LGSM框架基础上引入了一个可学习的序列提取器，负责在将节点传播状态序列送入状态空间模型之前，决定如何提取跳序列。该提取器支持多种传播机制：（1）特征条件传播（feature-conditioned）、（2）结构感知传播（structure-aware）、（3）图和跳自适应传播（graph- and hop-adaptive），并保持排列等变性。标准邻接矩阵和non-backtracking LGSM序列可作为该提取器族的特例。

Result: 在ECHO-Synth基准上HOPPER达到SOTA或竞争性性能；在LRIM物理长距离依赖基准上，调节最大邻域尺寸（即消息回溯取消的结构记忆窗口大小）可优化准确率。实验结果验证了可学习序列提取在长范围图表示学习中的灵活性和有效性。

Conclusion: 可学习的序列提取为长范围图表示学习提供了一种灵活且有效的方法，通过自适应地构造跳序列，HOPPER在不增加深度的情况下突破长距离信息传播瓶颈，同时保持了排列等变性等关键性质。

Abstract: Graph neural networks typically propagate information through repeated message-passing layers, coupling the distance over which information travels with the number of nonlinear transformations applied. This coupling can make deep architectures difficult to optimize and can lead to over-smoothing, over-squashing, and the loss of long-range information. Linearized Graph Sequence Models (LGSMs) address this issue by separating information depth from processing depth and treating the successive propagation states of each node as a sequence. However, existing LGSMs construct these sequences using fixed graph operators, limiting their ability to adapt propagation to the input graph, node features, and downstream task. We introduce HOPPER, an end-to-end learnable extension of LGSM that learns how hop sequences should be extracted before they are processed by a modern state-space model. Our framework supports feature-conditioned, structure-aware, graph- and hop-adaptive propagation mechanisms while preserving permutation equivariance. Standard adjacency-based and non-backtracking LGSM sequences arise as special cases of our proposed extractor family. We show that HOPPER is state-of-the-art or competitive across the ECHO-Synth benchmark, and that varying the maximum neighborhood size of message backtracking cancellation (i.e. structural memory window) can optimize accuracy on the LRIM physics-based long-range dependency benchmark. These results demonstrate that learnable sequence extraction provides a flexible and effective approach to long-range graph representation learning.

</details>


### [248] [F2STNet: Fair and Federated Spectral-Temporal Modeling for Graph Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09082)
*Jiayi Zhang,Jinfeng Xu,Hewei Wang,Siyuan Cen,Haidong Huang,Yiyao Zhan,Zheyu Chen,Jinjiang You,Ai Jian,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出F²STNet联邦预测框架，结合截断图傅里叶特征、轻量对角状态空间时序编码器、图卷积与公平感知联邦聚合（FFA），在PeMS04、HZMetro和KnowAir数据集上取得优于基线的预测精度，并在联邦场景下改善最差客户端性能与客户端间差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图结构数据的时空预测（如交通流量预测、环境监测）面临两个核心挑战：一是去中心化且异质的数据使得序列建模困难；二是联邦协作训练中客户端数据分布不均导致模型公平性差。需要一种既能高效建模时空依赖、又能在联邦框架下处理数据异质性的方法。

Method: F²STNet包含三个关键组件：(1) 频谱分支采用截断图-傅里叶特征来暴露图的频域结构；(2) 轻量级对角状态空间时序编码器以线性复杂度建模长时依赖；(3) Fairness-aware Federated Aggregation (FFA) 通过客户端验证损失和递增公平调度来调整FedAvg的先验聚合权重，实现公平聚合。

Result: 在PeMS04、HZMetro和KnowAir三个数据集上，相对于评估的基线方法取得了更好的预测精度；在PeMS04上的联邦实验中，最差客户端性能指标和客户端分散度指标均得到改善。

Conclusion: F²STNet通过频谱+状态空间混合架构有效建模时空依赖，同时FFA公平聚合机制缓解了联邦学习中数据异质带来的不公平问题，验证了该框架在联邦时空预测任务中的有效性与可行性。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal prediction on graph-structured data is central to traffic forecasting and environmental monitoring, yet decentralized and heterogeneous data complicate both sequence modeling and collaborative training. We propose F$^2$STNet, a federated forecasting framework that combines truncated graph-Fourier features, a lightweight diagonal state-space temporal encoder, graph convolution, and Fairness-aware Federated Aggregation (FFA). The spectral branch exposes graph-frequency structure, while the state-space layer models long temporal dependencies with linear complexity in the sequence length. FFA adjusts the FedAvg prior using client validation losses and an increasing fairness schedule. Experiments on PeMS04, HZMetro, and KnowAir show favorable forecasting accuracy relative to the evaluated baselines; federated experiments on PeMS04 additionally improve worst-client and client-dispersion metrics.

</details>


### [249] [RAVEN: Frozen Random Graph Reservoirs with Physics-Informed Interaction Fingerprints for Protein-Ligand Binding Affinity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09099)
*Qingyang Zou,Jiaye Huang,Hangbo Xie,Jiayue Yin,Youyi Song,Jinfeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAVEN提出了一种利用多头冻结原子图编码器作为随机储备池（reservoir）生成多样化结构投影，结合物理化学相互作用指纹和异构监督回归器融合的蛋白质-配体结合亲和力预测框架，在相似性隔离的PDBbind 2020R1数据集和CASF-2016子集上表现出强预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质-配体结合亲和力的定量预测是结构计算化学的核心任务，但现有数据量有限、实验异质性高、构象依赖性强且对数据集划分敏感，导致可靠预测极具挑战。传统端到端优化的图神经网络在有限数据下泛化能力受限。

Method: RAVEN采用随机化原子视图与集成神经储备池：1) 使用多个独立初始化且完全冻结的原子级图编码器作为多头储备池，无需端到端优化即可生成多样化的结构投影；2) 将图投影与确定性的物理化学相互作用指纹（physicochemical interaction fingerprint）整合；3) 通过异构监督读取器（包括神经网络和基于树的回归器）处理融合特征；4) 通过基于验证集的非负融合策略组合各读取器输出。

Result: 在利用GEMS相似性资源重建的相似性隔离PDBbind 2020R1划分以及受保护的CASF-2016子集上评估，RAVEN展现出强预测性能，验证了冻结多视图图表示、显式物理化学统计量以及异构模型融合的有效性。

Conclusion: 冻结的多视图图表示结合显式物理化学描述符与异构模型融合，为蛋白质-配体结合亲和力预测提供了一种稳健且灵活的框架，避免了端到端图表示优化的需求，同时利用随机储备池扩展结构特征覆盖范围。

Abstract: Quantitative estimation of protein-ligand binding affinity from three-dimensional complex structures is a fundamental task in structure-based computational chemistry and molecular modeling. Reliable prediction remains challenging because available structure-affinity data are limited, experimentally heterogeneous, conformation-dependent, and sensitive to dataset partitioning. RAVEN (Randomized Atomistic Views with Ensemble Neural Reservoirs) utilizes a multihead reservoir of independently initialized and fully frozen atomistic graph encoders to generate diverse structural projections without end-to-end optimization of the graph representation. These projections are integrated with a deterministic physicochemical interaction fingerprint and processed by heterogeneous supervised readers, including neural and tree-based regressors, whose outputs are combined through validation-based nonnegative fusion. The random reservoir expands structural feature coverage across independent encoder realizations, whereas the explicit physicochemical descriptors and heterogeneous readers contribute complementary information and distinct inductive biases. Evaluation on a similarity-isolated PDBbind 2020R1 split reconstructed using GEMS similarity resources, together with the protected CASF-2016 subset, demonstrated strong predictive performance. The results indicate that frozen multi-view graph representations, explicit physicochemical statistics, and heterogeneous model fusion provide a robust and flexible framework for protein-ligand binding-affinity prediction.

</details>


### [250] [Real Data Closes Synthetic-to-Real Gap in Optical Chemical Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09100)
*Yani Guan,Dengpan Dong,Zi Wei,Shuang Luo,Dan Hannah,Yumin Zhang,Kang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了化学结构识别（OCSR）在真实文档上的性能差距问题。通过对21个识别器在混合数据（合成渲染+真实标注）上进行微调，发现标注的真实训练图像是最大的性能改进来源（ACS匹配率从0.15提升至0.46），而视觉塔LoRA的效果因基础模型而异（对Qwen无效，对InternVL3大幅提升）。最佳配置在ACS、CLEF-IP、UOB和USPTO上分别达到0.49、0.65、0.84和0.76的精确匹配率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然OCSR在合成图像上看似已解决（准确率超过91%），但在真实文档上表现很差（低于16%）。作者希望找出性能差距的主要来源，理解不同模型架构和训练策略对真实数据性能的影响，从而指导视觉结构识别系统的设计。

Method: 对21个识别器进行微调实验：(1) 混合使用合成渲染结构和标注的真实专利图、期刊图和手绘图作为训练数据；(2) 改变三个变量：视觉语言模型（VLM）基础模型（Qwen2.5-VL-7B、InternVL3-8B、GLM-4.1V-9B）、真实训练数据比例、视觉塔适配策略（是否使用LoRA）；(3) 在ACS、CLEF-IP、UOB、USPTO四个真实基准上进行评估，包括配对t检验验证统计显著性。

Result: 真实标注数据是最大改进来源：Qwen2.5-VL在无真实数据时ACS精确匹配0.15，9.5%真实数据时0.37，50.2%时0.46。视觉塔LoRA效果因模型而异：对Qwen无帮助（+0.00，p=1.00），对InternVL3大幅提升（+22.8至+34.6个百分点），对GLM-4.1V效果中等（+1.0至+9.6个百分点）。最佳配置达到合成图像0.96，ACS 0.49，CLEF-IP 0.65，UOB 0.84，USPTO 0.76的精确匹配。不同基础模型间的差距随真实数据比例增加而缩小（从0.21降至0.06），且模型排名会发生变化。

Conclusion: 模型与适配策略的选择应基于目标任务进行评估，而不能仅凭合成基准或单一模型的表现做决定。基础模型和真实数据混合比例必须一起选择，因为两者的交互决定了最终性能。这一结论也扩展到了化学之外的领域（手写图像到LaTeX识别、图表转换任务）。

Abstract: Millions of chemical structures appear in patents and papers only as drawings, and using that information at scale requires reading the drawings. OCSR appears nearly solved on synthetic images yet remains difficult on real documents: the starting recognizer, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, exceeds 91% accuracy on synthetic renders but falls below 16% on three real-world benchmarks (ACS, CLEF-IP, USPTO). To identify the main source of improvement, 21 recognizers were fine-tuned on mixtures of synthetically rendered structures and labeled real depictions from patents, journal figures, and hand-drawn collections, varying the vision language model (VLM) base, the fraction of real training data, and the vision-tower adaptation strategy. Labeled real training images make the largest difference. For Qwen2.5-VL, ACS exact match rises from 0.15 with no real data to 0.37 at 9.5% and 0.46 at 50.2%; a controlled experiment across three base models reproduces the trend. A vision-tower LoRA, in contrast, does nothing for Qwen (+0.00, paired p=1.00), substantially helps InternVL3-8B (+22.8 to +34.6 pt), and modestly helps GLM-4.1V-9B (+1.0 to +9.6 pt), so its value depends on the base model. The best configuration reaches 0.96 exact match on clean renders and 0.49, 0.65, 0.84, and 0.76 on ACS, CLEF-IP, UOB, and USPTO, respectively. Gaps between base models are largest without real data (0.21), shrink to 0.06 at 70% real data, and reorder the ranking; base model and real-data mixture must therefore be selected together. Small-scale experiments on handwritten image-to-LaTeX recognition and chart-to-table conversion show that base-model rankings also vary beyond chemistry. More generally, model and adaptation choices for visual structure recognition should be evaluated on the target task.

</details>


### [251] [A Probabilistic Circuit-Induced Pseudo-Metric for Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09117)
*Bhumika K,Vidhya S,Narayanan C Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层次似然向量（HLV）与层次似然距离（HLD），利用概率电路（PC）内部节点的层次化概率摘要进行无监督OOD检测，无需保留分布内数据，可精确计算假设检验所需量并近似解析决策阈值，在表格和MNIST数据集上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于概率电路的OOD检测方法仅利用根节点处的标量似然（或其不确定性），忽略了PC内部节点编码的层次化概率摘要信息，未能充分利用电路结构所蕴含的分布信息。

Method: 提出HLV表示（选取PC内部节点的似然作为向量分量）和HLD伪度量（通过HLV的期望比较概率分布）；证明HLD是PC自然诱导函数类上的积分概率度量；基于此构建原则性的拟合优度假设检验用于无监督OOD检测；所有检验所需量均可直接从训练好的电路中精确计算，得到近似解析决策阈值。

Result: 在表格数据集和MNIST数据集上的实验表明，利用PC编码的层次化概率摘要比基于根似然、不确定性、典型性和核方法的基线有更好的OOD检测性能，并能自然地将分布偏移定位到导致偏移的PC节点上。

Conclusion: 利用概率电路内部的层次化概率摘要可以显著提升OOD检测性能，且训练好的PC本身即可作为分布内数据的表示，部署时无需保留分布内数据。

Abstract: Probabilistic Circuits (PCs) are tractable generative models whose internal nodes encode a hierarchy of probabilistic sum- maries over different variable scopes. Existing PC-based out- of-distribution (OOD) detection methods ignore this hierar- chy, reducing the entire circuit to the scalar likelihood (or its uncertainty) computed at the root. We introduce Hierar- chical Likelihood Vector (HLV), a representation whose en- tries are the likelihoods associated with selected PC nodes and define the Hierarchical Likelihood Distance (HLD), a PC-induced pseudo-metric that compares the probability dis- tributions through the expectations of their HLVs. We show that HLD is an integral probability metric over a function class naturally induced by the PC and develop a principled goodness-of-fit hypothesis test for unsupervised OOD detec- tion. Unlike existing approaches, the trained PC alone serves as the representation of the in-distribution: no held-out in- distribution data are required at deployment. We further show that the quantities required by the hypothesis test can be com- puted exactly, directly from the trained circuit, yielding an ap- proximate analytic decision threshold. Experiments on tabular and MNIST datasets demonstrate that exploiting the hierarchi- cal probabilistic summaries encoded through the PC improve OOD detection over root-likelihood, uncertainty-, typicality- and kernel-based baselines, while naturally localizing distri- bution shifts to the PC nodes responsible for the shift.

</details>


### [252] [MARA: Flow-Matching-Guided Multi-Agent Resource Allocation for Computational Resource Efficient Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09130)
*Hanye Zhao,Muning Wen,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARA框架，在离散计算节点上为具有截止时间和未知训练需求的并发学习任务分配有限计算资源。MARA结合条件流匹配预测损失轨迹与多智能体自回归策略协调节点，通过基于势能的进度奖励提供中间训练反馈。实验表明MARA在多种工作量下均优于基线LARA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将计算视为连续可分的吞吐量，而实际场景中计算由离散节点提供，任务随时间到达且训练需求未知。这种离散节点设置同时引入需求不确定性（训练所需计算量未知）和约束性顺序决策（节点数量有限、任务需在截止时间前达到目标损失），现有方法难以应对，需要新的解决方案。

Method: MARA包含两个核心组件：(1) 条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching）用于预测未来损失轨迹，从而估计任务的剩余资源需求；(2) 协同多智能体自回归策略（Cooperative Multi-Agent Autoregressive Policy）用于协调多个计算节点的调度决策。训练采用基于势能的进度奖励（Potential-Based Progress Reward），在提供中间训练反馈的同时保持无折扣的任务完成目标的优化不变性。

Result: 在分布内、强化学习和视觉任务工作量上，流匹配相比加权最小二乘显著降低了剩余资源预测误差。在调度器训练负载下，MARA平均完成63.46%的任务，比强基线LARA高出8.54个百分点；在未见过的更重负载下，MARA仍保持领先优势。

Conclusion: MARA验证了在离散计算节点约束下，将生成式轨迹预测与多智能体强化学习相结合可以有效提升有限计算资源的调度效率，且具备一定的泛化能力以应对训练时未见的更高负载场景。

Abstract: Allocating limited computation among concurrent learning tasks is difficult when each task must reach a target loss before a deadline but its required training effort is unknown. Existing approaches combine online loss prediction with adaptive resource allocation, yet commonly treat computation as continuously divisible throughput. We instead study a practical setting in which tasks arrive over time and computation is provided by discrete nodes. This setting introduces both uncertain demand and constrained sequential decisions. We propose MARA, which predicts future loss trajectories with conditional flow matching and coordinates compute nodes through a cooperative multi-agent autoregressive policy. A potential-based progress reward supplies intermediate training feedback while preserving the undiscounted task-completion objective. Across in-distribution, reinforcement-learning, and vision workloads, flow matching reduces remaining-resource prediction error relative to weighted least squares. At the scheduler's training load, MARA completes 63.46% of tasks on average, 8.54 percentage points above strong baseline Learning with Adaptive Resource Allocation (LARA), and remains ahead under unseen heavier workloads.

</details>


### [253] [SwiftQK: Fast and Communication-Efficient Tensor Parallelism for Query-Key Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09160)
*Gyudong Kim,Wonjun Han,Young Geun Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SwiftQK是一种多GPU RMSNorm内核，通过仅交换标量归一化统计量并采用无死锁的持久化内核将点对点规约与独立的逐元素计算重叠，从而消除张量并行下QK-Norm引入的额外跨GPU通信。在最新LLM上，QK-Norm延迟降低81.4-93.9%，端到端服务中TPOT平均降低29.5%（相对于全向量All-Gather基线）和14.3%（相对于优化的标量聚合实现）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Query-Key Normalization (QK-Norm) 能提升现代大语言模型的训练稳定性和质量，但在张量并行（TP）设置下，由于归一化因子依赖于完整的隐藏向量，逐层的QK-Norm需要在GPU间引入额外的跨通信，造成性能开销。

Method: 提出SwiftQK，一个多GPU RMSNorm内核。其核心设计包括：(1) 仅交换标量归一化统计量（而非完整向量），大幅减少通信量；(2) 采用无死锁的持久化内核，将剩余的Peer-to-Peer归约操作与独立的逐元素计算相重叠，隐藏通信延迟。

Result: 在最近的LLM上，SwiftQK相比使用全向量All-Gather的标准TP QK-Norm，延迟降低81.4%-93.9%。端到端服务测试中，相比All-Gather基线的TPOT平均降低29.5%，相比优化的标量聚合实现平均降低14.3%。

Conclusion: SwiftQK通过精巧的通信最小化和计算-通信重叠策略，有效解决了张量并行下QK-Norm的跨GPU通信开销问题，显著提升了LLM在张量并行场景下的推理性能。

Abstract: Query-Key Normalization (QK-Norm) improves the training stability and quality of modern Large Language Models (LLMs). However, under Tensor Parallelism (TP), layerwise QK-Norm introduces additional cross-GPU communication because the normalization factor depends on the full hidden vector. We present SwiftQK, a multi-GPU RMSNorm kernel that exchanges only scalar normalization statistics and overlaps the remaining Peer-to-Peer reduction with independent element-wise computation in a deadlock-safe persistent kernel. Evaluations on recent LLMs show that SwiftQK reduces QK-Norm latency by 81.4--93.9% relative to the standard TP QK-Norm using full-vector All-Gather. In end-to-end serving, SwiftQK reduces TPOT on average by 29.5% over the All-Gather-based baseline and by 14.3% over an optimized scalar-aggregation implementation.

</details>


### [254] [Tabular Numeric Stretch Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09162)
*Zihao Ye,Juyong Kim,Johnna Sundberg,Burak Varici,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出stretch变换框架，将数值特征预处理形式化为优化问题以提升目标函数的平滑性和可学习性，包含无监督和有监督两种变体，在38个TALENT基准数据集上全面超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据的数值特征具有异质性（分布、尺度、统计性质各异），虽然深度学习在表格数据学习上已有进展，但数值特征如何转换为模型友好的表示这一问题仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出stretch变换框架，将数值特征预处理建模为优化问题以提升目标函数平滑性。包含两个变体：(1) 无监督stretch——通过极小极大优化均匀重新分布特征密度；(2) 有监督stretch——通过最小化变换空间中目标函数的Dirichlet能量，从目标函数平滑性角度优化目标感知的数值特征变换。理论分析将该框架与多种流行变换建立联系（与分段线性编码的几何关联、经验CDF变换的渐近等价、目标编码的细分箱极限关联）。

Result: 在TALENT基准的38个数据集上进行全面实验，有监督stretch持续优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: 显式优化目标函数平滑性是表格深度学习中一种强大但未充分探索的策略，stretch变换框架为此提供了系统性方法。

Abstract: Tabular data presents unique challenges for deep learning due to its heterogeneous nature, where numeric features exhibit diverse distributions, scales, and statistical properties. Although recent advances have improved how models learn from tabular data, how numeric data are transformed into model-friendly representations remains comparatively underexplored. We introduce the stretch transformation framework, which formulates numeric feature preprocessing as an optimization problem to make the target function smoother and thus more learnable. Our framework has two variants: (1) unsupervised stretch, which uniformly redistributes feature density via minimax optimization, and (2) supervised stretch, which optimizes target-aware numeric feature transformations from the perspective of target-function smoothness by minimizing the target function's Dirichlet energy in the transformed space. Our theoretical analysis further connects this framework to several popular transformations: unsupervised stretch is closely related to Piecewise Linear Encoding through a shared piecewise-linear geometry and approaches the empirical CDF transformation as the number of bins grows, while supervised stretch becomes closely related to target encoding in the fine-binning limit. Comprehensive experiments on 38 datasets from the TALENT benchmark demonstrate that supervised stretch consistently outperforms all baselines. These results show that explicitly optimizing for target function smoothness is a powerful and underexplored strategy for tabular deep learning.

</details>


### [255] [A Time-Frequency Dual-Domain Multi-Scale Convolutional Neural Network for Bearing Fault Diagnosis under Strong Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09174)
*Yanxi Ding,Tingyue Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种时频双域多尺度卷积神经网络，通过时域多尺度卷积核和频域FFT特征提取，在强噪声下提升轴承故障诊断精度，CWRU数据集上干净条件下达99.75%，-4dB SNR下保持92.50%，比单域基线提升7.25个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴承故障诊断在强噪声条件下精度显著下降，现有方法难以在噪声环境下保持稳定的特征提取能力，亟需一种对噪声鲁棒的诊断模型。

Method: 设计双分支架构：时域分支使用三个并行卷积核提取多尺度冲击特征；频域分支通过快速傅里叶变换提取抗噪的频谱结构信息；将两分支特征融合后进行故障分类，模型参数仅110,122个。

Result: 在CWRU数据集7个SNR水平下，干净条件准确率99.75%，-4dB SNR下达92.50%，较单域基线提升7.25个百分点，且强噪声下增益单调递增；消融实验验证双分支各自贡献；对比WDCNN、DRSN-CW、MCNN、1D-LeNet均表现最优。

Conclusion: 时频双域多尺度CNN通过综合利用时域冲击特征与频域谱结构信息，在强噪声条件下显著优于单域方法及现有先进模型，证明了双域特征融合对噪声鲁棒性的有效提升。

Abstract: To address the degradation of bearing fault diagnosis accuracy under strong noise, this paper proposes a time-frequency dual-domain multi-scale convolutional neural network. The time-domain branch employs three parallel convolutional kernels to capture multi-scale impulse features, while the frequency-domain branch applies the Fast Fourier Transform to extract noise-robust spectral structure information. Features from both branches are fused for fault classification, yielding a compact model of 110,122 parameters. Experiments on the CWRU bearing dataset across seven signal-to-noise ratio levels demonstrate that the proposed method achieves 99.75% accuracy under clean conditions and maintains 92.50% at -4 dB SNR, representing a 7.25 percentage-point improvement over the single-domain baseline with monotonically increasing gains under stronger noise. Ablation experiments validate the independent performance contributions of the time-domain multi-scale branch and the frequency-domain branch. Comparative experiments against WDCNN, DRSN-CW, MCNN, and 1D-LeNet confirm the superiority of the proposed method under strong noise conditions.

</details>


### [256] [FedA2L: Adaptive layer-wise learning rate adjustment in decentralized federated learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09208)
*Van Truong Vo,Khoa Nguyen,Taehong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedA2L，一种基于模型发散信号动态调整逐层学习率的去中心化联邦学习（DFL）优化方法，在不增加通信或协调开销的前提下，通过平衡基础层的网络共识维持与专精层的本地数据适配来解决数据异构下的梯度冲突问题，实现最高4.94倍的收敛加速和最多59%的通信轮次削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习在异构设备和有限协调条件下越来越重要，但标准的统一学习率忽略了不同网络层（基础层负责网络共识，专精层适配本地数据）的差异化优化需求，导致非独立同分布（non-IID）数据下梯度冲突和收敛效率低下。

Method: FedA2L根据模型发散信号动态调整逐层学习率，利用本地更新强度和网络共识约束来协同优化。该方法无需额外通信或协调，可无缝集成到现有DFL协议中。

Result: 在多种DFL算法、模型架构和数据集上的大量评估表明，FedA2L相比vanilla DFL实现最高4.94倍收敛加速，相比基于调度器的基线减少最多59%的通信轮次；对严重数据异构、更大网络规模和稀疏拓扑展现出良好的鲁棒性，并显著降低通信开销。

Conclusion: FedA2L是一种适用于资源受限或大规模分布式学习（边缘计算和物联网部署）场景的通用优化工具，代码已开源。

Abstract: Decentralized intelligence systems with heterogeneous devices and limited coordination increasingly rely on decentralized federated learning (DFL). However, DFL suffers from convergence inefficiency under data heterogeneity due to the use of a uniform learning rate (LR) that ignores layer-specific optimization needs. Foundational layers are responsible for maintaining network consensus, while specialized layers adapt to local data characteristics, leading to conflicting gradients and degraded performance under non-IID conditions. To address this fundamental tension, this work introduces FedA2L, a method that dynamically adjusts layer-wise LRs based on model divergence signals. By leveraging local update intensity and network consensus constraints, FedA2L seamlessly integrates into existing DFL protocols without additional communication or coordination. Extensive evaluations across DFL algorithms, various model architectures, and datasets demonstrate that FedA2L achieves up to 4.94 times faster convergence than vanilla DFL and reduces communication rounds by up to 59% compared to scheduler-based baselines. Furthermore, FedA2L exhibits resilience to severe data heterogeneity, larger network sizes, and sparse topologies, reducing communication overhead and establishing it as a versatile optimization tool for resource-constrained or large-scale distributed learning in edge and IoT deployments. The code is released at https://github.com/nclabteam/FedA2L.

</details>


### [257] [Beyond Solvability: Task Learnability as a Static Prior for LLM RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09217)
*Ting Zhou,Zhenqing Ling,Daoyuan Chen,Qianli Shen,Yilun Huang,Ying Shen,Yaliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajVal，一种基于轨迹的轻量级任务可学习性估计器，用于在强化学习后训练阶段优化任务采样策略。它通过短探测运行和两个端点评估来近似任务的预期正向优化响应，弥补了现有快照式任务评估方法（如当前通过率）忽略任务对训练敏感度差异的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练中采用统一任务采样，忽略了不同任务对优化响应的差异。已有的任务评估方法（如当前通过率或奖励）仅提供快照式信号，估计的是任务在当前策略下的可解性，但相似可解性的任务在继续训练时的正向响应可能差异巨大。需要一个能够预测任务对训练"可学习性"的度量来指导数据高效采样。

Method: 1) 定义"任务可学习性"为固定RL后训练机制下任务对持续训练的预期正向响应的状态条件度量；2) 通过分析逐任务奖励轨迹，验证可学习性在不同独立采样训练环境中的可复现性及对下游效用的预测能力；3) 提出TrajVal——一种轻量级探针式估计器，仅需一次短探测运行和两个端点评估即可估计逐任务可学习性；4) TrajVal既可独立作为静态先验用于任务采样，也可作为乘性先验整合进现有在线调度器。

Result: 在数学与逻辑推理基准上的多模型规模实验中，TrajVal相对于均匀采样提升了数据效率，并且与在线调度方法结合时能提供互补性增益。

Conclusion: 任务可学习性是比当前可解性更丰富的任务价值维度，TrajVal提供了一种务实、轻量的方式在训练开始前估计该信号，从而在不增加显著开销的前提下提升RL后训练的数据效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central post-training paradigm for eliciting reasoning capabilities in large language models, yet uniform task sampling allocates compute without regard to differences in how tasks respond to optimization. Existing task-valuation methods mostly rely on snapshot-based signals such as current pass rate or reward, which estimate how solvable a task is under the current policy. However, tasks with similar current solvability can still differ substantially in how positively they respond to further training. We study this residual axis as task learnability: a regime-conditional measure of expected positive response to continued training under a fixed RL post-training regime. By analyzing per-task reward trajectories, we find that learnability is reproducible across independently sampled training contexts and predictive of downstream utility. To make this signal practical before training begins, we propose TrajVal, a lightweight probe-based estimator that approximates per-task learnability from a short probe run and two endpoint evaluations. TrajVal can be used either as a standalone static prior for task sampling or as a multiplicative prior for existing online schedulers. Experiments on mathematical and logical reasoning benchmarks across multiple model scales show that TrajVal improves data efficiency over uniform sampling and provides complementary gains when combined with online scheduling methods.

</details>


### [258] [FedTVD: Balancing Data Quality and Quantity for Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09221)
*Radwan Selo,Majid Kundroo,Taehong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedTVD，一种新的联邦学习算法，通过结合数据质量（使用总变差距离TVD衡量客户端标签分布与均匀分布的偏差）和数据数量（数据集大小）双重权重机制，在聚合时对客户端贡献进行加权，从而有效缓解数据异构性带来的模型偏置问题，实验证明其在FMNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中数据异构性（标签分布偏移和数据集大小差异）会导致模型更新偏置并阻碍收敛，传统的FedAvg仅基于数据集大小进行客户端加权，无法有效处理标签分布严重倾斜的客户端对全局模型的负面影响。

Method: 提出FedTVD算法，在客户端聚合时采用双重加权机制：(1) 引入总变差距离(TVD)衡量每个客户端本地标签分布与均匀全局分布之间的散度，标签分布越倾斜的客户端获得越低权重；(2) 同时纳入数据集大小因素以保证可扩展性和公平性。通过这两个维度的结合，实现更稳定和泛化的全局模型。

Result: 在FMNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100三个数据集及所有异构性等级下均一致优于最先进方法。在CIFAR-10高倾斜数据场景下相比FedAvg提升高达10.6%，在中等异构和IID设置下也保持最优性能。

Conclusion: FedTVD通过兼顾数据质量和数量的双重加权机制，有效缓解了联邦学习中数据不平衡的影响，在多种数据异构场景下均表现出稳定的优越性，是一种实用的联邦学习改进方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed client devices while preserving data privacy. However, FL faces significant challenges due to data heterogeneity, particularly in terms of label distribution skewness and variations in dataset sizes, which can lead to biased model updates and hinder convergence. To address this, we propose FedTVD, a novel FL algorithm that weights client contributions during aggregation by considering both data quality and quantity. Unlike traditional FL approaches such as FedAvg, which rely solely on dataset size for client weighting, FedTVD integrates Total Variation Distance (TVD) to measure the divergence between each client's local label distribution and a uniform global distribution. Clients with highly skewed distributions receive lower weights, preventing unbalanced datasets with imbalances from disproportionately influencing the global model. At the same time, dataset size is incorporated to ensure scalability and fairness. This dual-weighting mechanism effectively mitigates the impact of data imbalance, leading to more stable and generalized global models. Experimental results show that FedTVD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all datasets (FMNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100) and all levels of data heterogeneity. Notably, it achieves up to 10.6% improvement over FedAvg on CIFAR-10 under highly skewed data, while maintaining top performance even under moderate and IID settings.

</details>


### [259] [Privileged Solutions or Context-Induced Teacher Behavior? Dissecting On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09228)
*Yuki Ichihara,Naoto Iwase,Mohammad Atif Quamar,Junpei Komiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过提出OP²SD方法，分解了在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中参考解法的两个效应：揭示答案本身与改变教师提供token级监督的上下文。实验发现，即使替换为其他问题的参考解，OP²SD仍能与OPSD性能相当，说明OPSD的收益并非来自目标实例的参考解，而主要源于教师上下文行为的改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD通常被解释为特权信息转移（教师看到参考答案并监督学生），但这种解释混淆了两个效应：参考解的答案信息本身，以及参考解改变教师逐token监督上下文的效应。作者希望厘清目标特异性特权信息在OPSD中的实际作用。

Method: 提出OP²SD（On-Policy Self-Distillation from Other Problems），将配对的目标参考解替换为来自其他示例的问题与解答，同时保持学生rollout、教师和蒸馏目标不变。在三个模型和三个数学基准上进行实验。

Result: OP²SD在所有设置下均优于基础模型，并与OPSD性能相当，表明目标参考解并非OPSD收益的必要条件。

Conclusion: OPSD的收益不一定来自对参考解的访问，教师上下文诱导的行为是更重要的因素。这一发现促使重新审视OPSD及其类似方法的设计原理和理解框架。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) is commonly interpreted as the transfer of privileged information: a teacher observes the verified solution to the target problem and supervises the student's trajectory. However, this interpretation conflates two effects. The reference solution not only reveals the answer to the current instance but also changes the context under which the teacher provides token-level supervision. We investigate the role of target-specific privilege with $\mathrm{OP}^{2}\mathrm{SD}$ (On-Policy Self-Distillation from Other Problems), which replaces the paired reference with a problem and solution from a different example, while preserving the student rollout, teacher, and distillation objective. Across three models and three mathematics benchmarks, $\mathrm{OP}^{2}\mathrm{SD}$ improves over the base model, remains competitive with OPSD. The success of $\mathrm{OP}^{2}\mathrm{SD}$ implies that OPSD gains do not necessarily come from access to the reference solution, and that the teacher's context-induced behavior is an important factor.

</details>


### [260] [DreOPD: Degraded-Reference Extrapolative On-Policy Distillation for Flow-matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09233)
*Mingfeng Lin,Chengfei Cai,Lin Xu,Yuxiang Wei,Liang Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DreOPD是一种面向流匹配模型的降级参考外推式在线策略蒸馏方法，将隐式奖励外推转化为闭式速度回归，结合轻度降级参考增强教学对比度，在单教师和多教师场景下均优于OPD和多任务RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型在生成领域已成为主流，但其适应下游任务通常依赖后训练，可能引发任务间目标冲突；强化学习虽能直接优化任务奖励，但轨迹级优化存在高方差梯度和跨任务干扰问题；现有在线策略蒸馏虽提供密集稳定监督，但传统教师匹配仍是模仿式学习，缺乏外推能力。

Method: DreOPD将隐式奖励外推转化为闭式速度回归，使外推式后训练兼具OPD的稳定性；同时引入轻度降级参考（mildly degraded reference）来强化教师参考的对比度，从而获得更清晰的外推方向。

Result: 在单教师和多教师设置下的实验表明，DreOPD在平均性能上优于OPD和多任务RL基线，并在大多数指标上超越专门化的教师模型。

Conclusion: DreOPD成功地桥接了RL和OPD两种范式，既实现了外推式优化，又保持了训练稳定性，为流匹配模型的下游适配提供了一种高效且性能优越的方案。

Abstract: Flow-matching models are now a mainstream method to image generation, but its adaptation to diverse downstream scenarios typically relies on post-training, which may cause conflicts among task-specific optimization objectives. Reinforcement learning enables direct optimization of task-specific rewards beyond the original models, yet trajectory-level optimization may incur high-variance gradients and cross-task interference. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers dense and stable supervision on student rollouts, but conventional teacher matching remains imitation-based. We propose DreOPD, a Degraded-reference extrapolative OPD method for flow-matching models that bridges these two paradigms. Our DreOPD converts implicit reward extrapolation into closed-form velocity regression, enabling extrapolative post-training with the stability of OPD. It further uses a mildly degraded reference to strengthen the teacher-reference contrast, yielding a clearer extrapolation direction. Experiments on single- and multi-teacher settings show that DreOPD outperforms OPD and multi-task RL baselines in average performance, while surpassing specialized teachers on most metrics.

</details>


### [261] [Label Granularity Skew in Federated Learning with Hierarchical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09236)
*Jaeheon Kim,Hokeun Kim,Bong Jun Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出标签粒度偏斜（label granularity skew）这一联邦分层分类中的新型统计异质性，并基于条件softmax分类器的鲁棒性洞察，提出分支解耦微调（BDFT）及其联邦版本FedBDFT，在CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet和ImageNet上大幅提升严重标签粒度偏斜下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端不仅存在数据分布差异，还可能在领域知识和标注能力上不同。现有研究未考虑客户端在共享类层级中提供不同详细程度标签的场景，即标签粒度偏斜。这种新的统计异质性会显著影响分层分类模型的性能，需要专门的建模与训练方法。

Method: 首先通过概率关系邻居分类器生成客户端特定的局部标签层级，并利用基于轮廓系数的粗化构建WordNet引导的共享类层级。分析发现强耦合的分层模型对不完整监督敏感，而条件softmax分类器更鲁棒。据此提出BDFT，对分支级分类器进行微调；进一步提出FedBDFT，通过联邦优化实现分支分类器的聚合。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet和ImageNet上的实验表明，FedBDFT在严重标签粒度偏斜下显著提升鲁棒性，在偏斜度0.6和0.9时平均分别提升27.9%和56.4%。零样本结果进一步表明FedBDFT能更好地保留未见细粒度类别的分层表示。

Conclusion: FedBDFT有效应对异构标签粒度下的联邦分层分类任务，验证了分支解耦策略在标签粒度偏斜场景下的优越性，为联邦学习在真实异构标注环境中的应用提供了新的方法。

Abstract: Federated learning enables privacy-preserving collaboration across distributed devices without centralizing local data. However, clients may differ not only in data distributions but also in domain knowledge and annotation capabilities. In this paper, we introduce label granularity skew, a new form of statistical heterogeneity in federated hierarchical classification, in which clients provide taxonomy-consistent labels at different levels of detail within a shared class hierarchy. To model this heterogeneity, we generate client-specific local label hierarchies using a probabilistic relational neighbor classifier and construct a WordNet-guided hierarchy via silhouette score-based coarsening. Our analysis shows that strongly coupled hierarchical models are sensitive to incomplete supervision, while the conditional softmax classifier is more robust. Based on this insight, we propose Branch-wise Decoupled Fine-Tuning (BDFT) and its federated version, FedBDFT, which fine-tune branch-wise classifiers and aggregate them through federated optimization. Experiments on CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and ImageNet show that FedBDFT substantially improves robustness under severe label granularity skew, with average gains of 27.9% and 56.4% at skewness levels of 0.6 and 0.9, respectively. Zero-shot results further indicate that FedBDFT better preserves hierarchical representations for unseen fine-grained classes. These findings demonstrate its effectiveness for federated hierarchical classification with heterogeneous label granularities.

</details>


### [262] [Multimodal Federated Learning under Dual-Axis Modality Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09240)
*Adiba Orzikulova,Jaehyun Kwak,Jaemin Shin,Yunqi Guo,Xiaomin Ouyang,Guoliang Xing,Steven Euijong Whang,Sung-Ju Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flux是一个多模态联邦学习框架，专门解决双重轴模态缺失问题（不同客户端拥有不同模态集，且单个样本可能仅包含本地可用模态的子集）。通过"模态感知置信度调节"和"梯度解耦的私有适配"两个互补组件，Flux在四个多模态数据集上取得了最高的平均宏F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态联邦学习在隐私敏感的医疗和健康感知场景中广泛应用，但现实部署中普遍存在"双重轴模态缺失"问题：一方面不同客户端拥有的模态集合不同，另一方面单个训练样本可能只包含客户端本地可用模态的子集。现有方法通常将这两个轴分开处理，缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: Flux框架由两个互补组件构成：(1) 模态感知置信度调节——通过掩码感知的单模态监督学习每个模态的样本级置信度，并将观测模态的置信度估计融合为样本自适应的温度，该温度根据证据质量和完整性调整预测锐度；(2) 梯度解耦的私有适配——该温度仅应用于客户端私有的预测通路，而共享联邦模型使用标准的非调节目标进行训练，从而在不允许置信度相关梯度干扰共享表征学习的前提下，实现样本级、客户端本地的置信度适配。

Result: 在四个多模态数据集上，Flux在每个数据集上均取得最高的平均宏F1分数，超过最强数据集专用基线0.8~2.2个点，平均提升1.6个点。进一步分析表明Flux具有良好的校准性能，温度对模态缺失和输入损坏均敏感，且仅在私有通路上进行温度调节时共享优化更加稳定。

Conclusion: Flux通过将样本级置信度适配限制在客户端私有预测通路，同时保持共享联邦模型的标准优化，有效地解决了双重轴模态缺失问题，在性能、校准性和优化稳定性方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Multimodal federated learning (FL) supports collaborative modeling in privacy-sensitive health-sensing and medical settings, but realistic deployments often exhibit dual-axis modality missingness: clients have different modality sets, and individual samples may contain only subsets of the modalities available locally. Existing methods typically address these two axes separately. We propose Flux, a multimodal federated learning framework built around two complementary components. First, modality-aware confidence tempering learns sample-specific confidence for each modality through mask-aware unimodal supervision and fuses the confidence estimates from observed modalities into a sample-adaptive temperature that adjusts predictive sharpness according to evidence quality and completeness. Second, gradient-decoupled private adaptation applies this temperature only to a client-private prediction pathway, while training the shared federated model with a standard, untempered objective. This enables sample-specific, client-local confidence adaptation without allowing confidence-dependent gradients to perturb shared representation learning. Across four multimodal datasets, Flux achieves the highest average macro-F1 on every dataset, outperforming the strongest dataset-specific baseline by 0.8~2.2 points and by 1.6 points on average. Additional analyses demonstrate favorable calibration, temperature sensitivity to both modality missingness and input corruption, and more stable shared optimization under private-only tempering. Our code is available at https://github.com/AdibaOrz/Flux.

</details>


### [263] [FEAST: Federated Shared-Space Training for Resource-Heterogeneous Clients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09250)
*Bostan Khan,Masoud Daneshtalab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FEAST，一种联邦共享空间训练框架，通过预算定制的子超网路由和稀疏聚合，在异构设备推理预算下公平训练弹性超网模型，解决了参数仅被少数高端客户端覆盖导致的失衡问题，并在多个数据集上取得最优准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习需要服务计算能力各异的设备。固定模型无法适配所有设备，而按部署限制单独训练模型成本过高。联邦超网训练学习一个弹性模型，但其参数对高成本子网络是专属的，只有少数客户端能触及，导致训练失衡，需要一种方法来平衡不同预算客户端对超网参数的更新贡献。

Method: FEAST框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 在每个客户端推理预算内联合训练多个子网络以平衡参数可达性；(2) 预算定制的子超网路由，仅传输超网中相关的部分而非全部参数；(3) 稀疏聚合，返回的参数切片被有效合并。此外引入γ分配协议，用单一参数解耦客户端训练数据量与推理预算之间的耦合，避免异构FL模拟中的准确率-推理成本对比失真。

Result: 实验表明，SuperFedNAS和DeepFedNAS在25M MACs下接近随机，在596M MACs下仅达17.09%；FEAST在596M MACs下达到71.06%，比最强模型异质权重共享基线最大档高2.4个百分点。在CIFAR-100、CINIC-10和TinyImageNet-200上，FEAST在每客户端获得最大可负担子网络时取得最高种群平均准确率。子超网路由相较全超网传输将聚合模型参数流量减少6.8倍。

Conclusion: FEAST通过预算定制子超网路由与稀疏聚合，有效解决了联邦超网训练中因客户端推理预算差异导致的参数覆盖失衡问题。训练的每个子网络无需联邦重训练即可直接部署，还能支持事后提取额外子网络，同时大幅降低通信开销，是模型异质联邦学习中高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) must serve devices with varying computational capabilities. A fixed model cannot suit all devices, while training one model per deployment limit is costly. Federated supernet training instead learns one elastic model with differently sized subnetworks, then deploys a suitable one to each device. When client inference budgets differ, however, parameters exclusive to high-cost subnetworks are reachable by fewer clients. We propose FEAST, a federated shared-space training framework that counters this imbalance by jointly training multiple subnetworks within each client's limit. Budget-tailored sub-supernet routing sends only the relevant supernet portion, and sparse aggregation merges the returned parameter slices. The trained supernet directly serves the subnetworks used during federation and supports post-hoc extraction of additional subnetworks without federated retraining. We further show that independently assigning clients' training-data volumes and inference budgets can distort accuracy--inference-cost comparisons in heterogeneous FL simulations, and introduce a one-parameter $γ$-allocation protocol to control this coupling. In our experimental setup, the SuperFedNAS and DeepFedNAS supernet training procedures remain near chance at 25M and reach at most $17.09\%$ at $596$M inference MACs; FEAST reaches $71.06\%$ at $596$M, $2.4$ points above the strongest model-heterogeneous weight-sharing baseline at its largest tier. Across CIFAR-100, CINIC-10, and TinyImageNet-200, FEAST achieves the highest population-averaged accuracy among the evaluated weight-sharing methods when each client receives its largest affordable subnetwork. Sub-supernet routing reduces aggregate model-parameter traffic by $6.8\times$ relative to full-supernet transmission.

</details>


### [264] [Full-Feature versus Limited-Input Machine Learning for Residential Energy Estimation: A Comparative Analysis of RECS and ResStock Under Realistic Input Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09255)
*Aditya Ramnarayan,Fatih Evren,Patti Gunderson,Samuel Rosenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究使用RECS和ResStock两个美国全国代表性住宅能耗数据集，量化了预测精度与输入数据可获取性之间的权衡。在完整特征下CatBoost表现最佳（ResStock R²=0.90, RECS R²=0.73）；当特征限制为住宅所有者可获取的十个低负担变量时，R²收敛到0.61-0.62。但在同质性更高的子群（单户独栋、天然气供暖、气候区6A、2000-2010年建成）中，简化输入模型仍能达到R²=0.85。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅能耗估算往往需要在获得详细的围护结构特征、设备效率、渗透率、传感器或账单数据之前进行，因此需要探索仅依赖低获取负担输入变量即可实现可靠预测的方法，并量化精度与输入可获取性之间的权衡。

Method: 使用两个数据集（基于调查的RECS和基于模拟的ResStock），先建立全特征模型作为基准，然后将特征限制为十种可从住户、行政记录或位置天气数据获得的低负担变量。比较CatBoost、XGBoost、LightGBM、Random Forest和神经网络五种模型在两种特征条件下的预测性能。

Result: 全特征分析中CatBoost表现最佳（ResStock R²=0.90, RECS R²=0.73）；特征限制为十个住户可获取变量后，各模型性能收敛（RECS R²=0.61, ResStock R²=0.62）；对同质性更高的特定子群（单户独栋、天然气供暖、气候区6A、2000-2010年建成），简化输入模型可达R²=0.85。

Conclusion: 基于树的集成模型可作为全国尺度住宅能耗数据集的高保真模拟器，但需谨慎考虑特征可获取性、数据集来源（经验vs.合成）及适用场景。算法复杂度无法完全弥补缺失的物理和行为信息，但对同质性人群进行针对性建模可获得显著提升。

Abstract: Residential energy estimates are often needed before detailed envelope characteristics, equipment efficiencies, infiltration, sensor, or billing data are available. This study quantifies the trade-off between predictive accuracy and input accessibility using two nationally representative U.S. residential-energy datasets: the survey-based Residential Energy Consumption Survey (RECS) and the simulation-based ResStock dataset. Full-feature models were first used to establish dataset-specific performance benchmarks. For total-energy estimation, the models were subsequently restricted to ten low-burden variables obtainable from occupants, administrative records, or location-based weather data without an on-site energy audit. Among CatBoost, XGBoost, LightGBM, Random Forest, and Neural Networks, CatBoost consistently achieved the highest predictive performance for the full-feature analysis, reaching R2 = 0.90 for ResStock and R2 = 0.73 for RECS. When the feature set was restricted to ten homeowner-accessible inputs to simulate realistic deployment conditions, model performance converged to R2 = 0.61 for RECS and R2 = 0.62 for ResStock, showing that algorithmic complexity cannot fully compensate for missing physical and behavioral information. However, for a more homogeneous ResStock cohort consisting of single-family detached, natural-gas-heated homes in Climate Zone 6A constructed between 2000 and 2010, a reduced-input model improved accuracy to R2 = 0.85, demonstrating the value of targeted modeling for homogeneous populations. The results indicate that tree-based ensemble models can serve as high-fidelity emulators of national-scale residential energy datasets. However, careful consideration of feature availability, dataset origin (empirical vs. synthetic), and applicable use cases are also important.

</details>


### [265] [SoftmaxGRPO: Learning to Reason using Softmax Advantage Group Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09271)
*Jefferson Hernandez,Jaywon Koo,Zilin Xiao,Chen Wei,Vicente Ordonez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SoftmaxGRPO，一种替代GRPO的组级别强化学习目标，通过温度缩放的softmax优势替代z-score归一化优势，解决GRPO在二元奖励下对不同难度提示产生发散性权重分配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等基于组的强化学习目标在不同提示难度下学习信号分配不佳：二元奖励下组归一化会导致对简单提示的发散性加权，使得困难提示得不到足够的学习信号。

Method: 引入Softmax Advantage Group Estimation (SoftmaxGRPO)，用温度缩放的softmax优势替代z-score归一化的组优势，使权重始终有界。对二元奖励推导精确的有限组群体目标，并识别MaxRL为其低温极限；对有界标量奖励，证明大组更新精确优化log-矩生成函数目标，同时指出在无奖励分布额外假设下不存在普适的有限组标量目标。

Result: SoftmaxGRPO将测量到的梯度预算从接近已解决的提示重新分配，在相同奖励条件下持续优于GRPO。在DeepMath上达到51.8%（可验证奖励），在Poetry上将1.5B指令微调模型从35.0%提升至68.0%（仅使用轻量级文本相似度奖励）。

Conclusion: SoftmaxGRPO作为GRPO的直接替代方案，解决了二元奖励下组归一化导致的发散性加权问题，保持了权重有界性，并在多个任务上取得一致改进，验证了其作为稳健强化学习目标的可行性。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning objectives such as GRPO can allocate learning signal poorly across prompt difficulty: under binary rewards, group normalization induces a divergent weighting on easy prompts. We introduce Softmax Advantage Group Estimation (SoftmaxGRPO), a drop-in alternative that replaces z-score-normalized group advantages with temperature-scaled softmax advantages, keeping weights bounded regardless of prompt difficulty. For binary rewards, we derive the exact finite-group population objective and identify MaxRL as its low-temperature limit. For bounded scalar rewards, we show that the large-group update exactly optimizes a log-moment-generating-function objective, while a universal finite-group scalar objective cannot exist without additional assumptions on the reward distribution. Empirically, SoftmaxGRPO reallocates measured gradient budget away from near-solved prompts and consistently improves over GRPO under identical rewards. It reaches 51.8% on DeepMath with verifiable rewards and improves a 1.5B instruction-tuned model from 35.0% to 68.0% on Poetry using only lightweight text-similarity rewards.

</details>


### [266] [VeinCast: Physics-Guided Dynamic Field Graphs with Graph-Conditioned Fusion for Global Medium-Range Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09286)
*Zhisheng Chen,Jinhan Li,Yuxuan Li,Yuan Gao,Hao Wu,Zheng Lu,Jinlong Du,Kun Wang,Bo An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeinCast是一个物理引导的动态场图与图条件融合框架，用于全球中程天气预报，可联合预测69个地表和高空气象场，在1.5°ERA5基准上实现最高14天领先时间的高竞争力预报性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球中尺度天气预报需要对异构大气场之间的结构化且状态依赖的相互作用进行建模。现有数据驱动模型大多隐式学习这些相互作用，而基于方程级别的物理约束可能继承近似误差和模型形式的偏差。

Method: VeinCast提出两个核心模块：1）物理引导动态场图（Physics-Guided Dynamic Field Graph），在每个局部窗口内结合预定义的大气关系与状态依赖的Top-K残差边，并利用图上下文自适应地球窗口注意力；2）图条件潜在融合（Graph-Conditioned Latent Fusion），利用图上下文和源节点中心性引导场到潜在空间的聚合，并通过有界反馈保留场特定信息。

Result: 在1.5° ERA5基准上，VeinCast在长达14天的领先时间内在全部69个气象场上展现出与FuXi、Pangu-Weather、GraphCast、FengWu和ARROW等代表性全球天气预报模型相比具有竞争力的预报性能。消融实验证实两个模块提供互补增益。

Conclusion: 关系层面的物理引导对数据驱动天气预报是有效的，VeinCast通过显式建模状态依赖的场间相互作用，避免了方程级约束带来的模型形式偏差，同时提升了预测性能。

Abstract: Global medium-range weather forecasting requires modeling structured yet state-dependent interactions among heterogeneous atmospheric fields. Existing data-driven models largely learn these interactions implicitly, whereas equation-level physical constraints may inherit approximation and model-form biases. We present VeinCast, a physics-guided dynamic field graph and graph-conditioned fusion framework that jointly forecasts 69 surface and upper-air fields. Within each local window, its Physics-Guided Dynamic Field Graph combines predefined atmospheric relations with state-dependent Top-K residual edges and adapts Earth-window attention using the resulting graph context. Graph-Conditioned Latent Fusion further employs graph context and source-node centrality to guide field-to-latent aggregation, while bounded feedback preserves field-specific information. On the $1.5^\circ$ ERA5 benchmark, VeinCast demonstrates competitive forecasting performance across all 69 meteorological fields at lead times of up to 14 days, compared with representative global weather forecasting models including FuXi, Pangu-Weather, GraphCast, FengWu, and ARROW. Ablations confirm that the two modules provide complementary gains, demonstrating the effectiveness of relational-level physical guidance for data-driven weather forecasting.

</details>


### [267] [Beyond the Capability Boundary: Zeroth-Order Optimization for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09292)
*Bingzhen Liu,Xiaomeng Fan,Yuwei Wu,Zhi Gao,Mingyang Gao,Chuanhao Li,Yunde Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种零阶自进化框架（ZOForLLMAgents），通过扰动LoRA参数并利用损失差异估计梯度来更新LLM参数，使智能体能够学习超出其固有能力边界的困难示例，形成闭环自进化流程，在多个深度研究基准上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化方法通过从LLM本身采样轨迹来学习，但由于智能体无法在困难示例上采样出正确轨迹，导致它们难以突破智能体的内在能力边界，限制了进一步改进的空间。

Method: 1) 扰动LoRA参数运行智能体，计算扰动前后参数下的损失，利用损失差值估计梯度并更新LoRA参数；2) 用更新后的LLM采样轨迹进行监督微调，突破能力边界，形成闭环自进化；3) 引入并行扰动推理机制和自适应查找机制降低零阶优化时间开销；4) 设计答案困惑度损失（answer perplexity loss）提供平滑稳定的零阶损失值。

Result: 在多个深度研究基准上的实验表明，该方法能获得显著更多的成功轨迹，在困难示例上始终优于强基线。

Conclusion: 零阶优化为自进化智能体提供了超越能力边界的可行路径，配合工程优化手段在效率和效果上均具优势。

Abstract: Self-evolving methods improve the capabilities of LLM agents by sampling trajectories from the underlying LLMs and learning from these trajectories. However, these methods struggle to learn beyond the inherent capability boundary of the agents, since the agents cannot sample correct trajectories on difficult examples for further improvements. In this paper, we propose a zeroth-order self-evolution framework that enables agents to learn beyond their capability boundary by perturbing LLM parameters to adapt to difficult examples without any trajectory annotations. Specifically, we perturb LoRA parameters of LLMs, run the agent, compute the losses under the perturbed and original parameters, and use the loss difference to estimate gradients and further update the LoRA parameters. We sample trajectories using the updated LLMs for supervised fine-tuning to break through the capability boundary of the agents, forming a closed self-evolution loop. We introduce a parallel perturbation inference mechanism and an adaptive lookup mechanism to reduce time consumption in zeroth-order optimization, with an answer perplexity loss that provides smooth and stable zeroth-order loss values. Experiments on multiple deep research benchmarks show that our method obtains substantially more successful trajectories and consistently outperforms strong baselines, especially on difficult examples. The code and released artifacts are available at https://github.com/hidk1911/ZOForLLMAgents.

</details>


### [268] [Targeted Label-Flipping and Oversampling Attacks on Federated Conditional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09314)
*Panav Shah,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦GAN中标签翻转攻击的有效性，提出过采样变体，并通过理论分析和在FEMNIST、MNIST、CIFAR10上的实验证明：语义损害随有效投毒强度线性增长，而偏离真实目标分布仅二次增长，因此攻击有效且难以用与标签无关的指标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的GAN架构面临多种对抗攻击威胁，其中标签翻转攻击通过恶意客户端在本地训练中篡改标签信息来操纵全局生成器，但目前对这类攻击在联邦GAN中有效性的系统性研究尚不充分。

Method: 通过理论分析和经验评估研究联邦GAN中标签翻转攻击的效果；提出过采样变体，即恶意客户端在本地训练中对投毒样本施加更高权重以放大其对全局模型聚合的影响；用KL散度量化干净与投毒条件下类别条件分布之间的偏移。

Result: 在FEMNIST、MNIST和CIFAR10上验证：攻击的语义损害随有效投毒强度线性增长，而对真实目标分布的偏离仅二次增长，表明攻击在有效的同时难以被与标签无关的指标检测到。

Conclusion: 标签翻转攻击在联邦GAN中构成严重威胁，其在产生显著语义损害的同时保持了较低的检测可见性，为联邦GAN的防御设计提出了新挑战。

Abstract: In a federated learning setup for GANs, several adversarial attacks are possible. One such attack is label flipping, in which malicious clients deliberately alter label information during local training in order to manipulate the global generator. The objective of this attack is to skew the learned generation distribution so that samples conditioned on a target label are instead mapped to a source class. In this work, we investigate the effectiveness of label flipping attacks in federated GANs through both theoretical analysis and empirical evaluation. We further consider an oversampling based variant, in which malicious clients upweight poisoned samples during local training to amplify their influence on the aggregated global model. We quantify the resulting distributional shift by computing the Kullback Leibler divergence between the clean and poisoned class conditional distributions, and show both analytically and on FEMNIST, MNIST, and CIFAR10 that the semantic damage of the attack grows linearly in the effective poisoning strength while deviation from the true target distribution grows only quadratically, making the attack effective yet difficult to detect from label agnostic metrics.

</details>


### [269] [MaxModShift: Model Privacy via Designed Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09328)
*Nomaan A. Kherani,Urbashi Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联邦学习中的窃听者防御方案，通过信号设计将窃听者的Fisher信息矩阵驱动至奇异，使其无法学习模型。提出的MaxModShift方案在满足传输功率约束下最大化窃听者与中央服务器所学模型之间的差异，优于先前ModShift方案和噪声注入方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习环境中，窃听者可能通过截获通信来学习模型，这构成隐私威胁。已有方案如ModShift和噪声注入存在功率消耗高、带宽需求大等不足，需要更优的防御设计。

Method: 将窃听者的模型学习问题建模为估计问题，由Fisher信息矩阵衡量其估计精度。通过信号设计将Fisher信息矩阵驱动为奇异矩阵，使窃听者无法完成估计。设计了两种模型偏移（model shift）方案，其中MaxModShift通过最大化窃听者与中央服务器学习模型之间的差异，同时满足各代理节点的传输功率约束。

Result: MaxModShift方案在性能上优于先前提出的ModShift设计，同时所需的传输功率更低。与噪声注入方案相比，MaxModShift在性能更优的同时，所需保密信道带宽更窄、平均功耗更低。

Conclusion: MaxModShift是一种更高效、更节能的联邦学习防窃听方案，在降低传输功率和带宽需求的同时提供了更强的隐私保护性能。

Abstract: Model learning by an eavesdropper is treated as an estimation problem in a federated environment. The Fisher Information Matrix for the eavesdropper's estimation problem is driven to singularity through a signaling design; this ensures that the eavesdropper cannot learn the model. Herein, the innovation of prior designs is that model shifts are designed to maximize the difference in the model learned by Eve and the central server while satisfying a transmission power constraint for the agents. Two shift schemes are provided. MaxModShift outperforms a prior ModShift design while requiring lesser transmission power. Compared to a noise injection scheme, MaxModShift performs better while requiring a lower bandwidth secret channel and a reduced average power consumption.

</details>


### [270] [Hallucinations and Constraints : Regulating surgical workflow recognition beyond accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09332)
*John S. H. Baxter,Pierre Jannin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将医学图像处理中的拓扑错误视为"幻觉"的一种可测量形式，通过将拓扑属性转化为线性时序逻辑谓词，并利用概率图模型显式约束这些谓词，从而为医学AI提供数学上的正确性保证。在机器人辅助子宫切除术的手术阶段识别任务中，该方法将准确率提升约10%，并消除了绝大多数拓扑错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻觉是AI整合到医学领域的主要担忧，但在医学图像处理领域研究较少。与自然文本理解中的幻觉不同，医学图像预测结果是否正确并不直观。作者认为拓扑错误可以作为幻觉的一种可测量形式，从而更便于监管和规范。

Method: 作者提出将某些类型问题（如生物医学信号分割）的拓扑属性重述为线性时序逻辑（LTL）谓词，其中一部分谓词可以借助概率图模型（PGMs）进行显式强制执行，从而实现拓扑约束的硬性保证。

Result: 模拟实验表明，在机器人辅助子宫切除术的自动手术阶段识别任务中，该方法将准确率提高约10%，并消除了绝大部分拓扑错误。

Conclusion: 数学保证的正确性可以补充医学图像计算和计算机辅助干预中其他形式的经验性机器学习监管方法，为医学AI系统的可靠性提供新的保障路径。

Abstract: Hallucinations are a major concern for the integration of artificial intelligence into medicine, although less explored in the realm of medical image processing. Unlike problems in natural text understanding and reasoning therewith, determining whether or not predictions derived from biomedical images and signals is less intuitively clear. This article suggests that topological errors could constitute hallucinations in a way that can be more readily measured and thus regulated. Certain of these properties for certain types of problems, such as biomedical signal segmentation, can be rephrased as linear temporal logic predicates, a number of which can be explicitly enforced using probabilistic graphical models. Our simulations show the potential of these explicitly constrained predicates for the case of automatic surgical phase recognition in robot-assisted hysterectomy, improving accuracy by approximately 10% while removing the vast majority of topological errors, suggesting that mathematical guarantees of correctness can supplement other empirical forms of regulating machine learning in medical image computing and computer-assisted interventions.

</details>


### [271] [In-Context Density Estimation for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09348)
*Patryk Marszałek,Jacek Tabor,Marek Śmieja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ICED是一种基于Transformer的上下文能量密度估计器，通过单次预训练即可在多个表格数据任务（密度估计、分布外检测、无监督异常检测和生成增强）中无需重新训练或调参即可工作，且与任务专用方法竞争力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的密度估计及其下游任务（异常检测、分布外检测、数据增强）都需要针对每个数据集单独拟合模型并进行超参数调优，成本高昂。尽管这些任务本质上都归结为概率质量分布的问题，但通常需要分别构建专用管线。作者希望消除这种每数据集调优的开销，实现一个通用的密度估计器。

Method: ICED是一个基于Transformer的模型，在专门为密度估计设计的合成先验上进行预训练。其训练目标在log-density信息丰富的地方进行拟合，在其他位置保持其排序关系。推理时，模型将数据集作为上下文读取，通过单次前向传播即可返回任意查询点的非归一化log-density，无需拟合、采样或超参数选择。单个冻结的ICED模型可直接驱动四个任务：密度估计、分布外检测、无监督异常检测和生成增强。

Result: 在密度估计、分布外检测、无监督异常检测和生成增强四个任务上，ICED与最强的任务专用方法竞争力相当，并且是唯一在任务间切换时无需重新训练、无需调参、无需标签的方法。

Conclusion: ICED证明了在上下文学习框架下，一个统一的密度估计器可以替代多个专用管线，在保持性能竞争力的同时大幅降低计算和调参成本，为表格数据的无监督学习提供了更高效的范式。

Abstract: Density estimation underlies many unsupervised tasks on tabular data such as anomaly detection, out-of-distribution detection, and data augmentation. Although all these problems reduce to questions about where probability mass lies, they are typically solved individually by fitting a separate model to each dataset, with its own hyperparameters and tuning budget. We introduce ICED, an in-context, energy-based density estimator that removes this per-dataset cost. ICED is a transformer-based model pretrained once on a synthetic prior built specifically for density estimation under an objective that fits log-density where it is informative and preserves its ordering elsewhere. In the inference, it reads a dataset as context and returns an unnormalized log-density for any query point in a single forward pass, with no fitting, sampling, or hyperparameter selection. A single frozen ICED model then drives four tasks usually handled by four specialized pipelines: density estimation, out-of-distribution detection, unsupervised anomaly detection, and generative augmentation. Across all four, it is competitive with the strongest task-specific method, while being the only approach that needs no retraining, no tuning, and no labels to move between them. The code is available at https://github.com/gmum/iced.

</details>


### [272] [Beyond Binary: Continuous State Optimization with Graph-Structured Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09366)
*Corinna Cortes,Yishay Mansour,Mehryar Mohri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lazy Graph-LinUCB算法，将多目标学习系统的优化从二元状态扩展到连续状态空间，通过懒惰更新、异步调度、自适应图和联合估计器四种机制，在最小化目标求和的同时降低切换成本（运动成本），实验表明可将运动成本降低三倍以上且保持相近的累计损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模学习系统常需在公平性、准确性和延迟等多个可能相互竞争的目标之间权衡。现有工作将该问题形式化为二元状态上的优化，但现实中许多控制参数（如公平阈值、多样性混合率、资源预算）本质上是连续的，因此需要将框架扩展到连续状态空间。

Method: 将问题建模为在运动成本（惩罚系统不稳定）约束下最小化线性目标之和；用依赖图/因子图捕获目标的局部结构；提出Lazy Graph-LinUCB算法，通过懒惰更新最小化切换成本并保持近最优regret；另引入三种机制：异步更新调度（消除稀疏图的同步开销）、自适应算法（从数据中学习图结构）、联合估计器（利用相关目标间的数据共享收紧regret界）。

Result: 实验表明，这些结构利用机制在异构系统中可将运动成本降低三倍以上，同时保持相似的累计损失（regret）。

Conclusion: 通过利用依赖图结构并结合懒惰更新策略，框架成功在对目标优化的探索与系统稳定性之间取得平衡，实现了显著降低系统切换成本而不牺牲过多累计性能，为连续状态空间下的多目标学习系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale learning systems often face the challenge of balancing multiple,
  potentially competing objectives, such as fairness, accuracy, and
  latency. While recent work has formalized this as an optimization problem over
  binary states, many real-world control parameters, such as fairness
  thresholds, diversity mixing rates, or resource budgets, are continuous. In
  this work, we extend the framework to \emph{continuous state spaces}. We model
  the problem as minimizing a sum of linear objectives subject to \emph{movement
  costs} that penalize system instability. We capture the local structure of
  the objectives using a \emph{dependency graph} (or factor graph), where each
  objective is determined by a subset of the state attributes. To address the
  tension between exploration and stability, we propose \emph{Lazy
  Graph-LinUCB}, an algorithm that performs lazy updates to minimize switching
  costs while maintaining near-optimal regret. Beyond stability, we introduce
  three advanced mechanisms to exploit the underlying graph structure: (1) an
  \emph{asynchronous} update schedule that eliminates synchronization overhead
  in sparse graphs; (2) an \emph{adaptive} algorithm that learns the graph
  structure from data; and (3) a \emph{joint estimator} that leverages data
  sharing among correlated objectives to significantly tighten regret
  bounds. Empirically, we demonstrate that these structural exploitations reduce
  movement costs by more than a factor of three in heterogeneous systems while
  maintaining similar cumulative losses.

</details>


### [273] [Imaginative Generative AI: Crossing the Entropy Wall into Worlds Beyond Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09385)
*Hossein Goli,Farzan Farnia,Amin Gohari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Imaginative Generative AI (IGA)——一种将多样性纳入目标分布设计问题的生成框架。通过谱熵（von Neumann熵）衡量生成分布的核协方差算子的表征多样性，定义数据分布的"熵墙"（Entropy Wall）。在熵墙以下进行多样性修复（恢复生成器丢失的变化），超出熵墙则主动偏离数据分布实现"创造性生成"。理论推导表明最优解满足指数倾斜关系，并据此提出无需重训练的推理时方法IGA Guidance，适用于score-based和diffusion模型（DDPM/DDIM）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI模型的主要目标是模仿数据分布，这一目标既不能纠正生成器丢失的多样性，也没有定义生成应如何超越数据本身的多样性。作者希望将多样性作为目标分布的显式设计问题，而非单纯模仿数据，从而弥合从"模仿"到"想象"之间的差距。

Method: (1) 将多样性定义为生成分布核协方差算子在固定表征空间中的von Neumann熵；(2) 提出熵约束投影问题：在接近参考分布的分布簇中，选择谱多样性达到预设水平的分布；(3) 以KL散度锚定预训练生成器，推导最优解满足自洽的指数倾斜（exponential-tilt）关系；(4) 基于该理论构建IGA Guidance——一种无需重训练的推理时采样方法，适用于score-based与diffusion模型（DDPM/DDIM）。

Result: 在合成数据和视觉基准上的实验表明：(1) 在熵墙以下，IGA能够有效进行多样性修复，恢复学习生成器丢失的变化；(2) 在熵墙以上，IGA实现受控的谱外推（spectral extrapolation），产生超出数据多样性的分布。

Conclusion: IGA框架将生成建模从纯模仿转向可量化的多样性控制，通过统一的"从模仿到想象"的正则化路径为生成模型提供了可论理的多样性调节机制，并且其推理时方法无需重新训练即可在现有扩散模型上应用。

Abstract: Generative AI models are primarily designed to imitate the data distribution, an objective that neither corrects diversity lost by a learned generator nor defines how generation should extend beyond the diversity of the data itself. We introduce Imaginative Generative AI (IGA), a framework that makes diversity part of the target-distribution design problem: among distributions close to a reference, IGA selects one whose spectral diversity reaches a prescribed level. Diversity is measured by the von Neumann entropy of the generated distribution's kernel covariance operator in a fixed representation space, providing a reference-free representation-guided measure of how broadly probability mass occupies embedding directions. The spectral entropy of the population data distribution defines an Entropy Wall. Below the wall, IGA performs diversity repair, recovering variation that a learned generator has lost while remaining within the diversity level of the data. Beyond the wall, the data distribution itself becomes infeasible, and IGA deliberately departs from it to produce distributions with greater representation-relative spectral diversity, an operational notion of imaginative generation. These regimes form a single regularization path from imitation to imagination and define an i.i.d. target distribution at each prescribed diversity level. We develop the theory of this entropy-constrained projection and show that, under a KL anchor to a pretrained generator, the optimum satisfies a self-consistent exponential-tilt relation. This characterization leads to IGA Guidance, a retraining-free inference-time method for score-based and diffusion models, including DDPM and DDIM samplers. Experiments on synthetic and vision benchmarks demonstrate diversity repair below the Entropy Wall and controlled spectral extrapolation beyond it.

</details>


### [274] [From Objectives to What Models Learn: A Landau Theory of Invariant Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09396)
*Pinli Wang,Yue He,Peng Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将表示学习类比为多模式磁化，从不变学习目标中推导出Landau型有效自由能，其低阶系数构成目标签名并预测不同正则化表型（包括相边界、模态消除、残差加载和塌缩），在双线性模型中得到闭式解，并在ReLU网络和矩阵扩展中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不变学习旨在学习跨环境保持预测能力的表示，但其目标沿正则化路径的行为往往不透明，存在"目标-行为鸿沟"（objective-behavior gap）。作者希望弥合这一鸿沟，使目标函数的性质能够预测正则化变化时模型实际学到的内容。

Method: 将表示学习视为多模式磁化（multimode magnetization），从具体的不变学习目标推导Landau型有效自由能。分析低阶系数（二次与四次修正）如何决定相边界移动、模态消除、振幅调节和塌缩等行为。在双线性模型（canonical bilinear model）中推导闭式相边界和稳态载荷，并扩展到ReLU网络和矩阵情形。

Result: 在双线性模型中得到了闭式相边界与稳态载荷，发现了捷径模式与稳定模式的不同临界强度，定义了选择性保留窗口（selective-retention window）。受控实验验证了预测的相边界、载荷和正则化表型。在单层与双层ReLU网络中，目标签名在深度引起的尺度偏移下仍能预测定性正则化路径行为。矩阵扩展产生了谱相边界准则。

Conclusion: 该框架将低阶目标签名转化为正则化表型的预测，最终能够预测模型在正则化变化时学习到何种表示，为理解不变学习目标的动态行为提供了宏观热力学式视角。

Abstract: Invariant learning seeks representations that remain predictive across environments, yet the behavior of its objectives along the regularization path is often opaque. We address this objective-behavior gap by viewing representation learning as multimode magnetization and deriving, from concrete invariant-learning objectives, a Landau-type effective free energy whose low-order coefficients form objective signatures and induce distinct regularization phenotypes. Effective quadratic corrections move the phase boundary and enable finite-strength mode elimination; quartic corrections regulate post-onset amplitude and typically leave residual loading at finite strength; higher-order structure governs non-monotone tails, instability, and collapse at large regularization. In a canonical bilinear model, the theory yields closed-form phase boundaries and steady-state loadings, as well as distinct critical strengths for shortcut and stable modes that define a selective-retention window. Controlled experiments confirm the predicted phase boundaries, loadings, and regularization phenotypes. In one- and two-hidden-layer ReLU networks, the same signatures remain predictive of qualitative regularization-path behavior despite depth-dependent shifts in scale. A matrix extension generalizes the framework to coupled collective modes and yields a spectral phase-boundary criterion. Together, the framework turns low-order objective signatures into predictions of regularization phenotypes and, ultimately, of what models learn as regularization varies.

</details>


### [275] [A Mechanistic Diagnostic of Rank Collapse in Post-Norm Decoder Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09417)
*Xingjian Wang,Qingyu Han,Xiaodong Luo,Yin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Post-Norm解码器Transformer中的秩坍缩问题进行了两阶段理论分析：在初始化阶段，因果注意力近似为前缀平均算子，导致token相似性随深度增长；在高相似度阶段，残差范数增长使RMSNorm反向因子收缩，导致梯度呈几何级数衰减。研究还刻画了坍缩网络的性质：其最优预测器为频率分布，损失地板较高，且坍缩层的梯度在频率分布处消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度解码器Transformer常以Pre-Norm替代Post-Norm，因为Post-Norm在传统初始化方案下对warmup和学习率高度敏感。虽然先前工作已识别出秩坍缩和梯度消失等症状，但因果注意力如何产生高相似性表征以及训练动态为何无法修复这些问题仍缺乏深入理解。
