### [344] [Revisiting the Coupling of Thermodynamics and Electromagnetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07595)
*Stefanie Braun,Henning Struchtrup,Manuel Torrilhon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了运动中的可极化和可磁化物质中连续介质热力学与电磁理论的耦合问题，分别沿公理化体理论和统计力学两条路径推导并比较，发现两者在重新定义极化与磁化后结构上一致，唯一不可约差异是微观场涨落带来的动量贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动物质中电磁理论与连续介质热力学的耦合一直存在争议，不同理论出发点和建模选择导致结果形式上不一致。作者希望通过严格比较公理化方法（Dreyer-Guhlke-Müller）和统计力学方法（Mazur），澄清哪些量是必要出现的内在量、哪些是建模选择，从而消除理论间的表面矛盾。

Method: 作者沿两条路径进行：其一为Dreyer、Guhlke和Müller的公理化体理论，通过熵原理封闭普遍平衡律，证明内能平衡源必须由非对流电流构建，极化电流与Lorentz磁化通过统一恒等式关联并约束熵变量与束缚电流假设；其二为Mazur的统计力学方法，通过对含内部电荷载流子的原子系统进行系综平均得到宏观Maxwell方程，并在此基础上下推到守恒律，估计质量修正项的量级。

Result: 比较结果表明，在重新定义极化与磁化之后，两套方程在结构上完全一致。唯一不可消除的差异是微观场涨落产生的动量贡献，这在纯粹宏观理论中无法复现。此外，电动势强度E和Lorentz磁化M并非人为建模选择而是自然涌现的量；熵函数的不对称外观源于所选能量变量而非理论缺陷。

Conclusion: 两种独立的理论路线最终收敛于同一组方程，增强了结果的可靠性。公理化方法与统计力学方法的一致性表明连续介质电磁热力学的基本框架是自洽的，为后续在复杂流体、铁磁流体等实际系统中的应用提供了坚实理论基础。

Abstract: We revisit the coupling of continuum thermodynamics and electromagnetic theory for polarisable and magnetisable matter in motion. Two routes are followed and then compared. The first route is the axiomatic bulk theory of Dreyer, Guhlke and Müller, in which universal balance laws are closed by an entropy principle. We show that the source of the internal energy balance must be built with the non-convective electric current, that the polarisation current and the Lorentz magnetisation enter through one single identity, which Dreyer et al.\ do not write down, and that this identity fixes both the admissible entropy variables and the signs of the bound-current ansatz. The second route is the statistical-mechanical one of Mazur, in which the macroscopic Maxwell equations are obtained by ensemble averaging over a system of atoms with internal charge carriers. Mazur stops before the conservation laws, so we derive them, and we estimate the size of the mass-correction terms that appear. The comparison shows that after a redefinition of polarisation and magnetisation the two sets of equations agree structurally. The only irreducible difference is a momentum contribution from microscopic field fluctuations, which can not be reproduced in a purely macroscopic theory. We further show that the electromotive intensity $\mathcal{E}$ and the Lorentz magnetisation $\mathcal{M}$ are not modelling choices but appear by themselves, and that the asymmetric look of the entropy function is a consequence of the chosen energy variable and not a defect of the theory.

</details>


### [345] [Hexagonal Stacking Maximizes Proton Configurational Entropy among Ice-I Polytypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07613)
*Zhengyue Chen,Sheng Ran*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究冰I的多型体（立方、六方及混合层堆叠）的构型熵/配置常数问题，通过非负传递算子与Schatten-Hölder不等式等方法，给出了周期均匀注册多型体的配置常数上下界：1.503360 ≤ w ≤ 1.540196，并证明六方堆叠使冰规则计数最大化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰I存在立方、六方和混合层堆叠等多种多型体，但严格的熵比较此前仅针对两种理想端元（纯立方与纯六方）。作者希望对该问题给出统一的、覆盖所有周期均匀注册多型体的严格分析。

Method: 将每个循环均匀注册堆叠表示为非负传递算子K及其转置构成的词；对偶数长度的词应用Schatten-Hölder不等式证明六方交替堆叠在每一有限横截面上最大化冰规则计数。下界通过将Nagle的正偶子图展开限制到精确枚举的不相交块获得；上界通过Finner的二次超图Hölder不等式及二复制棱柱传递算子的有理Collatz-Wielandt证书获得。

Result: 得到配置常数界1.503360 ≤ w ≤ 1.540196，且w(Ic) ≤ 1.527699。

Conclusion: 六方堆叠在所有周期均匀注册多型体中最大化冰规则计数（因此配置常数最大），并给出了所有此类多型体配置常数的严格数值区间。

Abstract: Ice I admits cubic, hexagonal, and mixed layer stackings, but rigorous entropy comparisons have focused on the two ideal endmembers. We represent every cyclic uniform-registry stacking by a word in a nonnegative transfer operator K and its transpose. For every such even-length word, applying the Schatten-Hölder inequality proves that alternating hexagonal stacking maximizes the ice-rule count at every common finite cross-section; the configuration constant is therefore maximal among all periodic uniform-registry polytypes. We obtain the lower endpoint by restricting Nagle's positive even-subgraph expansion to exactly enumerated disjoint blocks. Finner's degree-two hypergraph Hölder inequality and rational Collatz-Wielandt certificates for two-replica prism transfer operators give the upper endpoints. These constructions yield $1.503360 \le w \le 1.540196$, with $w(\mathrm{Ic}) \le 1.527699$.

</details>


### [346] [Fuzzy Spectroscopy of Bound States in Massive Quantum Field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07655)
*Joseph Taylor,Matthew Yusuf,Zlatko Papić*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文利用基于非交换"模糊"几何的正则化方法，对Ising量子场论进行了非微扰能谱分析。在模糊环面上成功再现了(1+1)维微扰Ising QFT的E₈介子质量谱；通过增大环面纵横比，连续追踪了从1D到2D维数交叉过程中第二轻E₈介子的演化；在二维模糊环面和球面上，发现了一个亚阈值标量能级和与早期胶球质量估计一致的阈值以上响应特征；这些激发态在非平衡淬火动力学中同样被揭示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介子和胶球是夸克禁闭量子场论中的范式束缚态，但在连续统中，它们在超过一维空间的情况下进行非微扰谱学分析仍然具有极大挑战性。现有方法难以在连续极限下处理多维强耦合QFT的束缚态谱，因此需要发展新的非微扰正则化方案。

Method: 采用基于非交换"模糊"几何的正则化方法：在薄模糊环面上验证(1+1)D Ising QFT的E₈介子质量谱；通过调节环面纵横比实现从1D到2D的维数交叉；在二维模糊环面和球面上研究标量能级和胶球质量特征；利用淬火动力学在非平衡条件下揭示同样的激发态。

Result: 在薄模糊环面上精确复现了微扰(1+1)D Ising QFT的普适性低能E₈介子质量谱；连续追踪了维数交叉过程中第二轻E₈介子的演化；在二维模糊环面和球面上发现了亚阈值标量态及与先前胶球质量估计一致的阈值以上响应特征；淬火动力学同样揭示了这些激发态。

Conclusion: 模糊几何被确立为质量QFT的有效非微扰谱学探针，能够捕获束缚态在维数交叉过程中的演化，为更高维量子场论中束缚态的连续统非微扰谱学研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Mesons and glueballs are paradigmatic bound states of confining quantum field theories (QFTs), but their nonperturbative spectroscopy in the continuum remains challenging beyond one spatial dimension. Here we perform such spectroscopy for the Ising QFT using a recently developed regularization based on noncommutative ``fuzzy'' geometry. On a thin fuzzy torus, we reproduce the universal low-lying $\mathbb E_8$ meson masses of the magnetically perturbed $(1{+}1)\mathrm{D}$ Ising QFT. By increasing the torus aspect ratio, we continuously track the second-lightest $\mathbb E_8$ meson as the system effectively crosses over from 1D to 2D. On the 2D fuzzy torus and sphere, we find a subthreshold scalar level and above-threshold response features consistent with previous estimates of glueball masses. The same excitations are revealed away from equilibrium using quench dynamics. Our results establish fuzzy geometries as nonperturbative spectroscopic probes of massive QFTs, including their bound-state evolution through dimensional crossover.

</details>


### [347] [Classical fractons with cosmological fixed points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07672)
*Akash Singh,Dileep P. Jatkar,S. L. Sondhi,Abhishodh Prakash*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一族保持偶极矩的标度不变fracton哈密顿系统 H_{α,β}。通过将坐标分解为标度与形状变量，系统演化出自治的形状动力学，其不动点对应幂律Riesz势的中心构型。其中(α,β)=(-2,1)的独特模型展现出爱因斯坦-德西特形式的标度演化、均匀球状的大N分布，并具有零能牛顿引力对偶。该模型无需微调即可复现平坦物质主导宇宙学的主要结构，可作为宇宙学动力学的玩具模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典fracton系统虽在完整相空间无吸引子，但在投影到配置或形状变量后能发展出吸引子。作者希望研究一类标度不变、偶极守恒的fracton哈密顿族，探索其形状动力学的不动点结构以及这些吸引子是否能够自然再现宇宙学中的关键特征，如平坦物质主导宇宙的膨胀行为，而无需精细调节。

Method: 将坐标分解为标度变量R和形状变量，推导自治的形状动力学方程，分析其不动点条件（等价于功率律Riesz势的中心构型方程）。对特殊参数(-2,1)进行重点分析，包括不动点稳定性分析、大N均匀球分布的推导、零能牛顿引力对偶的构造，并通过中等N和大N数值模拟验证，同时提出尺度分离猜想以处理不等质量的中心动力学。

Result: 系统演化取R(t)∝|t|^{α/(α-β)}的纯标度形式。独特模型(-2,1)的标度演化满足爱因斯坦-德西特形式R(t)∝|t|^{2/3}，不动点方程即等质量牛顿中心构型，大N分布为均匀球。不动点局部稳定，中等N模拟从随机初值趋近。大N模拟发现更丰富的不动点类型：束缚簇保持内部运动而中心趋近不等质量牛顿中心构型。轨迹普遍展示从Janus点出发的双向时间箭头，Boltzmann熵对数增长。

Conclusion: 该独特fracton模型的所有宇宙学类比（膨胀标度律、中心构型分布、时间箭头、熵增长等）都作为吸引子性质自然涌现，无需微调即可作为宇宙学动力学的玩具模型。大N下束缚簇中心的动力学可通过尺度分离猜想理解为等价的不等质量fracton动力学，并具有相应的零能牛顿引力对偶。

Abstract: Classical fractons are Hamiltonian systems that can develop attractors after projection onto configuration or shape variables, although the full phase space admits none. We study a scale-invariant, dipole-conserving two-parameter family of fracton Hamiltonians $H_{α,β}$. By separating coordinates into scale and shape, we obtain autonomous shape dynamics that admit fixed points which leave a purely scale evolution of the form $R(t)\propto |t|^{α/(α-β)}$. The shape fixed points, which determine the distribution of the expanding particles, are central configurations of power-law Riesz potentials. The distinguished model $(α,β)=(-2,1)$ is unique: its scale evolution takes the Einstein-de Sitter form $R(t)\propto |t|^{2/3}$, its fixed-point equation is the equal-mass Newtonian central-configuration, its large-$N$ distribution is a homogeneous ball, and its homothetic trajectories admit a zero-energy Newtonian gravitational dual. The fixed points are locally stable, and simulations at moderate $N$ approach them from random initial data. Large $N$ simulations reveal a richer class of fixed-points: bound clusters of approximately fixed physical size retain internal motion, while their centers approach unequal-mass Newtonian central configurations and preserve large-scale homogeneity. A scale-separation conjecture yields an effective unequal-mass fracton dynamics for the centers and a corresponding zero-energy Newtonian gravitational dual. Trajectories generically exhibit a bidirectional arrow of time: scale and shape complexity grow away from a Janus point, while Boltzmann entropy grows logarithmically. Together, these features reproduce the salient structure of a flat matter-dominated cosmology. In the distinguished fracton model, all these cosmological analogues emerge as attractor properties, making it a toy model for cosmological dynamics without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [348] [Turning angle analysis reveals hidden anisotropies in the anomalous diffusion of molecules in live cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07975)
*Michał Balcerek,Adrian Pacheco-Pozo,Agnieszka Wyłomańska,Diego Krapf*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出利用转向角统计（turning-angle statistics）来检测细胞内异常扩散运动中的空间各向异性，解决了传统方法难以从短、噪声、随机取向的轨迹中提取各向异性信息的问题，并通过理论推导、模拟及单粒子追踪数据验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞内分子运动轨迹通常很短、含有噪声且方向随机，传统分析方法难以检测空间各向异性，因此阻碍了人们对细胞内组织与运输物理原理的理解。需要一种鲁棒的方法来从这类轨迹中提取各向异性信息。

Method: 采用二维各向异性分数布朗运动（anisotropic fractional Brownian motion）模型并引入随机取向，从理论和模拟两方面证明转向角分布能够保留各向异性特征；随后将该方法应用于单粒子追踪数据，包括HeLa细胞细胞质中的量子点和海马神经元中的膜蛋白。

Result: 转向角分析揭示了量子点和Nav 1.6通道运动中的隐藏各向异性，并识别出糖蛋白CD4特定动力学状态下的各向异性特征，证明各向异性异常扩散是细胞内动力学中被忽视的特征。

Conclusion: 转向角分析是检测复杂环境中分子运动组织结构的有力策略，为理解细胞内异常扩散的各向异性行为提供了新的分析工具和理论框架。

Abstract: Molecular motion within living cells provides a window into the physical principles underlying intracellular organization and transport. Yet a fundamental limitation remains: measured trajectories are often short, noisy, and randomly oriented, rendering spatial anisotropies inaccessible to conventional analyses. We show that turning-angle statistics provide a robust approach for uncovering anisotropies in anomalous diffusion. Using a two-dimensional anisotropic fractional Brownian motion model with random orientations, we show theoretically and through simulations that turning-angle distributions preserve signatures of anisotropy. We apply this approach to single-particle tracking data, including quantum dots in the cytoplasm of HeLa cells and membrane proteins in hippocampal neurons. Turning angles reveal hidden anisotropies in the motion of quantum dots and Nav 1.6 channels, and in specific dynamical states of glycoprotein CD4. These results establish turning-angle analysis as a powerful strategy for detecting organization in complex environments and reveal that anisotropic anomalous diffusion is an overlooked feature of intracellular dynamics.

</details>


### [349] [Low-frequency output fluctuations in an open exclusion process with particle pausing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08074)
*Quentin Thommen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个可逆切换激活与暂停状态的有限开放完全非对称简单排斥过程（TASEP），发现缓慢的内部状态（粒子暂停）不仅降低平均通量，还显著改变输出的时间组织。暂停时间尺度随系统长度按k_p^max∝L^{-1}缩放，长时间窗Fano因子呈现强非单调行为，在平均暂停粒子数N_p≈1.5-2处达到峰值。作者提出一个最小常数出生-线性死亡近似模型，在独立暂停、强阻塞极限下给出N_p^*≈1.50的理论预测。模拟显示暂停数过度分散，慢解堵同时增大相关时间和噪声幅度，低频谱噪声可识别由缓慢缺陷动力学与交通堵塞重组共同塑造的间歇性有限尺寸区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解慢内部状态如何重塑驱动晶格气体的宏观输运特性，特别是低频谱噪声如何揭示有限尺寸系统中的间歇性与缓慢动力学效应，这有助于深入理解生物分子马达等系统中暂停（pausing）现象对输运性能的影响。

Method: 采用有限开放TASEP模型，粒子可逆地在激活态与暂停态之间切换。通过出口计数统计（exit-counting statistics）分析长时间窗Fano因子、平均电流等宏观量随暂停率的变化。同时提出最小常数出生-线性死亡（constant-birth, linear-death）近似解析模型，并结合大规模模拟（L=50-500）验证近似适用范围，进行停留时间（residence-time）与结构（structural）分析以区分相关慢变量。

Result: 暂停率的增加使平均电流平滑下降，而长时间窗Fano因子强烈非单调，在参考边界速率下其在N_p≈1.5-2处取得最大值且对L=50-500不敏感，对应k_p^max∝L^{-1}。近似模型给出N_p^*≈1.50，模拟表明暂停数过度分散，固定N_p时减缓解堵会增加相关时间和噪声幅度；噪声幅度跟随最大粒子簇的波动而非其均值。

Conclusion: 低频谱噪声输出可作为间歇性有限尺寸区域的有效标识，该区域由缓慢缺陷动力学与交通堵塞重组的协同作用所决定。停留时间刻画与簇结构分析进一步验证了相关慢变量的主导作用，为理解驱动输运系统中的慢动态效应提供了定量框架。

Abstract: Slow internal states reshape both the mean throughput and the temporal organization of a driven lattice gas. Exit-counting statistics reveal this effect in a finite open totally asymmetric simple exclusion process whose particles reversibly switch between active and paused states. Increasing pausing lowers the mean current smoothly, whereas the long-window Fano factor is strongly nonmonotonic. At the reference boundary rates, the maximum remains near a measured mean paused population \(N_p=Lρ_{\rm paused}\simeq1.5\)--\(2\) across lattice lengths L=50-500, while the corresponding pausing rate scales as \(k_p^{\rm max}\propto L^{-1}\). A minimal constant-birth, linear-death approximation translates an order-one collective crossover into this finite-size displacement and gives \(N_p^\star\simeq1.50\) in the independent-pause, strong-blocking limit. The simulations delimit this approximation: the pause number is overdispersed, and at fixed \(N_p\), slower unpausing increases both the correlation time and the noise amplitude. Residence-time and structural analyses further separate the relevant slow variables. The pause-free versus pause-containing residence-time scale tracks the fitted output-correlation time, whereas the noise amplitude follows fluctuations, rather than the mean size, of the largest particle cluster. Low-frequency output noise therefore identifies an intermittent finite-size regime shaped jointly by slow-defect kinetics and traffic-jam reorganization.

</details>


### [350] [Lee-Yang Theory Guided Force Field Refinement Based on Phase Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08546)
*Bin Jin,Qijun Ye,Xinzheng Li,Kuang Yu,Shenzhen Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Lee-Yang相变理论的通用力场精炼框架，以配分函数模作为优化目标（在实轴附近评估，而非显式求解Lee-Yang零点），无需系统特定的序参量或响应性质，可通用于各类一级相变和材料体系。在Lennard-Jones势（气-液和固-液相变）及Cu嵌入原子法势上验证，精炼后的力场显著改善了目标相图，且Cu的焓和热容等非目标可观测量也同步得到改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统力场参数化通常需要系统特定的序参量或响应性质来表征相变点，缺乏通用性；而直接求解Lee-Yang零点存在数值困难。作者希望建立一个无需这些特定量、可通用于各类不连续相变和不同材料体系的自动力场精炼框架，并以相图作为优化指导。

Method: 以Lee-Yang相变理论为基础，将配分函数模作为相图引导的优化目标。具体做法是：在实轴附近、Lee-Yang零点主要与相变关联的区域评估配分函数的模，从而避免显式求解零点的数值困难。该框架不依赖任何系统特定的序参量或响应性质，可统一应用于不同类型的一级相变体系。通过在Lennard-Jones势和Cu嵌入原子方法势上的参数精炼进行验证。

Result: 精炼后的力场在气-液、固-液等所有测试系统中均显著改善了目标相图的复现精度。对Cu体系，精炼同时改进了焓和热容的预测，这两者超出了优化目标范围，表明该方法具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作将Lee-Yang理论确立为当代力场开发中的实用工具，为相图引导的自动力场精炼提供了通用且物理上有意义的框架，并展示了超越目标观测量的额外预测改进。

Abstract: We propose a general framework for automatic force field refinement guided by phase diagrams, grounded in Lee-Yang phase transition theory. The central idea is to directly use the partition function modulus as a phase-diagram-guided optimization target. Evaluating the modulus at points close to the real axis, where the Lee-Yang zeros are mostly associated with the phase transition, is more physically meaningful and avoids the numerical difficulty of explicitly solving for the zeros. This approach requires no system-specific order parameters or response properties for characterizing phase transition points, making it universal across various discontinuous phase transitions and material systems. We validate the method on refining parameters of a Lennard-Jones potential covering both gas-liquid and solid-liquid transitions, and a Cu embedded-atom method potential based on experimental melting curves. The refined force fields reproduce the target phase diagrams with significant improvement across all systems. For Cu, the refinement simultaneously improves predictions of enthalpy and heat capacity, which are observables beyond the optimization target. These results establish Lee-Yang theory as a practical tool for contemporary force field development.

</details>


### [351] [Cubic-Equivariant Neural Density Functional Theory for Three-Dimensional Lattice Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08137)
*Jens Weimar,Martin Oettel,Alessandro Simon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个直接作用于三维密度分布的全卷积神经经典密度泛函（neural classical density functional），以三维平行硬方块在简单立方晶格上的系统为测试平台，学习单体直接关联泛函c(1)[ρ]，并通过立方点群对称性约束实现精确的立方等变性，显著改善了均匀态方程和平面硬墙处的密度分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典密度泛函理论（cDFT）在描述非均匀流体方面具有重要价值，但传统泛函对复杂系统（如硬方块等各向异性粒子）的表达能力有限。开发能够直接处理完整三维密度分布的可学习密度泛函，是突破人工设计泛函精度限制的关键路径。

Method: 采用全卷积神经网络学习单体直接关联泛函c(1)[ρ]，训练数据来自随机外势下的大正则蒙特卡洛模拟。训练与推理均使用完整密度分布，通过输出位点的随机伯努利掩码（stochastic Bernoulli mask）实现全分布训练而无需显式提取重叠局部窗口。将第一层卷积核在立方点群的48个旋转和反射操作上取平均，在不做数据增强的情况下实现精确的立方等变性。

Result: 与独立的模拟数据及Lafuente和Cuesta的晶格基本度量泛函（lattice fundamental-measure functional）相比，神经泛函在均匀态方程和平面硬墙密度分布上均有显著改进。在固定粒子周围的各向异性对分布函数方面，两种泛函都能重现主要堆积壳层，相对精度依赖于晶体学方向。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在完整三维密度分布上进行神经密度泛函计算的可行性，同时指出获得准确的完整维度训练数据、保证热力学一致性以及正确处理结构关联，是向连续流体推广的核心挑战。

Abstract: We construct a neural classical density functional that acts directly on unrestricted three-dimensional density profiles. As a computationally tractable test bed, we consider parallel hard cubes of side length three on a simple cubic lattice. A fully convolutional network learns the one-body direct-correlation functional $c^{(1)}[ρ]$ from data obtained with grand-canonical Monte Carlo simulations in randomized external potentials. Complete profiles are used during both training and inference; a stochastic Bernoulli mask on the output sites makes full-profile training effective without explicitly extracting and storing overlapping local density windows. Averaging the first-layer kernels over all 48 rotations and reflections of the cubic point group additionally imposes exact cubic equivariance without data augmentation. We compare the learned functional with independent simulation data and with the lattice fundamental-measure functional of Lafuente and Cuesta. The neural functional markedly improves the homogeneous equation of state and the density profile at a planar hard wall. For the anisotropic pair distribution around a fixed particle, both functionals reproduce the principal packing shells, with their relative accuracy depending on crystallographic direction. These results demonstrate neural density-functional calculations on complete three-dimensional profiles while also identifying accurate full-dimensional training data, thermodynamic consistency, and structural correlations as the central challenges for extensions to continuum fluids.

</details>


### [352] [Out-of-equilibrium inhomogeneous XX chains: Exact results and the hydrodynamic limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08205)
*Vincenzo Alba,Federico Rottoli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了XX链在阶梯形磁场下的非平衡动力学，通过Jordan-Wigner变换映射为不均匀紧束缚链，利用傅里叶和拉普拉斯变换将问题转化为单位圆上的标准Riemann-Hilbert问题，精确求解了量子淬火后费米子两点关联函数，并在流体动力学极限下给出了只依赖原点有效透射系数的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非平衡量子系统在空间不均匀扰动下的动力学行为，特别是如何精确刻画时间和空间依赖的关联函数，以及流动动力学极限下关联函数的普适形式。

Method: 将XX链在阶梯形磁场下的哈密顿量通过Jordan-Wigner变换映射为不均匀紧束缚链，对初态（包括均匀和非均匀的乘积态）进行量子淬火后，利用傅里叶和拉普拉斯变换的组合将问题转化为单位圆上的标准Riemann-Hilbert问题，从而得到费米子两点关联函数的精确解析表达式。

Result: 对于任意位置和时间，关联函数不以初等函数表达；但在流体动力学极限（x,y,t同时趋于无穷且比值固定）下，推导出只依赖原点有效透射系数的显式公式。数值模拟验证表明，解析预测在流体动力学极限下与精确数值结果高度吻合，仅存在有限时间修正。

Conclusion: 该工作为空间不均匀系统的非平衡动力学提供了一个精确可解的范例，证明了Riemann-Hilbert方法可有效处理这类问题，并揭示了流体动力学极限下关联函数的普适形式。

Abstract: We study the out-of-equilibrium dynamics in the XX chain with step-like magnetic field, which maps to an inhomogeneous tight-binding chain after Jordan-Wigner transformation. We obtain exact analytic expressions for the fermionic two-point correlation function after a quantum quench from several initial product states, both homogeneous and inhomogeneous ones. This is achieved by using a combination of Fourier and Laplace transforms, which al low us to map the problem to a standard Riemann-Hilbert problem on the unit circle. For arbitrary positions and times the correlators are not expressed in terms of elementary functions. However, in the hydrodynamic limit $x,y,t\to\infty$ with fixed ratios, we provide explicit formulas that depend only on the effective transmission coefficient across the origin. We benchmark our analytic predictions against exact numerical simulations and find excellent agreement in the hydrodynamic limit, apart from finite-time corrections.

</details>


### [353] [High-Capacity Generalized Hopfield Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08226)
*Victor Galitski*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文引入了广义Hopfield网络，将记忆和神经元设为黎曼流形上的连续变量，重点关注SU(d)对称空间。通过李代数方法精确描述经典神经网络，发现相较于向量网络，其临界容量从d=3起提升了近一个数量级且随d快速增大。记忆回忆对应尖峰矩阵的顶特征向量对齐，并通过广义Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert动力学自然实现物理回忆机制。文中还展示了SU(3)的RGB图像编码/解码协议，并将广义Hopfield网络量子化为Sachdev-Ye玻璃型模型，其谱具有暗带和记忆带两种结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Hopfield网络在存储容量和神经形态计算方面存在局限，尤其是连续神经元变量的模型容易受到随机矩阵串扰影响。作者希望通过将神经元和记忆推广到黎曼流形（特别是SU(d)对称空间），利用更高维度的几何结构来显著提升网络容量和鲁棒性，同时探索物理可实现平台。

Method: 采用李代数方法（基于Galitski的线性化技巧），将SU(d) Hopfield网络中非线性的几何约束转换为辅助Hilbert空间中的线性代数问题；使用数值模拟和replica理论解析方法分析临界容量；利用尖峰随机矩阵理论刻画记忆回忆机制；通过广义Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert动力学实现物理回忆；最后对模型进行量子化得到Sachdev-Ye玻璃型模型。

Result: SU(d) Hopfield网络相较传统向量网络在临界容量上有近一个数量级的提升（从d=3开始），且随d增加快速增强；回忆机制对应于尖峰矩阵顶特征向量对齐，对随机矩阵串扰更不敏感；SU(3) RGB图像恢复实验成功；量子化后的谱同时具有暗带和记忆带，记忆带呈现Wigner-Dyson混沌统计。

Conclusion: 广义SU(d) Hopfield网络为提升联想记忆容量提供了一种全新几何框架，兼具更高的抗噪能力和物理可实现性（如通过Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert动力学），其量子化版本与Sachdev-Ye玻璃模型紧密联系，为量子神经网络研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Generalized Hopfield networks are introduced where memories and neurons are continuous variables that lie on a Riemannian manifold. We explicitly focus on symmetric spaces associated with the special unitary groups SU(d), and use both numerical and analytical (replica) techniques to demonstrate an almost order of magnitude enhancement in critical capacity over the vector networks starting with d=3 and further rapidly growing with d. To circumvent the non-linear geometric constraints, we use a Lie algebraic method [following V. Galitski, Phys. Rev. A 84, 012118 (2011)] to exactly describe the classical neural network in terms of linear algebra in an auxiliary Hilbert space. It is shown that in contrast to the traditional Hopfield networks, memory recall in SU(d) Hopfields corresponds to neuron alignment along a top eigenvector of a spiked matrix, which is less susceptible to random matrix crosstalk than other models with continuous neuron variables. Physical platforms to realize SU(d) Hopfields are briefly discussed and physical (in addition to algorithmic) recall mechanism is demonstrated, where memory recovery occurs naturally through generalized Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert dynamics. To illustrate SU(3) memory recall, we introduce a color (RGB) image encoding/decoding protocol and explicitly run image recovery on corrupted cues. Finally, we quantize the generalized Hopfields which are shown to reduce to Sachdev-Ye glassy type of models. Their many-body spectra generally feature two types of dark and memory bands, where the latter exhibits chaotic Wigner-Dyson level statistics that hides Hebbian data.

</details>


### [354] [Persistence probability based dynamics and phase diagrams in biased q-voter models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08324)
*Amit Pradhan,Pratik Mullick,Parongama Sen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了二元意见动力学中的非线性q-voter模型，通过平均场理论和数值模拟计算了两种意见的持久概率，发现了长期行为要么趋于饱和要么呈指数衰减，并基于此构建了参数空间中的相图，揭示了相图区域与模型不动点行为之间的强相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久概率是描述意见动力学中个体保持初始意见不变倾向的重要指标，但此前对非线性q-voter模型中不同意见类型持久概率的研究尚不充分，尤其是参数空间中相图结构与不动点行为之间的关联未被系统探讨。

Method: 考虑两种具有二元意见的非线性q-voter模型，其动力学由非一致q小组时的有偏选择驱动。使用平均场理论推导解析结果，并结合数值模拟，分别计算两种意见类型的持久概率。根据长期行为（饱和或指数衰减）在参数空间中绘制相图。

Result: 持久概率的长期行为在一般参数条件下要么趋于饱和值，要么随时间呈指数形式衰减，具体取决于所选参数。所得相图区域与相应模型中不动点的行为表现出强相关性，这一结果在持久概率研究中是非平凡的发现。

Conclusion: 通过相图揭示了q-voter模型中持久概率与不动点动力学之间的深层联系，为理解意见动力学中个体持久性行为提供了新的理论框架，并指出持久概率的行为可作为判定系统动力学阶段的重要依据。

Abstract: Persistence probability in opinion dynamics models estimates the tendency of the agents not to change their initial opinion till the present time. Here we consider two nonlinear q-voter models with binary opinions, where the dynamics are governed by a biased choice when the q panel is not unanimous. The models are studied for different parameter ranges corresponding to the known stationary states. Mean field theory and numerical simulations are used to compute the persistence probability for the two types of opinion separately. The long time behavior in general is either a saturation or a decay that can be approximated by an exponential form, depending on the chosen parameters. Based on this, phase diagrams in the parameter space are presented for both the models. The regions in the phase diagrams indicate a strong correlation with the behavior of fixed points in the corresponding models, which is non-trivial as far as the persistence probability is concerned.

</details>


### [355] [A Shared Observation Shields Collective Fluctuations while Preserving Local Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08358)
*Hu Cang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文精确计算了条件化（conditioning）对随机观测系统群体产生的效应：当一个标记粒子与其z个邻居的轨迹被共同记录（共享记录）时，通过Girsanov路径变换证明，条件化仅将独立联合分布乘以一个单项——沿记录可观测的集体方向上的中心平方惩罚。固定粒子对保持近独立（传播混沌性质，协方差O(z^-1)，互信息O(z^-2)），但z(z-1)个弱对关联相干叠加形成集体涨落的有限抑制（Schur盾）。这一条件化自身对动力学异质性χ_4贡献可计算的非正基线，真实协同信号应为测量χ_4超出此基线的部分而非超出零的部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃转变附近动力学变得异质，四点评率χ_4表征的异质性急剧增长。但解释这一增长是微妙的，因为实验记录的集体信号（标记粒子轨迹、重叠函数、均场）是由被描述的同一些粒子产生的。作者想要区分：χ_4的增长有多少只是条件化（观测同一粒子产生的记录）带来的伪效应，有多少是真实的协同物理信号。

Method: 针对一类广泛的随机观测系统，精确计算条件化对产生记录的群体的效应。以标记粒子及其z个邻居形成的笼子为引导例子。使用Girsanov路径变换证明条件化将独立联合分布乘以恰好一个单项：沿记录可观测的单个集体方向的中心平方惩罚。通过可精确求解的布朗模型校准构造。

Result: 任意固定粒子对保持近独立（协方差O(z^-1)、互信息O(z^-2)），即传播混沌性质；但z(z-1)个弱对关联相干叠加，产生集体涨落的有限抑制，称为Schur盾D - C = -C^2(aI + C)^{-1} ≤ 0。条件化对χ_4贡献一个可计算的、非正的基线，因此真实协同信号是测量χ_4超出此基线（而非超出零）的超额部分。

Conclusion: 建立了动力学异质性的可计算基线：条件化本身对条件系综的χ_4产生可计算的非正贡献，真实的协同信号应为测量χ_4超出该基线的超额部分而非超出零的部分。这一比较可由现有模拟数据直接执行，为解释玻璃转变附近的χ_4增长提供了更精确的基准。

Abstract: As a liquid approaches its glass transition, its dynamics turns heterogeneous: mobile and immobile regions coexist, and the four-point susceptibility $χ_4$ that quantifies this heterogeneity grows sharply. Interpreting that growth is subtle, because the collective signals experiments record, such as a tagged particle's trajectory, an overlap function, or a mean field, are generated by the same particles they describe. Here we compute exactly what conditioning on such a shared record does to the population that produced it, for a broad class of stochastically observed systems; the guiding example is a tagged particle and the cage of $z$ neighbors that drives its force history. Using a Girsanov path transformation, we prove that the conditioning multiplies the independent joint law of the $z$ trajectories by exactly one term: a centered-square penalty along the single collective direction the record can see. Any fixed pair of particles stays nearly independent, with covariance falling as $O(z^{-1})$ and mutual information as $O(z^{-2})$, the property known as propagation of chaos, yet the $z(z-1)$ weak pair correlations add coherently into a finite suppression of collective fluctuations, the Schur shield $D - C = -C^2(aI + C)^{-1} \preceq 0$. An exactly solvable Brownian model calibrates the construction. The physical consequence is a calculable baseline for dynamical heterogeneity: conditioning itself contributes a computable, nonpositive amount to the susceptibility of a conditioned ensemble, so the genuine cooperative signal is the excess of the measured $χ_4$ over this baseline rather than over zero, a comparison that existing simulation data can already perform.

</details>


### [356] [Solving the Gibbs Paradox by Local Free Space and Collision Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08457)
*Chuang Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需量子力学"全同粒子"概念即可解决Gibbs佯谬的方法。通过引入"局域自由空间"假设和碰撞势能，用正则系综重新推导了气体混合熵增公式，使熵增与分子质量、有效半径和碰撞特征时间等气体物性参数相关，从而真正从数学上解决了Gibbs佯谬。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Gibbs佯谬的解决依赖于量子力学中全同粒子的概念，引入N!因子来修正熵的广延性。然而作者认为这一处理存在不足：它导致气体混合熵增与气体性质无关，即无论混合何种气体，熵增相同，这违背物理直觉。作者试图在经典框架内，不依赖全同粒子概念，找到一种真正可计算且与气体物性相关的熵增公式。

Method: 论文提出两个核心假设：(1) 局域自由空间假设——气体分子在不同区域的运动空间在每一时刻都是局域的；(2) 碰撞势能不可忽略——碰撞是气体分子间的主要相互作用方式，也是系统达到平衡的根本驱动力。基于这两个假设，采用正则系综方法重新推导气体混合熵增公式。新公式引入了分子质量、有效半径和碰撞特征时间等参数，这些参数随气体分子类型而变化。

Result: 推导出的气体混合熵增公式包含了分子质量、有效半径、碰撞特征时间等依赖于气体种类的参数，从而解决了原公式中气体混合熵增与气体性质无关的问题。这表明该方案不仅给出了一个概念性的解释框架，而是真正从数学上消解了Gibbs佯谬。

Conclusion: 本文证明无需引入量子力学的全同粒子概念和N!因子，仅基于经典的局域自由空间假设和碰撞势能，即可通过正则系综方法获得与气体物性相关的混合熵增公式，从而真正解决Gibbs佯谬。该方案保留了经典统计力学的框架，同时提供了更具物理深度的熵增描述。

Abstract: This paper argues that the Gibbs paradox can be resolved without using the concept of identical particles in quantum mechanics. The molecules in different regions of the gas can be distinguished, so there is no need to introduce the N! factor. For each molecule, the volume of its free movement space is local at every instant. Moreover, collisions are the primary way of interaction between gas molecules and the fundamental driving force for reaching equilibrium. The potential energy during collisions cannot be ignored. Based on the local free space assumption and collision potential energy, this paper uses the canonical ensemble method to rederive the entropy increase formula for gas mixtures. It includes parameters such as molecular mass, effective radius, and collision characteristic time, which vary with the type of gas molecules. This solves the problem that the entropy increase of gas mixtures is independent of gas properties, that is, it truly resolves the Gibbs paradox instead of providing a new conceptual explanation.

</details>


### [357] [Nonisospectral Integrability and Exact Current Fluctuations in the Two-Dimensional SSEP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08480)
*Tingfei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维对称简单排他过程中穿过圆形被动计数边界的退火电流涨落，利用宏观涨落理论作用的凸性和旋转平均证明了最小化子可选取为径向对称形式，并通过非等谱散射构造与标量因式分解，得到了尺度化累积生成函数的闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维对称简单排他过程（SSEP）的电流涨落统计是理解非平衡态系统中大偏差行为的关键问题。此前对一维情形已有较完整的理论，但二维情形由于变分问题难以求解，其退火电流涨落的精确闭式结果一直缺失，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 运用宏观涨落理论（MFT）框架，首先利用作用泛函的凸性以及旋转平均论证，将全二维变分问题约化为径向对称问题；随后通过合适的变量代换将径向问题转化为半直线上的非等谱散射公式化；最后将散射构造与标量因式分解结合，推导出尺度化累积生成函数（SCGF）的闭式表达式，从而获得退火大偏差统计。

Result: 获得了二维SSEP中穿过圆形被动计数边界电流的尺度化累积生成函数的闭式表达式，给出了退火电流涨落的精确大偏差统计结果。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了二维退火电流涨落与可积结构（非等谱散射）之间的深刻联系，为二维非平衡系统的大偏差分析提供了新的解析工具，并可能推广至更一般的相互作用粒子系统。

Abstract: We study annealed current fluctuations in the two-dimensional symmetric simple exclusion process across a circular passive counting boundary. The initial density is $ρ_1$ inside a disk of radius $R$ and $ρ_2$ outside, and the observable is the net decrease of the particle number in the disk over a finite time. Using convexity of the macroscopic fluctuation theory action and rotational averaging, we show that the minimizer of the full two-dimensional variational problem may be chosen radially symmetric. For the resulting radial problem, a suitable change of variables leads to a nonisospectral formulation on the half-line. Combining the associated scattering construction with a scalar factorization, we obtain a closed expression for the scaled cumulant generating function and hence the annealed large-deviation statistics of the current.

</details>


### [358] [Reliability-Safety Trade-off in AI Distillation: A Renormalization-Group Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08572)
*Y. M. Du,Miao-Miao Yi,Tan-Ji Zhou,C. P. Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于统计力学的粗粒化模型，将知识蒸馏中教师向学生传递的不仅是任务能力，还包括响应倾向、拒答策略、错误边界和潜在安全偏置，归纳为"行为遗传"。模型通过单一参数K（危险判别能力）刻画可靠性与安全性的权衡关系，并揭示多代蒸馏中的重正化群类流动及三临界结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏研究主要关注任务能力的迁移，而忽略了教师模型的行为倾向、拒答策略与安全偏置同样会被传递给学生。作者希望从统计力学视角形式化这一"行为遗传"现象，建立可靠性与安全性之间的可量化权衡关系，并为多代蒸馏中的安全性演化提供理论预测。

Method: 将学生的回答与拒答决策定义为两个宏观态（macrostates），将教师视为施加于学生自由能景观上的有效场。基于粗粒化思想构建模型，推导出由单一参数K（危险判别能力）控制的可靠性与安全性权衡关系；进一步将重复蒸馏建模为迭代重正化群类变换，分析K在代数间的流动，并识别三临界结构。

Result: 模型预测的权衡关系与拒答token数据[arXiv:2412.06748]一致。危险判别能力强的教师可提升学生可达的可靠性与安全性上限，而判别能力弱的教师则限制可达权衡。K的迭代流呈现三种行为区间：K损失、稳定传递和阈值依赖的遗传继承，并产生可检验的多代蒸馏标度律预测。

Conclusion: 该工作为知识蒸馏中的安全性与行为遗传提供了统计力学理论基础，揭示了蒸馏过程中可靠性与安全性之间的内在张力，为多代蒸馏的安全设计提供了可检验的定量框架。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation transfers more than task competence: it also transmits response propensities, refusal policies, error boundaries, and latent safety biases. We formulate this behavioral inheritance as a coarse-graining model grounded in statistical mechanics, in which the student's answer and refusal decisions define two macrostates, while the teacher induces an effective field that reshapes the student's free-energy landscape. The model yields a reliability-safety trade-off relation controlled by a single parameter K, which we term the hazard discrimination capability. The predicted trade-off is consistent with refusal-token data [arXiv: 2412.06748]. In knowledge distillation, a teacher with strong hazard discrimination improves the student's attainable reliability and safety, whereas poor discrimination limits the attainable trade-off. Repeated distillation acts as an iterated renormalization-group-like transformation, under which K follows a flow across generations. The flow exhibits a tricritical structure separating regimes of K loss, stable transmission, and threshold-dependent inheritance, and yields testable scaling predictions for multigenerational distillation.

</details>


### [359] [Retained hidden excess generates memory in price-limited markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08625)
*Debraj Das*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个最小随机隐状态模型，研究股票日涨跌停板（价格带限制）对收益序列记忆性的影响。模型表明，日收益被限制后未被观察到的超额部分会部分保留到次日，产生记忆效应。在宽涨跌停板极限下，触及涨跌停板后次日收益同向且与带宽成正比，且再次触及同侧限幅的概率趋于有限值，而触及对侧限幅的概率呈幂律衰减。模拟与实证数据均支持该预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股票日收益常受交易所设定的涨跌停板限制，超出该带的期望价格变动被截断，产生未观察到的超额部分。这种截断机制如何影响收益序列的统计性质（尤其是记忆性和尾部行为）尚未被系统建模，作者旨在通过一个最小化模型揭示这一机制的本质效应。

Method: 构建一个最小随机隐状态模型：每日的随机驱动冲击为独立同分布，但被截断的超额收益中以固定比例保留到次日，产生记忆。对具有正则变化尾部的对称驱动冲击进行理论分析，推导平稳隐状态收益的尾指数和尾幅度；在宽带极限下分析触及涨跌停板事件后的条件统计量（次日均值、同侧再次触及概率、对侧触及概率），并进行数值模拟验证。

Result: 平稳隐状态收益保持噪声的尾指数但尾幅度增强；触及涨跌停板后次日平均收益与涨跌停板宽度成正比且符号相同；再次触及同侧限幅的概率趋于有限常数；触及对侧限幅需要相反的极端冲击，概率呈幂律衰减。模拟结果与分析一致，实际受涨跌停板约束的股票数据在定性上符合同向响应及响应随带宽增大的预测。

Conclusion: 涨跌停板制度通过"超额保留"机制在日收益序列中引入系统性记忆，即便底层驱动冲击独立。该机制导致触及限幅后的同向动量效应和带宽依赖的非对称响应，为理解受限市场中收益的统计规律提供了简洁而有力的理论框架。

Abstract: The daily return of a stock is often restricted to an exchange-imposed band to curb extreme fluctuations. Any attempted price movement beyond this band is clipped, leaving an unobserved excess. We introduce a minimal stochastic latent-state model in which a fraction of this hidden excess is retained for the next day. This retention generates memory, even though the daily stochastic driving shocks are independent. For symmetric driving shocks with regularly varying tails, the stationary latent return preserves the tail index of the noise, but has an enhanced tail amplitude. In the wide-band limit, a close of the daily return at either limit of the band admits a single-dominant-shock description. We show that after such an event, the mean return on the following day has the same sign and grows proportionally to the band width, while the probability of reaching the same limit again approaches a finite value. Reaching the opposite band limit on the following day requires a second extreme shock of opposite sign and is power-law suppressed. Simulations support these analytical predictions. Empirical data from stocks subject to daily price limits are qualitatively consistent with the predicted same-sign response and its increase across wider price bands.

</details>


### [360] [A Systematic Comparison of q-Gaussian Fitting Methods across Complex Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08666)
*Facundo Máximo Abaca,Darío Javier Zamora,Bruno S. Zossi,Ana G. Elias*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文对三种估计非广延统计参数q的方法（PDF直接非线性拟合、q-logarithm线性化、CDF数值拟合）进行了跨学科的系统和比较分析，发现q估计对直方图分箱高度敏感，而基于CDF的方法不依赖分箱，能提供更稳定的估计结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非广延统计（非玻尔兹曼-吉布斯统计）能更好地描述远离平衡态的非线性现象，如长程关联和重尾分布，但非广延参数q的估计方法因学科而异且缺乏系统性比较，需要一种跨学科稳健的q估计方法。

Method: 对三种拟合方法进行了系统比较：（1）概率密度函数（PDF）的直接非线性拟合；（2）使用q-logarithm函数的线性化方法；（3）累积分布函数（CDF）的数值拟合。将这些方法应用于地球物理学、空间天气和经济学等多个领域的现象，并重点分析了离散化（直方图分箱）对结果的关键影响。

Result: 结果表明，q估计值对直方图分箱（binning）极其敏感，不同分箱方式会导致q估计显著变化；而基于CDF的方法不依赖分箱，在分析的多个数据集中提供了更稳定、更一致的q估计。

Conclusion: 该研究为跨学科寻找正确确定重尾分布参数的方法提供了重要的参考信息，推荐CDF-based方法作为更稳健的q估计方案。

Abstract: Nature exhibits a wide variety of nonlinear phenomena characterized by strong fluctuations that push the system away from equilibrium. Recent studies have shown that non-extensive statistics are more suitable for analyzing these phenomena, as they can capture long-range correlations and heavy-tailed distributions that conventional Boltzmann-Gibbs statistics cannot represent. However, estimating the non-extensiveness parameter ($q$) is not trivial, and methods vary significantly across disciplines.
  This paper presents a systematic and interdisciplinary comparison of three fitting methods: direct nonlinear fitting of the probability distribution function (PDF), linearization using the q-logarithm function, and numerical fitting of the cumulative distribution function (CDF). We applied this analysis to various phenomena, from areas such as geophysics, space weather and economics, analyzing the critical impact of discretization. The results show that $q$ estimates are highly sensitive to histogram binning, while the CDF-based method provides a bin-free alternative that yields more stable estimates for the datasets analyzed here. This contribution provides relevant information for the search for a method that allows the correct determination of the parameters of heavy-tailed distributions in various disciplines.

</details>


### [361] [Scalene Yang--Baxter triples as a source of hidden symmetries beyond the ordinary Yang--Baxter equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09081)
*Pramod Padmanabhan,Somnath Maity,Vladimir Korepin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究了一个由精确非辫状不等边Yang-Baxter三元组构造的最近邻非厄米自旋链。由于哈密顿密度不满足Reshetikhin条件，常规转移矩阵无法自对易，但不等边Yang-Baxter关系赋予了它与三元组第三成员构造的转移矩阵的交叉对易性。在偶数周期链上，该转移矩阵可作为交错幂零对称性的生成函数，展示了一种超越常规自对易转移矩阵框架的代数对称性发现机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标准Yang-Baxter框架之外的对称性发现途径。传统上，可积自旋链依赖于转移矩阵的自对易性，但该论文旨在展示不等边Yang-Baxter三元组即使在无法构造自对易转移矩阵的情况下，仍能作为发现非平凡对称性的代数机制。

Method: 从一个精确的非辫状不等边Yang-Baxter三元组出发构造最近邻非厄米自旋链。由于哈密顿密度违反差形式和非差形式的Reshetikhin条件，无法用常规可微齐次正则Yang-Baxter解实现。利用不等边Yang-Baxter关系建立与三元组第三成员构造的转移矩阵的交叉对易性，并在任意链长下求值该转移矩阵。

Result: 在偶数周期链上，由第三成员构造的转移矩阵是交错幂零对称性的有限生成函数。由此得到的守恒层级完全由这一单一对称性生成，不构成由代数无关守恒量构成的广泛族。但该例子验证了不等边Yang-Baxter三元组可以作为常规自对易转移矩阵框架之外的代数对称性发现工具。

Conclusion: 不等边Yang-Baxter三元组能够在标准可积框架失效的场景中提供可用的对称性结构。尽管由此产生的守恒层级并不丰富，但它展示了一种新的对称性发现范式，扩展了Yang-Baxter方程的代数应用范围。

Abstract: We study a nearest-neighbor non-Hermitian spin chain obtained from one member of an exact non-braided scalene Yang--Baxter triple. Its local Hamiltonian density violates both the difference-form Reshetikhin condition and its general non-difference counterpart, obstructing its realization by a differentiable homogeneous regular solution of the ordinary Yang--Baxter equation. The transfer matrices constructed from the regular member do not commute among themselves at distinct spectral parameters. Nevertheless, the scalene Yang--Baxter relation implies cross-commutativity with another transfer matrix constructed from the third member of the scalene triple. We evaluate the latter for arbitrary chain length and show that, on even periodic chains, it is a finite generating function of a non-obvious staggered nilpotent symmetry. The resulting conserved hierarchy belongs entirely to the algebra generated by this single symmetry and hence does not constitute an extensive family of algebraically independent charges. Nevertheless, this example demonstrates that scalene Yang--Baxter triples can act as an algebraic symmetry-discovery mechanism beyond the ordinary self-commuting transfer-matrix framework.

</details>


### [362] [Pseudo-Distributions: Thermodynamic Geometry and an Empirical Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09297)
*Negin Bayrami,Hosein Mohammadzadeh,Hamzeh Agahi,Hossein Mehri-Dehnavi,Zahra Ebadi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于g-演算构建了一个一致的伪解析框架用于构造变形统计分布，通过单调生成函数映射标准代数运算，系统地推导出伪对数与伪指数结构。该框架在概率密度和累积分布层面推广了非广延统计力学，经典与标准非广延统计是其极限情形。作者利用Ruppeiner度量研究了热力学几何，微扰分析表明至领头阶标量曲率仅由生成函数的变形参数决定。实证部分用该模型分析WTI原油日绝对偏差数据，信息准则比较显示伪分布优于标准基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计力学在处理重尾分布和高频金融波动等复杂现象时存在局限性，需要一种系统化的数学框架来生成具有物理基础的变形统计分布，并理解其几何性质。

Method: 基于g-演算（伪解析框架），通过单调生成函数将标准代数运算映射为变形运算，推导伪对数和伪指数结构；在此基础上构造新的伪分布族，限定经典与非广延统计作为极限情形；利用Ruppeiner度量在平衡流形上分析热力学几何，并围绕经典极限进行微扰展开。

Result: 微扰分析显示，至领头阶热力学标量曲率仅由生成函数的变形参数决定，而非广延参数保持解耦；实证中，该模型对WTI原油价格绝对偏差（相对百日均线）的重尾分布拟合优于标准基准模型，信息准则对比支持其优越性。

Conclusion: g-演算为生成变形统计分布及分析其几何性质提供了灵活且具有物理基础的数学工具，所提出的伪分布在描述高频金融波动方面表现出良好的经验鲁棒性。

Abstract: We develop a consistent pseudo-analytic framework based on $g$-calculus for constructing deformed statistical distributions. By mapping standard algebraic operations through a monotone generator function, we systematically derive the associated pseudo-logarithmic and pseudo-exponential structures. Applying this formalism, we introduce a new family of pseudo-distributions that generalizes nonextensive statistical mechanics at both the probability density and cumulative distribution levels, recovering classical and standard nonextensive statistics as limiting cases. We investigate the thermodynamic geometry of the proposed models using the Ruppeiner metric on the equilibrium manifold. A perturbative analysis around the classical limit reveals that, to leading order, the thermodynamic scalar curvature is governed solely by the generator deformation parameter, while the nonextensivity parameter remains decoupled. To evaluate the empirical robustness of the framework, we apply the model to analyze the absolute deviations of daily West Texas Intermediate crude oil prices from their hundred-day moving average. Model comparison based on information criteria demonstrates that the proposed pseudo-distributions provide a superior description of these high-frequency financial fluctuations and their heavy-tailed characteristics compared to standard benchmarks. These results suggest that $g$-calculus offers a flexible and physically grounded mathematical tool for generating deformed statistics and analyzing their geometric properties.

</details>


### [363] [Ensemble-level loopy message passing with generalized-edge closure for percolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09397)
*L. -H. Wang,Y. -M. Du*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义边统计的环状消息传递框架，用于预测高度聚类随机网络集合的渗流阈值，并引入广义边闭合系数（GECC）作为先验诊断指标来评估近似的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高度聚类的网络中，短环破坏了树状消息传递所依赖的独立性假设，使得从局部统计预测渗流阈值变得困难。现有方法要么通过预设局部模体处理环状连接，要么仅针对单一网络的实际拓扑，缺乏对任意连接模式进行集合层面的处理。

Method: 提出基于广义边统计的环状消息传递框架，利用节点邻域之间的重叠模式构建逐步精细化的近似方案。低阶近似恢复已有随机网络集合的方程，二阶近似则产生改进的阈值预测。同时引入广义边闭合系数（GECC），完全基于局部统计计算，无需渗流计算即可作为近似可靠性的先验诊断指标。

Result: 在合成网络和真实网络上，通过二阶和低阶近似评估渗流阈值，并与蒙特卡洛模拟比较，结果表明GECC能够捕捉强烈影响渗流阈值的关键结构特征，框架的有效性取决于短环密度和广义边的内部一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了基于集合的环状消息传递方法，为在大规模聚类网络中高效预测渗流阈值提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Predicting the percolation threshold of highly clustered networks from local statistics remains difficult, because short loops break the independence assumption underlying tree-like message passing. Existing remedies address loopy connectivity either through prescribed local motifs in random-graph ensembles or through a single network's realized topology, leaving an ensemble-level treatment of arbitrary connectivity patterns absent. Here, we develop a loopy message-passing framework for random clustered graph ensembles based on generalized-edge statistics, which characterize overlap patterns among the neighborhoods of different nodes. This yields a progressively refined approximation scheme based on neighborhoods of increasing size around each node. The low-order approximations recover previous equations for random network ensembles, and the new result that yields refined threshold prediction is developed by the second-order approximation. We show that the effectiveness of this framework depends not only on short-cycle density but also on the internal consistency of generalized edges. To diagnose this effectiveness, we introduce the generalized-edge closure coefficient (GECC) to quantify this consistency. Because GECC is computed entirely from local statistics and does not rely on any percolation calculation, it serves as an a priori diagnostic for the reliability of the approximation. Using synthetic and real networks, the threshold is evaluated via the second-order and lower-order approximations. Comparisons with Monte Carlo simulations show that GECC captures key structural features that strongly affect the percolation threshold. These results establish ensemble-based loopy message passing as an efficient route for predicting the percolation threshold in large clustered networks.

</details>


### [364] [Sampling Free Energy Landscapes of Ionic Colloidal Crystal Systems using Machine-Learned Proxy Collective Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09714)
*Michael S. Chen,Stefano Sacanna,Glen M. Hocky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文采用SE(3)-等变Transformer网络作为局部环境相似性描述符的高效机器学习代理，用于计算带聚合物刷的带电胶体体系在不同实验条件下各亚稳态晶体结构的相对自由能，从而理性地调控实验条件以靶向目标晶体结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电胶体涂覆聚合物刷后可通过调节易于操控的实验条件优先自组装成不同晶体结构，但实验和模拟中观察到热力学（亚）稳定晶体结构的分布。标准晶体序参数无法区分某些晶体结构或晶体与非晶聚集体，而局部环境相似性描述符虽能区分但计算成本过高，无法用于偏置分子动力学模拟。因此需要一种高效且能准确区分相关亚稳态的方法来量化自由能景观。

Method: 借鉴机器学习相互作用势领域的进展，采用SE(3)-等变Transformer网络作为局部环境相似性描述符的高效代理（proxy），用于区分相关的亚稳态结构，并将其集成到偏置分子动力学模拟中以计算相对自由能。通过改变不同的实验相关物理参数（如表面电势），探索系统在两种观测到的晶体类型之间的转变。

Result: 证明了SE(3)-等变Transformer网络能够高效地替代昂贵的局部环境相似性描述符，成功计算出可达胶体结构的相对自由能随不同实验相关物理控制参数的变化。作为示例，展示了在保持吸引能不变的情况下，改变正负胶体表面电势可以改变哪种晶体结构更受青睐。

Conclusion: SE(3)-等变Transformer网络作为机器学习代理为胶体体系自由能景观的量化提供了一种高效且准确的方法，使得研究者能够理性地选择实验条件以优先靶向特定的晶体结构，为胶体自组装的结构控制提供了新的工具和思路。

Abstract: Charged colloids coated with a polymer brush can be designed to preferentially self-assemble into different crystal structures by varying easy-to-tune experimental conditions. For a given set of conditions, we have observed in experiments and simulations a distribution of thermodynamically (meta)stable self-assembled crystal structures. Properly quantifying the free energy landscape of these colloidal systems is essential for rationally choosing conditions to preferentially target particular crystal structures. For some of the structures we have formed, standard crystalline order parameters are not able to differentiate between crystals or between crystals and amorphous aggregates. We show that local environment similarity descriptors are able to distinguish the relevant metastable states, but are too expensive for use in biased MD simulations. Here, we adopt an approach from machine-learned interaction potentials showing that SE(3)-equivariant transformer networks can serve as an efficient-to-evaluate machine-learned proxy. As a result, we can compute the relative free energies of accessible colloidal structures as a function of different experimentally-relevant physical knobs that can steer our system between two observed crystal types. As an example application, we then show how changing surface potentials of positive and negative colloids while maintaining the same attractive energy can shift which crystal structure is favored.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [365] [Eikonal Regularisation in Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Three-Dimensional Level-Set Advection: Transferability of Two-Dimensional Design Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08322)
*Muhammad Akbar Khan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了物理信息神经网络（PINN）在水平集界面平流公式中的eikonal正则化权重选择问题，将先前的二维研究扩展到三维，采用多种子预注册协议在四个3D基准上验证了权重排序的可迁移性，发现最优权重跨越四个数量级且仅在特定基准间可继承，同时证明小权重下种间方差等于误差本身而正则化可显著提高可重复性。作者还对比了经典五阶WENO求解器，后者在所有问题上更精确，并指出相对L2误差无法保证薄特征的保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前二维研究表明eikonal正则化权重是PINN水平集公式中的主导超参数，且其最优值在刚体和变形流动间相差四个数量级，但这一结论是否适用于三维、以及单种子结果能否经受住运行间变异性尚不清楚。作者旨在回答这两个开放问题，以建立更可靠的三维PINN界面平流训练协议。

Method: 在4个三维基准（平移球、旋转球、开槽球、反转涡流）上重复权重选择实验，对6个权重值×3个种子在完整训练预算下进行扫描，采用预注册选择规则；对比五阶WENO求解器在相同网格和误差度量下的表现；提出特征受限误差度量来替代相对L2误差以认证薄特征保持。

Result: 权重排序可在三维中迁移：最优权重追踪精确解偏离符号距离函数的程度，跨越从10^-1（精确保持）到10^-5（界面拉伸）的四个数量级。具体数值仅部分迁移（4个基准中2个不变、2个变化）。多种子协议显示小权重下种子间标准差等于误差本身，正则化可将方差降低一个数量级以上。WENO在全部4个问题上更精确，平滑刚体平流时优2个数量级，差距随几何难度缩小且在体积守恒上比场范数更小。相对L2误差无法认证薄特征保持，特征受限度量可以。

Conclusion: eikonal正则化权重的最优值选择原理可以从二维推广到三维，但具体权重值必须在每个基准上单独验证，不能盲目继承。多种子协议是PINN训练不可或缺的组成部分，因为它揭示了单种子结果可能掩盖的高方差问题。PINN在界面平流精度上仍不及经典WENO方案，且评价指标的选择（尤其是薄特征保持）需要更谨慎。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks applied to the level-set formulation of interface advection commonly augment the residual and initial-condition losses with an eikonal regulariser, penalising the deviation of $\|\nablaφ\|$ from unity. A previous two-dimensional study identified this weight as the dominant hyperparameter and found its optimum shifts by four orders of magnitude between rigid-body and deforming flows, but left open whether these principles transfer to three dimensions and whether single-seed results survive run-to-run variability. We answer both by repeating the weight selection across four 3D benchmarks (translating sphere, rotating sphere, slotted sphere, reversed vortex), sweeping six weights with three seeds at full training budget under a pre-registered selection rule. The ordering transfers: the selected weight tracks how far the exact solution departs from the signed-distance property, spanning four decades from $10^{-1}$ where it holds exactly to $10^{-5}$ where the interface is stretched. Values transfer only benchmark by benchmark; two of four carry over unchanged and two do not, so inheritance must be verified. The multi-seed protocol reveals that at small weights the seed-to-seed standard deviation equals the error itself, and the regulariser reduces it by more than an order of magnitude, buying reproducibility as well as accuracy. We benchmark against a fifth-order WENO solver on identical grids and error measures; the classical scheme is more accurate on all four problems, by two orders of magnitude on smooth rigid advection, with a margin that narrows with geometric difficulty and is smaller in volume conservation than in the field norm. Finally, we show that the relative $L_2$ error cannot certify the preservation of thin features, and report a feature-restricted measure that can.

</details>


### [366] [Fluid-Structure Interaction and Underwater Hydrostatic Implosion of Thin-Walled Metallic Cylinders in Semi-Confined Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07647)
*Bolaji Oladipo,Helio Matos,Arun Shukla,Sumanta Das*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究利用LS-DYNA中的结构化ALE方法，对半受限流体环境中金属圆柱体在静水压溃下的动态行为与流固耦合进行了全面数值研究。模型验证了3.69 MPa的实验压溃压力，水锤峰值预测误差仅为1.01%。研究表明钛合金比铝合金产生更剧烈的压溃和更高的水锤压力（超过70 MPa），低长径比导致更突然的坍塌，而更大的约束直径加剧了射流形成和流体速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深水环境下潜艇压力壳、海洋管道等结构面临极端静水载荷，其压溃行为与流体相互作用（水锤、射流、空化）对结构安全至关重要。仅依靠压力-时间历史无法捕捉径向射流、峰值流体速度和内部空化等关键物理现象，因此需要全场的流固耦合数值分析来提供机理洞察和定量设计指导。

Method: 采用LS-DYNA中结构化的任意拉格朗日-欧拉（ALE）公式对金属圆柱在受限流体中的静水压溃进行数值模拟。首先通过与实验压溃压力（3.69 MPa）和水锤峰值（误差1.01%）对比验证模型，随后系统考察材料类型（铝/钛）、长径比（L/D）和约束直径对压溃行为、压力演化和流体运动的影响。

Result: 钛圆柱表现出更尖锐的压溃、超过70 MPa的更高水锤压力和更大的动能/应变能积累，这是由其更高的刚度和屈服强度所致。低L/D产生更突然的压溃，高L/D促进更渐进、轴对称的变形。更大的约束直径增强了射流形成并提高了流体速度。全场面FSI分析捕获了径向射流、峰值流体速度和内部空化等仅靠压力时间历史无法观测的现象。

Conclusion: 研究表明结构几何、材料刚度和约束条件共同主导压溃诱发的能量传递。该全场面FSI数值框架为海底压力壳、海洋管道及其他水下结构在极端静水载荷下的设计和安全评估提供了定量指导。

Abstract: This study presents a comprehensive numerical investigation of the dynamic behavior and fluid-structure interactions (FSI) of metallic cylinders undergoing hydrostatic collapse in semi-confined fluid environments using a structured Arbitrary Eulerian-Lagrangian (ALE) formulation in LS-DYNA. The numerical model reproduces the experimentally measured collapse pressure of 3.69 MPa and predicts the first water hammer peak with a 1.01% error, demonstrating high predictive fidelity. Following validation, the effects of material type (aluminum and titanium), cylinder slenderness ratio (L/D), and confinement diameter on collapse behavior, pressure evolution, and fluid motion are examined. Titanium cylinders exhibited sharper collapses, higher water hammer pressures exceeding 70 MPa, and greater kinetic and strain energy accumulation than aluminum due to their higher stiffness and yield strength. Lower L/D ratios produced more abrupt collapses, whereas higher L/D ratios promoted more gradual, axisymmetric deformation. Larger confinement diameters intensified jet formation and increased fluid velocities. The simulations provide mechanistic insight into the coupling between structural deformation and surrounding fluid, showing that geometry, material stiffness, and confinement govern collapse-induced energy transfer. Full-field FSI analysis captures key phenomena, including radial jetting, peak fluid velocities, and internal cavitation, that are not evident from pressure-time histories alone. These findings provide quantitative guidance for the design and safety assessment of subsea pressure housings, marine pipelines, and other underwater structures subjected to extreme hydrostatic loading.

</details>


### [367] [Set-up and Characterisation of Atmospheric Boundary Layers in the 10'x5' Wind Tunnel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07764)
*Sita M. Nair,Kevin Gouder*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本论文在风洞中利用Irwin尖塔（未铺设地板粗糙元）复现了两种海洋大气边界层（ABL）剖面：一种作为国际风工程标准的通用框架，另一种用于模拟英吉利海峡和北海风电场入流。通过激光多普勒测速（LDA）和多孔探针（MHP）测量验证，结果显示MHP与LDA偏差小于1%，速度分量标准差小于5%，剖面与目标指标（归一化速度和湍流强度）吻合良好，且展向均匀性和频谱分析确认了其鲁棒性。Irwin尖塔被证明是模拟海洋ABL的经济可靠方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气边界层（ABL）对暴露于大气条件下的物体具有关键影响，但由于真实环境测试成本高昂，论文旨在通过在风洞中复现海洋ABL来降低研究成本。同时，现有国际风工程标准缺乏共性框架，以及对风电场入流模拟存在特定需求，推动了两种剖面的开发。

Method: 采用Irwin尖塔（不使用地板粗糙元）生成两种海洋ABL剖面；使用激光多普勒测速（LDA）和多孔探针（MHP）测量流场特性；通过归一化速度、湍流强度、展向均匀性及频谱分析对模拟结果进行验证与比较。

Result: MHP相较LDA具有较高精度（流向速度偏差<1%，三个速度分量标准差<5%）；两种剖面均与目标指标良好吻合；多种入流速度下展向均匀性和频谱分析均确认了模拟的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Irwin尖塔是模拟海洋ABL的一种经济高效且可靠的方法，可为优化海上风能系统提供有价值的参考依据。

Abstract: The Atmospheric Boundary Layer (ABL) plays a critical role in influencing objects exposed to atmospheric conditions, making its study crucial. Due to the high cost of real-world testing, this thesis focuses on replicating marine ABLs in a wind tunnel environment. Two profiles were developed: one that served as a framework for establishing commonality among the various international wind engineering standards ('Profile 1'), and a second profile, which is more suitable for modelling the inflow to wind farms in the English Channel and the North Sea ('Profile 2').
  The ABLs were generated using Irwin spires without floor roughness elements, and the flow characteristics were measured using Laser Doppler Anemometry (LDA) and a multi-hole probe (MHP). MHP showed a reasonably high accuracy when compared to LDA, with less than 1% deviation in the streamwise velocity and under 5% standard deviation in the streamwise, spanwise, and wall-normal velocity components. The profiles achieved good agreement with target metrics such as normalised velocity and turbulence intensity. Spanwise uniformity and spectral analysis confirmed the robustness of the simulation across varying inflow velocities.
  Overall, Irwin spires proved to be a cost-effective and reliable method for simulating marine ABLs, offering valuable insights for optimising offshore wind energy systems.

</details>


### [368] [Splashing velocity of a viscous liquid squeezed between two parallel disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07960)
*Navin Kumar Chandra,Pierre Perrier,David Brutin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个预测两平行圆盘间液体被挤压时喷射速度的理论框架，通过修正液体惯性处理（同时考虑局部与对流惯性）并引入动量平均喷射速度，解决了现有理论高估实验值的问题，在广泛参数范围内与实验取得了良好一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挤压液膜产生的周边飞溅在许多工业和自然现象中至关重要，但定量预测飞溅速度的问题尚未解决。现有理论显著高估了实验结果，说明理论存在系统性缺陷。作者旨在找出理论偏差的根本原因并提供一个与实验一致、可泛化的预测框架。

Method: 作者分析了理论-实验偏差的两个根源：(1) 对液体惯性处理不完整——现有理论未同时考虑局部惯性（local inertia）和对流惯性（convective inertia）；(2) 实验测得的飞溅速度与传统的峰值喷射速度在物理含义上不一致。作者通过修正惯性项并引入动量平均喷射速度（momentum-averaged ejection velocity）来替代峰值速度作为飞溅速度的表征量，构建了统一的预测框架。

Result: 通过同时纳入局部和对流惯性，并以动量平均喷射速度代替峰值喷射速度，修正后的理论在广泛的参数范围内与实验数据取得了良好的一致性，解决了此前理论系统性高估实验值的问题。

Conclusion: 挤压液膜飞溅速度的理论预测偏差源于惯性的不完整处理以及用峰值速度表征飞溅速度的不当选择。考虑完整的惯性效应并采用动量平均喷射速度后，该理论框架能够定量准确地预测两圆盘间液体挤压产生的喷射速度，为挤压流体动力学提供了更可靠的预测工具。

Abstract: Squeezing of a liquid film between two approaching solid surfaces can generate a high-speed peripheral splash. Despite extensive studies on squeeze-film hydrodynamics, quantitative prediction of the splash velocity remains unresolved, with existing theory significantly overestimating experiments. Here, we present a theoretical framework to predict the ejection velocity of viscous liquid squeezed between two parallel circular disks. We show that discrepancy of theory from experiments arise due to incomplete treatment of liquid inertia and from using peak ejection velocity to represent splash velocity. By accounting for both local and convective inertia, and introducing a momentum-averaged ejection velocity, we obtain good agreement with experiments over a wide range of parameters.

</details>


### [369] [Finite basis physics-informed neural networks with hard constraints for viscous fluid flow in highly perforated domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08114)
*Jeeeun Lee,Denis Korolev,Miro Duhovic,Seong Su Kim*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文针对高度穿孔域中Stokes方程驱动的黏性流体流动问题，提出将基于域分解和局部化的有限基PINNs（FBPINNs）与硬边界约束相结合的方法。该方法通过将穿孔相关的边界条件精确编码到网络ansatz中，克服了传统PINNs在大量穿孔情况下因软边界惩罚项导致的刚度、梯度冲突和近边界流动结构分辨率差等问题，同时缓解了谱偏差，提高了整体精度，且收敛性受穿孔数量影响较弱，计算高效且可高度并行化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穿孔微结构在高度穿孔域中引入复杂的边界条件和精细尺度流动特征，标准神经网络难以解析。传统PINNs即使结合高级训练技术，随着穿孔数量增加也会出现精度和效率下降，主要根源在于通过惩罚项软性施加边界条件会带来刚度问题、梯度冲突以及近边界流动结构分辨率不足的问题。

Method: 采用基于域分解和局部化原理的有限基PINNs（FBPINNs），并结合硬边界约束来高效编码穿孔相关的边界条件。与软惩罚项不同，硬约束将边界条件精确嵌入网络ansatz中，避免非局部效应和训练困难。同时提供了关于FBPINNs局部化和逼近性质的理论论证。

Result: 提出的方法有助于缓解谱偏差，提高整体精度，展现出收敛性仅受穿孔数量较弱影响的特性，从而提供了一种高效且高度可并行化的神经网络框架。理论分析进一步支持了该方法在局部化和逼近性质方面的有效性。

Conclusion: FBPINNs结合硬边界约束是为高度穿孔域中Stokes流动建模的有效方案，在精度、效率、可扩展性和并行性方面均优于传统PINNs，具有坚实的理论基础支撑。

Abstract: In this work, viscous fluid flow governed by the Stokes equations in highly perforated domains is studied using physics-informed neural networks (PINNs). Perforated microstructures induce complex boundary conditions and fine-scale flow features that are difficult for standard neural networks to resolve. Conventional PINNs, even when combined with advanced training techniques, can suffer from a loss of accuracy and efficiency as the number of perforations increases. One important source of this difficulty is the soft enforcement of boundary conditions through penalty terms, which can lead to stiffness, gradient conflicts, and poor resolution of near-boundary flow structures. Hard constraints provide an alternative by encoding boundary conditions exactly into the network ansatz, but may introduce undesirable non-local effects due to the global nature of the approximation. To address these challenges, finite basis PINNs (FBPINNs), which are based on domain decomposition and localisation principles, are used together with hard boundary constraints that efficiently encode perforation-related boundary conditions. This approach helps mitigate spectral bias, improves overall accuracy, and exhibits convergence that is only weakly affected by the number of perforations, thereby providing an efficient and highly parallelisable neural network framework. The proposed approach is further supported with theoretical arguments, specifically focusing on the localisation and approximation properties of FBPINNs.

</details>


### [370] [Correlated collisions and history filtering: unraveling and reproducing the statistics of coalescing particles in turbulence from the ghost-particle framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08379)
*Fanxi Gong,Ewe-Wei Saw*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了湍流中聚并粒子与无碰撞"幽灵粒子"系统的统计关系。通过三组DNS模拟（幽灵粒子、带补给聚并粒子CR、无补给聚并粒子CN），发现幽灵粒子系统的碰撞核和径向分布函数均高于聚并系统。作者引入速度滤波和碰撞历史滤波的幽灵粒子核，并定义碰撞关联时间τ_cc，成功用历史滤波框架统一了聚并系统与幽灵粒子系统的统计描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流中粒子碰撞聚并（如云滴、气溶胶增长）是重要的物理过程，但传统上常用无相互作用的幽灵粒子系统来近似估计碰撞核。然而聚并粒子因消耗会改变局部粒子密度和关联结构，与幽灵粒子存在本质差异。本文旨在从统计物理层面理解两种系统的差异来源，并建立统一的描述框架。

Method: 在相同流动条件下执行三族直接数值模拟：(1) 无相互作用的幽灵粒子；(2) 碰撞即聚并且单体在随机位置补给的CR系统；(3) 碰撞聚并但无补给的CN系统。全部为单分散粒子，Stokes数覆盖St=0.01-3.0。计算碰撞核K、近接触径向分布函数g(r)，进一步引入速度滤波RDF g_G^(-)(r)和历史滤波核（排除连续碰撞间的关联），并定义碰撞关联时间τ_cc量化碰撞对后续事件的影响时长。

Result: 在全部St范围内，幽灵粒子系统的碰撞核K和近接触RDF g(r)均高于聚并系统。速度滤波RDF g_G^(-)(d)能较好估计CR系统的接触RDF。残余差异源于幽灵粒子系统中连续碰撞间的关联；历史滤波（去除在τ_cc时间窗口内发生碰撞的粒子）后，幽灵粒子的核与RDF均能复现两种聚并系统。碰撞关联时间τ_cc在研究的St范围内变化范围有限且窄。重复碰撞（相同粒子对反复碰撞）的比例随St呈指数衰减。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的历史滤波框架，将传统幽灵粒子近似与真实聚并系统联系起来。核心发现是：聚并系统与幽灵粒子的统计差异完全可由碰撞历史关联解释，去除近期碰撞粒子的幽灵粒子统计即可精确复现聚并系统行为。这为在无需显式模拟聚并过程的情况下预测湍流中聚并粒子的碰撞率提供了新的理论途径。

Abstract: This is the first in a series of papers aimed at understanding the statistics of coalescing particles in turbulent flow and their relation to collisionless ghost particles. We perform three families of Direct Numerical Simulations (DNS) under identical flow conditions: ghost particles without mutual interactions; particles that coalesce upon collision with lost monomers replenished at random positions (CR); and particles with the same collision-coalescence kinetics without replenishment (CN). All analyses are monodisperse and concern only monomers. Across $St=0.01\text{-}3.0$, the ghost-particle system has higher collision kernels ($K$) and radial distribution functions (RDFs, $g(r)$) near contact than the coalescing systems. A velocity-filtered RDF, $g_{\mathrm{G}}^{(-)}(r)$, provides a reasonable estimate of the CR contact RDF, $g_{\mathrm{CR}}(d)$. We show that the residual discrepancy between $g_{\mathrm{G}}^{(-)}(d)$ and $g_{\mathrm{CR}}(d)$, and between the corresponding kernels, arises from correlations among successive collisions in the ghost-particle system. A history-filtered ghost-particle kernel, excluding such correlations, reproduces the coalescing-system kernel. We introduce a collision-correlation time $τ_{\mathrm{cc}}$ that quantifies how long current collisions influence future events and find that it has a finite, narrow range across the studied $St$. Filtering particles with collisions within a period comparable to $τ_{\mathrm{cc}}$ yields a history-filtered RDF that reproduces those of both coalescing systems. Finally, the fraction of repeated collisions involving identical particles decays exponentially with $St$. These results unify the coalescing and ghost-particle systems as a history-filtered framework.

</details>


### [371] [K41 Scaling in Bubble-Induced Turbulence Arises from Single-Bubble Wakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08502)
*Dabao Li,Zhilong Jin,Guangzhao Zhou*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率界面解析直接数值模拟（DNS）研究气泡诱导湍流（BIT）中Kolmogorov（K41）标度的起源，发现2/3标度仅出现在气泡尾流区域内，且完整BIT场中的K41标度来源于单个气泡尾流的叠加，并据此推导出稀薄BIT中耗散率的标度律，与文献中的实验结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解气泡诱导湍流中K41标度的起源，揭示其背后的物理机制，并建立与实际实验相符的耗散率标度律。

Method: 采用高分辨率界面解析DNS，进行区域划分的（region-wise）速度结构函数分析，并与匹配条件的单气泡DNS进行比较，以区分尾流区域与整体湍流场的贡献；在此基础上推导稀薄BIT中的耗散率标度。

Result: 2/3标度仅出现在气泡尾流区域；完整BIT场中的K41标度是单个气泡尾流叠加的结果；推导出的稀薄BIT耗散率标度与文献实验结果一致。

Conclusion: 气泡诱导湍流中的K41标度并非来自经典意义上的湍流级联，而是多个独立气泡尾流空间叠加的统计效应；该认识可用于建立稀薄BIT的耗散率标度模型。

Abstract: We use high-resolution, interface-resolved direct numerical simulations (DNS) to investigate the origin of Kolmogorov (K41) scaling in bubble-induced turbulence (BIT). Region-wise velocity structure functions show that the 2/3 scaling appears only within bubble wake regions. Comparison with matched single-bubble DNS further indicates that the K41 scaling observed in the full BIT field arises from the superposition of individual bubble wakes. On this basis, we derive a scaling for the dissipation rate in dilute BIT that is consistent with experimental results from the literature.

</details>


### [372] [Data-Driven Surrogate Modeling for Micromixing of Non-Newtonian Fluids in Sinusoidal Converging-Diverging Microchannels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08547)
*Kritagya Sharma,Bimalendu Mahapatra*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究通过高保真有限体积模拟系统研究了Carreau-Yasuda非牛顿流体在二维正弦收敛-扩张微通道中的被动微混合传输机制，并构建了基于高斯过程回归（GPR）和NSGA-II的多目标优化框架，实现了混合性能与压降之间的帕累托最优平衡设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非牛顿流体的微混合面临挑战，因为微尺度下的层流将横向输运限制主要为分子扩散。现有研究缺乏对非牛顿流体在收敛-扩张微通道中混合机制的深入理解，且多维设计空间的探索效率较低。

Method: 在蠕动流条件下，通过高保真有限体积模拟系统变化壁面振幅比、相位偏移和波数三个几何参数；基于模拟数据构建多种代理模型，筛选出高斯过程回归（GPR）作为混合指数和压降的最准确预测器；将GPR与NSGA-II耦合以生成帕累托前沿。

Result: 连续的收缩-扩张单元通过界面拉伸、剪切率升高和剪切变稀引起的黏度降低三种机制协同增强混合，但同时增大压降，形成了固有的权衡关系。GPR代理模型准确预测混合指数和压降，与NSGA-II耦合后能精确复现高保真模拟得到的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 所提出的机器学习框架为非牛顿流体被动微混合器的多目标设计提供了快速、准确且物理一致的策略，能够在不牺牲精度的前提下大幅提升设计空间探索效率。

Abstract: Micromixing of non-Newtonian fluids remains challenging because laminar flow at microscales restricts transverse transport primarily to molecular diffusion. In this study, we investigate the transport mechanisms governing passive micromixing of a Carreau--Yasuda fluid in two-dimensional sinusoidal converging--diverging microchannels and develop a surrogate-assisted framework for their multi-objective design. We perform high-fidelity finite-volume simulations by systematically varying the wall-amplitude ratio, phase offset, and wave count under creeping-flow conditions. The results show that successive contraction--expansion units enhance mixing through the combined effects of interface stretching, elevated shear rates, and shear-thinning-induced viscosity reduction. These mechanisms improve scalar transport but simultaneously increase pressure drop, creating an inherent trade-off between mixing performance and hydraulic resistance. To efficiently explore the multidimensional design space, we construct surrogate models from high-fidelity numerical simulations and identify Gaussian Process Regression (GPR) as the most accurate predictor of both the mixing index and pressure drop. Coupling the validated GPR surrogate with Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm II (NSGA-II) accurately reproduces the Pareto front obtained from the high-fidelity simulations and identifies optimal microchannel geometries that balance mixing enhancement against pressure loss. The proposed machine learning framework provides a fast, accurate, and physically consistent strategy for the multi-objective design of passive micromixers for non-Newtonian fluids.

</details>


### [373] [Deformation dynamics of Oldroyd B drop in alternating electric field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08699)
*Sarika Shivaji Bangar,Gaurav Tomar*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过渐近分析和Basilisk直接数值模拟，研究了均匀交变电场中Oldroyd-B粘弹性液滴的变形动力学，揭示了六种电液动力学区域的不同变形模式、流动方向及非线性响应，并探讨了频率和粘弹性对变形行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变电场中粘弹性液滴的变形与微流控、喷墨打印和液滴操控等电液动力学应用密切相关。理解其动力学行为对于优化这些应用具有重要的实际意义和科学价值。

Method: 采用渐近分析（针对小变形和弱弹性条件）与开源求解器Basilisk的直接数值模拟相结合的方法，研究悬浮在Oldroyd-B介质中的Oldroyd-B液滴在轴对称Stokes流泄露介电模型下的行为。推导了解析解并识别了六个电液动力学区域。

Result: 识别出六种电液动力学区域，具有不同的变形模式、流动方向和非线性响应。变形以两倍场频率振荡，其均值和振幅受场频率和粘弹性控制。高频下保持稳态场对应的变形方向，中频出现短暂的相反变形偏移，低频下则发生大幅度振荡。突变形随De的变化可能单调或非单调，取决于频率和电学性质比，且在特定条件下可出现尖端分裂、多瓣变形或扁椭球破裂。

Conclusion: 粘弹性液滴在交变电场中的变形行为高度依赖于频率、电学性质比和粘弹性参数（De和Ca_E），不同参数组合下表现出从稳定球状变形到破裂的丰富动力学行为。这些发现为电液动力学应用中液滴行为的预测和控制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The deformation of viscoelastic drops in alternating electric fields is relevant to electrohydrodynamic applications such as microfluidics, inkjet printing, and drop manipulation. We investigate the dynamics of a neutrally buoyant Oldroyd-B drop subjected to a uniform alternating electric field using asymptotic analysis and direct numerical simulations with the open-source solver Basilisk. An analytical solution is derived for small deformation and weak elasticity for an Oldroyd-B drop suspended in an Oldroyd-B medium, with both fluids modeled as leaky dielectrics under axisymmetric Stokes flow. Depending on the conductivity and permittivity ratios, six electrohydrodynamic regions are identified, characterized by distinct deformation modes, flow directions, and nonlinear responses; while some exhibit stable spheroidal deformation, others undergo pointed-tip, multi-lobed, or oblate breakup beyond a critical electric capillary number. Across high-, intermediate-, and low-frequency regimes, the deformation oscillates at twice the applied field frequency, with its mean and amplitude governed by the field frequency and viscoelasticity. For cases that produce prolate deformation under a steady field, the drop remains prolate at high frequency, exhibits brief oblate excursions at intermediate frequency, and undergoes large-amplitude oscillations between near-spherical and highly deformed states at low frequency. The mean deformation varies monotonically or non-monotonically with $De$, depending on frequency and electrical property ratios. For cases producing oblate deformation under a steady field, the drop remains oblate at high frequency, develops dimples at intermediate frequency, and breaks up at low frequency for sufficiently large $De$ and $Ca_E$; the mean deformation increases monotonically with $De$.

</details>


### [374] [Turbulence anisotropy in a bubbly vertical channel flow with topological change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08763)
*Arturo A. Arosemena,Davide Procacci,Simone Di Giorgio,Jannike Solsvik,Shahab Mirjalili*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过高保真数值模拟研究了垂直通道中气泡上升流动中表面张力对载流体湍流各向异性的影响，发现除通道核心区外，多相流比单相流更加各向同性，这归因于大气泡增强的扫掠事件改善了能量再分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索气泡流动中表面张力变化对载流体湍流各向异性的影响机制，理解气泡拓扑变化（破裂与聚并）对湍流结构的调控作用。

Method: 采用高保真数值模拟方法，模拟竖直通道中上升构型的气泡流动（包含破裂与聚并的拓扑变化），通过定性分析速度波动、速度谱、重心图及湍流各向异性指标对单相流与多相流进行对比。

Result: 大气泡尾迹中速度波动增强，这些结构尺度与气泡尺寸相关并靠近壁面的湍流结构相互作用；除通道核心区外，多相流比匹配摩擦雷诺数的单相流更各向同性；该现象归因于大气泡存在时扫掠事件（高速流体向壁面运动）的增强促进了能量再分配。

Conclusion: 气泡的拓扑变化及其表面张力的调节效应可通过增强扫掠事件来改善湍流能量的再分配，从而降低湍流各向异性，这一发现对两相湍流的建模与机理理解具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: High-fidelity numerical simulations of bubble-laden, vertical channel flow in the upward configuration, where the bubbles undergo topological changes (breakup and coalescence), were performed with the purpose of exploring the effect of the surface tension on the turbulence anisotropy in the carrier phase. A qualitative analysis shows that velocity fluctuations are enhanced in the wake of large bubbles. Moreover, as shown by the velocity spectra, these structures seem to scale with bubble size and interact with those closer to the wall. Finally, a barycentric map and other indicators of turbulence anisotropy clarify that, except at the core of the channel where the largest bubbles reside, the multiphase flow cases are actually more isotropic than the single-phase flow at a matching friction Reynolds number. This unexpected behavior is attributed to a better redistribution of energy due to an enhancement of sweep events (high-speed fluid towards the wall) in the presence of large bubbles.

</details>


### [375] [Exact solution for the motion of a rigid particle with $\boldsymbol{S_4}$ and $\boldsymbol{C_{2v}}$ symmetry settling under gravity in a viscous fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09585)
*Piotr Zdybel,Maria L. Ekiel-Jeżewska*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文给出了具有C2v和S4对称性、均匀密度的刚性颗粒在低雷诺数粘性流体中重力沉降的精确完整动力学解。S4对称性使问题完全可积，所有轨道由单一守恒量0≤C≤1标记。对于0<C<1，存在两个时间尺度导致准周期演化，水平位移呈玫瑰花结状轨迹，由两个同心包络圆约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决低雷诺数下具有特定对称性的刚性颗粒沉降动力学问题，该问题此前缺乏精确完整的解析解，特别是颗粒取向与平移运动耦合所导致的复杂准周期行为。

Method: 利用S4对称性将问题化为完全可积系统。通过Jacobi椭圆函数表示倾斜角θ和自旋角ψ；方位角φ由具有复特征的第三类不完全椭圆积分表示，分解为均匀漂移和严格周期调制。垂直质心位移用周期不完全椭圆积分表示，轨道平均沉降速度化为完全椭圆积分之比。水平运动通过三个欧拉角在实验室坐标系中代数精确表达。

Result: 获得取向周期和漂移率的闭式表达式；水平质心运动呈玫瑰花结状一般开放曲线，由两个同心包络圆约束，并给出两个包络半径的简单精确表达式；证明了当旋转-平移耦合趋近零时包络半径趋于无穷；解释了轨迹上尖点的起源，并提供选择严格周期玫瑰花结的可公度性条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有S4对称性的颗粒在低雷诺数重力沉降提供了完整的精确解析框架，揭示了准周期水平漂移的几何结构（玫瑰花结轨迹及其包络圆），并对可积分性条件与轨迹拓扑给出了严格的数学刻画。

Abstract: We provide an exact and complete solution for the dynamics of a rigid particle of uniform density with $C_{2v}$ and $S_4$ symmetry, settling under gravity in a viscous fluid at a Reynolds number much smaller than unity. The $S_4$ symmetry renders the problem exactly integrable, with all orbits labelled by a single conserved quantity $0\le C\le 1$. We show that, for $0<C<1$, there are two different time scales, which lead to quasi-periodic evolution, with a significant time-dependent horizontal displacement. We obtain the tilt $θ$ and spin $ψ$ Euler angles as periodic Jacobi elliptic functions of time, and the azimuthal angle $φ$ through an incomplete elliptic integral of the third kind with a complex characteristic, which splits $φ$ into a uniform drift and a strictly periodic modulation. The orientation period and the drift rate (corresponding to a constant angular velocity around the gravity direction) follow in a closed form. The vertical centre-of-mass displacement is obtained in terms of periodic in time incomplete elliptic integrals, and the periodic orbit-averaged settling velocity reduces to a single ratio of complete elliptic integrals. The horizontal component of the motion in the laboratory frame of reference is obtained exactly and algebraically in terms of all three Euler angles, so it is quasi-periodic. The horizontal component of the centre-of-mass position traces rosette-like, in general open curves confined by two concentric `envelope' circles. We determine very simple exact expressions for radii of both envelopes and demonstrate that they tend to infinity for a family of shapes with the rotation-translation coupling decreasing to zero. We also explain the origin of cusps at the rosette-like trajectory and provide a commensurability condition selecting strictly periodic rosettes.

</details>


### [376] [Memory-Generated Transport Geometry: Curvature, Holonomy, and Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09607)
*Mounir Kassmi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全新框架，将有限记忆视为输运几何结构的生成器而非传统意义上的本构修正，证明即使在时间周期性、无旋流动中，有限记忆也能通过曲率、和乐等几何机制产生不可逆的拉格朗日输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统输运理论中，记忆仅作为已知运动学结构上的本构修正项，未能揭示记忆本身对几何结构的生成作用。作者旨在建立记忆驱动的几何输运框架，解释无需涡量、非线性本构、随机强迫或对称性破缺即可实现的不可逆输运机制。

Method: 从因果输运历史重建变形，用记忆依赖的输运联络取代瞬时速度梯度，通过联络的有序演化引入非交换性、曲率和和乐。引入与输运表示无关的内蕴曲率不变量，定义无量纲参数(ωτ_m)刻画普适标度，并通过数值模拟验证累积几何积累过程。

Result: 有限记忆在时间周期、无旋流动中产生非零几何输运贡献；框架预测由(ωτ_m)控制的普适标度，识别出特征记忆尺度τ_c将快速几何积累与渐近饱和区分离；记忆磁化率单调递减，积累动力学全局凹性；数值模拟证实几何不可逆性源于渐进曲率累积而非共振放大。

Conclusion: 有限记忆是内蕴几何结构的生成器，而非仅仅修改动力学演化的因子。因果历史是曲率、和乐及不可逆输运的微观起源，该框架适用于广泛的非马尔可夫系统。

Abstract: Memory is traditionally incorporated into transport theory as a constitutive correction acting on an already prescribed kinematic structure. Here we develop a different framework in which finite memory itself generates the geometry of transport. By reconstructing deformation from causal transport histories, the instantaneous velocity gradient is replaced by a memory-dependent transport connection whose ordered evolution gives rise to noncommutativity, curvature, and holonomy in transport-history space. We show that finite memory generates a nonvanishing geometric contribution to transport even in time-periodic, irrotational flows, providing a purely kinematic mechanism for irreversible Lagrangian transport without invoking vorticity, constitutive nonlinearities, stochastic forcing, or explicit symmetry breaking. We introduce an intrinsic curvature invariant, independent of the transport representation that measures the accumulated geometric structure generated by transport history. The framework predicts universal scaling governed by the dimensionless parameter (ωτ_m), identifies a characteristic memory scale (tau_c) separating rapid geometric accumulation from asymptotic saturation, and reveals a monotonically decreasing memory susceptibility with globally concave accumulation dynamics. Numerical simulations confirm these predictions and show that geometric irreversibility emerges through progressive curvature accumulation rather than resonance-driven amplification. These results establish finite memory as a generator of an intrinsic geometric structure rather than merely a modifier of dynamical evolution, revealing causal history as the microscopic origin of curvature, holonomy, and irreversible transport across a broad class of non-Markovian systems.

</details>
